### 1. [Learning and Reasoning with Geometry: How Generation Helps Spatial Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36022v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36022

**作者：** Rui Yang、Yanwei Li、Sili Chen、Jingjia Huang、Yi Lin、Hengshuang Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36022v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36022v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出统一几何理解与生成模型 GeoUG，设计多尺度深度编解码器，使预训练视觉自编码器可生成度量深度，并训练自适应交错推理：先给出答案草稿，再由强化学习决定是否生成深度/多视角几何证据以确认或修正草稿。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 LOOK/STOP 的收益与成本权衡：把生成式几何证据作为显式视觉旁路，用 RL 决定是否调用及何时停止，可供核查证据在何种几何判别问题上真正起因果作用，以及生成成本与答案修订之间的贡献分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning and Reasoning with Geometry: How Generation Helps Spatial Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Image and video generators can produce spatially consistent visual content, but how to use this capability to improve spatial understanding remains underexplored. We study this problem from two perspectives: geometric supervision during training and explicit geometric evidence during inference. To this end, we introduce a unified geometric understanding and generation model (GeoUG) that integrates depth and multi-view generation with spatial understanding. First, we design a multi-scale depth codec that enables metric-depth generation using a pretrained visual autoencoder, and show that joint training yields task-dependent gains in spatial understanding. Second, we introduce 3DCueBench to control geometric difficulty while balancing selected 2D cues, and find that generated depth provides larger reasoning gains on questions requiring fine-grained geometric discrimination. Building on this finding, we develop adaptive interleaved reasoning, in which GeoUG first drafts an answer and then decides whether to generate geometric evidence to confirm or revise it. We train this decision through reinforcement learning, balancing accuracy gains over retaining the draft against generation cost. GeoUG achieves circular accuracies of 97.9%, 90.8%, and 94.2% on CV-Bench-3D, 3DCueBench, and DA-2K with fewer generation calls than always generating.

**comment/C1：** demo: https://yangr116.github.io/geoug-project-page

</details>

<a id="paper-2"></a>

### 2. [Look Only When Needed: Certificate-Guided Visual Routing for Efficient Diffusion VLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36671v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36671

**作者：** Donghyeon Cho、Joowon Kim、Seungho Shin、Eunho Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36671v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36671v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 diffusion VLM 每个去噪步都重复处理占输入 89–97% 的视觉 token 这一开销，提出无需训练与调阈值的 LOWN：每步先做无图预测，仅当它与上一次有图预测不一致时才处理图像，并用获「证书」的 token 数决定该步 unmask 多少。

**相关性简评：** 逐步比较有图/无图预测并据此决定是否看图的机制，与「何时 LOOK、是否 STOP」及视觉旁路、动态计算分配直接相关，可作现成基线；其仅约 22.8% 步需要图像也提供视觉证据依赖的经验线索，但未涉及 latent 推理状态表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Look Only When Needed: Certificate-Guided Visual Routing for Efficient Diffusion VLMs

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion vision-language models (DVLMs) process their entire input at every denoising step. Since visual tokens make up 89-97% of this input on the benchmarks we study, most of the decoding computation is spent repeatedly processing the same image. Visual token pruning methods partially relieve this burden by removing part of the visual tokens, but they lose the removed information and still process the remaining image at every step. To measure how often the image is actually needed, we compare LLaDA-V's predictions with and without the image at every decoding step and find that removing the image changes a token being unmasked at only 22.8% of steps on average and at as few as 7.9%. We therefore propose LOWN, a decoding method that decides at every step whether to process the image and how many tokens to unmask. LOWN predicts the masked tokens without the image and processes the image only when this prediction disagrees with the last image-conditioned prediction. Agreement certifies the earlier prediction for reuse, and the number of certified tokens sets how many are unmasked in that step. We further observe that the need for the image differs across benchmarks, so LOWN's adaptive decision is needed rather than a fixed schedule. Without training or a tuned threshold, LOWN retains 99.6% of LLaDA-V's score on ten benchmarks at 35.3% of the decoding FLOPs on average. LOWN also generalizes across LLaDA-based DVLMs, composes orthogonally with visual token pruning, and runs under key-value caching for further efficiency gains. Code and project page are available at https://hyeon9698.github.io/LOWN/

</details>

<a id="paper-3"></a>

### 3. [Latent Visual Contrastive Reasoning with Multi-Branch Exploration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36809v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36809

**作者：** Kegeng Tang、Jingbo Wang、Yangming Zhang、Huanshu Zhang、Shaogang Ren、Zihao Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36809v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36809v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Latent Visual Contrastive Reasoning 框架，在视觉 latent 空间生成多条以问题为条件的视觉分支作为软假设，并用边界框证据构造目标表示、以软对比对齐训练，在 Qwen2.5-VL 上验证感知密集型推理基准。

**相关性简评：** 直接研究 latent 视觉推理中的多假设分支、视觉证据选择与分支监督，与单路径 latent 推理形成对照，其多分支探索与证据对齐机制值得优先核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Latent Visual Contrastive Reasoning with Multi-Branch Exploration

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal large language models have made substantial progress in visual question answering, but complex visual reasoning often requires locating and comparing multiple pieces of question-relevant visual evidence. Existing methods that move intermediate reasoning into the visual latent space usually follow a single linear reasoning trajectory, which can be limiting when multiple visual hypotheses or competing evidence regions are relevant. We propose Latent Visual Contrastive Reasoning, a branch-aware latent reasoning framework that generates multiple question-conditioned latent visual branches as soft hypotheses over image tokens. To train these branches, our method uses bounding-box evidence annotations to build target evidence representations and introduces a soft contrastive branch-evidence alignment objective. This objective encourages evidence-relevant branches to align with annotated visual regions while contrasting them against negative evidence. We instantiate our method on Qwen2.5-VL backbones and train it with standard supervised fine-tuning. Experiments on perception-intensive visual reasoning benchmarks show that the proposed method achieves overall improvements over the corresponding backbone and single-path latent reasoning baselines at both 3B and 7B scales.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at IEEE BigData 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-4"></a>

### 4. [Beyond the Readout: A Latent Numerical Frame Shared Across Modality and Operation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37422v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37422

**作者：** Oriol Juan Sabater、Alex Ferrando de las Morenas、Pau Rodríguez、Xavier Suau

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37422v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37422v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用因果干预在 Gemma4-12B-it 的残差流中学习可交换答案的数值子空间，比较文本/图像与加法/减法条件，并区分输出层可读与不可读的方向，发现多模态共享的是经旋转缩放的几何结构，隐藏方向保留完整数值并用于逐位输出。

**相关性简评：** 直接把 RSA/CKA 相似度与因果效应、读出可辨识性拆开，为视觉—语言 latent 内容是否真正参与计算及「表示相似但仅预备输出」的混淆提供了可核查的干预范式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond the Readout: A Latent Numerical Frame Shared Across Modality and Operation

**abstract/A1：** When a multimodal model computes 17+35 from text or 91-39 from a rendered image, it produces the same answer, 52. Does it use one internal concept of number for both, or separate machinery for each path? Similarity metrics such as RSA or CKA cannot settle this question. They give the same score to identical and to rotated directions, and two representations can look alike only because both prepare the same output digits. We therefore use causal interventions. For four conditions (text or image, addition or subtraction), we learn a small subspace of Gemma4-12B-it's residual stream in which swapping coordinates swaps the model's answer. The subspaces answer three questions. First, do the conditions write numbers along the same directions? They do not. Reusing a subspace from text in an image on the same operation recovers only 53.2% of its causal effect. A single rotation and rescaling maps one subspace onto the other and raises recovery to 91.4%, so the conditions share one geometry expressed in different coordinates. Second, does this shared geometry only reflect the digit the model is about to print? To test this, we split each subspace into 9 directions that the output layer reads to choose the next digit and 13 directions that it cannot see. The 13 hidden directions still carry the number, yet changing them barely alters the digit being written. Third, what are these hidden directions for? A model must emit a multi-digit answer one digit at a time, so it can compute the full result early and keep it in reserve. When two numbers share their first digit, swapping the number in the hidden directions leaves the digit being written intact and redirects the next digit in 77.6% of cases. The model thus forms an abstract numerical concept upstream and unfolds it across time: multimodal representations share meaning through relational geometry across distinct coordinate frames.

</details>

<a id="paper-5"></a>

### 5. [VideoLVR: Latent Visual Reasoning over Grounded Video Evidence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37613v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37613

**作者：** Shilong Li、Xingyuan Bu、Hao Shi、Yun Xing、Weixin Feng、Qiangpeng Yang、Shilei Wen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37613v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37613v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P0

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VideoLVR，在推理过程中从相关视频时刻的局部区域构造 latent visual evidence 作为中间推理步骤的视觉支撑，并用「视觉 token 预测」目标（在输入视频导出的词表上重建视觉 token）监督其生成，使文本与 latent 状态在同一预测框架下交错推理；进一步把采样的视觉 token 视为动作，与文本 token 一起用最终答案奖励做强化学习。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 LVR 主线中的视觉证据筛选、latent 状态的生成与监督以及「何时重新取用证据」问题；其视觉 token 重建式监督与 RL 式贡献分配可供核查是否能减少信息坍缩，并值得与文本坐标、裁剪回插两类基线对比成本。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VideoLVR: Latent Visual Reasoning over Grounded Video Evidence

**abstract/A1：** Chain-of-thought reasoning has substantially advanced video understanding in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, language-based reasoning traces do not provide a reliable mechanism for models to revisit relevant visual evidence, leading to visual forgetting and impairing their ability to reason over video content. Existing approaches maintain visual grounding during reasoning in two main ways: they either refer to evidence using textual coordinates, which specify symbolic locations rather than the visual content itself, or reinsert cropped regions or frames into the multimodal context, preserving visual details at the cost of substantial context expansion. To address these limitations, we introduce VideoLVR, which constructs latent visual evidence from local regions at relevant video moments, thereby providing visual support for intermediate reasoning steps. To supervise latent visual evidence generation, we introduce a visual token prediction objective that formulates visual token reconstruction as prediction over a vocabulary derived from the input video, thereby unifying visual evidence reconstruction and text generation within the same token prediction framework. This shared prediction framework enables MLLMs to reason over interleaved text tokens and latent visual states while integrating evidence from different moments. We further extend VideoLVR to reinforcement learning by treating sampled visual tokens as actions alongside text tokens and jointly optimizing the policy using final-answer rewards. By efficiently revisiting task-relevant visual information during reasoning, VideoLVR improves video understanding without substantially expanding the multimodal context. Experiments on multiple video benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness, especially on tasks requiring fine-grained visual evidence.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-6"></a>

### 6. [Diagnosing and Correcting Bias in MLLM for Long Video Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36210v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36210

**作者：** Xusheng Liang、Jianqiao Sun、Hao Zhang、Yulei Niu、Hengshuang Zhao、Jiawei Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36210v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36210v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 S-MME 基准，系统诊断多模态大模型在长视频问答中因语言虚假关联与显著但无关视觉片段产生的捷径偏见，并提出免训练框架 CLEAR：以仅保留问题或局部显著片段的增强视图估计偏见效应，再在推理期干预隐状态以纠偏。

**相关性简评：** 其对「局部显著但无关视觉证据」的偏见诊断与推理期隐状态干预，可能直接关联 LVR 关注的视觉证据筛选与内部状态控制，可供核查这种干预是否真正改变了答案对关键片段的依赖。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Diagnosing and Correcting Bias in MLLM for Long Video Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Long-video question answering is challenging since the answer often hinges on a few decisive moments scattered throughout the video, while memory constraints force Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to sample frames at extremely low rates, resulting in sparsity that risks omitting key evidence. Though aggressively enlarging the context size is intuitive, we argue that performance remains fundamentally constrained by the bias, \\emph{i.e.}, the misleading cues caused by spurious \\textit{linguistic} correlations and salient yet irrelevant \\textit{visual} observations. In this paper, we first introduce {S-MME}, a benchmark designed to systematically diagnose shortcut-biased behavior in MLLMs. To mitigate this failure, we further propose a training-free framework, Counterfactual Long-video Evidence-Aware Reasoning (CLEAR). By treating the full video-question pair as a complete view, CLEAR estimates bias effects via augmented views by only keeping the question or locally salient visual clips. Then, we intervene hidden states to mitigate the discrepancy at inference such that the correct answer can be predicted confidently. With experimental studies across five benchmarks, we show that our method is generalizable and can be integrated in different MLLMs, yielding consistent gains in prediction and robustness. We hope CLEAR can offer a complementary direction in test-time scaling for long-video understanding.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 6 figures. Code:https://github.com/Xiaoqiovo/CLEAR

</details>

<a id="paper-7"></a>

### 7. [RouteDRAG: Learning to Route and Control Agentic Execution for Multimodal Document RAG](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36444v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36444

**作者：** Zekai Chen、Hao Zhu、Yu Liu、Xunkai Li、Zejian Kang、Rui Qian、Quanjiang Li、Rong-Hua Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36444v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36444v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对多模态长文档 RAG，实证发现 agentic 迭代检索的收益依查询而异、额外操作甚至可能降低准确率，据此提出 RouteDRAG：用图结构文档索引与查询表示、路径路由预测 Agent 相对短路径的增益，并以动态工作区和序贯策略学习控制工具调用与终止。

**相关性简评：** 把「是否值得多步推理、何时停止」显式建模为依查询预测的收益—成本决策，与 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益归因和停止决策直接相关；但其推理发生在检索与工具调用层面而非 latent 状态，路由信号能否迁移到 latent 步数分配可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RouteDRAG: Learning to Route and Control Agentic Execution for Multimodal Document RAG

**abstract/A1：** Web-based applications increasingly rely on understanding long multimodal documents, where answering questions often requires combining information across pages, tables, and figures. Agentic retrieval-augmented generation addresses these tasks through iterative retrieval and reasoning. However, Agentic execution is not always necessary, introducing additional computation and potentially incorrect answers. Our empirical analysis reveals that Agentic execution provides query-dependent benefits, while additional operations may improve, preserve, or degrade answer accuracy. These findings motivate controlling both Agent invocation and subsequent execution. To address these challenges, we propose RouteDRAG, a learning-based framework comprising four components. Unified Document Index organizes multimodal content into a graph-backed index and derives query representations from initial retrieval. Adaptive Path Routing preserves exact answering and predicts the relative benefit of Agent over shorter execution paths. Dynamic Agent Execution maintains an evolving workspace and refines Planner proposals to control tool use and termination. Sequential Policy Learning first trains operation control from observed outcomes and then trains path routing using the resulting Agent's performance. Across two benchmarks, RouteDRAG achieves an average accuracy improvement of 3.47 percentage points over shorter-path-only execution while assigning only 79.3% of queries to Agent. Notably, it also surpasses uniform Agent execution on both benchmarks while requiring fewer answering calls. Our code is now available at https://github.com/Ereshkigal1/RouteDRAG.

**comment/C1：** Our code is now available at https://github.com/Ereshkigal1/RouteDRAG

</details>

<a id="paper-8"></a>

### 8. [A Heart Means Recursion: Exploring Visual Meaning and Communication in VLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36449v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36449

**作者：** Alexa R. Tartaglini、Zhengxuan Wu、Thaddäus Wiedemer、Martin Wattenberg、Been Kim、Yuxuan Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36449v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36449v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察 VLM 能否用简单视觉符号进行交流，发现红心等符号会隐式影响编程等无关任务的行为，且模型的显式解释与思维轨迹同实际行为显著背离；在多模型视觉传意游戏中，模型倾向发送直观的几何形状，却常无法有效引导对方，由此提出“生成—解释鸿沟”。

**相关性简评：** 视觉符号对行为的隐式因果影响及与显式轨迹的背离，与 VLM 是否真正使用视觉证据、是否经 latent 路径影响答案的问题直接相关，可供核查视觉旁路与解释忠实性；但未涉及推理期 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Heart Means Recursion: Exploring Visual Meaning and Communication in VLMs

**abstract/A1：** Do vision-language models (VLMs) share our ability to communicate visually? We share preliminary findings, starting from a surprising observation: simple visual symbols like a red heart can strongly influence model behavior on seemingly unrelated tasks, such as steering models towards recursive solutions on simple coding problems. Notably, this steering effect is largely implicit, as models' direct interpretations and thinking traces about visual symbols diverge substantially from their observed behavior. In visual communication between models, this leads to a generation--interpretation gap: while models tend to send shape primitives that they (and humans) find intuitive, these visual cues often fail to steer other models. In further communication games involving abstract concepts and stories, models can creatively use visuals to transmit meaning to one another, although some communications remain non-intuitive to humans. These findings reveal that VLMs might be capable of rich, creative, and visually-grounded communication, but their interpretation and production of visual meaning diverges from human conventions in surprising ways. Together this work highlights both the promise and challenges of visual communication between humans and VLMs.

</details>

<a id="paper-9"></a>

### 9. [Confident First, Reflective Second: Reinforcement Learning of Confidence-Preserving Reflection for Video Understanding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36467v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36467

**作者：** Zihao Lin、Jingyuan Liu、Yucheng Zhang、Yuguang Yao、Lifu Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.MM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36467v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36467v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出视频模型的「先作答再回看修订」循环在用 RL 训练时会出现「置信侵蚀」：因错误可在后续修补，正确的初始答案反而奖励偏低而退化；据此提出「先可靠、后反思」原则与 CoFire，从奖励激励、参数漂移、梯度干扰三方面约束初始答案不低于参考策略下限，在 Video-MME、MLVU、LongVideoBench 上取得 67.0% 平均准确率。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及 LOOK/反思循环中初始状态与最终答案的贡献分配和监督设计，其「仅按最终结果给奖励会削弱中间答案」的负结果可供核查 latent 轨迹训练中中间状态是否也会被最终答案掩盖。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Confident First, Reflective Second: Reinforcement Learning of Confidence-Preserving Reflection for Video Understanding

**abstract/A1：** Video models answer from sampled frames and may miss the decisive moment. A common remedy is to commit to an answer, re-examine the video, and confirm or revise it, a loop now mainly learned with reinforcement learning (RL). We find that RL erodes the initial answer, a phenomenon we term confidence erosion, where confidence means the label-verified reliability of the initial answer instead of self-reported certainty: because errors can be repaired later, a correct initial answer earns less reward, and the initial answer stalls even as the final answer's gain over it rises. We propose the principle confident first, reflective second: the initial answer must stay reliable on its own, above a floor set by a reference policy, and reflection may only add to it. Our method, CoFire, implements this principle with one mechanism for each erosion channel: reward incentives, parameter drift, and gradient interference. CoFire reaches 67.0% mean accuracy on Video-MME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench, 1.7 points above the strongest compared 7B method, and improves initial-answer and final accuracy over outcome-reward RL by 4.1 and 3.7 points on average: a stronger initial answer carries through to a stronger final one.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-10"></a>

### 10. [PRISM-BENCH: Diagnosing Grounded and Faithful Multimodal Reasoning Beyond Final-Answer Accuracy](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36554v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36554

**作者：** Vansh Bhatia、Mehdi Rezagholizadeh、Parsa Ashrafi Fashi、Igor Gilitschenski、Vikram Appia、Emad Barsoum

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36554v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36554v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PRISM-BENCH：1000 道专家标注题目，每题配有人工撰写并交叉校验的理想推理轨迹，用「感知探针」和轨迹对齐诊断区分视觉定位失败与推理失败，并提出兼顾效率的 CORE 指标，评测 25 个开闭源系统。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及 VLM 推理中的视觉证据使用与失败归因：感知探针把约 85% 失败归为错误感知，可为「LOOK 阶段是否必要」及 latent 状态是否保留视觉细节提供对照，轨迹对齐与 CORE 亦可供核查推理内容与计算成本的评价方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PRISM-BENCH: Diagnosing Grounded and Faithful Multimodal Reasoning Beyond Final-Answer Accuracy

**abstract/A1：** Final-answer accuracy reports whether a vision-language model was right, but not whether its answer was grounded in the image, whether its stated reasoning reflects that evidence, or what the answer cost to produce. Those are the properties that decide whether a system can be deployed. We present PRISM-BENCH, a benchmark of 1,000 expert-curated questions spanning five under-represented reasoning categories, in which every item carries an idealized reasoning trace written by one human annotator and validated by another. The traces support two diagnostics that accuracy cannot provide. A perception probe, in which the model answers from a description it wrote itself, attributes roughly 85% of frontier-model failures to misperception and places the bottleneck at grounding. Trace alignment predicts answer correctness for all 25 systems tested (macro AUROC 0.66) and survives length control, so it measures reasoning content and not verbosity. We further introduce CORE, an efficiency-aware metric that reorders 24.2% of model pairs relative to accuracy while remaining uncorrelated with response length. Across 25 open and closed systems, including GPT-5 and Claude Opus 4.6, the best accuracy is 66.0%. On the four categories with a human baseline the best system trails humans by 18.4 points, and recent frontier gains fall on recognition and not on spatial grounding.

**comment/C1：** Accepted as a poster at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Grounded and Faithful Vision-Language Models for Real-World Deployment (VLM4RWD). 18 pages, 10 figures, 7 tables. Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/vanshbhatia/PRISM-BENCH

</details>

<a id="paper-11"></a>

### 11. [Who Knows What - and What Does It Support? Perspective-Bounded Evidence Calibration for Multimodal Theory of Mind](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36824v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36824

**作者：** Ziqi Liao、Jiaqi Shen、Yiming Na、Kedi Lyu、Shao-Yuan Lo、Hongxia Xie

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36824v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36824v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将多模态心理理论中的错误归因于「证据可采纳性」问题，提出 PIP-ToM 接口：为每个智能体构建可获取的证据库，与结构化心理状态主张对齐，并在保留贝叶斯推断的前提下校准假设分数；在三个 ToM 基准上取得较高准确率。

**相关性简评：** 直接关联多模态推理中的视觉证据筛选：其按视角可及性、来源保持与诊断性约束证据贡献，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据是否被正确筛选和使用，以及校准式打分如何抑制无关或越界证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Who Knows What - and What Does It Support? Perspective-Bounded Evidence Calibration for Multimodal Theory of Mind

**abstract/A1：** Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer others' beliefs, goals, and intentions, is a cornerstone of social intelligence. Multimodal ToM requires inferring an agent's mental state from evidence that is available to that agent, rather than from the complete episode observed by the solver. Existing LLM-based and Bayesian model-based approaches reconstruct global event traces, yet they do not explicitly distinguish objective events from agent-admissible evidence during hypothesis evaluation. Consequently, agent-inaccessible events or semantically misaligned actions and utterances may influence the inferred belief, goal, or social intention, leading to perspective leakage and source or route confusion. We formulate this limitation as an evidence-admissibility problem: evidence should contribute to a mental-state hypothesis only when it is accessible to the relevant agent, preserves its information source, and is diagnostic of the queried mental-state variable. To operationalize this principle, we propose PIP-ToM, an evidence-admissibility interface that constructs agent-specific evidence stores, aligns them with structured mental-state claims, and calibrates model-based hypothesis scores while preserving the underlying Bayesian inference process. PIP-ToM with GPT-4o outperforms existing ToM methods across MuMA-ToM, MMToM-QA, and Hi-ToM, achieving average accuracies of 92.56%, 95.00%, and 95.00%, respectively.

</details>

<a id="paper-12"></a>

### 12. [Live-OPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Proactive Streaming Large Video Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36959v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36959

**作者：** Kaixuan Du、Xin Wan、Dai Guan、Ming Chen、Xiaoxi Jiang、Guanjun Jiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36959v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36959v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Live-OPSD：策略先在流式上下文中自行生成续写，再由同一模型借助仅训练期可用的证据提示重新评估该续写，用其分布监督无提示策略，从而改进流式视频助手「何时回应、如何回应」。

**相关性简评：** 其「证据是否充分再决定回应或沉默」直接对应 THINK/LOOK/STOP 中的停止与证据筛选问题；用特权提示重评自身输出前缀的 on-policy 自蒸馏，也可能为 latent 状态的贡献分配与监督提供核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Live-OPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Proactive Streaming Large Video Models

**abstract/A1：** Streaming multimodal assistants must make response decisions while events are still unfolding. They need to recognize when the available evidence warrants a response, produce an appropriate continuation, and remain silent otherwise. Supervised fine-tuning learns from recorded assistant turns, but does not directly train on the response prefixes produced by the evolving policy itself. We introduce Live-OPSD, an on-policy self-distillation method that uses privileged hints available only during training to supervise the policy on its own outputs. The policy first generates a continuation from the streaming context alone. The same model then re-evaluates that continuation with a hint describing the relevant evidence, and the resulting distributions guide the unhinted policy. On Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B, Live-OPSD attains an average score of 59.64 on LiveProBench, 5.26 points above the strongest continued-SFT baseline, and stays competitive on OVO-Bench (65.84). These results indicate that guiding the policy on its own outputs with privileged hints improves both when and how a streaming assistant responds.

</details>

<a id="paper-13"></a>

### 13. [V-LUMEN: Visual Lookup Memory for Embedding Scaling in Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37019v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37019

**作者：** Miso Choi、Daekeun Kim、Eunji Kim、Jungbeom Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37019v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37019v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 V-LUMEN：用冻结码本离散化视觉特征、以空间聚合哈希构造记忆键，并用文本条件相关性信号调制检索，将召回嵌入经门控残差注入 VLM 主干，在冻结的 Qwen3-VL-2B 上以存储而非逐 token 计算扩展容量。

**相关性简评：** 与 VLM 推理中视觉证据检索及其注入 latent 状态的问题直接相关，其「外部视觉记忆是独立容量轴」的结论可供核查；但检索到的内容是否被答案实际使用、以及是否等同推理期状态生成仍需读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** V-LUMEN: Visual Lookup Memory for Embedding Scaling in Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Embedding-level scaling expands the capacity of language models through lookup-based memory rather than by increasing model depth, width, or dense computation. Extending this idea to vision-language models (VLMs) is challenging because visual features are continuous, spatially structured, and query-dependent, unlike discrete text tokens that naturally support n-gram lookup. We introduce V-LUMEN, a visual lookup memory for VLMs. V-LUMEN discretizes visual features with a frozen codebook, builds spatially aware memory keys through spatial aggregation-based hashing, and modulates them with text-conditioned relevance signals; all addressing is non-parametric, and the retrieved embeddings are fused into the backbone through a gated residual pathway. On five reasoning-intensive benchmarks, V-LUMEN substantially improves a frozen Qwen3-VL-2B backbone and transfers zero-shot to three benchmarks whose images are disjoint from the training data. At a matched parameter budget, moving part of a LoRA or MoE adapter's capacity into V-LUMEN preserves accuracy on two backbones, indicating that external visual memory is an independent capacity axis rather than a stronger adaptation method. Because lookup cost is constant in table size and incurred only once at prefill, this capacity grows in storage rather than in per-token compute. Code is available at https://github.com/miso-choi/V-Lumen.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-14"></a>

### 14. [Zooming without Zooming v2: From Seeing Details to Connecting Evidence for Generalizable Multimodal Perception](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37037v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37037

**作者：** Lai Wei、Yatai Li、Liangbo He、Jun Lan、Renjie Gu、Linhan Cao、Junkai Lin、Huijia Zhu、Weiqiang Wang、Linghe Kong、Yue Wang、Zhuosheng Zhang、Weiran Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37037v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37037v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 EvidenceConnect 数据合成框架：利用同一图像内相关区域的放大视图构造需比较、聚合或串联视觉证据的问答数据，并以多 VLM 答案一致性和区域消融筛选质量，再用 RLVR 训练得到 ZwZ2，在细粒度与空间感知及多模态长上下文等任务上取得泛化提升。

**相关性简评：** 把「连接证据」（定位视觉事实、绑定实体、恢复关系）作为可单独监督的共享能力，其区域消融验证答案是否真正依赖指定视觉区域，为视觉证据筛选与视觉贡献分配提供可供核查的训练与诊断线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Zooming without Zooming v2: From Seeing Details to Connecting Evidence for Generalizable Multimodal Perception

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal perception forms the foundation of multimodal intelligence. In particular, spatial perception and multimodal long-context understanding are two pivotal tasks that underpin embodied AI and multimodal agents. However, synthesizing targeted, high-quality training data for these tasks remains difficult and costly. Our key insight is that these tasks share an underlying capability, which we call connecting evidence: identifying relevant visual facts, binding them to the correct entities, recovering their relations, and composing them into an answer. Crucially, targeted training data are far easier to synthesize for this capability than for these tasks themselves. To strengthen this capability, we propose EvidenceConnect, an easy-to-implement data synthesis framework that uses zoomed views of related regions within a single image to generate questions requiring comparison, aggregation, or chaining of visual evidence. To ensure quality, a reference answer is retained only when independent VLMs agree on it, and region-ablation tests discard questions whose answer survives the removal of selected regions. We then train base VLMs on the resulting data with RLVR, obtaining Zooming without Zooming v2 (ZwZ2). Although the synthesized data are not tailored to any downstream benchmark, ZwZ2 improves fine-grained and spatial perception, with further gains on embodied spatial reasoning, multimodal long-context understanding, multimodal agent tasks, and general visual reasoning. These results establish EvidenceConnect as an effective framework for supervising evidence connection and suggest that strengthening this shared capability leads to more generalizable multimodal perception.

</details>

<a id="paper-15"></a>

### 15. [Learning to Look Beyond Screens: Transferable Visual Observation from GUI Grounding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37093v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37093

**作者：** Ruilin Yao、Xinyu Qiu、Tianyu Zou、Yi Rong、Shengwu Xiong、Shuheng Shen、Changhua Meng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37093v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37093v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 LBS：以教师生成的 GUI grounding 轨迹做监督初始化，再用强化学习训练多轮「裁剪-检查」决策，检验这种观察行为能否迁移到 GUI 之外的视觉感知与图表基准。

**相关性简评：** 把「看哪里、再局部细看」的证据获取策略作为可训练对象，并区分 SFT 建立检查基元、RL 提升下游有效性的贡献，可供核查 LOOK 的收益与成本及泛化；但其依赖外部裁剪而非内部 latent 状态，与 LVR 表示层面的可比性仍需阅读原文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Look Beyond Screens: Transferable Visual Observation from GUI Grounding

**abstract/A1：** Visual perception is evolving from passive recognition toward active, task-oriented observation. We investigate GUI grounding as a scalable source of spatial supervision for learning observation behaviors that transfer beyond interfaces. We introduce LBS (Look Beyond Screens), a vision-language model trained through supervised initialization on teacher-generated GUI grounding trajectories, followed by reinforcement learning for multi-turn crop-and-inspect decisions. Under fixed inference settings, LBS models based on Qwen3.5-35B-A3B and Qwen3.8-27B consistently improve over their corresponding baselines. Gains beyond supervised fine-tuning transfer to PerceptionBench for both backbones and to HR-Bench and ChartQAPro for Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, supporting observation strategies that generalize beyond GUI-specific imitation. Further analysis suggests complementary roles for the two stages: SFT establishes visual inspection primitives, while RL improves their downstream effectiveness. These findings support GUI grounding as a practical supervision paradigm for transferable active visual perception and broader multimodal reasoning. Our code and model are publicly available at https://github.com/inclusionAI/UI-Venus/tree/LBS and https://huggingface.co/inclusionAI/LBS-27B, respectively.

**comment/C1：** Technical Report

</details>

### 16. [SplatScout: Query-Driven Part-Level Referring Segmentation in Gaussian Scenes](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37098v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37098

**作者：** Yihan Zang、Da Li、Shinkyu Park、Ivan Viola、Dominik Engel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37098v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37098v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SplatScout，让 VLM 在 Gaussian 场景中主动选择视角、指导分割并检查渲染结果以迭代细化部件级指代分割，同时发布 PartRef-ScanNet++ 基准。

**相关性简评：** 其「选视角—分割—复核」闭环与 THINK/LOOK/STOP 中的视觉证据获取、迭代细化收益及工具反馈利用直接相关，可供核查主动感知的调用成本、细化次数与停止条件。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SplatScout: Query-Driven Part-Level Referring Segmentation in Gaussian Scenes

**abstract/A1：** Existing referring segmentation methods on Gaussian scenes primarily target whole objects, relying on segmenter-generated candidates from which a vision-language model (VLM) selects. Such predetermined fixed candidates may omit fine-grained parts needed as targets or cues for identifying objects. We introduce SplatScout, a query-driven, closed-loop approach that places perception under VLM control. The model actively selects views, directs segmentation, and inspects rendered 3D results to refine its predictions. For part-level evaluation, we introduce PartRef-ScanNet++, comprising 261 manually verified queries across 12 ScanNet++ scenes with mesh-derived ground truth. On PartRef-ScanNet++, SplatScout achieves 0.538 mIoU, substantially outperforming existing methods. Ablations demonstrate the benefits of iterative refinement, while comparisons across VLM backbones highlight the importance of effective tool-feedback utilization. SplatScout also achieves higher scores on four existing referring benchmarks. Code and the PartRef-ScanNet++ benchmark are available at https://github.com/kaust-violab/SplatScout.

</details>

<a id="paper-17"></a>

### 17. [Uncovering Shortcut Amplification in Video RLVR through the Lens of Perceptual Uncertainty Collapse](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37211v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37211

**作者：** Jiafeng Liang、Xianhao Ou、Tao Ren、Rongchuan Mu、Runxuan Liu、Dandan Tu、Ming Liu、See-Kiong Ng、Bing Qin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37211v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37211v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出视频 RLVR 的结果级优化会放大捷径：模型越来越依赖文本先验而忽略视觉证据，称为「感知不确定性坍缩」；据此提出 BSPO，从组内正确/错误 rollout 提取推理敏感 token，并用捷径感知的视觉扰动惩罚「视觉证据被削弱后预测不变」，以重加权 RL 优势。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及「答案是否真正使用视觉证据」及视觉旁路、信息坍缩问题；其不确定性分解（视频条件 vs 纯文本）与扰动不变性检验，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否承载任务必要视觉信息。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Uncovering Shortcut Amplification in Video RLVR through the Lens of Perceptual Uncertainty Collapse

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as an effective post-training paradigm for enhancing the video reasoning capability of Large Multimodal Models (LMMs). Despite its empirical effectiveness, we show that outcome-level optimization can introduce an undesirable shortcut amplification effect, whereby models increasingly favor text-prior answers even when they are not supported by the visual evidence. To characterize this behavior, we investigate the training dynamics of video RLVR through the lens of perceptual uncertainty, analyzing how predictive uncertainty evolves under video-conditioned and text-only contexts. Our analysis identifies a perceptual uncertainty collapse phenomenon, in which RL training continuously sharpens the model's answer distribution while the uncertainty reduction attributable to visual evidence progressively diminishes. A further gradient-direction analysis reveals that such confidence enhancement becomes increasingly less aligned with visually grounded uncertainty reduction and is instead dominated by text-prior compression. Motivated by these findings, we propose Bifocal Self-distillation Policy Optimization (BSPO), a self-distillation framework that regulates policy updates with two complementary distributional signals. Specifically, BSPO derives empirical reasoning principles from intra-group correct and incorrect rollouts to identify reasoning-sensitive tokens, and introduces shortcut-aware visual perturbation to penalize predictions that remain invariant when task-relevant visual evidence is weakened. By adaptively reweighting the original RL advantage, BSPO preserves the task-solving benefits of RLVR while promoting updates that are both reasoning-sensitive and visually grounded. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of BSPO in improving video reasoning performance and strengthening visual faithfulness.

</details>

<a id="paper-18"></a>

### 18. [When Visual Evidence Is Insufficient: How Instructions, Post-Training, and Internal Activations Shape Refusal in Multimodal Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37430v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37430

**作者：** Ananth K Suresh、Aveen Dayal、Gouthaman KV、Aditya Pola、Vineeth N Balasubramanian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37430v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37430v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在 MoHoBench 上研究多模态大模型面对视觉证据不足问题时拒答行为的三种控制来源：推理期指令的含义与位置、监督／偏好式后训练，以及对由自然回答与拒答样本导出的拒答相关激活方向做内部干预。

**相关性简评：** 直接关系 STOP 决策与视觉证据筛选：它显示拒答可由安全约束、指令措辞或内部方向驱动而与证据识别脱钩，且激活干预可在提示条件间迁移，可供核查「证据充分性判断」是否真被模型内部表示使用，以及聚合拒答率掩盖的样本级差异。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Visual Evidence Is Insufficient: How Instructions, Post-Training, and Internal Activations Shape Refusal in Multimodal Language Models

**abstract/A1：** For reliable deployment, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) should recognize when an image provides insufficient evidence to answer a question and withhold unsupported answers. Yet refusal alone is not necessarily evidence of such recognition: models may refuse because of unrelated safety constraints or without identifying the missing evidence. We study how refusal on visually unanswerable questions is shaped by three sources of control: inference-time instructions, post-training, and internal activation interventions. Across three MLLMs on the MoHoBench benchmark, we systematically manipulate the meaning and placement of refusal instructions, compare supervised and preference-based post-training, and intervene on a refusal-related activation direction derived from naturally answered and refused examples. We find that refusal is highly sensitive to both the meaning and placement of instructions, with model-specific changes at the sample level that are obscured by aggregate refusal rates. Evidence-aware and general refusal instructions have stronger effects than an intentionally mismatched safety instruction. Post-training produces larger increases in refusal, but can also induce responses that combine refusal language with attempted answers. In contrast, activation intervention produces smaller yet transferable changes across prompt conditions, without evidence of indiscriminate refusal on tested visually answerable samples. These findings show that refusal is jointly shaped by external instructions, post-training, and internal representations, highlighting the need to disentangle evidence recognition from a model's learned propensity to refuse.

**comment/C1：** 31 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-19"></a>

### 19. [Representing the Unseen: Rethinking Visual Imagination for Spatial Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37437v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37437

**作者：** Zengqi Zhao、Zun Wang、Shoubin Yu、Mohit Bansal、Yue Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37437v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37437v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ImagiRep 基准，用 922 个空间问题在 Viewpoint Imagination 与 Cross-view Integration 两种设定下，为同一未观测状态同时提供 oracle RGB 及深度、全景、俯视几何等六种替代表示，检验表示形式对空间推理的影响，并据此设计测试时自适应选择表示的 GEARS。

**相关性简评：** 直接对应 LVR 中「想象状态应以何种表示进入推理」与视觉证据筛选问题：其结果显示精确的 RGB 想象不保证推理正确、不同问题受益于不同表示，为 latent 表示设计与测试时选择提供了可核查的对照证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Representing the Unseen: Rethinking Visual Imagination for Spatial Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Visual world models increasingly support spatial reasoning by generating RGB views of unobserved states. However, RGB has become the default interface largely because it is well suited for visual generation, rather than necessarily being the most effective representation for downstream reasoning. Different spatial questions may benefit from different representations of the same imagined state: top-down geometry can facilitate global distance comparisons, while panoramic views provide broader context for reasoning about open space. To isolate the role of representation in spatial imagination, we introduce ImagiRep, a simulator-grounded benchmark of 922 questions spanning seven question types across two settings, Viewpoint Imagination and Cross-view Integration, providing oracle RGB and six alternative representations (e.g., depth, panoramic views, and top-down geometry) of the same unobserved state. This controlled comparison reveals that even accurate imagined RGB does not guarantee accurate spatial reasoning, and different questions benefit from different representations. Similar trends hold for representations derived from world-model-estimated states. Notably, estimated RGB alone can mislead spatial reasoning, while alternative representations retain complementary benefits. Building on these findings, we introduce GEARS, a lightweight test-time strategy for adaptive representation selection, outperforming existing spatial imagination methods and showing that reasoning about unseen states requires more than generating RGB views.

**comment/C1：** Webpage:https://imagirep.github.io/

</details>

<a id="paper-20"></a>

### 20. [PerceptRL: Making Visual Perception Rewardable in MLLMs from Answers to Interactions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37471v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37471

**作者：** Tongkun Guan、Zhibo Yang、Mingkun Yang、Haocheng Wang、Wei Shen、Xiaokang Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37471v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37471v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PerceptRL：用多智能体流程从密集图像描述中抽取可验证的感知错误并转为固定答案 QA，以规则奖励做单轮感知 RL，再通过多轮视觉工具检查、坐标注入和 grounding-aware 过程奖励强化细粒度感知。

**相关性简评：** 其「错误裁剪导致证据失真并向下游传播」的观察及过程奖励分配，与 LVR 关注的视觉证据筛选、工具式 LOOK 的收益成本、贡献分配直接相关，可供核查；但论文属感知后训练，未涉及 latent 推理状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PerceptRL: Making Visual Perception Rewardable in MLLMs from Answers to Interactions

**abstract/A1：** Fine-grained visual perception remains a bottleneck during post-training of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), as perceptual errors can constrain gains from reasoning-focused optimization. Although image-caption pre-training improves perception, its influence can weaken as subsequent stages change data mixtures and objectives. Continuing caption supervision during post-training is challenging because subjective, information-dense descriptions complicate verifiable reward design. We present PerceptRL, which strengthens perception at two complementary levels. Perception RL uses a multi-agent pipeline to extract verified errors from dense image captions and convert the corresponding visual facts into QA pairs with fixed answer labels and rule-based rewards. Chain QA links correlated errors, while multiple-choice QA combines unrelated errors. A specialized route targets high-error domains with tailored verification, and a domain-agnostic general route covers errors beyond these domains. Perception Agentic RL addresses fine-grained and dense cases unresolved by single-turn Perception RL through multi-turn inspection with visual tools. We observe coordinate drift even in models trained to use tools, where incorrect crops or annotations distort evidence and propagate errors. Coordinate Injection corrects spatial operations in cold-start trajectories, while a grounding-aware process reward supervises intermediate spatial fidelity alongside final-answer correctness. Training proceeds through shared cold-start SFT, single-turn Perception RL, and multi-turn Perception Agentic RL. Across seven benchmarks under a shared tool-use protocol, PerceptRL achieves 71.6% average accuracy, surpassing the base model with the same tools and the strongest evaluated frontier VLM by 15.7 and 5.2 percentage points, respectively.

</details>

<a id="paper-21"></a>

### 21. [Gym-Anything-RT: Scaling Real-Time Environments for Vision-Language Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37567v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37567

**作者：** Pranjal Aggarwal、Sean Welleck

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37567v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37567v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Gym-Anything-RT：用 agentic 流程按实时交互所需的六类能力自动生成 100 个视觉小游戏，并可在「实时／暂停／放慢」间切换做对照，评测显示前沿 VLM 在实时条件下通过率极低（9.0%），暂停后升至 75.0%。

**相关性简评：** 实时与暂停的同任务对照可量化「多看多想」的时间成本与环境状态演化下的感知—行动失败，为 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益-成本权衡和动态计算评价提供基准线索；但论文不分析内部 latent 状态或停止决策机制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Gym-Anything-RT: Scaling Real-Time Environments for Vision-Language Agents

**abstract/A1：** AI agents have made substantial progress in domains where they have a non-trivial amount of time to deliberate between actions, such as mathematics, software engineering, and performing tasks on desktop applications. It is less clear how such models fare in settings that demand real-time interaction, such as playing a video game or operating a physical device. In these settings, the environment state may evolve as the agent is reasoning, or even as the underlying model is performing a forward pass. To gain insight into the real-time interaction capabilities of vision-language agents, we introduce Gym-Anything-RT. Our key idea is to construct visual minigames that demand a portfolio of capabilities required for interactive real-time settings. Gym-Anything-RT uses an agentic pipeline to automatically generate such environments based on a taxonomy of six capabilities required for real-time interaction, including the ability to perceive the environment and act under an evolving environment state. A key feature of Gym-Anything-RT is that it allows for controlled comparison of real-time interaction capabilities: we can run the same minigame in real time, or pause or slow down the environment to relax the requirement for real-time actions. We use Gym-Anything-RT to construct 100 real-time minigames and evaluate AI agents on them. We find large capability gaps in real-time interaction. For example, GPT-6 Astra (xHigh) achieves a pass rate of only 9.0% on the real-time minigames, which rises to 75.0% when the environment is paused. We also identify intriguing behaviors by the most powerful frontier agents: for instance, when given the ability to write code, these agents write code that bypasses the need to perceive and act in real time. We publicly release Gym-Anything-RT and the generated minigames to spur further research on AI agents in real-time interactive settings.

</details>

<a id="paper-22"></a>

### 22. [Can Discrete Tokens Serve as Semantically Lossless Representations of Visual Signals?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37662v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37662

**作者：** Jialv Zou、Xiaotong Li、Zhengrong Yue、Xu Huang、Yaxin Luo、Wenhao Yan、Haodong Li、Wenyu Liu、Xinggang Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37662v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37662v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文质疑「量化误差必然导致离散视觉 token 语义损失」的假设，以多模态理解为语义保真度的代理指标，发现有效比特预算而非量化误差是关键决定因素，并提出 Factorized Vector Quantization，使离散表示在大比特预算下达到与连续表示相当的理解性能。

**相关性简评：** 直接涉及视觉细节是否保留在内部表示中的问题，其「量化误差不可靠、比特预算与码本饱和决定语义容量」的发现，可供核查离散视觉状态在 LVR 中的信息坍缩与证据保真度线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can Discrete Tokens Serve as Semantically Lossless Representations of Visual Signals?

**abstract/A1：** Discrete visual tokens represent vision in a discrete form analogous to language, offering a native interface for multimodal modeling under a shared next-token prediction objective. However, quantization error is widely assumed to cause unavoidable semantic loss, fundamentally limiting the semantic fidelity of discrete visual tokens and positioning continuous visual representations as an intrinsic upper bound. Using multimodal understanding as a proxy for semantic fidelity, we revisit this assumption and find quantization error unreliable, as discrete representations with substantially larger errors can achieve better understanding. Instead, the effective bit budget of discrete indices emerges as a key determinant of downstream performance. However, existing quantizers eventually saturate as additional codebooks contribute progressively less semantic information. As one approach to alleviating this saturation, we introduce Factorized Vector Quantization, which quantizes complementary, fixed-dimensional views of the visual feature with symmetric parallel codebooks. It continues to improve at larger bit budgets, ultimately matching continuous visual representations in multimodal understanding. Trained on industrial-scale multimodal data, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance among discrete-representation models, outperforming UniAR and Emu3-Chat by 22.8% and 59.2%, respectively, on a nine-metric average. To our knowledge, it is also the first discrete-representation model competitive with strong continuous-representation models such as Qwen3-VL, including on fine-grained OCR tasks. We further identify the key principles and a practical recipe enabling this competitiveness. These results and insights support the potential of discrete visual representations to underpin large-scale native multimodal foundation models. Code and models are available at https://github.com/hustvl/FVQ

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://doctor-james.github.io/FVQ-Page/

</details>

<a id="paper-23"></a>

### 23. [How Much Does the Hint Matter? Scope Conditions for Hindsight Self-Distillation in Financial Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36082v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36082

**作者：** Johnny Yu、Cyril Gorlla

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36082v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36082v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Fin-SD：学生自行定位首个错误步骤并写简短批评，截断轨迹保留正确前缀，用同一前缀加 hint 条件化冻结的学生副本作为教师，在短 rollout 上以 reverse KL 做 on-policy 自蒸馏，用于受数据驻留约束的金融多步推理。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本蒸馏方法，但「替换为通用反馈仍获得同等增益」与「hint 提供者无需更强」两条结果，可供核查 LVR 中 hint/latent 监督的内容与施加位置孰为关键、以及在线自蒸馏的监督分配设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Much Does the Hint Matter? Scope Conditions for Hindsight Self-Distillation in Financial Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Financial institutions operating under data-residency constraints are restricted to open-weight models small enough to locally host, which trail frontier systems on multi-step quantitative reasoning. On-policy distillation narrows such gaps by supervising a student on its own rollouts, but traditional OPD requires a strong external teacher, and on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) conditions on a full reference solution. We ask how much of that signal is necessary. We present Fin-SD, a localized hindsight self-distillation recipe for multi-step reasoning. Given a problem the student fails, the student itself identifies the first incorrect reasoning step and writes a short critique of that step. We truncate the student's trajectory immediately before the error (aiming to obtain a correct prefix), condition a frozen copy of the student on the same prefix plus the hint, and distill that hint-conditioned teacher into the student over a short rollout under reverse KL. No external model contributes a gradient. We apply Fin-SD to GPT-OSS-20B on a custom multi-step financial reasoning benchmark that requires deriving formula inputs from realistic scenarios via accounting conventions. We find: (i) replacing the critique with a generic-feedback control matches Fin-SD's entire 3.03 pp accuracy gain over the base model on the FinanceReasoning Hard subset, suggesting the gain comes from where supervision is applied, not hint content; (ii) the model writing the hint does not need to be stronger than the model receiving it: hints from same-family GPT-OSS-120B, at roughly 6x the parameters, yield no materially greater benefits than self-written hints; and (iii) Fin-SD matches on-policy self-distillation from full reference solutions on accuracy while generating 9.5% fewer tokens. With Fin-SD, general reasoning on MMLU-Pro and a fourteen-metric compliance audit on FinTrust show no material regression.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables. Accepted at the 7th ACM International Conference on AI in Finance (ICAIF '26), Milan, Italy. Code and data: https://github.com/johnnyhyu/fin-sd

</details>

<a id="paper-24"></a>

### 24. [Beyond Update Counts: A Control Map for Evaluating Test-Time Model Updates](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36109v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36109

**作者：** Zehao Zhao、Shangjian Yin、Rui Liu、Chongyang Bai、Mark Ibrahim、Kavosh Asadi、Yuchen Lu、Hang Cui、Hamed Firooz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36109v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36109v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Control Map 评测框架：记录每个被计分回答之前已提交的更新历史，并分别度量行为变化、即时习得、后续保持、无关行为保持与新场景表现，以检验测试时模型更新。

**相关性简评：** 它把「更新次数」拆成更新了什么、何时、以何顺序，并分测习得、保持与局部性，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态或记忆模块的更新调度与贡献分配评价；但该工作面向通用测试时编辑、未涉及视觉推理状态，可迁移性需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Update Counts: A Control Map for Evaluating Test-Time Model Updates

**abstract/A1：** Test-time updating lets a language model change persistent state after training---such as model weights, adapter parameters, or a memory module---before or while answering queries. Existing evaluations often compare systems using update count or frequency and performance measured immediately afterward. These summaries omit what is updated, where, when, and in what order. The same total can therefore hide an important difference: one system may answer after one update while another answers after two. We introduce the Control Map, an evaluation framework that records the committed updates preceding each scored answer and separately evaluates behavioral changes, immediate learning (acquisition), later recall (retention), preservation of unrelated behavior (locality), and performance in new settings. We prove when counts alone determine final state and show, under stated assumptions, how differences between pre-answer histories limit differences in average scores. Using ControlMap, our experiments reveal three insights. First, update scheduling matters: at a 760M-parameter checkpoint, 24 schedules with the same update count yield 19.7%--34.2% accuracy. Second, learning an edit does not guarantee that it lasts or remains contained: all six selected CounterFact LoRA settings learn the new information, while ROME retains edits at all 13 operating points but fails locality at each. Third, no editor performs best consistently across models, benchmarks, and tests. Evaluations should therefore report update histories and assess acquisition, retention, and locality separately.

**comment/C1：** 44 pages, 6 figures, including appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-25"></a>

### 25. [When Retrieval Is Not Enough: Obsolete Facts in Agent Memory](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36308v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36308

**作者：** Oleeviya Babu Poikarayil、Tony Basil Babu、Abdulrahman Nahhas、Het Darshan Mehta、Jawid Ahmad Baktash、Ernesto William De Luca、Klaus Turowski

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36308v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36308v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 MemoryAgentBench 的 FactConsolidation 单跳任务上区分「检索不到更新」与「检索到却未使用更新」两类失败，发现增大检索证据预算反而降低准确率，且等尺寸下过时竞争陈述比无关证据更有害。

**相关性简评：** 其「gold 辅助事后审计＋等尺寸证据配对替换＋预算扫描」的诊断范式可供核查 VLM 是否真正使用检索到的视觉证据，以及过时证据如何干扰内部状态中的信息筛选。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Retrieval Is Not Enough: Obsolete Facts in Agent Memory

**abstract/A1：** Memory-agent benchmarks often collapse retrieval and answer generation into one score, making it hard to tell whether a failure comes from missing an update or from not using an update that was already retrieved. We examine this distinction on the single-hop FactConsolidation task of MemoryAgentBench. With Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct on 6K-history BM25 accuracy drops from 45% to 32% when the retrieved evidence budget grows from 64 to 512 tokens, even though a gold-assisted post-hoc audit finds the newest statement in every retrieved set. Most of the errors occur on questions that also contain an obsolete statement version. Under a frozen prompt and equal four-statement evidence size, replacing one unrelated statement with the obsolete competing statement reduces accuracy from 100% to 44.6% for Qwen2.5 and from 100% to 87.8% for Qwen3-14B, with all 50 harmful paired flips classified as stale. A 32K-history replication likewise favors BM25-64 over BM25-512 (69% versus 58%). These results suggest that retrieving the correct fact is not enough. An obsolete competing statement can be more harmful than an unrelated piece of evidence of the same size.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Personalized, Aligned, Long-Term Memory for AI Systems (PALM). 23 pages, including supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-26"></a>

### 26. [Identity or Evidence? Auditing Evidence Dependence in Agentic Life-Science Benchmarks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36510v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36510

**作者：** Danai Brilli、Maria Georgila

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36510v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36510v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在临床结果基准及 Biomni、LAB-Bench 任务上独立操纵实体身份与证据，并通过身份盲化、证据删减和无证据回忆探针，审计智能体得分是否真正由证据而非名称先验决定。

**相关性简评：** 其身份盲化与无证据正对照可供核查 VLM/LVR 回答是否真正依赖视觉证据而非实体或语言先验，可能为视觉证据筛选与基准效度诊断提供借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Identity or Evidence? Auditing Evidence Dependence in Agentic Life-Science Benchmarks

**abstract/A1：** Agentic life-science benchmarks give an agent evidence about a named entity and score an answer against a recorded outcome. That score tests reasoning over evidence only if the evidence, rather than the name, determines the answer. We examine this distinction by varying identity and evidence independently on a clinical-outcome benchmark and published tasks from Biomni and LAB-Bench. Blinding identity matters little when a prompt contains a decisive sequence or passage. It matters a great deal when the prompt is mostly a name and candidate list (0.742 named versus 0.082 blinded; chance 0.093). Across 96 LAB-Bench LitQA2 questions, the gap increases under one fixed passage-redaction scheme (+0.000 to +0.135; per-instance slope +0.147, p= 0.004). On the clinical benchmark, a no-evidence recall probe matches prediction accuracy and recovers a database-specific programme count. So, we argue that benchmarks intended to measure evidence-dependent reasoning should report identity-blinded results, retrieval telemetry, and positive controls.

</details>

<a id="paper-27"></a>

### 27. [Monitor Accuracy Isn't the Answer: A Decision-Value Framework for Evaluating AI Agent Restart Monitors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36544v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36544

**作者：** Siddharth Jain、Venkat N. Vedam

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36544v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36544v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文主张以决策价值而非分类准确率评估 AI agent 的重启监控器，给出「决策充分性」判据与有限样本、无额外模型调用的决策审计，并在 SWE-bench 代码 agent 轨迹上检验重启收益的稳定性。

**相关性简评：** 其决策充分性与统计审计思路可供核查如何评价 LVR 中支撑 STOP／重启判断的 latent 进度表示，提示监控准确率本身可能误导；从代码 agent 迁移到多模态推理状态仍需验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Monitor Accuracy Isn't the Answer: A Decision-Value Framework for Evaluating AI Agent Restart Monitors

**abstract/A1：** Classification accuracy and true-positive and false-positive rates alone do not determine whether restarting an AI agent improves success. Using an exact alarm-to-action identity for an independent clean restart with zero monitoring overhead, we show analytically that a monitor with fixed 90% accuracy can swing from a five-percentage-point loss to a 35-point gain in success rate, depending solely on the replacement attempt's residual-budget success probability. We introduce two techniques for evaluating monitors and progress signals in decision terms. First, a decision-sufficiency criterion, a binary-action specialization of Blackwell's comparison of experiments, identifies which progress representations or context compressions preserve the information needed to choose optimally between the specified actions, independent of trace reconstruction fidelity. Second, a finite-sample, model-free decision audit uses simultaneous binomial intervals over existing agent traces, without additional model calls, to certify a preference for "restart" or "continue" under stated sampling assumptions, or return "abstain" when evidence is insufficient. In a study of 194 externally evaluated model executions on 19 selected SymPy issues from SWE-bench Verified, with a retrospective audit of 130 of these traces, we find a 2.29-point apparent restart gain traced to a single surviving failed attempt, a 42.29-point loss on a second model, no positive replication of the initial gain on prospective task cohorts, and zero of 103 live comparisons meeting the specified certification bar. These results motivate evaluating agent monitors through recovery opportunity, decision value, and statistical support, rather than classification performance alone.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 6 figures, 14 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-28"></a>

### 28. [Select-TTT: Query-Conditioned Selective Fast Weight Updates for In-Place Test-Time Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36794v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36794

**作者：** Yue Wu、Yang Zhang、Weinan Gan、Chongming Gao、Xinsen Guo、Xiaoyuan Li、Fuli Feng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36794v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36794v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文发现 In-Place TTT 在真实长上下文问答中收益消失，归因于等强度写入使无关内容稀释记忆，遂提出 Select-TTT：用冻结输出头读出的 query token 概率作为无监督相关性信号，经温度 softmax 调节各 chunk 的 fast weight 写入强度。

**相关性简评：** 其「按 query 相关性决定写入强度、抑制无关内容稀释」的可核查线索，可能对应 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 状态信息坍缩问题；但实验仅在纯文本长上下文 QA 上，迁移到多模态 latent 状态尚属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Select-TTT: Query-Conditioned Selective Fast Weight Updates for In-Place Test-Time Training

**abstract/A1：** Test-time training (TTT) enables a deployed language model to internalize incoming context on-the-fly into a small set of fast-weight parameters, rather than merely treating it as transient context for attention. In-Place TTT recently made this idea practical for large language models by repurposing the down-projection of standard MLP blocks as fast weights. However, we find that its benefit largely disappears on realistic web long-context question answering. We attribute this to In-Place TTT's uniform write schedule: every chunk is written with equal strength, so irrelevant content dilutes query-relevant information in fast-weight memory. Building on this insight, we propose Select-TTT, a model-agnostic, query-conditioned rule for In-Place TTT that scales each chunk's write strength by its relevance to the user query. This relevance signal requires no supervision and no new parameters: it is read directly from the model's frozen output head, as the probability mass that a chunk's hidden states assign to the query tokens. A temperature-scaled softmax turns these scores into learning-rate multipliers, leaving the chunk-wise, context-parallel computation of In-Place TTT intact. Select-TTT consistently improves long-context question answering on two benchmarks and two backbones. These findings highlight the value of query-conditioned fast weight writes for practical test-time training on long-context question answering, which is also potentially promising for long-horizon agent tasks involving multiple user queries over evolving contexts.

</details>

<a id="paper-29"></a>

### 29. [HiLo-RD: Cross-Budget Self-Distillation for Masked Diffusion Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36901v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36901

**作者：** Xiang Li、Chengting Yu、Jungang Li、Yilun Zheng、Junzhuo Li、Xingyao Zhang、Wenbo Su、Xuming Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36901v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36901v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HiLo-RD：把同一模型在大窗口下的教师状态与小窗口裁剪视图配对，在「已提交位置」匹配隐藏表示做自蒸馏，以缓解 masked diffusion 语言模型在预算受限时过早固定答案片段的问题，并提升低预算下的数学与代码生成表现。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本扩散模型、不含视觉输入，但其关于「何时固定/提交 token」的顺序监督与大窗口轨迹向小预算解码的迁移，可能为 LVR 的 STOP 决策与预算受限下的 latent 状态训练提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HiLo-RD: Cross-Budget Self-Distillation for Masked Diffusion Language Models

**abstract/A1：** We propose HiLo-RD (High-to-Low Budget Representation Distillation) to improve masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) under a limited generation budget. Unlike autoregressive models, MDLMs choose both what to generate and where to commit it. This creates two coupled challenges: predicting useful content before reasoning is complete, and deciding when to fix answer tokens that shape later predictions. We identify budget-induced premature commitment: even when a correct solution fits, the model can fix answer fragments near the window boundary before resolving intermediate steps. These fragments become context for later predictions and redirect subsequent reasoning. Existing post-training methods do not directly transfer the stronger trajectories that the same model follows with a larger window. HiLo-RD addresses this gap by (1) pairing large-window teacher states with cropped small-window views and (2) matching their hidden representations at committed positions. This supervision guides both token prediction and commitment order without an external teacher. Across mathematics benchmarks, HiLo-RD improves low-budget accuracy and shortens responses; these gains extend to code generation without code-domain training. On GSM8K, accuracy rises by 9.4 percentage points as mean readable length falls by about 25%. Attention diagnostics and fixed-budget order interventions support the proposed mechanism. Together, these results show that large-window reasoning can improve small-window decoding.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 9 figures, 3 tables; Under review

</details>

<a id="paper-30"></a>

### 30. [Vision-Language Agents Can Learn in Their Own Latent Worlds](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36925v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36925

**作者：** Haoxi Li、Sikai Bai、Jianfei Ma、Qinglin Hou、Xiaocheng Lu、Tao Han、Jie Zhang、Song Guo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36925v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36925v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Dwell，一个基于模型的强化学习框架，将预训练 VLM 改造成循环 latent 转移算子，使其在自身表示空间中预测动作后果、进行 latent imagination，并持续从真实交互中学习。

**相关性简评：** 虽面向视觉 agent 控制而非 LVR 基准，但其不重建未来、仅学习对动作有用的 latent 动态与想象式状态更新，可能为 LVR 的 latent 状态生成和训练监督提供方法线索，值得核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Vision-Language Agents Can Learn in Their Own Latent Worlds

**abstract/A1：** Learning from experience is essential for building the next generation of agents that continually expand their capabilities. Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) already internalize rich world knowledge, yet this knowledge is rarely leveraged to drive policy improvement through online interaction. Realizing this potential efficiently remains challenging, as policy updates shift the agent's interaction distribution, requiring the world model to adapt accordingly, while continually updating large pretrained models is computationally expensive. To address this challenge, we introduce Dwell, a model-based reinforcement learning (RL) framework that reframes a VLM as a recurrent latent transition operator, endowing it with the ability to predict, adjust, and act in latent space. Leveraging the VLM's own representations and its dual role as both world model and policy, the agent continually learns from real interactions to anticipate and evaluate action outcomes, and from latent imagination to improve its behavior. Across five interactive visual environments, Dwell achieves the strongest overall performance among trainable methods while substantially reducing the compute required for imagination, showing that effective world models for VLM agents need not reconstruct the future; they need to predict futures that are useful for action. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Tom111-lhx/Dwell

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-31"></a>

### 31. [TDLM: Adaptive Temporal Integration for Diffusion Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36940v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36940

**作者：** Bohang Sun、Max Zhu、Francesco Caso、Christopher Irwin、Jialin Yu、Pietro Liò

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36940v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36940v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TDLM，在冻结的掩码扩散语言模型上加入时间自条件化模块，让未解码位置通过因果时间注意力读取自身历史隐藏表示，并用自生成轨迹与交叉熵训练，在 LLaDA 和 Dream 上以少量额外参数换取最高约 7.15 倍的生成加速。

**相关性简评：** 其「跨去噪步保留并复用同一位置隐状态」的机制与训练监督，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的跨步更新、重复计算复用及轨迹训练提供可直接核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TDLM: Adaptive Temporal Integration for Diffusion Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Masked diffusion language models repeatedly compute representations for unresolved tokens, but conventional denoisers do not explicitly retain these representations for reuse across steps. We introduce Temporal Self-Conditioning Diffusion Language Model (TDLM), which lets each unresolved position attend to its own history of hidden representations within a frozen denoiser. This gives later predictions access to computation already performed. Training uses self-generated trajectories and a simple cross-entropy objective for tokens revealed between consecutively sampled decoding steps, while causal temporal attention permits parallel evaluation during training. On LLaDA, the temporal modules add only approximately 0.84% trainable parameters. Across LLaDA and Dream, the main comparison shows up to 7.15$\\times$ faster generation than the corresponding original diffusion language model, with competitive answer quality on the evaluated tasks. These results motivate temporal memory as a practical way to make more effective use of repeated denoising computation.

</details>

<a id="paper-32"></a>

### 32. [Compote: Collapse-Resistant Multiple Hypothesis Learning For Sequential Recommendation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37005v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37005

**作者：** Louis Castres Saint Martin、Clement Calauzenes、Alain Rakotomamonjy、Patrick Gallinari

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37005v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37005v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 COMPOTE 多假设 Transformer 序列推荐方法，用从均匀监督退火到 winner-takes-all 的目标训练，使各假设专门化并避免头坍缩，推理时采用无归一化的最大相似度规则。

**相关性简评：** 其防止多假设坍缩的退火监督与 max-similarity 聚合，可能为 LVR 中维持多种 latent 假设、避免表示坍缩及假设筛选提供方法线索，需核查向视觉推理迁移的可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Compote: Collapse-Resistant Multiple Hypothesis Learning For Sequential Recommendation

**abstract/A1：** Next-item prediction is inherently ambiguous: a single interaction history often admits multiple plausible continuations across distinct catalogue regions. Standard sequential recommenders compress these alternatives into a single prediction, conflating user intents. We introduce COllapse-resistant Multiple hyPOThesis lEarning (COMPOTE), a multi-hypothesis transformer trained with an annealed objective that transitions from uniform supervision to a winner-takes-all assignment. This mechanism forces hypotheses to specialize into distinct catalogue regions, while inference relies on an efficient, normalizer-free max-similarity rule. Theoretically, we show that in the population case, the optimal solutions partition the conditional next-item distribution according to a max-entropy quantization principle, while pooled scores preserve the Bayes-optimal ranking. We also prove a generalization bound scaling logarithmically with the number of hypotheses. Across three Amazon Reviews 2023 benchmarks, COMPOTE outperforms strong ID-based, generative, and multi-interest baselines using item IDs alone. Matched-capacity ablations confirm that performance gains stem directly from the annealed objective, preventing the head collapse observed in conventional multi-head architectures, rather than added capacity, ensembling, or routing.

</details>

<a id="paper-33"></a>

### 33. [Prospector: learning a compact graph traversal policy for scientific reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37121v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37121

**作者：** Haiqian Yang、Markus J. Buehler

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cond-mat.mtrl-sci、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37121v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37121v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 训练紧凑模型 Prospector，在领域知识图谱上逐步调用工具、依据已有证据逐边遍历构建上下文并以可核查的证据链作答，仅在半导体图谱训练即可迁移到其他领域图谱。

**相关性简评：** 其「latent 状态指示下一步动作、调制 latent 可切换行为模式」以及按证据逐步推进的轨迹，可能为 LVR 的动态取证、状态使用与 LOOK/STOP 决策提供可供核查的线索，但无视觉模态、需自行判断迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Prospector: learning a compact graph traversal policy for scientific reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Scientific reasoning in specialized engineering fields depends on knowledge scattered across the literature, often proprietary and absent from frontier LLM training data. In such domains, frontier models tend to give generic answers. Knowledge graphs (KGs) can ground LLM reasoning. Because a graph is too large to provide in full, typical LLM+KG systems preselect a portion, deciding relevance before reasoning begins. Here we train a compact model, Prospector, to reason over a domain knowledge graph by traversing it step by step, seeking evidence in light of what it has already found. Prospector calls a small set of tools one at a time to progressively build its context, and answers with a chain of evidence that can be checked edge by edge against what its tools retrieved from the graph. On a knowledge graph built from the semiconductor-manufacturing literature, Prospector approaches the strongest off-the-shelf models given the same graph and tools, and it far exceeds off-the-shelf models answering closed-book or from a pre-selected portion of the graph. Although trained only on the semiconductor graph, Prospector transfers to unseen knowledge graphs from three other distinct fields: bio-inspired materials, per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFAS) and materials properties. Served on a single GPU, Prospector answers faster and at a fraction of the cost of a hosted service, while keeping proprietary data on the owner's hardware. We find that the policy's latent state is indicative of its next move on both seen and unseen graphs, and modulating the latent state can steer the policy from one behavior mode to another. These results suggest a practical LLM + KG paradigm for scientific and engineering reasoning over private data, in which a compact model trained on a graph from published literature transfers its traversal to unseen graphs that stay with their owners.

</details>

<a id="paper-34"></a>

### 34. [Uncovering Why LLMs Fail: From Internal Mechanisms to Adversarial Reasoning Trajectories](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37369v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37369

**作者：** Zirui Ding、Xinxin Fan、Hao Yuan、Shaoye Luo、Men Niu、Yunfeng Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37369v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37369v1>)
