**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出白盒诊断框架 CauTrace，用七种功能状态、表示传播、时序状态协调与轨迹拓扑刻画 LLM 推理失败的内部成因，并在 Qwen 与 LLaMA 系模型上对比自然失败与配对对抗扰动。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本诊断，但其「承诺与验证的相对时序」「状态更新中 Attention-MLP 一致性」等过程指标，可供核查用于分析 LVR 的 latent 状态更新质量及 THINK/LOOK/STOP 时机与停止决策的失败模式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Uncovering Why LLMs Fail: From Internal Mechanisms to Adversarial Reasoning Trajectories

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) are usually evaluated by final-output correctness or safety, yet an endpoint label does not reveal how a failure develops throughout the reasoning process. We therefore ask whether ordinary reasoning failures and adversarially induced failures can be characterized using the same internal coordinates. Towards this end, we in this paper study a white-box diagnostic framework CauTrace to disclose the root-cause of why LLM fails from three fundamental inspections: Functional States, Propagation of Representation, Temporal-State Coordination and Reasoning Trajectory. First, seven functional states are creatively defined to represent the support and constraint processing in LLMs. Second, the propagation of representation change measures how the changes among the seven states persist across subsequent Transformer blocks. Third, temporal-state coordination and trajectory topology characterize when the commitment and verification occur relative to the support, and how the dominant state sequence evolves over the full inference process. Finally, we evaluate CauTrace on both clean reasoning failures and paired adversarial perturbations. Across Qwen and LLaMA-family models, the failed traces show weaker Attention-MLP agreement on support-state updates and less verification activity relative to commitment, while the exact representation-level signatures and timing shifts vary by architecture. Held-out repair and steering tests demonstrate consistent operational signals that were in line with our claims for certain models and specific settings, with aligned steering producing the predicted directional changes in process measures. Overall, CauTrace provides a unified diagnostic framework for analyzing failure dynamics through changes in support, commitment, and verification, which would shed light on the in-depth research on LLM security.

</details>

<a id="paper-35"></a>

### 35. [Rare, Relevant, and Still Wrong: How Memory Fails LLM Group Decisions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37624v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37624

**作者：** Anouar Imel、Imad Charradi、Abderrahman Sghir、Khalil El Khammar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.MA、cs.SI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37624v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37624v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CrossProfile 诊断基准（60 个模板、480 个任务），在隐藏画像任务中交叉控制支持、诱饵与无关事实的归属方而固定其文本，考察把 LLM 群体讨论压缩成简短记忆时「保留最稀有主张」与「保留最相关主张」两种策略的失效模式，以及决策模型对多智能体归属标签的社会证明偏差。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本多智能体场景，但其「记忆保留哪类证据、准确率取决于证据平衡而非筛选策略」的结论，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态保留何种证据及信息坍缩的评价方式；归属标签带来的额外权重也类似 latent 相似度中的位置或表面线索混淆。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rare, Relevant, and Still Wrong: How Memory Fails LLM Group Decisions

**abstract/A1：** When an LLM group's discussion is compressed into a short memory for a final decision maker, what should the memory keep? Two natural heuristics keep the rarest claims or the claims relevant to the decision criteria. On hidden-profile tasks both look attractive, because how widely a fact is held is confounded with whether it helps. We introduce CrossProfile, a diagnostic benchmark of 60 templates with automatically verified answer keys, in which the ownership of supporting, decoy, and irrelevant facts is crossed while their text is held fixed (480 tasks). Across Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B, and two DeepSeek models, both heuristics fail in predictable places. Selecting by relevance readmits decoys, because decoys are on topic: even a perfect relevance filter loses 23 to 31 points to rarest-first selection in the classic hidden-profile configuration. Rarest-first selection is no better than random selection on average and wins only where the rare facts happen to be the useful ones. In both cases accuracy follows the balance of evidence the memory keeps, not the policy that kept it. The decision model adds a third failure, a form of social proof: it gives extra weight to facts attributed to many agents. Removing the attribution tags, or reassigning them at random with line order fixed, raises accuracy in the classic configuration by 13 to 24 points. In natural discussions the evidence is lost before any memory acts: the complete transcript yields lower accuracy than no notes at all.

</details>

<a id="paper-36"></a>

### 36. [CASEL: Consequence-Aware Selective Execution for Reliable Long-Horizon Tool-Using Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36570v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36570

**作者：** Yanyan Zhang、Fei Shen、Ting Yan、Hao Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CR、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36570v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36570v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CASEL，一个模型无关的运行时策略层，用校准后的失败概率、状态相关后果、可逆性、歧义与证据新鲜度，以逐模式成本最小化为每个工具调用选择直接执行、验证、沙箱、澄清、审批或拒答，并在仿真中对各种分配规则做等算力比较。

**相关性简评：** 其按代价权衡「继续执行还是先获取证据/停下」的分配框架，可能为 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的收益–成本比较与等成本对照提供参照；但领域是工具智能体、仅有仿真结果，迁移需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CASEL: Consequence-Aware Selective Execution for Reliable Long-Horizon Tool-Using Agents

**abstract/A1：** Tool-using language agents increasingly modify external systems, yet most runtime safeguards allocate scrutiny by generic uncertainty scores or coarse mutation heuristics. We introduce CASEL (Consequence-Aware Selective Execution Layer), a model-agnostic runtime allocation policy that combines calibrated failure probability, state-dependent consequence, reversibility, ambiguity, and evidence freshness to choose, for each proposed tool call, among direct execution, verification, sandboxing, clarification, approval, and abstention by minimizing a per-mode cost. We formalize direct execution as selective prediction and state and test the validity conditions of a finite-sample threshold and a trajectory risk ledger. In a controlled simulation over 20 seeds with 120,000 test actions each, in which every method has the same intervention menu and the same compute cost, CASEL reduces harmful-side-effect trajectories from 14.75 percent for an uncertainty-ranked gate to 7.02 percent and mean normalized harm from 0.184 to 0.123. A gate ranked by the product of failure probability and consequence reaches similar harm (5.82 percent, 0.120) but sends 45 percent more actions to human approval, and removing reversibility-aware routing raises the rate to 15.53 percent. A trajectory-level risk budget, in contrast, increases harm at equal cost (11.82 percent). Intervention effectiveness is assumed, not measured: the results rank allocation rules under the simulator's assumptions and do not establish deployment effectiveness.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 4 figures. Accepted at the 2026 5th International Conference on Cloud Computing, Big Data Application and Software Engineering (CBASE 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-37"></a>

### 37. [OpenGray: a benchmark for evaluating the decisions of radiotherapy planning agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37081v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37081

**作者：** Humza Nusrat、Kundan Thind

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、physics.med-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37081v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37081v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P1

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建 OpenGray 基准，在头颈放疗数据集上加入答案已知的任务（含需解剖学算术才能识别的目标矛盾与可行对照），比较从规则脚本到完整 LLM agent 的四种系统在「提交计划 / 请求复核」决策上的表现，并记录剂量、问题识别与失败情况。

**相关性简评：** 其核心是「何时停止、何时请求复核」的决策评价，且以任务内证据冲突和已知答案衡量，可为 LVR 中 STOP 决策、放弃/求助与证据核查的评价设计提供参考；该文超出多模态推理领域，迁移价值待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OpenGray: a benchmark for evaluating the decisions of radiotherapy planning agents

**abstract/A1：** Background and purpose: Radiotherapy planning agents based on large language models (LLMs) decide when to submit a plan or request review but are evaluated mainly by dose. This study developed OpenGray, a benchmark of planning tasks with known correct decisions. Materials and methods: OpenGray adds tasks with known answers to the OpenKBP-Opt head-and-neck dataset, including two contradictions with dose goals proven incompatible and a feasible control with a stored plan meeting every hard goal. Four systems, from a rule-based planning script to a full LLM agent, were evaluated on 30 patients, the LLM-based ones with a commercial (Terra) and a local (Ministral 3 8B) LLM. Decisions, achieved dose, problem recognition, and technical failures were recorded and also read from logs of 39 stereotactic radiosurgery runs of SAGE, a clinical LLM agent. Results: With Terra, the script preceded by an LLM review and the full agent had decision accuracies of 95.4% and 92.1%, respectively, yet escalated only 50% (95% confidence interval, 25-75%) and 25% (10-40%) of overlap contradictions, which required arithmetic on the anatomy. All four systems submitted every feasible control, but 24% of these plans missed a hard goal that the stored plan had met. In four SAGE runs, one goal failed at every attempt; the agent identified it in every reply but had no action for stopping. Conclusions: Favorable aggregate scores concealed specific decision failures. Planning agents should be evaluated on their decisions as well as their dose and given an explicit action for stopping and requesting review.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 4 figures, 1 table, and the supplementary material (19 tables, 5 figures). Ancillary files: the paper in Markdown, structured results with the sentence each comes from, and an llms.txt index for AI agents

</details>

<a id="paper-38"></a>

### 38. [3D-SceneQ: Empowering 3D LLM With Query-Guided Adaptive Pruning and Multi-Modal Feature Enhancement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37202v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37202

**作者：** Yongqi Shan、Lu Zhang、Jiazuo Yu、Yunzhi Zhuge、Huchuan Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37202v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37202v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 3D-SceneQ：用指令驱动的可学习 latent query 对 3D 物体 token 做查询引导自适应剪枝（QGAP），并融合 2D 预训练特征增强物体级语义（MOFE），以提升 3D 场景理解、推理与定位。

**相关性简评：** 其核心是剔除任务无关视觉 token 以减少噪声与幻觉，直接对应 LVR 关注的「视觉证据筛选」与「信息坍缩」问题；但 latent query 仅用于剪枝而非生成推理状态，剪枝是否损害几何与实体关系信息可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** 3D-SceneQ: Empowering 3D LLM With Query-Guided Adaptive Pruning and Multi-Modal Feature Enhancement

**abstract/A1：** Current 3D scene understanding pipelines typically concatenate vast numbers of 3D object tokens with text tokens and feed the resulting sequence to a Large Language Model (LLM). However, existing 3D-LLMs for scene understanding encounter two major limitations: (i) excessive inclusion of task-irrelevant object data, introducing noise that reduces reasoning accuracy and increases hallucinations; and (ii) reliance solely on point cloud data, which inherently lacks the rich semantic information available in complementary 2D modalities, such as color, material properties, texture, and high-level contextual relationships. To address these challenges, we introduce 3D-SceneQ, a 3D LLM that unifies adaptive token pruning with multimodal semantic enrichment, markedly advancing scene understanding, reasoning, and grounding. Specifically, we propose a Query-Guided Adaptive Pruning (QGAP) module that adaptively selects task-relevant objects based on the intended meaning of user instructions, leveraging a learnable latent query to retain critical information while effectively suppressing irrelevant noise. In addition, we introduce a Multi-modal Object-level Feature Enhancement (MOFE) module, which integrates 2D feature embeddings from pre-trained models into 3D representation to enrich object-level semantic information. Evaluations show that 3D-SceneQ outperforms current state-of-the-art methods across multiple benchmarks (e.g., +5.7 CIDEr on Scan2Cap and +17.0 EM@1 on SQA3D), demonstrating its remarkable capabilities in various 3D language reasoning tasks.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 8 figures, 8 tables. Published in IEEE Transactions on Multimedia

</details>

<a id="paper-39"></a>

### 39. [CodeCap-Bench: Can Code Serve as a Good Video Caption?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37341v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37341

**作者：** Jiafu Tang、Yifan Xu、Eric Tang、Junjie Zhao、Shipeng Luo、Te Sun、Haoxuan Hu、Yiyan Ji、Xinlong Chen、Zihao Wang、Yang Shi、Yuanxing Zhang、Pengfei Wan、Jiaheng Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37341v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37341v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出用可执行程序作为视频的互补描述表示，通过下游问答与视频重建比较代码字幕与自然语言字幕，并构建 CodeCap-Bench（323 个视频、五类结构化 2D 内容、多级评测）考察 MLLM 能否生成忠实的代码字幕。

**相关性简评：** 其显式符号表示与保真度评测可供核查视觉细节、空间布局和时序动态是否被完整保留，以及「忠实度」如何度量；但这是输出级表示而非内部 latent 状态，需阅读原文确认可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CodeCap-Bench: Can Code Serve as a Good Video Caption?

**abstract/A1：** Video captioning aims to preserve visual information through compact descriptions, predominantly using natural language. However, precisely expressing fine-grained spatial structures and temporal dynamics in natural language often leads to lengthy and potentially ambiguous descriptions. In this work, we explore Code as Video Caption, a paradigm where executable programs serve as a complementary representation of visual content through graphical primitives, layout rules, and functions of time. To examine whether code captions effectively preserve video information, we compare them with natural language captions on downstream question answering and video reconstruction tasks. Our results show that code captions achieve superior reasoning accuracy and reconstruction fidelity. Despite these advantages, realizing the potential of code captioning depends on whether current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate faithful code captions. To systematically assess this capability, we introduce CodeCap-Bench, which comprises 323 videos spanning five categories of structured 2D content, along with fine-grained annotations and a multi-level evaluation framework. Extensive experiments reveal that while leading proprietary MLLMs demonstrate promising code captioning capabilities, they still struggle to faithfully capture complex temporal dynamics, with open-source models lagging substantially behind.

</details>

<a id="paper-40"></a>

### 40. [Perceiving and Predicting Dynamic Sports: Spatiotemporal Intelligence in Vision Models for Egocentric Tennis Video](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37619v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37619

**作者：** Tudor Braicu、Joseph DelPreto、Jared Boyer、Mónika Farsang、Maximilian Stölzle、Tim Niklas Seyde、Daniela Rus

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37619v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37619v1>)

**研究分类：** MAIN · 直接相关

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建带标注的自我中心网球视频数据集（同步五个外部视角、全局位置、触球时刻与击球类型），比较图像编码器、视频编码器与 VLM 加预测头在七项时空预测任务（2D/3D 定位、未来轨迹、正反手预测、下一次触球时间与触点 3D 位置）上的表现，并用条件扩散模型从视觉预测实时生成全身网球动作。

**相关性简评：** 论文报告更长历史不改善球轨迹预测、模型更依赖场景上下文而非帧间运动，且深度信息对 3D 定位关键，为「内部状态保留哪些视觉信息」提供直接诊断；「下一次触球时间」预测也近似于时序上的停止决策，但其未涉及 latent 推理生成或答案使用，联系偏外围，可供核查表示与 STOP 时机的评估方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Perceiving and Predicting Dynamic Sports: Spatiotemporal Intelligence in Vision Models for Egocentric Tennis Video

**abstract/A1：** Agile robots require spatiotemporal intelligence to interpret scenes, reason about interactions, and anticipate future events. Yet it remains unclear how well foundation vision models support these capabilities, particularly from a moving egocentric camera, which is a natural view for robots. To investigate this, we collect an annotated tennis dataset and train a suite of vision models on spatiotemporal tasks. Our dataset contains egocentric video synchronized with five allocentric videos, estimated global positions, labeled contact times, and annotated stroke types. We then evaluate seven prediction tasks from egocentric images: 2D ego player and 3D ball localization, future player and ball trajectories, forehand or backhand stroke anticipation, time to the next ego player ball contact, and 3D ball position at that contact. We compare image encoders, video encoders, and vision-language models with jointly trained prediction heads. The best performing models achieve mean current-frame localization errors of 0.14 m for the ego player and 0.54 m for the ball, future forecasting errors of 0.34 m for the ego player and 0.91 m for the ball over 1.1 s, and a mean contact-time prediction error of 0.14 s. Auxiliary depth information substantially improves 3D ball localization, suggesting that inferring metric depth remains a challenge. Longer image histories do not improve ball forecasting, indicating that representations exploit scene context rather than rely solely on inter-frame motion. Finally, we propose a conditional diffusion model to generate whole-body kinematic tennis motion from vision encoder predictions on egocentric video at real-time rates, adapting movements as predictions are refined. These results move towards a deeper understanding of how foundation vision models can support real-time dynamic robots using egocentric video.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 5 figures. Project page: https://tennis-vision.csail.mit.edu/

</details>

<a id="paper-41"></a>

### 41. [Calibrated for Which Policy? Conformal Uncertainty for Fixed World Models Under Intervention](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35877v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35877

**作者：** Ayush Mittal、Dhruv Gupta

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.RO、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35877v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35877v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究部署策略改变后世界模型不确定性失效的问题：在条件机制 P(Y\|X) 不变的前提下，用源-目标测度比 w(X)（可分解为状态占用与动作倾向）做加权 conformal 校准，将覆盖保证迁移到目标策略，并用源-目标域分类器的 log-loss regret 约束比值估计误差，在 DINO 特征世界模型与 Franka 轨迹上验证，另以 Lean 4 形式化相关代数与因果恒等式。

**相关性简评：** 其按策略偏移重加权、使不确定性覆盖相对于部署分布成立的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中停止/弃权决策与推理轨迹分布偏移下的置信度评价；但对象是单步预测的世界模型，与 latent 视觉推理状态本身无直接联系，迁移需较多改造。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Calibrated for Which Policy? Conformal Uncertainty for Fixed World Models Under Intervention

**abstract/A1：** Changing a deployment policy can invalidate a world model's uncertainty while leaving the physical transition mechanism and predictor unchanged. For one-step prediction, if source and target laws share the conditional mechanism $P(Y\|X)=Q(Y\|X)$, then the intervention is identified by the pre-outcome change of measure $w(X)=dQ\_X/dP\_X$. For policy-controlled dynamics this ratio factors into state-occupancy and action-propensity terms. Weighted conformal calibration transports marginal coverage to the target law; with an estimated ratio $\\hat w$, $P\_Q\\{Y \\in C\_{\\hat w}(X)\\} \\ge 1-α-TV(Q,\\hat Q) \\ge 1-α-E\_P\|w-\\hat w\|$. Population log-loss regret for a source-versus-target domain classifier bounds the same ratio error. If $h$ is its clipped target posterior, $ρ$ is the target-domain prior, and $Δ(h)$ is population log-loss regret, then $E\_P\|w-\\hat w\_h\| \\le (1/(ργ))\\sqrt{Δ(h)/2}$. An exact cancellation improves the dependence from a generic odds-map bound of order $γ^{-2}$ to $(ργ)^{-1}$. Our experiments use a released DINO-feature world model and physical Franka trajectories. Under the strongest controlled state-action shift, ordinary 90% conformal sets cover 55.0%, whereas oracle and learned-ratio calibration cover 91.0% and 90.9%. The learned surrogate remains within 0.9% total variation of the target law. Calibration is relative to the deployment law: the model can remain fixed while calibration follows the intervention. We machine-check in Lean 4 the finite-support algebraic and causal identities used in the classifier-regret argument, including its coverage-composition step.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables. Lean 4 formalization: https://github.com/noumenal-ai/conformal-realrobot-lab

</details>

<a id="paper-42"></a>

### 42. [Task-Utility IQA: Rethinking Image Quality Assessment in Embodied Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35905v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35905

**作者：** Jirong Zha、Yanwei Liao、Yemin Wang、Xiangmin Yi、Siqi Peng、Yingfeng Chen、Chen Gao、Xinlei Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35905v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35905v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文主张把图像质量评估从任务无关的分数回归改为由具身任务效用定义的目标条件判断，并提出基于 MLLM 的具身 IQA agent，通过推理任务需求、定位质量证据来输出面向决策的评价；小规模 VisDrone 证据显示传统感知指标与通用 MLLM 质量分难以预测下游 VLM 任务成败。

**相关性简评：** 其「任务条件下的效用判断」可能充当视觉输入是否足够、是否值得继续 LOOK 或补充证据的信号，且通用质量指标与下游 VLM 成功脱节的诊断可供核查；但未涉及 latent 状态与推理轨迹，迁移需自行设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Task-Utility IQA: Rethinking Image Quality Assessment in Embodied Tasks

**abstract/A1：** Image quality assessment (IQA) underpins embodied imaging pipelines by judging whether visual quality satisfies downstream tasks, yet most existing methods learn task-agnostic scores aligned with generic human ratings on static benchmarks. This objective mismatches embodied and interactive settings, where image adequacy depends on task goals, context, and action requirements that shape an agent's decisions. We argue that IQA should shift from score regression to goal-conditioned judgment defined by embodied task utility. To this end, we propose an MLLM-based embodied IQA agent framework that reasons about task requirements, grounds quality evidence, and produces decision-facing assessments. A compact VisDrone evidence study shows that conventional perceptual metrics and generic MLLM-based IQA scores are weak proxies for downstream VLM task success, while a task-conditioned utility score better predicts whether visual input supports the target task. These findings support a task-utility-oriented reframing of IQA for embodied, explainable, and decision-aware visual perception.

</details>

<a id="paper-43"></a>

### 43. [Curriculum-Driven Deep Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Far-Range Rocket Landing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35954v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35954

**作者：** Atin Chowdhury、Piyush Joshi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35954v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35954v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出面向远距离火箭垂直软着陆的三阶段课程式深度强化学习框架，配合 critic 预热与 rehearsal 防遗忘机制，并重设奖励函数，使智能体从 1000–1500 单位外完成精确着陆。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理主线无直接关联，但其分阶段课程、critic 预热与防灾难遗忘的 rehearsal 设计，可能为 LVR 中长时程 latent 推理轨迹的分段训练与稳定性提供可供核查的训练线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Curriculum-Driven Deep Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Far-Range Rocket Landing

**abstract/A1：** Autonomous vertical landing of reusable launch vehicles is a critical yet formidable challenge in aerospace engineering, primarily due to highly nonlinear dynamics, stringent precision require- ments, and inherent environmental uncertainties. While reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising alternative to traditional model-based control, existing approaches frequently rely on flat training paradigms and highly simplified initial conditions, drastically limiting their real-world applicability. This paper proposes a comprehensive curriculum-driven deep reinforcement learning framework designed to master both far-range navigation and precision landing. By employing a three-stage curriculum augmented with a critic warm-up procedure and a rehearsal mechanism to prevent catastrophic forgetting, alongside a high-dimensional observation vector utilizing a full 3 X 3 rotation-matrix, the agent is trained to navigate from an initial horizontal distance of 1,000 to 1,500 units vastly farther than prior benchmarks before executing a soft touchdown. Crucially, we intro- duce a redesigned reward function that reduces unnecessary computational overhead and addresses limitations of earlier formulations, including poorly calibrated reward components and potentially conflicting optimization objectives. The redesigned function bounds the per-step reward magnitude by (O(1)), while its progress term accumulates the total distance closed over the trajectory. The available 100,000-episode results report a 99.98% success rate for the proposed policy and 68.31% for the baseline under the evaluation conditions. In the evaluation, the proposed policy retained 69.1% of its initial fuel capacity and achieved a mean final distance from the pad centre of 7.07 units, compared with 13.10 units for the baseline. Landing visualizations are available in Video 1 and Video 2.

**comment/C1：** This is the extension of our previous paper that got accepted in ICAART 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-44"></a>

### 44. [h1: A Taste of the Bitter Lesson in Automated Theorem Proving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35962v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35962

**作者：** Skander Moalla、Ahmad Rammal、Niket Patel、Remi Munos、Julia Kempe

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.LO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35962v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35962v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 h1：一个只给模型高效 Lean 4 接口、依靠验证器反馈与自我反思持续迭代的极简 agentic 循环，不加分解或搜索结构，并在 PutnamBench 等基准上与人工设计工作流做同模型对比和消融。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本形式化证明虽非多模态 LVR 主线，但其「验证器反馈驱动的循环式状态更新＋test-time compute 扩展」以及接口效率主导成本与解出率的结论，可供核查 LVR 中证据获取、停止决策与推理成本的对应关系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** h1: A Taste of the Bitter Lesson in Automated Theorem Proving

**abstract/A1：** While progress in informal mathematics has come largely from scaling the chain-of-thought and agentic reasoning of frontier language models, formal theorem proving has increasingly relied on human-designed workflows around these models, such as planning, decomposition, and orchestrating specialized provers. This raises a bitter-lesson question: can a simple agentic loop that scales test-time compute match these workflows? The answer matters as formal verification must scale to keep up with frontier models producing research mathematics, yet new workflows rarely settle it, as their gains are often confounded with stronger models or more compute. We study this question with h1, a simple harness that gives a model an efficient interface to Lean 4 and keeps it working through verifier feedback and self-reflection, without imposing a decomposition or search structure. h1 reaches state-of-the-art results on PutnamBench, FATE-X, and FormalConjectures100-Solved, settles eight open problems in FormalConjectures, and, at matched models, matches or outperforms specialized workflows such as Goedel-Architect and AlphaProof Nexus, surprisingly showing that a single attempt (pass@1) can often scale to solve open problems. Through ablations and matched-model comparisons with Claude Code and AxProverBase, we find that simplicity alone is not enough: an efficient Lean interface largely determines cost and solve rate. We thus recommend that future work evaluate frontier models with efficient execution feedback before prescribing workflows, at the risk of bitter lessons.

</details>

<a id="paper-45"></a>

### 45. [Data-driven Discovery of Lie Symmetries and First Integrals with Latent ODEs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35967v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35967

**作者：** Matt L. Wiemman、Peter Melchior

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35967v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35967v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LatentLieODE：先用自编码器把视频等时间序列观测映射为低维去相关潜坐标，联合拟合潜 ODE 与一簇线性 Lie 对称生成元（以可微 Lie 括号残差约束），再由第三个网络提取首次积分，在六个基准系统上恢复对称性与守恒量。

**相关性简评：** 其「潜状态 + 对称性/不变量约束」的结构化潜表示与状态更新方式，可能为 LVR 中保持任务必要信息、约束 latent 轨迹几何提供可核查的方法线索；但该文不涉及 VLM 推理，迁移需自行判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Data-driven Discovery of Lie Symmetries and First Integrals with Latent ODEs

**abstract/A1：** We present LatentLieODE, a novel neural ODE method to discover linear Lie symmetries and first integrals of the learned dynamics from timeseries observations such as video inputs. It starts with an autoencoder, which learns a low dimensional representation of the observed system and then maps trajectories to decorrelated latent coordinates $z \\in \\mathbb{R}^d$. A bank of linear infinitesimal generators $\\mathbf{A}\_k \\in \\mathbb{R}^{d \\times d}$ is fitted jointly against a differentiable Lie-bracket residual $\\mathbf{J}\_f(z)\\mathbf{A}z - \\mathbf{A} f(z)$, while an MLP optimises the latent ODE $f(z) = \\mathrm{d}z/\\mathrm{d}t$ to reconstruct the observation-space timeseries. After suitable generators and $f$ are found, we fit a third network $H\_φ$ to generate the first integral of the system. Across six benchmark systems (harmonic oscillator, simple and twin pendulums, Lotka-Volterra, pixel pendulum, sphere geodesics) we find each system's relevant symmetries, and the discovered invariants match ground-truth conserved quantities at $\|ρ\| \\geq 0.978$. On the sphere, the pipeline recovers a non-abelian $\\mathfrak{so}(3)$, certified by the negative-definite Killing form of its recovered structure constants. This work presents significant advances in data-driven discovery of symmetries and provides a new pathway for neural surrogates of dynamical systems.

**comment/C1：** Accepted into 2026 Neural Representations Proceedings

</details>

<a id="paper-46"></a>

### 46. [Let the Gate Decide: Per-Lookup Precision for Quantized Conditional Memory in LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35985v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35985

**作者：** Tianqi Chen、Xunchao Li、Linchuan Xie、Guanghua Yu、Jianchen Zhu、Peisong Wang、Jian Cheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35985v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35985v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究条件记忆层（Engram 类哈希 n-gram 表）低比特量化优先丢失长尾事实的问题，指出按访问频率分配精度不如随机，提出以门控值加权量化误差、按门控排序决定读取深度，用 1-bit 残差码阶梯存储，在免重训下压缩显存并提升吞吐。

**相关性简评：** 以门控值决定「读多深」的按需精度分配，可能为 LVR 中按证据或状态价值动态分配读取与计算深度提供线索；但对象是纯文本记忆表量化，与视觉 latent 状态无直接联系，可供核查其重要性度量的可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Let the Gate Decide: Per-Lookup Precision for Quantized Conditional Memory in LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Conditional memory layers, such as DeepSeek's Engram, attach large hashed n-gram tables to a language model, and removing them from DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash costs 36.8 PopQA points. Low-bit quantization loses long-tail facts first, and the standard remedy, giving more bits to frequently accessed rows, backfires: refining lookups by access frequency is worse than refining them at random. We trace this to the gate that scales each retrieved row. A lookup's quantization error is weighted by its gate, which is small for frequent n-grams, large for the rare n-grams of long-tail entities, and computable from a 1-bit code. GateRQ therefore stores each row as a ladder of 1-bit residual codes, keeps level 1 in GPU memory to compute the gate, and reads deeper levels only for the lookups the gate ranks highest. Without retraining, GateRQ cuts GPU table memory by 7.8x on DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, which raises vLLM throughput by 11%-31% over FP8 tables, while losing about 1 PopQA point at 1.50 bits read per lookup and 0.2 points at 2.10 bits across two models.

</details>

<a id="paper-47"></a>

### 47. [Instruction Capacity: Behavior and Degradation of Language Models under Controlled Load](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36095v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36095

**作者：** Hennadii Kutomanov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36095v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36095v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出「指令容量」测量工具：用替换单 token 词的规则构造原子化任务，把每次替换视为位置已知的伯努利试验，用统计模型刻画正确率随文本长度、词位置、替换密度与规则数等负载因素的变化，并给出带不确定性的容量值；在 GPT-4o mini 上测得 0.95 正确率下约 37–38 条规则。

**相关性简评：** 其「负载因子—性能退化律 + 带不确定性的容量阈值」测量范式可能可供核查地迁移到 latent 推理的计算/长度预算与长度泛化容量刻画，但论文本身不涉及视觉证据或 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Instruction Capacity: Behavior and Degradation of Language Models under Controlled Load

**abstract/A1：** The quality with which language models follow instructions falls as the load grows, yet there is still no quantity that expresses the limit of a model in units of load with an uncertainty. We propose an instrument in which the load consists of rules that replace single-token words in a text without meaning: such an instruction is atomic, the rules are independent by the construction of the task, and the outcome of every application of a rule is determined by a computable reference answer. Every occurrence of a word to be replaced becomes a Bernoulli trial with a known position in the answer and a known rule number. The statistical model first measures the shape of the dependence of the probability of a right replacement on the text length, the word position, the replacement density, the number of rules and the rule number, then describes it by smooth laws and restores a reference level that does not depend on the choice of the coding. The model yields the instruction capacity - the number of rules held at a given share of right replacements - with an uncertainty. A measurement of GPT-4o mini (2958 requests, 642,430 trials) gave regular laws for every load factor and for the collapse of the answer; the instruction capacity was about 37-38 rules at a share of right replacements of 0.95, and about 18-19 rules at the full replacement density. The code of the instrument, the measurement data and the passport are open, and every number of the passport is recomputed from the published points. The passport of a model serves as the calibration of a future meter of the instruction load of prompts.

**comment/C1：** 70 pages, 20 figures. Code and data: https://github.com/kutomanovhennadii/instruction-capacity

</details>

<a id="paper-48"></a>

### 48. [CohortFlow: A Steerable Clinical Cohort Curation Agent with Localized Semantic Correction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36100v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36100

**作者：** Neil Chen、Adrian Serapio、Hanxue Gu、Evan Lee、Kang Wang、Yang Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36100v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36100v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CohortFlow，一个可接受自然语言修正的临床队列筛选 agent：把修正表示为对结构化队列规范的局部更新，并在持久执行图上依据显式语义依赖选择性失效受影响计算、保留有效结果；在 100 个临床试验筛选场景上比较精度、修正延迟与模型调用数。

**相关性简评：** 其「依赖图 + 选择性失效/复用」机制可为 LVR 中 latent 状态的部分更新、缓存复用与重计算范围判定提供可核查线索，但论文本身无视觉或多模态推理实验。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CohortFlow: A Steerable Clinical Cohort Curation Agent with Localized Semantic Correction

**abstract/A1：** Language model agents for clinical cohort curation should accommodate evolving eligibility criteria without repeatedly processing unchanged requirements. We introduce CohortFlow, a steerable agent that represents natural language corrections as localized updates to a structured cohort specification and reconciles them with a persistent execution graph. Explicitly recorded semantic dependencies enable affected computations to be selectively invalidated while preserving valid prior results. On 100 scenarios derived from clinical-trial eligibility criteria, comprising 126 correction turns, localized steering achieves 77.0% requirement micro-F1 versus 76.4% for full regeneration using the same 8B model, a small numerical advantage that is not statistically significant ($p=0.493$). It reduces mean correction latency by 64.8% after completion and 44.3% during execution, with 38.1% fewer model calls. In a separate proof-of-concept evaluation over MIMIC, all 13 cohort states execute successfully, while correction trajectories demonstrate selective preservation of unaffected computations and recomputation of changed or dependent nodes. Together, these results show that localized semantic steering can substantially reduce correction latency and model calls while maintaining strong requirement-level accuracy.

</details>

<a id="paper-49"></a>

### 49. [SpatialPC: Deep Computation with Sublinear Local Credit Propagation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36139v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36139

**作者：** Benjamin Prada、Shion Matsumoto、Ankur Arjun Mali

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36139v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36139v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究链式状态上局部学习规则的信用传播延迟，论证该延迟由通信几何而非深度决定，并给出 Ω(N^{1/d}) 下界与匹配的稀疏图构造；在预测编码中提出 SpatialPC，用伴随预测动力学（APD）的截断反向路径和实现次线性信用深度，并在 MNIST/Fashion-MNIST 上验证有限轮梯度与收敛梯度的对齐及精度。

**相关性简评：** APD 的截断反向路径信用分配与「图几何决定信用到达、Jacobian 尾部决定精度」的分析，可能为长轨迹 latent 推理中的贡献分配与监督、以及深度与信用延迟的权衡提供可供核查的方法线索，但原作未涉及视觉语言任务或 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SpatialPC: Deep Computation with Sublinear Local Credit Propagation

**abstract/A1：** On a chain of $N$ states, any local learning rule with bounded communication range needs $Θ(N)$ rounds before the loss can influence the earliest parameters, and this latency is usually attributed to depth. We show that it is a property of the communication geometry rather than of depth. For $N=m^d$ states placed at constant density in $d$ dimensions, a light-cone argument gives an $Ω(N^{1/d})$ lower bound on the number of bounded-range rounds that any local algorithm needs before its gradient estimates can be sign-correct at every parameter. We construct a unit-range, degree-$2d$ directed acyclic graph that contains a Hamiltonian computation of length $N-1$ and in which every state lies within directed distance $d(m-1)=Θ(N^{1/d})$ of the output, matching the lower bound. In the realizable-circuit model, depth and credit depth are therefore independent parameters: represented depth $Θ(N)$ and causal credit latency $Θ(N^{1/d})$ coexist, with a separation of order $N^{1-1/d}$. SpatialPC realizes this separation within predictive coding. Credit is propagated by adjoint prediction dynamics (APD), a local update whose $K$-round state on a settled acyclic graph is the truncated reverse-path sum $a^K=\\sum\_{k&lt;K}(J^\\top)^k b$, with error exactly $(J^\\top)^K a^\\star$. Graph geometry fixes when credit can first arrive at a state; the Jacobian-weighted path tail fixes how many further rounds accurate credit requires. A sparse two-dimensional Moore compiler retains the $N-1$ backbone with $\\tfrac32N-2$ unit-range edges and has measured worst-node reachability $\\sqrt N+2$ up to $N=65{,}536$, where a 65,535-edge backbone is reached in 258 local hops. With $T=2\\sqrt N$ rounds, SpatialPC keeps finite-round task gradients aligned with settled gradients (cosine $0.93$ on MNIST and $0.97$ on Fashion-MNIST at $N=256$) and reaches $97.6\\%$ and $88.1\\%$ test accuracy after 20 epochs.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-50"></a>

### 50. [From Structural Feedback to Prompt Policies: Learning Faithful Text-to-Image Prompt Editors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36146v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36146

**作者：** Wentao Ye、Yali Ye、Zhiqing Xiao、Ru Peng、Xinpeng Ti、Liyao Li、Zhanming Shen、Peng Lu、Jie Zhang、Haobo Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36146v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36146v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 StruPO：以校准后的场景图作为策略条件与图像监督的共享表示，用删除、重排、插入等 token 对齐操作编辑 prompt，把结构反馈转为可复用编辑策略的训练信号，并在多个冻结生成骨干上改善关系保真度与美感的权衡。

**相关性简评：** 其场景图条件、面向不确定 grounding 的平滑奖励塑形与多样本平均后的组相对优势，可能为 LVR 的轨迹监督和贡献分配提供借鉴；而「一次生成取代迭代 critique–rewrite」的做法可供核查 THINK/STOP 的成本取舍，但本身不涉及视觉推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Structural Feedback to Prompt Policies: Learning Faithful Text-to-Image Prompt Editors

**abstract/A1：** Prompt optimization should improve the image a user receives without changing the scene the user requested. Yet stylistic rewriting can weaken object attributes and relations even when global text--image similarity remains high. We propose StruPO, a structure-aware prompt editor that uses a calibrated scene graph as a shared representation for policy conditioning and image-grounded supervision. The graph exposes what should be preserved, while deletion, reordering, and insertion provide a token-aligned way to modify the prompt. This turns structural feedback from an external critique into a training signal for a reusable editing policy. Learning from that signal requires handling both uncertain grounding and stochastic image generation. StruPO retains grounding confidence through smooth reward shaping and averages multiple images for each edit before computing group-relative advantages. Experiments span three disjoint 2K-prompt test sets and four frozen generation backbones, with comparisons to learned prompt optimizers, iterative structural feedback, diffusion-model fine-tuning, and inference-time attention control. StruPO improves the faithfulness--aesthetics trade-off, with consistent gains in relational fidelity. Independent automatic evaluators and a 15-annotator study provide complementary evidence, and human preference margins increase with relation complexity. Controlled ablations connect these gains to graph conditioning, local editing, and image-grounded learning. The resulting policy produces reusable optimized text in one pass after graph calibration, without an iterative critique--rewrite loop.

</details>

<a id="paper-51"></a>

### 51. [Don't Pick a Winner: Knowledge Conflicts Need a Prior, Not a Rule](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36169v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36169

**作者：** Minh Pham Van、Cong Tran

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36169v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36169v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究检索增强模型在「检索上下文」与「自身知识」冲突时如何取舍，用决策论说明任何固定规则都只在部分情形最优，并构建分别刻画各来源证据与上下文正确率先验、允许弃权的选择系统，在 GRACE 等基准上验证。

**相关性简评：** 其「对称散度不携带哪一来源正确的信息」的论断可供核查以有/无视觉输入输出差异度量视觉依赖的做法；按来源分别建模证据并施加先验、可弃权的选择机制，可能启发视觉证据与语言先验冲突时的取舍与停止决策设计。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Don't Pick a Winner: Knowledge Conflicts Need a Prior, Not a Rule

**abstract/A1：** A retrieval-augmented language model answers from two sources, the retrieved context and its own knowledge, and the two disagree whenever the context is incomplete or wrong. Existing methods settle the disagreement with a rule fixed at design time: follow the context, amplify it in proportion to how far it moves the model, or trust the model's memory. Each rule is confidently wrong in some deployment. In a decision-theoretic model of two sources, the score of any fixed rule is linear in the share of reliable contexts while the best attainable score is convex, so every fixed rule is beaten wherever it is not exactly optimal; and a symmetric divergence between the two sources, the signal adaptive decoding uses, carries no information about which one is right. Resolving a conflict therefore needs two inputs that no existing method keeps: evidence about each source separately, and a prior on how often the context is right. We test these claims on GRACE, a benchmark that intervenes on the knowledge-graph context of a fixed question, so that what the model knows, what the context says and what is true are measured separately. There, every baseline from four families is strong on right contexts or on wrong ones, never on both. We then build the two inputs: the model states its knowledge as notes, a small policy voices each note with a calibrated confidence, a reader answers in two voices, and a selector commits or abstains under a prior $π$ applied as an exact prior shift. With $π$ set to the true share of right contexts, the system is above the best baseline at every mixture of GRACE contexts, by up to 26 points. Its overall score is 45.3 and 35.0 (Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, Llama-3.1-8B), against 29.4 and 27.5 for the best baseline. On preregistered ClashEval it beats same-backbone CK-PLUG and TCR by 12 and 26 points. Where the model lacks the knowledge, the gains vanish, as the theory predicts.

**comment/C1：** 49 pages (9 main text + appendix and supplementary material), 14 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-52"></a>

### 52. [Bayesian Evidence Accumulation for Graph-Based Learning in Dynamic Data-Driven Applications Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36190v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36190

**作者：** Moises Ponce、Sarah Harkins Dayton、Ioannis Schizas、Vasileios Maroulas

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36190v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36190v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 B-EAGLe，将图分类重构为序贯证据累积过程：用 GNN 编码器配合贝叶斯累积器，随新观测逐步更新类别后验，累积到足够置信度才做出决策，并在 CIFAR10 上报告了性能与置信度估计。

**相关性简评：** 其「后验逐步累积＋置信阈值触发停止」的机制可供核查 LVR 中 STOP 决策与推理状态增量更新的设计线索，但论文面向静态图的序列分类而非视觉语言推理，迁移效果需进一步评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bayesian Evidence Accumulation for Graph-Based Learning in Dynamic Data-Driven Applications Systems

**abstract/A1：** Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are widely used in modern machine learning, yet most existing frameworks remain fundamentally static, limiting their applicability in Dynamic Data Driven Application Systems (DDDAS) where observations arrive sequentially under uncertainty. We introduce B-EAGLe (Bayesian Evidence Accumulation for Graph-Based Learning), a probabilistic framework inspired by CBGT-Net (cortico-basal ganglia-thalamic network) that reformulates graph classification as a sequential evidence accumulation process. B-EAGLe combines a GNN encoder with a Bayesian accumulator that incrementally updates posterior beliefs over class labels as new observations arrive, committing to a decision only when sufficient confidence is achieved. Experiments on CIFAR10 demonstrate competitive performance together with principled confidence estimation under sequential observations.

</details>

<a id="paper-53"></a>

### 53. [Beyond the Estimate: Wiener-Informed Decoding for Easier Training of Pixel-Space Flow Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36192v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36192

**作者：** Ziqiao Wang、Yang You

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36192v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36192v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Wiener-informed Decoding（WiD）：把冻结的 Wiener 二阶统计估计同时用作直接预测与解码器计算组织的依据，通过系数接口向原生解码器注入特征并回收系数修正，在原始像素空间速度目标下联合训练，从而加速 pixel-space flow model 的训练并改善生成质量。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结统计估计 + 可训练修正路径、不改动原生 block、按原目标联合优化」的模块组织方式，可能为 VLM 中增加辅助视觉统计或潜变量预测分支而不破坏主干提供训练结构线索，系数接口能否迁移到 latent 推理状态可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond the Estimate: Wiener-Informed Decoding for Easier Training of Pixel-Space Flow Models

**abstract/A1：** Learning velocity fields directly in pixel space is difficult and slow. We show that coarse second-order image statistics can ease this task: frozen Wiener estimates improve velocity learning, with gains extending beyond the linear predictions they explicitly provide. Moreover, we observe similar but complementary gains from frozen bases and pixel decoding. These observations motivate Wiener-informed Decoding (WiD), which uses statistical knowledge both for direct prediction and to organize decoder computation without modifying native blocks. WiD combines a trainable Wiener prediction path with coefficient interfaces that inject features into the native decoder and return its predicted coefficient corrections, with these components initialized with data statistics. All introduced parameters, including the spectrum and coordinate transforms, are trained jointly under the original pixel-space velocity objective. Across model scales and decoder architectures, WiD accelerates training and improves generation quality with modest parameter overhead. It also yields smoother gradient trajectories and remains effective with representation alignment. WiD improves class-conditional and text-to-image generation across different resolutions. On ImageNet 256 x 256, PixelDiT-XL with WiD reaches FID 1.53 after 500 epochs, achieving comparable quality to PixelDiT with 37.5% fewer training epochs. Statistical denoisers contribute beyond the estimate: their computational structure can also ease pixel-space training.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-54"></a>

### 54. [Base Models Can Detect Hidden Intentions that Prompted Classifiers Miss](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36202v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36202

**作者：** Oz Amram

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36202v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36202v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 persona-header likelihood evaluation（PHLE）：用基座预训练模型加特定 header 计算输出似然，从语义无关的数字串中解码出后训练模型被提示的隐藏人格（如偏好猫头鹰/鹰）与隐秘有害意图，且提示式分类器（含生成模型自身）接近随机；信号可部分跨模型族迁移。

**相关性简评：** 虽为纯文本、无视觉，但其「用基座模型似然读出下游分类器忽略的隐变量信息」可能为 LVR 中读取 latent 推理状态的内容、检验答案是否真正使用 latent 信息提供可核查的探针线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Base Models Can Detect Hidden Intentions that Prompted Classifiers Miss

**abstract/A1：** The Persona Selection Model (PSM) posits that post-training selects a persona the model learned during pre-training. Work on subliminal learning has shown that models can impart covert signals (like "love of owls") into semantically unrelated outputs like strings of numbers, and that students trained on these outputs inherit those traits. In this work I show that by evaluating the likelihood of such outputs under the base pre-trained model with an appropriate header, a method I call persona-header likelihood evaluation (PHLE), one can decode these subliminal signals and classify between them. For example, PHLE distinguishes number sequences generated by a post-trained model prompted with "You love owls" from those prompted with "You love eagles", and distinguishes answers from a model with a secret desire to harm the user, filtered to remove any noticeable misalignment, from those of a model with no system prompt. In both cases prompted classifiers, including the generating model itself, remain near chance. The signal partially persists across model families, with the Qwen base model reading signals generated by Olmo and vice versa. This is evidence for a non-trivial prediction of the PSM, and the method may be practically useful for auditing the hidden objectives of models.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 10 figures. Code available at https://github.com/OzAmram/psm-mixture-test

</details>

<a id="paper-55"></a>

### 55. [Hierarchical Multimodal Interaction Reasoning for Dyadic Relationship Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36249v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36249

**作者：** Wang Tang、Fethiye Irmak Dogan、Linbo Qing、Ce Zhu、Hatice Gunes

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.MM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36249v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36249v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 HiRe-DRR 框架，将面部、身体、声音与文本线索拆为低层/高层表示，构建同质与异质的跨参与者交互，并以 MoI-Former 的关系感知稀疏路由和层级条件损失完成双人社会关系识别。

**相关性简评：** 专家化稀疏路由筛选“关系相关证据”与粗到细层级监督，或可供核查 LVR 中 latent 证据筛选与结构化监督线索；但任务本身是社会关系分类，不涉及 VLM 推理状态生成与使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hierarchical Multimodal Interaction Reasoning for Dyadic Relationship Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Dyadic relationship recognition aims to identify the latent social relationship between two individuals from their multimodal interactions. Existing methods typically project heterogeneous cues into a shared representation and optimise a flat classification objective, overlooking that relationships may rely on different interaction patterns. For instance, distant relationships can often be inferred from direct behavioural correspondence, whereas close relationships are better reflected by coordinated affective, behavioural, and multimodal responses. To address this limitation, we propose HiRe-DRR, a Hierarchical Multimodal Interaction Reasoning framework that organises relationship evidence from three complementary perspectives: feature level, interaction type, and relationship hierarchy. HiRe-DRR decomposes facial, bodily, acoustic, and textual cues into low- and high-level representations and constructs homogeneous and heterogeneous cross-participant interactions. We further introduce the Mixture-of-Interaction Transformer (MoI-Former), which treats diverse interactions as specialised experts and employs relation-aware sparse routing to identify relationship-relevant evidence while preserving contextual interaction information. The proposed Hierarchical Conditional Loss (HC-Loss) exploits the hierarchical nature of social relationships through coarse-to-fine supervision, enabling structured relationship reasoning. Extensive experiments on three dyadic relationship benchmarks demonstrate that HiRe-DRR outperforms recent representative image-, video-, and multimodal methods. Our routing analysis further reveals that distant relationships tend to rely on low-level homogeneous interactions, whereas closer relationships focus on high-level heterogeneous interactions. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/tw-repository/HiRe-DRR.

</details>

<a id="paper-56"></a>

### 56. [What Should an Information Bottleneck Encoder Learn? Separating Representation Learning from Rate Control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36255v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36255

**作者：** Hassan Tavakoli、Bella Bose

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.IT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36255v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36255v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究信息瓶颈中表示学习与率控制的分离：在高斯情形证明编码器可分解为与 β 无关的信息方向 W 和标量增益 D(β)，并以充分性缺口界定一般情形下冻结表示的偏差；在多个图像数据集上比较冻结 CNN 加逐预算压缩器与端到端 VIB 的精度和计算成本。

**相关性简评：** 其「表示方向与率控制分离」及充分性缺口可能为 LVR 中视觉任务必要信息的保留和计算预算分配提供诊断线索，但能否迁移到多模态推理的 latent 状态仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Should an Information Bottleneck Encoder Learn? Separating Representation Learning from Rate Control

**abstract/A1：** The information bottleneck (IB) trades compression of an input $X$ against prediction of a target $Y$ through a multiplier $β=1/λ$, yet each operating point is usually served by training, or fully conditioning, a new encoder. For jointly Gaussian data, we show that the IB solution factorizes as $A(β)=D(β)W$: the information-bearing directions $W$ do not depend on $β$, and $β$ acts only through scalar gains. We ask whether this separation of \\emph{representation} from \\emph{rate control} survives beyond Gaussian models, treating the budget as a given operating parameter rather than a quantity to be optimized. For a budget-free representation \\(F(X)\\), the \\emph{separation gap} is controlled by the \\emph{sufficiency deficit} $ε\_F=I(X;Y\\mid F(X))$: We show there is at least $ε\_F$ in the worst case for finite inputs, and it is bounded uniformly in $λ$ by a function of $ε\_F$ for finite label alphabets. The same certificate extends to a new target distribution and to pooled multi-domain data, where a conditional-alignment term appears. On MNIST, SVHN, CIFAR-10 and STL-10 (10 seeds, eight prespecified budgets), a frozen CNN representation with one compressor--decoder pair per budget reduces estimated training multiply--accumulate operations (MACs) by $7.7\\times$ and wall-clock time by about $2\\times$ relative to end-to-end VIBs, and attains higher budget-averaged accuracy ($65.6\\%$ versus $57.3\\%$ per domain; $67.1\\%$ versus $57.1\\%$ pooled), with the advantage concentrated at strong compression, where the VIBs collapse. At weak compression ($λ\\le0.036$), VIB has higher cross-entropy and ECE; its surrogate Lagrangian is higher per domain, indistinguishable from the pooled VIB in the macro average, and lower on the pooled test set.

</details>

<a id="paper-57"></a>

### 57. [Cross-modal Steering Vectors Exist Across Both Image and Text Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36269v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36269

**作者：** Donghao Zeng、Nishant Subramani、Tamar Rott Shaham、Benno Krojer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36269v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36269v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文在共享参数的统一多模态模型中构造跨模态 steering vector，检验同一概念在文本生成侧得到的方向能否零样本迁移到图像生成侧（反之亦然），并结合表示几何分析可迁移向量是否方向一致。

**相关性简评：** 其“干预而非相关”的因果式向量操控可供核查：能否用同类跨模态方向探测 VLM 内部状态是否真正携带并使用了视觉证据，而非走语言旁路；但原文针对生成可控性而非推理过程，迁移需较多验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Cross-modal Steering Vectors Exist Across Both Image and Text Generation

**abstract/A1：** Are concepts and mechanisms unified across modalities in current AI models, or instead implemented separately for each one? We operationalize this broad question of modality unification by studying cross-modal steering: 1) finding vectors that successfully steer generation on tokens of one modality, which then 2) transfer zero-shot to steer generation when added to tokens of another modality. Our study goes beyond simple correlational alignment (e.g. representational similarity) because steering is fundamentally causal. We study cross-modal steering in several unified multimodal models (UMMs), since they possess shared parameters for language and image generation, focusing on these two modalities. Across diverse steered concepts such as emotion and object size, we show that steering vectors transfer zero-shot in both directions (text-to-image &amp; image-to-text). Even for concepts that are highly unique to one modality (e.g. formal vs. colloquial language), steering vectors often induce a consistent, interpretable effect in the other modality, such as formal clothing and design. This transfer has a geometric signature too: despite a clear modality gap separating text and image representations, steering vectors with strong cross-modal transferability tend to point in similar directions for the same concept. Our findings shed light on how vision and language interact inside unified multimodal models and point towards more general, modality-agnostic approaches for controlling their behavior.

**comment/C1：** Pre-print. Code: https://github.com/saintsauceee/cross-modal-steering Data: https://huggingface.co/saintsauce Demo: https://saintsauceee.github.io/unified-cm-steer-demo

</details>

<a id="paper-58"></a>

### 58. [CRLoMTL: Conflict-Resolution through Low-rank matrices for Multi-Task Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36337v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36337

**作者：** Ahmed Agiza、Tasneem Shaffee、Rahil Badkul、Sherief Reda

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36337v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36337v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CRLoMTL：在低秩参数更新空间而非梯度空间缓解多任务干扰，各任务训练独立 LoRA，再用基于 SVD 的共享子空间解析器提取公共正交基、投影并合并为单一可折叠更新，在 Swin 骨干上验证精度与参数权衡。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关系，但其低秩共享子空间投影与任务更新融合方式，可能为组合多个推理期或训练期 latent 技能适配器、抑制相互干扰提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CRLoMTL: Conflict-Resolution through Low-rank matrices for Multi-Task Learning

**abstract/A1：** Multi-task learning (MTL) improves model efficiency by sharing representations across related vision tasks, but it often suffers from negative transfer when task updates interfere with one another. Existing conflict-mitigation methods typically operate in gradient space, requiring explicit task-gradient manipulation and additional optimization steps. We propose Conflict-Resolution through Low-Rank Matrices for Multi-Task Learning (CRLoMTL), a parameter-efficient framework that mitigates task interference in the low-rank parameter-update space. CRLoMTL learns task-specific LoRA updates through task-specific passes, where each task updates its own low-rank matrices, and integrates these updates through global shared-subspace fusion. Specifically, we introduce a shared-subspace resolver based on singular value decomposition (SVD), referred to as the SVD resolver, which extracts a common low-dimensional orthonormal basis across task adapters and projects task updates into this shared space before merging them into a single deployment-time update. The resulting update can be folded into the backbone, preserving the inference structure of the original model without additional runtime branches. Experiments with Swin Transformer backbones on PASCAL-Context, NYUD-v2, and CelebA show that CRLoMTL achieves a favorable accuracy-parameter trade-off, improving over single-task baselines by up to 2.77% on PASCAL-Context, 5.03% on NYUD-v2, and 3.50% on CelebA, while training only a small fraction of the parameters used by single-task models.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the British Machine Vision Conference (BMVC 2026)

</details>

<a id="paper-59"></a>

### 59. [Reliability-Aware Short-Term Glycemic Risk Prediction Using an Anchor-Based Comparative Framework of Sequence, Multimodal, and State-Updating Digital-Twin Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36363v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36363

**作者：** Pir Bakhsh Khokhar、Carmine Gravino、Fabio Palomba

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36363v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36363v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文基于 T1DiabetesGranada 构建约 550 万个标准化预测锚点，在 30/60/120 分钟尺度上比较 Hybrid LSTM-Transformer、多模态数字孪生与带缺口感知状态更新的数字孪生三类模型做低/高血糖风险预测，并额外评估校准、阈值、解释可靠性与扰动鲁棒性。

**相关性简评：** 其中「缺口感知的纵向状态推进」与校准、跨模型分歧、解释忠实度的评估协议，可供核查 latent 推理中推理状态更新与可靠性评价的借鉴线索，但领域为临床血糖时序，迁移性需人工判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Reliability-Aware Short-Term Glycemic Risk Prediction Using an Anchor-Based Comparative Framework of Sequence, Multimodal, and State-Updating Digital-Twin Models

**abstract/A1：** Anticipating short-term hypoglycemic and hyperglycemic risk is essential for proactive diabetes management. However, existing machine learning approaches often use inconsistent evaluation protocols and emphasize predictive performance over reliability. This study introduces an anchor-based benchmarking framework integrating longitudinal glucose sequences and multimodal patient information. Using the T1DiabetesGranada dataset, 5,503,441 standardized prediction anchors were constructed from 736 individuals with type 1 diabetes to evaluate hypoglycemia and hyperglycemia prediction at 30-, 60-, and 120-minute horizons. Three neural architectures, a Hybrid LSTM-Transformer, a Multimodal Digital Twin, and a State-Updating Digital Twin with gap-aware longitudinal state progression, were compared under identical patient-level validation protocols. Beyond predictive performance, the evaluation examined calibration, classification thresholds, explanation reliability, cross-model disagreement, and robustness to controlled perturbations. No statistically significant predictive differences were observed after Holm correction, although the models exhibited complementary reliability characteristics. The Multimodal Digital Twin achieved the lowest mean Brier score, while the State-Updating Digital Twin demonstrated the lowest mean expected calibration error (ECE-10). The Hybrid LSTM-Transformer showed strong explanation faithfulness and stability under its temporal attribution protocol. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating digital-twin models beyond predictive accuracy and emphasize the need for reliability assessment and external validation before clinical deployment.

**comment/C1：** 36 pages, including figures, tables, and references. Research article on reliability-aware glycemic-risk prediction using sequence models and multimodal digital twins

</details>

<a id="paper-60"></a>

### 60. [BoxWorld: Let World Engines Control and Video Generators Render](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36371v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36371

**作者：** Zike Wu、Ahmed Hatem、Chintan Trivedi、Wallace Michel Pinto Lira、Xuebin Qin、Lele Wang、Renjie Liao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36371v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36371v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 BoxWorld，将交互推理与生成渲染解耦：由编码 agent 依据用户规则生成世界引擎，用 layout box、持久属性与状态变量组成的轻量接口输出对象状态，再由生成式渲染器自回归地把状态流渲染为视频，并配套三阶段训练与成对交互控制基准。

**相关性简评：** 其「显式状态接口 + 渲染器只负责外观」的分工，以及基于自生成历史的 rollout 蒸馏，或可作为 LVR 中内部状态保留哪些任务必要信息、渲染/生成阶段如何使用该状态的可核查线索，但研究目标仍是交互视频世界模型而非 VLM 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BoxWorld: Let World Engines Control and Video Generators Render

**abstract/A1：** We tackle the challenge of building real-time interactive video worlds with user-defined behaviors. Such worlds must track object states, apply interaction rules, and render the resulting consequences as users act. Yet existing end-to-end video world models typically learn interaction dynamics within the generator, making it difficult to specify object-level rules and reason about their consequences. In this work, we introduce BoxWorld, which separates interaction reasoning from generative rendering. Given user-specified rules, a coding agent creates a Box World Engine that determines interaction outcomes and exposes the resulting object states through a lightweight interface of layout boxes, persistent object attributes, and dynamic status variables, leaving appearance and physical effects to generative rendering. A Box-State Generative Renderer then renders this evolving state stream into video autoregressively, enabling online control over object motion, state switches, and conditional events. Rendering this state stream is non-trivial: the renderer must respond to object-level state updates in real time while remaining coherent with the generated history. To this end, we introduce a three-stage training strategy that learns object grounding from open-domain videos and state transitions from gameplay videos, both annotated automatically, and finally performs rollout distillation on self-generated histories. We further propose PairedStateBench, a paired benchmark for fine-grained interaction control. BoxWorld substantially outperforms end-to-end world models and remains competitive across multiple public benchmarks. Project page: https://boxworld-web.github.io.

</details>

<a id="paper-61"></a>

### 61. [From Generation to Scoring: Scaling LLM-Based Semantic Labeling at LinkedIn](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36383v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36383

**作者：** Jian Sheng、Sriram Vasudevan、Sundara Raman Ramachandran、Yang Hu、Junyu Zhou、Xiaojing Ma、Haolan Qin、Tiffany Zhou、Vedant Vipul Jhaveri、Ashwini Bhingare、Chinmay Kini、Qing Lan、Xiaoqing Wang、Suleyman Cetintas、Fedor Borisyuk

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DC、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36383v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36383v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把 LinkedIn 的工业级 LLM 标注器从「打包生成 YES/NO 列表」改为语义打分：在单候选提示上微调，用仅 prefill 的一次前向取答案 token 分数作为判定，并将 Multiple-Item Scoring 改造为在单序列内对数百个候选打分且候选间加注意力掩码，报告顺序稳定性、与参考标签一致性及吞吐提升。

**相关性简评：** 「单候选打分 + 候选间注意力隔离的批量独立评分」可能为 LVR 中并行评估多个 latent 假设或证据候选、抑制候选间串扰并省去解码成本提供可核查的实现线索，但原文不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Generation to Scoring: Scaling LLM-Based Semantic Labeling at LinkedIn

**abstract/A1：** For efficiency, industrial large language model (LLM) labelers often pack many candidate labels into one prompt and generate a YES/NO answer for each, even when each decision depends only on the entity and one candidate. Generating these independent decisions as one answer list couples them, spends decoding on the list, and relies on the list being well-formed. In this paper, we present an end-to-end recipe that reformulates the labeler as semantic scoring, fine-tuning it on single-candidate prompts to return each decision as one answer-token score from a prefill-only pass. We also adapt Multiple-Item Scoring (MIS) to score hundreds of candidates in one sequence with attention masked between them. Deployed for profile qualification labeling at LinkedIn, semantic scoring changes at most 0.01% of decisions when candidates are reordered and matches the agreement with reference labels that packed generation reaches only with about 4.7x as many training pairs. The MIS-based stack raises throughput over the deployed stack by 1.24x offline and 1.9x in model serving under a one-second p99 latency target. For offline batch inference, we implement MIS-based scoring as a reusable, model-agnostic Ray Data API proposed for upstream adoption. Model serving uses MIS support contributed to SGLang.

</details>

<a id="paper-62"></a>

### 62. [LoRA on a Budget: Function-Aware Post-Hoc Compression for Adapter Fleets](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36386v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36386

**作者：** Shenghui Li、Thiemo voigt

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36386v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36386v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究如何在共享秩预算下压缩大量 LoRA adapter：为每个 adapter 构建「功能-秩失真前沿」，先用端到端测得的孤立模块截断成本作可加代理并用动态规划搜索，再对完整候选复测，最后在舰队层面做 exact minimax 分配；在三个公开 fleet 的 78 个 adapter 上优于统一谱压缩。

**相关性简评：** 其「谱冗余不等于功能可容忍度」的度量思路与按层、按单元分配预算的 minimax 方案，可供核查是否用于 latent 推理中逐层状态容量与计算预算分配、以及贡献分配的评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LoRA on a Budget: Function-Aware Post-Hoc Compression for Adapter Fleets

**abstract/A1：** Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a key mechanism for efficiently specializing foundation models into many task- and user-specific variants. Serving many LoRA adapters under a shared resource budget turns post-hoc compression into a two-level allocation problem: rank must be distributed across layers within each adapter and across adapters within the fleet. Although trained LoRAs contain substantial spectral redundancy, spectral reconstruction alone does not determine how much compression an adapter can functionally tolerate. We formulate fleet compression through a functional rank-distortion frontier for each adapter, followed by fleet-level minimax allocation under a shared rank budget. Given exact frontiers, this decomposition is equivalent to joint optimization over all layer ranks. We introduce Functional Rank Allocation (FRA), an unlabeled method for constructing practical frontiers. FRA measures isolated module truncations end to end, uses these measurements as an additive search surrogate solved by dynamic programming, remeasures every complete candidate, and takes the lower-risk envelope with a complementary spectral proposal before exact minimax allocation over the constructed frontiers. Across 78 adapters from three public fleets, FRA improves worst-case and tail utility over uniform spectral compression in constrained-budget regimes. Fixed-budget probes show that isolated costs rank candidates well, but their calibration shifts across proposal shapes; end-to-end verification and the spectral proposal therefore limit functional-search error.

</details>

<a id="paper-63"></a>

### 63. [SpecLST: Spectral-Enhanced Long-Short Term Spatio-Temporal Multimodal Modeling for Traffic Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36393v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36393

**作者：** Kenan Kang、Matthew M. Y. Kuo、Weihua Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36393v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36393v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向交通预测提出 SpecLST：用基于快速傅里叶变换的频域表示模块压缩多周历史依赖，规避长序列注意力的显存瓶颈，再以注意力融合异构外部因素、稀疏 MoE 加图增强骨干建模时空表征，并用频域残差头分离高频瞬变。

**相关性简评：** 交通时空预测本身与 LVR 主线距离较远，但其用频域紧凑表示替代长序列注意力、以稀疏专家与残差头分别承载长期上下文和瞬态信号的思路，可能可供核查 latent 推理状态的压缩与长上下文长度泛化问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SpecLST: Spectral-Enhanced Long-Short Term Spatio-Temporal Multimodal Modeling for Traffic Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Modeling multi-scale dynamics in urban road networks is essential for intelligent transportation systems but is often hindered by the computational cost of long-range historical context and the challenge of fusing heterogeneous modalities. Existing spatio-temporal frameworks face a trade-off between sequence length and memory overhead, while frequently neglecting the transient effects of exogenous factors such as road incidents and weather. To address these limitations, we propose SpecLST, a frequency-enhanced long-short-term spatio-temporal multimodal framework. SpecLST leverages a fast Fourier transform-based frequency-domain representation module to efficiently encode multi-week historical dependencies, bypassing the memory bottlenecks of standard attention mechanisms over raw long sequences. Heterogeneous context is integrated through attention-based fusion, followed by a sparse Mixture-of-Experts backbone with graph augmentation for spatio-temporal representation learning. Additionally, a frequency-enhanced residual head isolates high-frequency transients, improving sensitivity to abrupt traffic anomalies. At the 60-minute horizon, SpecLST achieves mean relative error reductions of 14.10% in MAE, 10.35% in RMSE, and 15.78% in MAPE across eight neural forecasting baselines and four datasets, with the dataset- and metric-specific average reductions ranging from 5.63% to 22.21%.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 2 figures, 5 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-64"></a>

### 64. [Persistence, Exposure, and Credit Routing in Linear Temporal Predictive Coding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36412v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36412

**作者：** Akira Tanase、Julian Gould、Jeffrey Seely

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.IT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36412v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36412v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用 cellular sheaf 理论分析线性 temporal predictive coding 的时序信用分配，引入 persistence 与 exposure 算子，得出空间推断的有效循环转移为 F=PA、未来信用被 P\* 滤波，并揭示长期影响保持与误差暴露之间的权衡及模式间路由的绕行方式。

**相关性简评：** 其「持久—暴露」权衡可为核查 LVR 中 latent 状态跨推理步的影响留存与监督信号能否暴露该影响提供分析工具，但结论限于线性模型且与视觉任务无关，迁移线索需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Persistence, Exposure, and Credit Routing in Linear Temporal Predictive Coding

**abstract/A1：** Temporal Predictive Coding (tPC) networks offer a layer-local alternative to backpropagation. For linear tPC, we show, by applying tools from cellular sheaf theory, that temporal credit assignment is a tug-of-war between spatial displacement and temporal errors, whose ratios are modulated by persistence and exposure operators $P$ and $Q$. Spatial inference therefore gives us the effective recurrent transition $F=PA$, whilst future credit is filtered by $P^\\ast$. This creates a persistence--error tradeoff in spatial modes: under non-amplifying invariant recurrence, modes that preserve influence over long delays simultaneously fail to expose that influence strongly as temporal error. Recurrence can circumvent this via routing between persistent and exposed modes.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Symmetry and Geometry in Neural Representations (NeurReps)

</details>

<a id="paper-65"></a>

### 65. [SpeeDiff: Scalable Pixel-Anchored End-to-End Latent Diffusion Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36419v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36419

**作者：** Bingliang Zhang、Wenda Chu、Yizhuo Li、Linjie Yang、Yisong Yue、Katherine L. Bouman、Yang Song、Qiushan Guo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36419v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36419v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SpeeDiff，从零联合训练 VAE 与扩散模型的 latent diffusion 方法，用 Tweedie 像素重建损失从中途噪声态解码预测的干净 latent，以缓解潜空间坍缩，并实现训练加速与高保真生成。

**相关性简评：** 其「扩散梯度直通编码器导致潜空间坍缩」的诊断与像素级重建锚定，可能为 LVR 中视觉细节在 latent 状态中丢失、以及用像素反馈约束表示提供可核查线索；但研究对象是图像生成，迁移到多模态推理需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SpeeDiff: Scalable Pixel-Anchored End-to-End Latent Diffusion Model

**abstract/A1：** We present Scalable Pixel-anchored End-to-end Diffusion (SpeeDiff), a latent diffusion method that jointly trains the VAE and the diffusion model from scratch. In principle, joint training allows the diffusion loss gradient to directly guide the VAE encoder, encouraging the formation of a generation-friendly latent space and potentially yielding faster convergence than the conventional two-stage approach with a pretrained frozen VAE. However, a naive end-to-end implementation severely degrades performance, as unrestricted backpropagation of the diffusion loss leads to latent space collapse. Our main technical contribution is a simple yet effective Tweedie Pixel Reconstruction (TPR) loss, which provides additional pixel-level feedback by decoding a predicted clean latent from an intermediate noisy state using Tweedie's formula, thereby alleviating collapse. Furthermore, our method enables jointly scaling a fully transformer-based architecture and enhances representation alignment within the end-to-end framework. Our SpeeDiff-XL model achieves over 140x and 61x faster training compared to Vanilla SiT and REPA, respectively, while attaining an FID of 1.50 without guidance on ImageNet 256x256 generation. With a more efficient 32x compressed VAE, our model further reaches an FID of 1.53 without guidance on ImageNet 512x512 generation.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to CVPR 2026. 29 pages, 17 figures, 14 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-66"></a>

### 66. [Repulsive Population Policy Gradients for Behaviorally Diverse Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36425v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36425

**作者：** Saeed Hedayatian、Harsh Shah、Celine Mneimneh、Stefanos Nikolaidis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36425v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36425v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RePPO，用群体依赖、动态塑形的奖励把 PPO 扩展为对一小群高质量且行为多样的策略做联合优化，并在连续控制、像素输入与 LLM 微调上考察质量—多样性权衡。

**相关性简评：** 其「以排斥性奖励维持群体多样性」的训练机制，或可供核查于缓解 latent 推理轨迹训练中的模式坍缩与多样性流失，但原文并不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Repulsive Population Policy Gradients for Behaviorally Diverse Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** The goal of Quality-Diversity Reinforcement Learning (QD-RL) is to find a population of high-performing policies that solve a decision-making problem in diverse ways. Despite recent progress, QD-RL methods predominantly rely on evolutionary operators--including black-box optimizers, mutations, and crossovers--to facilitate exploration and induce diversity. Scaling these methods becomes increasingly costly as policies and behavior spaces grow larger. Building on the Soft QD view of quality-diversity optimization, we introduce \\textbf{Re}pulsive \\textbf{P}opulation \\textbf{P}olicy \\textbf{O}ptimization (\\textbf{RePPO}), which adapts PPO to jointly optimize a compact population of diverse, high-quality policies using a population-dependent, dynamically shaped reward. On standard continuous-control tasks, RePPO achieves a strong quality-diversity tradeoff compared with state-of-the-art QD-RL methods. Ablations further characterize this tradeoff by examining how each component affects individual quality and collective diversity. To assess RePPO's scalability, we extend our evaluation to learning from pixels and fine-tuning large language models. In pixel-based tasks, where large policies make most traditional QD-RL methods infeasible, RePPO maintains quality on par with standard PPO while achieving substantially higher diversity. Its successful application to language model fine-tuning further demonstrates, for the first time, that QD-RL methods can scale to this domain. Together, these results show that directly optimizing compact populations with RePPO provides a scalable approach to QD-RL across increasingly complex domains.

</details>

<a id="paper-67"></a>
