### 67. [From Solver Trajectories to Teaching Trajectories: Cognition-Aligned Reasoning Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36445v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36445

**作者：** Yashuo Luo、Qianren Mao、Ziqi Qin、Junnan Liu、Haitao Li、Qili Zhang、Yuan Gao、Zhijun Chen、Xu Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36445v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36445v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 CARD，用 RL 把大模型的 solver 推理轨迹改写成更符合小学生「认知容量」的 teaching 轨迹，以分布对齐奖励和逻辑连贯奖励降低误导性错误步骤，避免昂贵的重采样，并在域外泛化上报告提升。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本 CoT 蒸馏，不涉及视觉或 latent 状态，但其「教师—学生认知不匹配下重写与筛选轨迹监督」的思路，可供核查 LVR 中轨迹训练、错误步骤抑制与监督贡献分配等问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Solver Trajectories to Teaching Trajectories: Cognition-Aligned Reasoning Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Knowledge distillation from large reasoning models (LRMs) often uses raw teacher trajectories as supervision. However, this practice creates a mismatch between the goal of distillation and the nature of teacher trajectories. Advanced LRMs typically improve their reasoning capabilities through reward-driven post-training, such as RLHF. This training optimizes them as strong solvers, rather than as teachers that produce reasoning processes smaller models can easily learn. Since smaller models have different "cognitive capacities" compared to their larger teachers, directly imitating raw teacher trajectories can sometimes be ineffective and often requires a substantial number of high-quality samples. To bridge this mismatch, we propose Cognition-Aligned Reasoning Distillation (CARD), an RL-based framework that transforms solver trajectories into teaching trajectories aligned with the student's cognition. Avoiding expensive resampling, our framework directly transforms existing trajectories. We optimize CARD with two reward signals: (1) a Distribution Alignment Reward that matches the student's predictive distribution, and (2) a Logical Coherence Reward that reduces misleading failed reasoning steps. Comprehensive evaluations across multiple student models and settings demonstrate that CARD outperforms distillation from raw teacher trajectories in in-domain settings while achieving substantial gains in out-of-distribution performance. Code is available at https://github.com/LiteSSLHub/CARD.

</details>

<a id="paper-68"></a>

### 68. [Correction or Manipulation? Measuring Epistemic Vigilance in LLM Agents Under User Pressure](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36453v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36453

**作者：** Nardine Basta、Venus Haghighi、Dali Kaafar、Amin Beheshti

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36453v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36453v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在七个 LLM 上用分级用户压力区分“纠正”与“操纵”，并借助信号检测论分离让步倾向与辨别力，同时考察客服代理的验证政策与实际行动。

**相关性简评：** 其把“是否真正辨别证据”与“整体让步倾向”分开测量的思路，可能为核查 LVR 是使用视觉证据还是受提示压力左右提供评价线索；但研究为纯文本代理行为，需进一步判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Correction or Manipulation? Measuring Epistemic Vigilance in LLM Agents Under User Pressure

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents that answer questions and act on behalf of users must decide when to yield to a user who disagrees. Disagreement may correct a genuine error or may be an attempt at manipulation. Prior work shows that models frequently abandon correct answers when challenged by users, yet it remains unclear whether they distinguish correction from manipulation or merely differ in their propensity to concede. We examine this question across seven LLMs from four vendors in two studies, one on the answers agents give and one on the actions they take as customer-support agents whose policy permits account changes only for verified users. Simulated users push toward correct and incorrect answers with graded pressure. Following research on epistemic vigilance, the pressure either appeals to the source, such as claimed authority, or offers content the agent cannot verify, namely a cited source. Drawing on signal detection theory, we measure on a set of benchmark questions how much more readily each agent concedes to correction than to manipulation, separately from its overall propensity to concede. Citations made five of seven models readier to concede and four worse at discriminating, while stronger wording added little. In the agentic study, five models never violated the verification policy, including models that readily abandoned correct answers, so concession on answers did not predict violations of policy. Ministral 3B violated the policy mainly under claimed authority, and Ministral 8B reported changes it had not made. An instruction to revise only on verifiable evidence chiefly shifted propensity to concede rather than improving discrimination. Concession rates alone are therefore misleading, and agents should be evaluated on both their discrimination and the actions they take.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 7 figures, 11 tables. 8-page main paper with supplementary material included as an appendix. Under review

</details>

<a id="paper-69"></a>

### 69. [Full Collapse Is Suboptimal: Controlled Partial Neural Collapse for Long-Tailed Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36460v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36460

**作者：** Hyeontaek Hwang、Cheonghwan Hur、Daeyoung Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36460v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36460v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出受控的部分神经坍缩，用类别自适应稀疏激活分类器和ETF约束的自适应间隔损失，让头部类沿ETF完全坍缩、尾部类保留类内多样性，在多个长尾识别基准上取得最佳总体准确率。

**相关性简评：** 其「按类别调节坍缩程度」与特征条件门控分配专属特征方向的做法，可能可供核查 latent 推理状态如何避免过度坍缩、保留多样推理方向，但任务属长尾图像分类，与 VLM 推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Full Collapse Is Suboptimal: Controlled Partial Neural Collapse for Long-Tailed Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Recent long-tailed recognition (LTR) methods incorporate neural collapse (NC) and align features to a fixed Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF) as a uniform target for all classes. However, forcing tail classes to fully collapse over-contracts their samples and weakens generalization. To address this, we propose a controlled partial neural collapse, realized through two coordinated controls on a backbone: the Class-Adaptive Sparse Activation (CASA) classifier and the ETF-constrained adaptive margin (ETF-AM) loss. Rather than forcing a uniform full collapse, this regime allows head classes to collapse along the ETF while tail classes retain within-class diversity. This prevents the tail from being forced into full collapse, which is suboptimal under imbalance. To set the dimensionality of collapse, CASA induces class-related node specialization through feature-conditioned gating so that tail classes occupy their own feature directions. For the clearance axis, ETF-AM applies a frequency-adaptive margin, with smaller margins for head classes and larger for tail classes. On CIFAR100-LT, ImageNet-LT, Places-LT, and iNaturalist-2018, our method achieves the best overall accuracy over prior methods. In the empirical analysis, within-class spread increases monotonically from head to tail classes, consistent with tail classes retaining variability rather than fully collapsing.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-70"></a>

### 70. [ClusterSplat: Semantic Cluster Selection for 3D Visual Grounding in Gaussian Splatting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36462v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36462

**作者：** Jaesung Lee、Hyeontaek Hwang、Gabriel Manalu、Daeyoung Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36462v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36462v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 3D Gaussian Splatting 中的三维视觉指代定位，把目标选择从逐基元的阈值筛选改为场景自适应簇候选上的查询条件打分，并由选中簇同时给出显式 3D 目标与渲染 2D 掩码。

**相关性简评：** 其「先将稠密相关性聚合为场景自适应候选簇、再用轻量打分器按查询排序」的思路，可能为 LVR 的视觉证据筛选与候选区域构造提供方法线索，但原文不涉及推理状态或推理期证据获取，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ClusterSplat: Semantic Cluster Selection for 3D Visual Grounding in Gaussian Splatting

**abstract/A1：** We study 3D visual grounding in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), where a referring expression should identify a target object as both a rendered 2D mask and a set of explicit 3D Gaussians. Prior methods typically extract 3D targets by applying heuristic thresholding or top-ratio filtering to dense primitive-level relevance scores. However, these scores often vary substantially across scenes and prompts, making such heuristics sensitive and leading to unstable targeting. ClusterSplat addresses this by reframing 3D target selection as cluster-level selection over scene-adaptive candidates. The method first learns instance-aware Gaussian features, forms feature-aware seed voxels, and merges neighboring seed voxels when a description-length criterion decreases, producing a scene-specific set of cluster candidates. Given a referring expression, a query-conditioned cluster scorer ranks the cluster candidates with a lightweight MLP, and the selected cluster directly defines the explicit 3D target while its cluster score is rendered for the 2D mask. On ScanRefer, ClusterSplat achieves the best rendered 2D segmentation and explicit 3D target metrics among the compared 3DGS baselines. It also obtains the best average Ref-LERF scores, supporting the same trend under fine-grained referring descriptions. On ScanNet v2 class name queries, ClusterSplat achieves the strongest rendered 2D results and the highest 3D matching accuracy, while maintaining competitive 3D IoU. Our project page is available at https://jaesung8.github.io/clustersplat/ .

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 as a Spotlight paper. Project page: https://jaesung8.github.io/clustersplat/

</details>

<a id="paper-71"></a>

### 71. [Proposing Better Rollouts for On-Policy Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36477v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36477

**作者：** Sirui Chen、Yunzhe Qi、Haobo Xu、Jiaru Zou、Mengting Ai、Zihao Li、Jingrui He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36477v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36477v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文分析 on-policy distillation 中教师对学生在轨轨迹监督失配的问题，提出以教师相对支持度构造学生锚定的几何混合 rollout 分布，并用单次前向的 proposal policy 近似采样。

**相关性简评：** 其按教师支持度筛选并重加权推理轨迹的思路，可供核查 LVR 轨迹训练中如何避免对教师不可达状态施加噪声监督，但与视觉表示和 latent 推理状态无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Proposing Better Rollouts for On-Policy Distillation

**abstract/A1：** On-policy distillation (OPD) improves language-model post-training by letting the student learn from its own sampled trajectories under teacher supervision, aligning the training process with inference-time behavior. However, the rollout policy in OPD is usually treated as fixed: each round samples directly from the current student. We show that this choice can create a compounding teacher--student mismatch, because it requires the teacher to provide effective supervision on whichever trajectories the student samples, including ones less reachable by the teacher itself. On such trajectories, teacher supervision can become noisy or even misleading, leading to undesirable student patterns such as repetition. Moreover, since the updated student in turn defines the rollout policy of subsequent rounds, these patterns persist and potentially amplify over training. This failure mode suggests that improving OPD requires treating the rollout policy itself as an optimization target, rather than only modifying the distillation objective. We propose Mixture Proposal On-Policy Distillation (MPOPD), which derives a student-anchored, teacher-guided rollout distribution from a local trust-region objective based on relative teacher support. The resulting target is a geometric mixture of the student and teacher policies, and we introduce a proposal policy that approximates this mixture, enabling rollout generation in a single forward pass. Experiments on 16 math, knowledge, and medical reasoning benchmarks show consistent gains over OPD and recent variants. On math reasoning, our method improves average accuracy by 6.91 over OPD on Qwen3-4B-Base while reducing average generation length by 41%, suggesting that rollout policy design provides an effective way to improve OPD.

</details>

<a id="paper-72"></a>

### 72. [AC-JEPA: Anchored Cross-Modal Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture for Visual-to-Symbolic Translation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36493v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36493

**作者：** Chaofei Hong、Ziyang Chen、Baochen Wang、Christina Yi Jin、Qing Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36493v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36493v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 AC-JEPA，在跨模态双向方向上预测被遮蔽的中间状态，把表示空间预测作为与 SFT 并列的插件式目标，并用于含噪伪标签的自训练；以化学结构识别为测试床，报告了相较纯 SFT 的准确率提升与噪声数据下的稳健性。

**相关性简评：** 其「以连续 latent 目标替代硬 token 监督、对局部标注错误更容忍」的训练思路，可能为 LVR 的轨迹监督、噪声监督与视觉旁路利用提供可核查线索；但任务属图像到符号的翻译，未涉及推理期 latent 状态生成，迁移性有待阅读正文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AC-JEPA: Anchored Cross-Modal Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture for Visual-to-Symbolic Translation

**abstract/A1：** Cross-modal translation requires models to capture shared semantics across modalities while preserving the fine-grained structure required by the target modality. Yet in real-world scientific domains, paired data are often imperfect, while obtaining clean pairs at scale is costly. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) directly optimizes cross-modal translation through categorical token-level supervision, but this objective is sensitive to errors in the target sequences. JEPA offers a complementary representation-space objective, but most existing formulations focus on prediction within a modality rather than translation across modalities. We introduce Anchored Cross-Modal JEPA (AC-JEPA), an SFT-compatible plug-in that predicts masked intermediate states in both cross-modal directions using paired data. The joint-embedding objective targets cross-modal structure predictable from context, while the causal SFT path retains direct token-level supervision for the target sequence. During self-training, AC-JEPA learns from continuous latent targets derived from imperfect pseudo-labeled pairs, offering intrinsic tolerance to local data errors without promoting pseudo-tokens to hard categorical supervision. Using optical chemical structure recognition as a testbed, AC-JEPA trained on clean pairs achieves 76.07% accuracy on the Simple set of the WildMol benchmark, compared with 67.40% for SFT alone. Using the same pseudo-labeled data for both approaches, AC-JEPA-based self-training further raises accuracy to 80.23%, whereas accuracy under direct SFT drops from 67.40% to 63.47%. These results support SFT-anchored joint-embedding prediction as an effective strategy for cross-modal translation under both curated and imperfect supervision.

</details>

<a id="paper-73"></a>

### 73. [ARISE: Authentic Reference-Informed Stability Enhancement for Continual Multimodal Forgery Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36509v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36509

**作者：** Yuchen Zhang、Yaxiong Wang、Yujiao Wu、Lianwei Wu、Qi Zhang、Li Zhu、Chaofeng Dong、Le Wu、Zhedong Zheng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36509v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36509v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文将多模态伪造检测置于攻击增量式持续学习设定，提出 Fable-Bench 基准与 ARISE 方法：用仅真实样本拟合的 GMM 表示历史参考，并以各向异性度量约束稳定表征方向、按阶段脆弱度分配回放权重。

**相关性简评：** 论文不涉及推理期 latent 状态或推理链，但其「用真实表征分布作参考、约束历史稳定方向」的训练线索，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据表征在持续训练下的漂移与保留问题；迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ARISE: Authentic Reference-Informed Stability Enhancement for Continual Multimodal Forgery Detection

**abstract/A1：** Multimodal forgery detection is commonly studied under a static assumption: training attacks are expected to approximate those encountered after deployment. This assumption becomes increasingly fragile as generators, editing systems, and multimodal large language models continually introduce new forgery mechanisms and forensic evidence. We formulate this problem as attack-incremental continual multimodal forgery detection, where the binary real/fake interface remains fixed while emerging attacks must be learned without forgetting previous ones. We introduce Fable-Bench, which organizes attack evolution through complementary joint-modality, text-side, and image-side protocols. We further propose ARISE (Authentic Reference-Informed Stability Enhancement), motivated by the open-ended forged class and the comparatively reusable geometry of authentic content. ARISE fits a real-only Gaussian mixture model to projected authentic replay representations as a compact historical reference. Meanwhile, the proposed Gaussian-Responsibility-Aware Stability Preservation (GRASP) derives a sample-adaptive anisotropic metric that strongly constrains historically stable representation directions while retaining plasticity elsewhere. Our Vulnerability-Aware Replay (VAR) further estimates stage-wise vulnerability from authentic-density degradation, forged-to-authentic boundary confusion, and authentic-mode occupancy drift, then assigns bounded replay emphasis to fragile stages. All adaptive signals are obtained from training-time replay statistics, while inference uses only the original multimodal backbone and binary classification head. Experiments across three attack-evolution protocols and multiple detector architectures demonstrate improved historical retention and final average performance over representative continual-learning baselines.

</details>

<a id="paper-74"></a>

### 74. [Folding Attention Biases into Keys Makes Compacted KV Caches Run on Standard Kernels](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36520v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36520

**作者：** Utkarsh Kanwat

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36520v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36520v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究如何把 Attention Matching 压缩 KV cache 时加入的注意力偏置，沿每个头的一个闭式方向折叠进 key 本身，从而得到可由 FlashAttention 和 vLLM 标准 kernel 直接服务的紧凑缓存，并给出输出误差随查询在该方向投影偏离 1 的程度而受限的界；在 Qwen3 与 Llama-3.1 的多项长文本基准上，其精度与 AM 相当或接近。

**相关性简评：** KV cache 压缩直接决定长上下文 VLM 推理时内部状态还保留多少视觉证据，其对「缓存与注意力结构如何影响 latent 相似度、细节是否被旁路」的问题可供核查；但该文只处理文本长上下文服务效率，与 LVR 推理机制无直接联系，需先核查压缩误差界能否用于测量视觉信息损失。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Folding Attention Biases into Keys Makes Compacted KV Caches Run on Standard Kernels

**abstract/A1：** Attention Matching (AM) compacts a KV cache by keeping some keys per head, refitting their values and adding a bias to each kept key's logit. FlashAttention and vLLM's paged caches have no input for it. We fold the bias into the keys along one closed-form direction per head, fitted on context queries, giving a plain cache with AM's size and head dimension and the model's own query path; the output error is bounded by how far each query's projection on that direction is from 1. With equal per-head budgets, unmodified vLLM serves folded caches through a KV connector, matching Hugging Face's answers on all 51 Qwen3-4B questions and its greedy output on 180 of 200 Qwen3-14B LongBench samples. With equal budgets on QuALITY (Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, 8B, Llama-3.1-8B) and LongHealth (three Qwen3 models), the fold is within noise of AM's accuracy in 13 of 14 settings, with lower KL to the full cache than dropping the bias in all 12 where both ran. Under the published per-head budgets, on Qwen3-4B, its Instruct-2507 variant and Llama-3.1-8B, it is within noise of AM in 9 of 10, no longer beats dropping the bias in KL, and the two each win once in accuracy.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 2 figures, 18 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-75"></a>

### 75. [FrameForge: Harnessing Priors of Video Foundation Models for Unified Image Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36545v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36545

**作者：** Yijing Lin、Zhendong Mao、Mengqi Huang、Yuxin Wang、Yongdong Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36545v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36545v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** FrameForge 利用视频基础模型的时间变换先验做统一条件图像生成：以 Mixture of Temporal Experts 动态选择不同虚拟时间间隔，并用 Chain of Temporal Reasoning 通过一个 latent 中间状态建模从条件到目标的变换。

**相关性简评：** 其「latent 中间状态 + reasoning-bottleneck 注意力掩码」强制信息经该通路传递，或可供核查 LVR 中如何抑制视觉旁路、保证输出真正使用 latent 内容；但任务为图像生成而非多模态推理，迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FrameForge: Harnessing Priors of Video Foundation Models for Unified Image Generation

**abstract/A1：** Unified conditional image generation requires a single model to preserve different aspects of a visual input while producing a target image that follows textual guidance. Pretrained video models provide a promising foundation by encoding visual transformations across time, yet existing approaches typically assign the condition and target to fixed temporal positions. We observe that condition-target relationships vary substantially across generation tasks, while video models naturally encode visual transformations at different temporal scales. This motivates us to model conditional generation through input-adaptive virtual temporal relations. We propose FrameForge, which captures two complementary aspects of such relations. Mixture of Temporal Experts (MoTE) models how far the target lies from the condition by dynamically selecting temporal RoPE experts with different virtual intervals. Chain of Temporal Reasoning (CoTR) models how the condition is transformed into the target through a latent intermediate state, with a reasoning-bottleneck attention mask encouraging information propagation through this pathway. Experiments on DreamBench, COCO2017, and GEdit-Bench show that FrameForge achieves superior performance in subject-driven generation, canny-to-image generation, and image editing among the compared methods.

</details>

<a id="paper-76"></a>

### 76. [Decoupling Consistency and Conflict across MLLM Layers for Fake News Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36569v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36569

**作者：** Kai Qian、Dongjie Xu、Weijie Shi、Jiaqi Wang、Haolun Ma、Jia Zhu、Jiajie Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36569v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36569v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MLCCD 用于多模态假新闻检测：先用 MLLM 最终决策表示筛出任务相关的视觉与文本 token，再保留相邻层表示变化剧烈的 token 以捕获各层新增知识，最后把层间知识解耦为一致性与冲突证据并分别用 router 跨层聚合。

**相关性简评：** 其「借最终决策表示做视觉证据筛选、以层间表示变化作信号并分别路由聚合」的流程，可供核查 LVR 中视觉细节保留、层深与表示差异对任务必要信息的影响；但属判别式检测任务，未涉及推理期 latent 状态的生成与使用，需较多核查才能迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Decoupling Consistency and Conflict across MLLM Layers for Fake News Detection

**abstract/A1：** The trustworthiness of Web content faces a growing threat from multimodal fake news. Real news usually shows aligned image-text semantics, while fake news often contains a few conflicts at varying levels, such as mismatches in objects, scenes, events, and claims. However, existing methods usually treat consistency and conflict in a coarse and static way, limiting their ability to capture sparse conflicts and adapt to varying conflict levels. Inspired by the distinct knowledge encoded across layers in large language models, we propose Multi-Level Consistency and Conflict Decoupling (MLCCD), which captures conflicts at different levels from different layers of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Specifically, we first use the MLLM's final decision representation to select visual and textual tokens relevant to the task, preventing irrelevant content from diluting sparse conflicts. Then, we preserve tokens whose representations change sharply between adjacent layers, capturing the knowledge each layer adds. Finally, we decouple this knowledge into consistency and conflict evidence and aggregate each across layers with its own router, adapting to varying conflict levels. Experiments on three benchmarks show that MLCCD outperforms existing methods.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-77"></a>

### 77. [Learning from Points and Patches: A Pre-trained Hybrid Attention Transformer for Time Series Analysis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36572v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36572

**作者：** Peiwang Tang、Cheng HE、Yupeng Ye、Weitai Zhang、Qinghua Sun、Defu Lian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36572v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36572v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TimeHAT，一个时间序列通用预训练框架：用统一点级与片段级掩码策略同时做上下文重建与因果外推，并在浅层采用前缀约束的双向注意力、深层采用方向混合注意力，以缓解秩退化；在分类、长期预测、插补与异常检测上验证微调、少样本与零样本迁移。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉多模态推理无直接关系，但其按深度区分的双向/因果混合注意力与多粒度掩码监督，或可供核查层级注意力结构如何影响 latent 状态的更新与相似度，以及推理轨迹的监督粒度选择。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning from Points and Patches: A Pre-trained Hybrid Attention Transformer for Time Series Analysis

**abstract/A1：** Time-series data generated by Web services and connected systems support applications such as user activity analysis, service monitoring, and resource management. However, learning transferable representations from heterogeneous time series remains challenging due to diverse temporal patterns, variable sequence lengths, and limited high-quality annotations. Despite the success of pre-training in natural language processing and computer vision, effective time-series pre-training requires strategies tailored to temporal data. We propose TimeHAT (Hybrid Attention Transformer), a general-purpose pre-training framework for time-series analysis that addresses two complementary questions: what to learn and how to learn. For what to learn, we introduce a Unified Point-Patch Masking Strategy (UPPMS) that integrates point-level and patch-level supervision within a unified framework for contextual reconstruction and causal extrapolation, enabling the model to capture both fine-grained temporal variations and broader temporal patterns. For how to learn, we develop a Hybrid Attention Encoder (HAE) that combines prefix-constrained bidirectional attention in initial layers with directional hybrid attention in deeper layers, aiming to preserve effective contextual interactions while mitigating rank degeneration. We evaluate TimeHAT across real-world datasets and four downstream tasks: classification, long-term forecasting, imputation, and anomaly detection. Experiments cover fine-tuning, few-shot, and zero-shot transfer settings, demonstrating competitive performance across diverse time-series analysis tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/TangPeiwang/TimeHAT.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages. Code: https://github.com/TangPeiwang/TimeHAT

</details>

<a id="paper-78"></a>

### 78. [ADTGuard: Adaptive Depth-Time Safeguard for Streaming Inference in Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36574v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36574

**作者：** Chuming Wei、Churui Yang、Zihan Zhang、Ruien Li、Wusheng Zhang、Yan Zhang、Guangwen Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36574v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36574v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ADTGuard，在流式生成中逐 token 释放前做安全检测：用 Depth-Time Safety Dynamics 跨层整合安全证据并沿 token 累积风险，用 Causal Depth Router 按当前前缀自适应选择观测层，并以 Risk-Onset Target Refinement 从 response 级标签逐步构造 token 级训练目标。

**相关性简评：** 非视觉工作，但其「按前缀动态选择读取层」与「由粗粒度标签反推细粒度监督」可为 LVR 中 latent 状态的层选择、证据筛选和贡献分配提供方法线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ADTGuard: Adaptive Depth-Time Safeguard for Streaming Inference in Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate fluent responses to diverse user requests, yet streaming may expose users to harmful content before generation is complete. Existing guardrails face two limitations in this setting. First, post-hoc moderation at response or chunk boundaries delays intervention, risking harmful exposure when content is released before verification. Second, even with token-level detection, coarse response-level supervision does not specify when harmful content begins, while fixed-layer readouts may miss relevant safety signals at other depths. To address these limitations, we introduce ADTGuard, which checks each token before release, progressively refines token-level training targets, and adapts layer selection to the current prefix. ADTGuard models risk across depth and time through Depth-Time Safety Dynamics (DTSD), which integrates safety evidence across layers at each token and uses it to guide risk accumulation over successive tokens. To adapt the choice of observation layers to the current prefix, we propose the Causal Depth Router (CDR). With only response-level labels available, we further develop Risk-Onset Target Refinement (RTR) to progressively construct token-level training targets for both components. Through extensive experiments across multiple safety benchmarks, we demonstrate that ADTGuard consistently achieves strong streaming detection performance, matching or surpassing state-of-the-art safeguards while requiring only 16M trainable parameters and maintaining competitive per-token latency.

</details>

<a id="paper-79"></a>

### 79. [Recall or Relearn? A Mixture of In-Context Experts for Recurring Concept Drift](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36585v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36585

**作者：** Zhangkai Wu、Xu Zhang、Zhihong Cui、Yiqi Wang、Yi Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36585v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36585v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练的 MICE：把表格数据流按检测到的变化切段，依据模型在该段上的额外误差是否在容限内决定是否将旧概念存入容量受限的上下文库；快速层按最近批次 log-loss 加权存储上下文与 FIFO 窗口，慢速层再与之混合，并给出相对重学习的准确率增益与 Brier 后悔界。

**相关性简评：** 其「复用存档状态还是重新学习」的决策与按近期预测损失加权混合多份上下文的机制，可能为 LVR 中缓存推理状态/视觉证据的复用与选择、以及停止是否重算提供可供核查的线索；但研究对象是表格流，非视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Recall or Relearn? A Mixture of In-Context Experts for Recurring Concept Drift

**abstract/A1：** Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are being applied to Web data streams such as network intrusion detection, where they adapt by updating the labelled rows of their context without retraining. When a past concept returns after leaving the context, a TFM faces a recall-or-relearn decision between restoring stored rows of the concept and relearning it from new labels. We propose MICE (Mixture of In-Context Experts), a training-free learner that cuts the stream into segments at detected changes. It merges a closed segment into a stored context when the TFM's estimated additional error on the segment is within a tolerance, under a capacity limit on stored contexts. A fast level weights the stored contexts and first-in-first-out (FIFO) windows by the log-loss of their predictions on the last labelled batch. We show that, with pure stored contexts and a margin condition, this fast level gains over relearning, in expected accuracy, the normalised positive area between the stored context's accuracy and the TFM's learning curve, up to a first-batch term. A slow level mixes the fast level's prediction with that of the FIFO window of the last labelled rows and bounds the discounted half-Brier regret of MICE against this window at every time. Learning curves measured beforehand predict the ordering of the observed recall gains. MICE outperforms reset, which empties the context at detected drifts, on grid streams with random boundary phases. Merging keeps fewer experts than pools that store every segment, and with fixed segmentation a time-matched snapshot pool matches MICE on the shifted grid. On real streams, MICE has the highest mean accuracy of all policies we compare.

</details>

<a id="paper-80"></a>

### 80. [WithinVAE: Compact Visual Autoencoding with Self-Distilled Semantics](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36593v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36593

**作者：** Yuzhou Ji、Jiepeng Wang、Tianyu Jin、Lizhuang Ma、Haibin Huang、Chi Zhang、Xuelong Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36593v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36593v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 WithinVAE，一种紧凑变分视觉自编码器：采用序列瓶颈、从自身 EMA 副本做 DINO/iBOT 式自蒸馏，并配合位置相关 token 掩码，在 4 倍压缩下同时提升重建指标与潜变量线性探测性能，且不依赖预训练识别编码器或外部语义目标。

**相关性简评：** 其「在自编码器自身重建表示内部学出语义、避免继承识别编码器不变性而丢弃反转所需细节」的思路，可能为 LVR 中潜状态如何兼顾视觉细节与语义结构、缓解信息坍缩提供可供核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WithinVAE: Compact Visual Autoencoding with Self-Distilled Semantics

**abstract/A1：** Visual autoencoders must balance reconstruction fidelity, compactness, and semantic structure. Existing semantic tokenizers typically inherit semantics from pretrained recognition encoders, whose invariances can discard the appearance detail required for inversion. We show that this inheritance is unnecessary: semantic structure can be learned inside an autoencoder's own reconstructive representation. We introduce WithinVAE, a compact variational visual autoencoder with a sequential bottleneck, DINO/iBOT-style self-distillation from an exponential-moving-average copy of the autoencoder itself, and position-dependent token masking to achieve a 4-fold compression ratio, which is free of pretrained representation encoder or external semantic target. On ImageNet-1K at $256\\times256$, WithinVAE improves all four reported reconstruction metrics over RPiAE including PSNR by 1.36 dB, while using half its latent scalar capacity. Under frozen evaluation, the same latents improve linear probing by 6.5 points over RPiAE and 5.1 points over our reconstruction-only baseline, attributing the semantic gains to self-distillation rather than architecture or external supervision. An auxiliary latent-to-latent model reproduces the ordered-compression trend, and a pixel-to-latent model transfers the objective to a pretrained generative space. These results separate the origin of semantic supervision from its use: a compact visual autoencoder can organize semantic structure without borrowing a recognition representation.

</details>

<a id="paper-81"></a>

### 81. [Learning to Formalize: Bridging Open-Ended Workloads and Fast Decision Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36595v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36595

**作者：** Chengxuan Pei、Ziyao Wang、Teng Mei、Zhe Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36595v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36595v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对开放式机器人任务（CALVIN-D）缺少候选列表的问题，训练一个小的候选形式化器，从任务上下文构造经过验证的备选项，再由冻结的快速决策模型从中选择，并报告成功率与端到端时延的概念验证结果。

**相关性简评：** 其「候选构造器 + 冻结轻量选择器」的两段式接口，可作为 latent 假设生成与廉价验证/筛选分工的参照，且时延对比可供核查 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的算力成本，但原文属机器人决策应用，状态与证据是否涉及视觉推理内部机制需读正文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Formalize: Bridging Open-Ended Workloads and Fast Decision Models

**abstract/A1：** Decision models offer fast choices over sup- plied alternatives, while general-purpose large language models interpret open-ended tasks through flexible generation and reasoning at the cost of sequential decoding. Many work- loads expect a generative backend to determine what can be done and therefore provide no well-defined candidate list. We bridge this in- terface gap with a small candidate formalizer trained on offline teacher-generated lists. The formalizer constructs explicit, validated alter- natives from task context, and a frozen deci- sion model selects an entry. A motivating com- parison with official TypeSafe Jev-1.13.0 on 200 CALVIN-D test episodes reports 11% suc- cess with generic motions versus 46.5% with DeepSeek V4 Flash pose candidates. Sepa- rately, a six-arm privileged-state evaluation on 1,011 CALVIN-D validation annotations finds that a locally deployed decision model can achieve at most 52.6% task success rate with the fine-tuned formalizer, compared with 11.8% without it, and the end-to-end latency is kept to 2.09 s, whereas Qwen3.5-27B is 3.67 s with the exact experiment setting.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 2 figures, only for concept verification now

</details>

<a id="paper-82"></a>

### 82. [Balanced State Subspaces for Mamba-2 State Compression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36617v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36617

**作者：** Shang Tingyu、Cao Wenfei

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、stat.AP、stat.ME

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36617v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36617v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 Mamba-2 定长循环状态的压缩：发现状态能量集中在偏置方向，而输出实际读取的是与坐标不对齐的低维子空间，故用可控/可观 Gramian 的平衡截断在写—读子空间上做后训练变换，并按 Hankel 谱逐层分配秩（HARP）。

**相关性简评：** 其「输出究竟读取哪些状态方向」的 Gramian 式分析与分层秩分配，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否被答案使用、任务信息落在哪个子空间；但论文面向纯文本 SSM 推理效率，不直接涉及视觉推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Balanced State Subspaces for Mamba-2 State Compression

**abstract/A1：** Mamba-2 compresses all context into a fixed-size recurrent state that dominates decoding memory and bandwidth. Existing compressors quantize state values or prune state coordinates, scoring each channel independently in the native basis. We examine trained Mamba-2 states and find that energy is almost rank-one, but the output does not read the energetic directions: energy concentrates in a bias-like direction accumulated by the recurrence, while the content the output reads lives in a low-dimensional subspace that is not aligned with the state's coordinates. Even output-aware scores are computed coordinate by coordinate, so they miss the directions that determine the input--output map. Compression should therefore target the write--read subspace rather than individual coordinates. We propose \\method{}, a post-training transform that instantiates balanced truncation with full controllability and observability Gramians and applies the rotation exactly via Mamba-2's scalar-identity transition. Because the useful rank varies widely across layers, we further introduce HARP, which allocates ranks per layer from the Hankel spectrum and protects retrieval-sensitive layers, improving both perplexity and retrieval over uniform budgets. Calibration takes a single forward pass, an order of magnitude faster than GHOST's released protocol, with lower peak memory. With HARP, \\method{} halves the state dimension at an average WikiText-2 perplexity increase of $0.3$ points ($1.8\\%$) across 130M--2.7B, versus $1.6$ ($9.3\\%$) for GHOST, and the gap widens at 25\\% retention.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-83"></a>

### 83. [Adapting Video Models for Robot Control via Motion-guided Asymmetric Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36624v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36624

**作者：** Taeyoung Kim、Junyoung Sung、Heeseung Kwon、Minseop Kim、Jinwoo Shin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36624v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36624v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文考察世界动作模型中视觉预测监督的空间分配，提出 MoGAP：以训练视频自带运动线索对动态区域施加更强噪声，让模型借助较完整的静态区域重建动态区域，作为仅训练期的辅助路径联合训练，提升机器人操作成功率。

**相关性简评：** 按运动对视觉区域差异化监督，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与细节保留问题；但该辅助路径仅在训练期使用、不生成推理期状态，属方法线索，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Adapting Video Models for Robot Control via Motion-guided Asymmetric Prediction

**abstract/A1：** World Action Models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video generative models for robotic control by jointly predicting future visual observations and actions. The visual prediction objective plays a key role in adapting pretrained video representations to the target robot domain. Yet standard visual prediction inherited from video pretraining treats all visual regions uniformly, despite their differing relevance to robotic control. We therefore investigate the spatial allocation of visual supervision in WAMs and show that focusing supervision on dynamic regions can substantially improve policy performance. Building on this finding, we introduce Motion-Guided Asymmetric Prediction (MoGAP), which augments WAM training with motion-focused visual prediction. MoGAP allocates noise asymmetrically across regions, training the model to reconstruct more heavily corrupted dynamic regions using less corrupted static regions as context. MoGAP is trained alongside standard video-action prediction through a training-only auxiliary prediction path within the shared video diffusion backbone. It uses motion cues derived directly from training videos, requiring neither an external teacher nor additional modalities. Across robot manipulation benchmarks and WAM architectures, MoGAP consistently improves policy performance, increasing success rates on the GR-1 tabletop benchmark from 38.8% to 47.5% for Fast-WAM (Wan2.2-5B) and from 52.3% to 62.3% for Cosmos3-Nano.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://ismty0805.github.io/mogap/

</details>

<a id="paper-84"></a>

### 84. [The Batch Size Can Be One: Test-Time Adaptation from Classifier Geometry](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36628v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36628

**作者：** Jaeho Hwang、Yujin Oh、Juhyeon Shin、Juhye Lee、Sungroh Yoon、Uiwon Hwang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36628v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36628v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 OriTTA：在 batch size 为 1 的测试时适应中，从冻结源模型构造固定几何目标，重选特征空间原点使类轴更均匀展开，并将测试特征对齐预测类轴、惩罚过大半径，还以每类一条记忆检测崩溃并重置模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「源模型几何目标＋记忆检测表示崩溃」可为 LVR 中 latent 状态稳定性、信息坍缩监测与视觉证据筛选提供可核查的方法线索，但不直接涉及推理轨迹生成或答案对 latent 内容的使用。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Batch Size Can Be One: Test-Time Adaptation from Classifier Geometry

**abstract/A1：** Test-time adaptation updates a deployed model on an unlabeled test stream. In practice this stream often arrives one sample at a time, with the classes in no particular order. A single sample then provides limited information for estimating distribution-level statistics, and selecting samples by confidence can admit predictions that are wrong yet confident. A corruption, however, shifts the features while leaving the decision regions of the frozen classifier unchanged. We therefore propose Origin-recentered Test-Time Adaptation (OriTTA), which constructs fixed geometric targets from the source model and adapts selected features toward them. Seen from the origin of the feature space, the classes lean toward a common direction. We choose a new origin to bring class scores closer together while keeping it near the mean feature of one synthetic prototype per class. From this origin, prototype directions spread more evenly, and each class obtains an axis and an ideal point before any test data arrives. During adaptation, OriTTA aligns selected features with their predicted class axis and penalizes excessive radius, keeps one input per predicted class in memory to limit feature changes for classes absent from the stream, and resets the model when re-classifying this memory reveals collapse. Without tuning for each setting, OriTTA achieves the highest mean accuracy at batch size 1 on CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C and ImageNet-C in all sixteen combinations of four backbones, two protocols and two stream orders. On ImageNet-C fog, recent methods collapse in up to two thirds of one hundred runs, while OriTTA never does.

</details>

<a id="paper-85"></a>

### 85. [Deep Bayesian Approaches for Interpretable Nonlinear Structural System Identification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36633v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36633

**作者：** Wei Liu、Konstantinos Vlachas、Eleni Chatzi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36633v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36633v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 一篇教程性论文，系统介绍深度贝叶斯滤波用于非线性结构系统辨识：以 Deep Markov Model 为数据驱动参照，对比基于摊销变分推断的 PgDMM 与基于可微卡尔曼滤波的 Neural EKF，在含滞回连接的三维剪切框架基准上做动态状态估计与状态—参数联合估计，并给出建模、训练与评估指南。

**相关性简评：** 其可微卡尔曼滤波式「隐状态随观测更新」与「观测条件重建 vs 开环预测」的评估区分，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态如何吸收视觉证据、以及如何检验 latent 是否被真正使用；领域相隔较远，迁移仅为可能线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Deep Bayesian Approaches for Interpretable Nonlinear Structural System Identification

**abstract/A1：** System identification, namely the inference of mathematical representations of dynamical systems from measured data, is fundamental to engineering diagnostics, prognostics, and decision-making. Bayesian filtering offers a principled framework for probabilistic state estimation but traditionally relies on prescribed transition and observation models. Recent advances in deep Bayesian filtering address this limitation by embedding learnable representations within probabilistic state-space formulations, enabling data-driven identification of unknown dynamics while retaining physical structure and uncertainty-aware inference. This tutorial provides a systematic introduction to deep Bayesian filtering for nonlinear structural system identification, focusing on the interplay between learned dynamics, physical inductive biases, and posterior inference. The Deep Markov Model serves as a data-driven reference, while the Physics-guided Deep Markov Model (PgDMM) and Neural Extended Kalman Filter (Neural EKF) illustrate complementary approaches based on amortized variational inference and differentiable Kalman filtering, respectively. Their capabilities are examined through dynamic state estimation and joint dynamic state and parameter estimation on a three-dimensional shear-frame benchmark with hysteretic Bouc-Wen connections and sparse acceleration measurements. The results highlight important distinctions between measurement-conditioned reconstruction and open-loop forecasting, demonstrate how physical structure can improve latent-state interpretability without necessarily ensuring predictive fidelity, and reveal differences in the robustness of response and parameter estimation. Practical guidelines for model construction, training, evaluation, and deployment are provided, together with an outlook on remaining challenges in uncertainty calibration, identifiability, and real-world validation.

</details>

<a id="paper-86"></a>

### 86. [Remembering Is Not Enough: Refining Memories and Learning When to Use Them in Multi-Agent Systems](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36652v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36652

**作者：** Yipeng Hao、Yuxuan Cai、Wenkai Ji、Jie Zhou、Liyang Yu、Qin Chen、Zhiguo Yan、Shulei Zheng、Liang He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36652v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36652v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练框架 DiscernMem，用缺陷感知记忆精炼（对比有/无该条记忆的团队执行来诊断规则缺陷并在新旧任务上验证修补），配合边界学习元记忆记录每条记忆的历史使用与效果、学习何时启用，在四个基准的多智能体系统上评测。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本多智能体记忆工作，非视觉 latent；但其「有/无记忆对照」判断某内容是否应被使用、以及学习启用边界的做法，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容是否真正被答案使用、贡献分配与 THINK/LOOK 触发条件。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Remembering Is Not Enough: Refining Memories and Learning When to Use Them in Multi-Agent Systems

**abstract/A1：** Memory helps LLM-based multi-agent systems reuse experience, but stored advice can be flawed or applied in the wrong situation. Team-level success or failure alone cannot reliably show whether a memory needs correction or should not have been used. We propose DiscernMem, a training-free framework that improves both memory quality and memory use. To improve quality, Defect-Aware Memory Refinement (DMR) compares team executions with a single memory and with no memory, diagnoses rule defects, and tests repairs on current and earlier tasks to guard against losing previously useful behavior. To guide appropriate use, Boundary-Learning Meta-Memory (BLM) records each memory's past uses and effects, learning when its owner role should apply or avoid it based on task context and incoming messages. Together, these components help teams correct flawed experience while retaining useful rules that were applied incorrectly. Experiments with Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 on four benchmarks show that DiscernMem achieves the highest average score on AutoGen, CAMEL, and MacNet, exceeding the strongest reported baselines by 0.51-4.32 points. It leads on code generation and symbolic planning across all three systems, including a PDDL score of 37.71 versus 28.48 on CAMEL.

</details>

<a id="paper-87"></a>

### 87. [Log-Derivative Trick for Parametric Markov Chains](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36655v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36655

**作者：** Arthur Gall、Ichiro Hasuo、Thomas Seiller

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.LO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36655v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36655v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将 log-derivative trick 推广到测度论形式，提出一种沿采样轨迹工作的蒙特卡洛梯度估计器，用于优化参数化马尔可夫链的期望奖励与可达概率，并在几乎必然终止等条件下证明估计无偏，避免显式构建全局状态空间与求解线性系统。

**相关性简评：** 其「仅沿采样轨迹、只对局部转移做黑盒求值」的无偏梯度估计，可能为 latent 推理轨迹上的贡献分配与训练监督提供可核查的方法线索，但该工作面向概率系统分析，迁移到 VLM 推理的可行性仍需判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Log-Derivative Trick for Parametric Markov Chains

**abstract/A1：** Optimizing expected rewards and reachability probabilities in parametric Markov chains (pMCs) is central to the analysis and synthesis of probabilistic systems with loops. While traditional symbolic synthesis approaches--such as parameter lifting and rational function generation--suffer from scalability bottlenecks, gradient-based optimization emerges as an effective alternative. However, existing gradient-based techniques still rely on symbolic gradient computation--such as solving large linear systems--over the global state space, inheriting scalability limits. Finite-horizon approximation is possible but introduces uncontrollable bias. In this paper, we introduce a scalable Monte Carlo gradient estimator based on a measure-theoretic generalization of the log-derivative trick. We prove that our estimator provides unbiased gradient estimates under almost-sure termination and standard regularity conditions. Crucially, our method avoids global state-space materialization and linear system solving entirely: it operates purely along sampled trajectories, requiring only black-box evaluations of local transition probabilities (or densities) and their derivatives. Unlike existing pMC techniques that are fundamentally restricted to finite discrete systems, our measure-theoretic framework naturally extends to continuous and general $σ$-finite state spaces. Empirical evaluations on synthetic discrete pMCs and continuous-state case studies demonstrate that our method scales to state spaces far beyond the reach of existing solvers, significantly reducing execution time without introducing bias.

</details>

<a id="paper-88"></a>

### 88. [SAGE: Semantic-Augmented Geometric Experts for Point Cloud Registration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36658v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36658

**作者：** Tomoyasu Shimada、Kazuhiko Murasaki、Shoichiro Takeda、Shogo Sato、Riku Inoue、Toshihiko Nishimura、Ryuichi Tanida

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36658v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36658v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对低重叠点云配准中几何相似但语义不同结构导致的错误对应，SAGE 将冻结的预训练点云语义表示接入配准流程，并用语义引导的 MoE 路由让各专家专注不同的语义-几何模式，同时配合结构感知估计器。

**相关性简评：** 其「用冻结外部语义表示消除几何歧义 + 语义驱动的专家路由」可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与专家/子状态路由提供方法线索，但该工作属 3D 配准，与推理状态生成无直接关系，需核查路由机制是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SAGE: Semantic-Augmented Geometric Experts for Point Cloud Registration

**abstract/A1：** Point cloud registration is an essential task for scene reconstruction, requiring the alignment of partially overlapping point clouds. Under low-overlap conditions, this task remains challenging because existing geometry-only methods cannot distinguish geometrically similar but semantically distinct structures (e.g., TV corners vs. desk corners and chair legs vs. table legs), resulting in incorrect correspondences. To address this geometric ambiguity, we present Semantic-Augmented Geometric Experts (SAGE), which integrates frozen pre-trained point cloud semantic representations into the registration pipeline without requiring additional sensors or fine-tuning the semantic encoder. To effectively leverage these semantic distinctions, SAGE employs a Semantic-Guided Mixture of Experts (SG-MoE) architecture. Through semantic-aware routing, SG-MoE enables each expert to specialize in distinct semantic-geometric patterns, reducing mismatches that confound geometry-only methods. With a structure-aware estimator, SAGE achieves the highest registration recall on 3DLoMatch while remaining competitive on 3DMatch. When evaluated with the same number of refinement iterations, SAGE further achieves the highest inlier ratio and registration recall on both benchmarks. These results indicate that frozen semantic representations provide effective cues for resolving geometric ambiguity in low-overlap registration.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027 (Round 1)

</details>

<a id="paper-89"></a>

### 89. [Haste Makes Waste? Towards Safe Few-Step Text-to-Image Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36665v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36665

**作者：** Lior Ziv、Mahmood Sharif

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36665v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36665v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究少步蒸馏文生图模型的安全生成问题，发现推理步数越少，不安全生成率越高、概念擦除越难生效，并从生成过程脆弱性与引导蒸馏方式给出机制诊断，提出与蒸馏流程对齐的擦除方法 DICE。

**相关性简评：** 其「latent 构建与生成保持损失需对齐产生该模型的蒸馏流程」以及少步生成脆弱性的分析，可能可供核查少步或循环式 latent 推理状态的训练监督与原有行为保持问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Haste Makes Waste? Towards Safe Few-Step Text-to-Image Models

**abstract/A1：** While few-step distilled text-to-image models have gained traction due to their fast generation times, the speed-up implications on their unsafe generation rates remain unknown. We uncover an inherent tension between speed and safety: few-step models are often more unsafe than their teachers, and reducing the number of inference steps consistently raises unsafe generation rates. We further find that acceleration makes unsafe concepts harder to erase in few-step models, with existing concept-erasure methods markedly less effective than in teachers. We offer a mechanistic diagnosis for why erasure methods fail on few-step models, finding that the fragility of few-step models' generation process and the way generation guidance is usually distilled into those models may hinder established concept-erasure methods. To address the speed-safety trade-off, we propose DICE (Distillation-Integrated Concept Erasure), a post-distillation erasure method tailored for few-step models. DICE aligns both its latent construction and its generation-preservation loss with the distillation method that produced the model, keeping erasure faithful to few-step models. DICE demonstrates strong results in our evaluation. For example, on FLUX Schnell, DICE reduces unsafe-generation rate to 0.046 (vs. at least 0.161 for prior erasure methods we test), while mostly preserving benign generation quality.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at IEEE S&amp;P 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-90"></a>

### 90. [RankFormer: Rethinking Time Series Transformers through Representation Capacity Analysis and Progressive Decoding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36703v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36703

**作者：** Peiwang Tang、Cheng HE、Xijie Liang、Defu Lian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36703v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36703v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 RankFormer 多变量时序预测模型，建立基于秩的分析框架，考察 tokenization、嵌入与注意力设计如何影响时序 Transformer 的表示容量，并据此设计带 Hourglass Attention 的秩增强编码器和交互式渐进解码器（IPD）。

**相关性简评：** 其「patchification 提升潜在容量、后续编码决定实际实现多少」的秩容量视角，可能为核查 latent 推理状态的信息坍缩与有效容量提供分析工具，渐进式解码也可能对 latent 状态迭代更新有借鉴，但需核查其是否依赖时序通道结构。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RankFormer: Rethinking Time Series Transformers through Representation Capacity Analysis and Progressive Decoding

**abstract/A1：** Multivariate time series forecasting plays a critical role in Web and cyber-physical systems, where large-scale services and IoT infrastructures generate complex temporal data. Transformer-based models have achieved strong empirical performance in this domain, yet the principles governing the expressive capacity of their learned representations remain insufficiently understood. Moreover, many existing models rely on simplified decoding strategies, including channel-independent modeling and direct projection heads, which limit their ability to capture complex multivariate dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose RankFormer, a novel encoder-decoder architecture for multivariate time series forecasting. We establish a rank-based analysis framework to investigate how tokenization, embedding, and attention designs affect the representation capacity of time series Transformers. Our analysis reveals that patchification increases the potential representation capacity by aggregating multiple temporal observations into each token, while subsequent encoding operations determine how much of this capacity is realized. Guided by these insights, we develop a rank-enhancing encoder with Hourglass Attention to improve rank stability during information interaction. Furthermore, we introduce the Interactive Progressive Decoder (IPD), which improves future trajectory modeling through global-guided cross-channel interaction and progressive refinement. Extensive experiments on real-world long-term forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that RankFormer achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art methods. Code repository: https://github.com/TangPeiwang/Rankformer.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-91"></a>

### 91. [Nonlinear Direct Feedback Alignment for Scalable Backpropagation-Free Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36718v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36718

**作者：** Wu Lin、Yan Fan、Chenxiang Ma、Yinglan Feng、Jia Wang、Ka-Chun Wong、Qiuzhen Lin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36718v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36718v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 Direct Feedback Alignment 中固定反馈矩阵难以刻画隐层到输出的非线性映射这一不足，提出 NDFA：用轻量级非线性替代子网络为每个隐层构造反馈，并设计更新该子网络的训练策略，在 ResNet、ViT 上缩小与 BP 的差距并保持并行训练。

**相关性简评：** 其「用可训练的非线性替代子网络近似隐层表示到输出的映射」可能为 LVR 中 latent 轨迹到答案的读出与训练贡献分配提供核查线索，但该文属纯视觉分类的免反传训练，与多模态推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Nonlinear Direct Feedback Alignment for Scalable Backpropagation-Free Training

**abstract/A1：** Backpropagation (BP) remains the predominant algorithm for training deep neural networks, but it is biologically implausible and suffers from the backward locking problem, constraining parallelization across layers. Direct Feedback Alignment~(DFA), which propagates the global output error directly to each hidden layer via fixed feedback matrices, has emerged as a promising alternative for addressing these limitations. However, a significant performance gap persists between DFA and BP, which we attribute to the limited ability of fixed feedback matrices to capture the nonlinear transformation from hidden representations to the network output. To address this limitation, we propose Nonlinear Direct Feedback Alignment (NDFA), which constructs each hidden-layer feedback using a lightweight nonlinear surrogate subnetwork. To enhance the effectiveness of the feedback throughout training, we further propose a learning strategy to update the surrogate subnetwork, thereby better approximating the underlying nonlinear transformation. Extensive experiments across residual networks and vision transformers on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, SVHN, and ImageNet datasets demonstrate that NDFA consistently outperforms existing DFA-based methods and substantially narrows the performance gap to BP, while maintaining efficient parallel training. The code is available at https://github.com/YanFan321/NDFA.

</details>

<a id="paper-92"></a>

### 92. [SAGE: Saliency-Annealed Gated Encoder for Universal Vision Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36719v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36719

**作者：** Le Yang、Yucheng Huang、Junyu Chen、Qi Dang、Xin Kong、Haifeng Huo、Qiang Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36719v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36719v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SAGE，一个增强的 ViT-Adapter 骨干：用 DINO 风格 CLS 显著性引导特征门控与高频细节增强，并配合退火训练与 focal 到 Varifocal Loss 的两阶段训练，在 COCO、ADE20K 等密集预测任务上取得 SOTA 或竞争性结果。

**相关性简评：** 其显著性引导门控与局部细节分支可能需要人工核查能否为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与细节保留提供特征调制线索，但论文不涉及推理状态生成、latent 使用或停止决策。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SAGE: Saliency-Annealed Gated Encoder for Universal Vision Recognition

**abstract/A1：** When adapting self-supervised Vision Transformers (ViTs) to dense prediction, existing methods often treat the ViT as a passive feature extractor and may underuse the rich priors encoded in its representations, including saliency, global context, and high-frequency details. We propose SAGE (Saliency-Annealed Gated Encoder), an enhanced ViT-Adapter backbone that explicitly exploits these priors through three complementary innovations: (1) a saliency-guided architecture that extracts DINO-style class-token (CLS) saliency to guide feature gating and high-frequency enhancement, together with dynamic multi-layer aggregation and a local-detail branch; (2) an annealing-based training paradigm designed to improve convergence and feature quality during early training while reducing feature drift later in training; and (3) a two-stage, quality-aware training mechanism that smoothly transitions from focal loss to Varifocal Loss (VFL) within a single training run. Across the evaluated tasks (Fig.~\\ref{fig:fig\_com}), SAGE achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) or competitive results on COCO (62.8 box AP and 55.3 mask AP), COCO keypoint estimation (78.5 AP), ADE20K (60.3 mIoU for semantic segmentation and 44.9 mask AP for instance segmentation), TinyImageNet classification (94.76\\% Top-1 accuracy), and DIOR-R oriented object detection (78.8 AP$\_{50}$). The same backbone is used across tasks, with task-specific decoders. To the best of our knowledge, SAGE is the first framework to achieve multi-task state-of-the-art results on Huawei Ascend NPUs. Code and pretrained models will be released after internal review.

</details>

<a id="paper-93"></a>

### 93. [Hy-Nomas: Towards Free-form Interactive World Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36729v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36729

**作者：** Qiaoling Chen、Jiaxiang Cheng、Xueqing Deng、Tiankai Hang、Ji Hou、Jiaqi Li、Yifan Liu、Tao Lu、Junshu Tang、Yu Tang、Yao Teng、Yu Tian、Heng Wang、Weizhi Wang、Xiyao Wang、Xilun Wu、Mingyu Yang、Yangbin Yu、Rui Zhao、Longjun Cao、Linghao Chen、Haoyi Duan、Jiaming Han、Mingji Han、Enhua Jiang、Shunkai Li、Yanzhe Liang、Qinwei Ma、Cong Wang、Lei Wang、Bing Wu、Ke Xing、Ray Ying、Zhentao Yu、Key Zhang、Yuan Zhou、Zhengguang Zhou、Kai Zou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36729v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36729v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Hy-Nomas，一个 8B 扩散骨干的视频世界模型，支持多轮自由文本指令与连续相机控制；核心设计是把结构化视频描述与动作感知因果注意力对齐，使描述性上下文跨段可访问、动作指令仅作用于对应时间窗，并配合 VLM 提示增强器与多阶段课程训练。

**相关性简评：** 按其时间窗限定动作指令、同时跨段缓存描述性上下文的设计，可能为 LVR 中持久视觉证据与瞬时任务指令如何在内部状态中分离共存提供线索，可供核查；其基于模型自生成历史的后训练也涉及轨迹监督问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hy-Nomas: Towards Free-form Interactive World Generation

**abstract/A1：** Interactive world generation requires models to follow successive commands while preserving relevant history and action consequences. We present Hy-Nomas, a video world model with an 8B diffusion backbone that supports free-form text instructions and continuous camera control across multiple interaction turns. It generates variable-length segments conditioned on current inputs and cached history. Our central design aligns structured video captions with action-aware causal attention: descriptive context remains accessible across segments, while action instructions are restricted to their intended temporal windows. A vision-language Prompt Enhancer (PE) translates user requests and multimodal interaction histories into structured conditions for the next segment. A multi-stage curriculum progressively introduces causal generation, text interaction, camera control, and training on model-generated histories, followed by diffusion distillation. In human evaluation, Hy-Nomas shows superior performance over existing world models. We release model weights, code and benchmarks, together with a lightweight adaptation workflow, to support reproducible research on interactive world generation.

**comment/C1：** Technical Report

</details>

<a id="paper-94"></a>

### 94. [Generative Refinement Learning for Temporal Interpolation in Scientific Machine Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36751v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36751

**作者：** Pu Ren、Michael W. Mahoney、N. Benjamin Erichson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、math-ph、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36751v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36751v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究科学机器学习中的时间插值，提出 iFLEX：先以加权平均给出中间时刻的粗略估计，再用条件扩散模型学习该估计与真实中间场之间的残差，并在湍流、雷达降水与海表温度数据上降低重建误差。

**相关性简评：** 「简单插值骨架 + 生成残差来修正中间状态」的范式可能为 LVR 的 latent 状态更新与轨迹监督提供线索，例如在已有先验状态上学习增量而非直接生成；但其对象是物理场重建、无语言答案使用，迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generative Refinement Learning for Temporal Interpolation in Scientific Machine Learning

**abstract/A1：** Computationally expensive simulations and real-world observations often provide data at a lower temporal resolution than downstream tasks require. Averaging observations at fixed grid points can introduce errors, and motion estimates alone do not determine how the physical field values change. In this work, we study whether learning to correct a simple numerical interpolation improves the reconstruction of intermediate scientific fields. Our model, interpolative FLow EXpert (iFLEX), first estimates the missing field by taking a weighted average of the two observations at each grid point, giving more weight to the observation closer to the requested time. During training, subtracting this estimate from an observed intermediate field gives the residual that a conditional diffusion model learns to generate. Both observations condition the model, together with the target time, the interval between observations, and available physical parameters. To reconstruct an unobserved intermediate field, the generated residual is added to the weighted average. We evaluate iFLEX on Navier-Stokes Kraichnan turbulence, Shanghai radar precipitation, and sea surface temperature. Across these datasets, iFLEX achieves lower reconstruction error than the evaluated baselines, including optical-flow and diffusion models developed for video frame interpolation in computer vision. In a controlled turbulence comparison at a Reynolds number of 36,000, learning the residual reduces relative Frobenius norm error from about 13.7 for direct field generation to 10.5, with the diffusion backbone and training setup held fixed. Code is available at https://github.com/paulpuren/iflex.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by Transactions on Machine Learning Research

</details>

<a id="paper-95"></a>

### 95. [Robust LLM Data Selection via Content-Invariant Gradient Attribution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36755v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36755

**作者：** Jia Gu、Liang Pang、Huawei Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36755v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36755v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文发现全局梯度数据归因存在表面形式偏差：措辞或格式等语义无关的改动会使归因分数剧烈波动；为此提出 CIGA，先筛出对归因有影响且在语义保持扰动下梯度稳定的层，再与输入的语义表示动态融合，用于 LLM 指令微调数据选择，在多个基准上提升了扰动下的排序稳定性与下游性能并降低成本。

**相关性简评：** 其「按语义保持扰动筛选稳定梯度层」的思路可能可供核查 latent 推理轨迹中的贡献分配与格式噪声抑制；但论文面向纯文本指令数据筛选，与视觉状态或推理期 latent 生成无直接联系，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Robust LLM Data Selection via Content-Invariant Gradient Attribution

**abstract/A1：** Gradient-based data attribution has become an important tool for high-quality data selection in the instruction fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs), as it estimates the contribution of training examples through their influence on model optimization. However, we find that existing global gradient attribution methods suffer from surface-form bias: minor changes to semantically irrelevant details, such as wording or formatting, can produce highly inconsistent attribution scores even when the underlying content remains unchanged. To address this issue, we propose Content-Invariant Gradient Attribution (CIGA), a robust framework for instruction data selection. CIGA first identifies layers whose gradients are both influential for attribution and stable under semantics-preserving surface-form perturbations, thereby reducing formatting noise in gradient features. It then dynamically fuses these attribution signals with semantic representations of the input, combining optimization-level influence with content-level information. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks show that CIGA produces more stable rankings under surface-form perturbations while preserving downstream capabilities and improving average performance. CIGA also reduces attribution cost, offering a robust and efficient approach to data selection for LLM instruction tuning.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 8 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-96"></a>

### 96. [Beyond the Evidence Coherence Trap: Agent-Driven Evidence Selection for Temporally Disordered Fake News Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36764v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36764

**作者：** Chi Wang、Min Gao、Yifei Zhang、Bincheng Gu、Zongwei Wang、Wentao Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36764v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36764v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ChronoFake 时间错乱假新闻基准与 DelSA 智能体：由 LLM 刻画候选证据，用 verifier 训练的重排器平衡局部相关性与时间线一致性，并对比文章隐含时间线与证据时间线来发现全局时间冲突。

**相关性简评：** 其「证据一致性陷阱」及经 verifier 训练的证据重排机制，可供核查 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与剔除错位、局部自洽但任务无关证据的做法；但该文为纯文本设定，与 latent 状态生成无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond the Evidence Coherence Trap: Agent-Driven Evidence Selection for Temporally Disordered Fake News Detection

**abstract/A1：** Fake news undermines public trust and the integrity of online information ecosystems. Temporally disordered fake news is especially elusive because it rearranges factual events into a false chronology. Individual events may remain factually valid even when their temporal relations are misrepresented. When similar events involving the same entity recur, retrieval may return semantically plausible evidence from the wrong period. Consequently, independent event verification may rely on locally coherent but temporally mismatched evidence and incorrectly validate the article. We call this the \\textit{evidence coherence trap}. Because existing benchmarks rarely isolate this setting, we construct ChronoFake, a source-disjoint benchmark of 30,706 articles derived from temporally anchored real events. It distinguishes temporal manipulation from valid narration, including flashbacks. To address this, we propose DelSA, a \\underline{D}ocument S\\underline{el}ection \\underline{S}elf-Updating \\underline{A}gent that iteratively refines evidence selection during training and applies a frozen rulebook for one-pass evidence selection at inference. DelSA uses an LLM to characterize candidate evidence, while a verifier-trained reranker balances local relevance against timeline consistency. Finally, the classifier compares the article-implied chronology extracted by the LLM with the evidence-derived chronology to reveal global temporal conflicts. DelSA outperforms existing methods on ChronoFake, while remaining competitive on PolitiFact and GossipCop.

</details>

### 97. [Evaluation-Guided Demonstration Refinement for Vision-Language-Action Policies: The Winning Entry in the MetaFine Open Competition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36765v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36765

**作者：** Lifan Zheng、Guotao Chen、Zhenyang Li、Shujie Cui、Qiang Huang、Fan Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36765v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36765v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为 MetaFine 竞赛的五个机器人任务分别微调 π0.5 策略并配置任务专属数据混合与执行步数，依据本地评估暴露的抓取、推动、插入失败来修订示范动作、补入从策略实际到达状态出发的专家续接数据，并逐指令比较 checkpoint，最终以 96.66 分排名第一。

**相关性简评：** 其「评估驱动示范修订＋从策略到达状态补专家续接」的监督数据筛选，以及逐指令细粒度验证以防收益掩盖损失的做法，可能为 LVR 的轨迹训练与贡献分配提供可供核查的线索，但对象是动作策略而非 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Evaluation-Guided Demonstration Refinement for Vision-Language-Action Policies: The Winning Entry in the MetaFine Open Competition

**abstract/A1：** We fine-tuned a separate $π\_{0.5}$ policy for each of the five tasks in the MetaFine Open Competition, with a task-specific training mixture and number of actions executed per prediction. Training started from the official demonstrations and expert procedures. Local evaluation exposed recurring grasping, pushing, and insertion failures. We addressed them by revising the demonstrated motions and by adding expert continuations from states the policy reached, while keeping nominal demonstrations in training. Checkpoints were compared on every instruction, so that a gain on one instruction could not hide a loss on another. Our submission, MAI, ranked first overall with a score of 96.66.

**comment/C1：** 1st place in the MetaFine Open Competition, RoboFineMani Workshop @ CoRL 2026

</details>
