### 98. [Can LLMs Reliably Grade Olympiad Proofs? A Study of Mathematical Verification with LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36767v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36767

**作者：** Azim Ospanov、Zijin Feng、Ding Ding、Chengwu Liu、Haoli Bai、Jiacheng Sun、Lifeng Shang、Farzan Farnia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36767v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36767v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建 USAMO 2025 候选解答的专家评分数据集，系统评测 20 个 LLM 证明评分器在多指标、多提示与多推理方式下的表现，并提出 TROJAN 自批判对抗证明生成流程与 MABGrader 多臂老虎机评分框架。

**相关性简评：** 该工作纯文本、无视觉内部状态，但逐步核验长推理链与把评分建模为离散分数空间上的臂选择，或可供核查 LVR 的步骤级验证、贡献分配与结果聚合线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Can LLMs Reliably Grade Olympiad Proofs? A Study of Mathematical Verification with LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Large Language Models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress on solving competition-level mathematics, yet their ability to verify natural-language mathematical proofs remains relatively underexplored. This gap is especially pronounced for Olympiad-level proofs, which require meticulous assessment of each claim in a long reasoning chain. Current proof verification thus primarily relies on expert inspection, which is costly to scale. In this paper, we present a systematic study of LLM-based verification in this setting. Specifically, we introduce a human-curated dataset of USAMO 2025 candidate solutions with expert grading, and conduct a broad evaluation of existing LLM verifiers across frontier models. We also propose TROJAN, an iterative self-critique pipeline for generating high-fidelity adversarial proofs at scale. Our comprehensive evaluation covers 20 models spanning seven metrics, two prompt styles, three inference methods, and three prompt templates, showing that state-of-the-art models still leave considerable room for improvement. To narrow this gap, we propose MABGrader, a Multi-Armed Bandit framework that reframes grading as arm selection over the discrete score space. MABGrader outperforms the SOTA ProofGrader by 26.4% in Quadratic Weighted Kappa and reduces grading error by 14%. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/aziksh-ospanov/TROJAN.

</details>

<a id="paper-99"></a>

### 99. [Batching the Reflective Optimization Loop: Parallel Proposals Make GEPA Faster and Better](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36774v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36774

**作者：** Jialin Zhang、Lakshya A Agrawal、Shangyin Tan、Donghyun Lee、Lutfi Eren Erdogan、Dan Klein、Koushik Sen、Alex Dimakis、Matei Zaharia

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36774v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36774v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作扩展开源 GEPA 反思式文本优化器，使每步并行提出并评估一批候选而非单个候选，并在两项任务上报告墙钟加速与更高的留出分数，同时用有限 worker 池的运行时模型解释加速来源，讨论固定优化预算在多个并行轴上的分配。

**相关性简评：** 其批量并行提案与「固定预算在并行维度上如何分配」的框架，可能为 LVR 中并行生成并验证多个 latent 提案的采样规模与计算预算调度提供线索；但优化对象是提示文本而非视觉推理状态，联系需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Batching the Reflective Optimization Loop: Parallel Proposals Make GEPA Faster and Better

**abstract/A1：** Reflective text optimization makes agentic systems better, but the optimization loop is itself a resource-hungry workload whose sequential structure leaves parallel evaluation capacity idle. We extend the open-source GEPA optimizer to propose and evaluate a batch of candidates on each optimization step instead of one candidate at a time. In our sweep on two tasks, most batched runs finished in half the wall-clock time or less, and the fastest in about a quarter to a third. Batched settings also achieved higher held-out test scores, from 68.9% to 72.1% on LiveBench-Math (with $2\\times2$) and from 49.0% to 60.0% on HoVer (with $8\\times1$). A simple runtime model explains the measured speedups under a finite worker pool, and batched settings also spend their metric-call and dollar budgets more efficiently. We frame the axes along which a reflective optimization step can scale in parallel, a first look at allocating a fixed optimization budget across them.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Resource-Aware Agentic AI. 9 pages, 7 figures. First four authors contributed equally. Code: https://github.com/gepa-ai/gepa

</details>

<a id="paper-100"></a>

### 100. [OmniGlimpse: Asymmetric Identity Memory for Online Multi-View Tracking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36785v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36785

**作者：** Rangya Zhang、Jiaping Xiao、Yujing Shang、Yuhang Zhang、Mingsheng Li、Mir Feroskhan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36785v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36785v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究在线多视角身份维护（OMIM）问题，提出 OmniGlimpse 非对称 propose-verify 框架：在自适应空间中用短期本视角与跨视角证据提出关联，并在冻结编码器定义的规范空间中维护长期身份分布做验证，且仅允许经验证的观测写入持久记忆或驱动适配。

**相关性简评：** 「验证通过才写入持久记忆」的写入门控，以及短期自适应表示与冻结编码器规范空间的分离，可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的更新门控、误差累积抑制与稳定/可塑表示权衡提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OmniGlimpse: Asymmetric Identity Memory for Online Multi-View Tracking

**abstract/A1：** Multi-view tracking extends visual coverage beyond a single camera, providing complementary observations when targets are occluded or leave the view. This raises a question: how can observations from different views be matched to the correct identities without offline scene-specific training or camera calibration? Progress toward this goal remains limited because many offline formulations assume such deployment-specific knowledge, while online adaptation can amplify association errors. We study this problem as online multi-view identity maintenance (OMIM): targets are initialized once per view, with no further identity labels provided after each initialization. To this end, we introduce OmniGlimpse, an asymmetric propose-verify framework that separates adaptive association from stable identity consolidation. At the representation level, short-term local and cross-view evidence is mapped into target-specific adaptive spaces to propose associations, whereas long-term identity distributions are maintained for verification in a canonical space defined by a frozen encoder. At the update level, short-term evidence may guide current associations, whereas, after bootstrapping, only verified observations may update persistent memory or drive adaptation. Under a unified target-specified evaluation protocol, OmniGlimpse improves over the best evaluated online baseline for each metric by 9.1 IDF1 and 22.1 CVIDF1 points on M3Track. The corresponding gains on MvMHAT are 20.3 and 26.2 points. Despite using no offline scene-specific training, it outperforms offline-trained references in IDF1 and remains competitive in cross-view association. These results support an approach to online identity maintenance that adapts to changing appearances while consolidating reliable evidence into stable identity anchors.

</details>

<a id="paper-101"></a>

### 101. [Visual Autoregressive Generation with Next Token Imagination](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36793v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36793

**作者：** Ruihang Jiang、Zongyao Zhao、Zhaolin Wang、Yuanwei Liu、Xiatian Zhu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36793v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36793v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Next Token Imagination（NTI），让视觉自回归模型在受约束的准则下探索 GT 之外的合理下一 token，从而在 ImageNet 上提升生成保真度。

**相关性简评：** 其“超越单一 GT 监督、约束地探索可行实现”的训练思路，可供核查 LVR 中多条 latent 轨迹均有效时的监督与贡献分配问题；但论文本身是图像生成任务，不涉及显式推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Visual Autoregressive Generation with Next Token Imagination

**abstract/A1：** The space of possible visual content is enormous, and arguably unbounded. Any training set, however large, therefore covers only a tiny fraction of it. Yet generative models are typically trained to fit only the images they are given. In visual autoregressive (AR) generation, for example, each token is predicted from the preceding ground-truth (GT) tokens and supervised solely by the GT next token. Confining learning to observed data in this way limits how much of the visual distribution a model can capture. In this work, we hypothesize that letting a model imagine beyond its training data, by exploring plausible visual realizations it has never observed, can ease this limitation. We use visual AR generation as a proof of concept, since its fixed, discrete token space makes imagination easier to steer and constrain. We propose Next Token Imagination (NTI), a method that allows the model to explore beyond the GT next token, subject to a carefully designed criterion that keeps this imagination bounded by the underlying visual distribution. Extensive experiments on ImageNet show that NTI substantially and consistently improves the fidelity of generated images, approaching state-of-the-art diffusion and AR models while using a smaller model and lower training cost.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 7 figures. Code and checkpoints: https://github.com/JackRuihang/NTI

</details>

<a id="paper-102"></a>

### 102. [LLM knowledge for improved Out-of-Distribution Tabular Prediction - A Cross-Validated Regularisation Approach](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36806v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36806

**作者：** Sølve Langseth、Kristoffer Herland Hellton、Hugo L. Hammer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、stat.ME、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36806v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36806v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出用 LLM 引出的系数先验代替零作为正则化收缩目标，在五个按真实协变量划分的表格数据集上，仅凭源域交叉验证选择模型提供方与先验结构，检验其能否改善 OOD 预测，并报告收益主要来自特征特定内容而非先验尺度。

**相关性简评：** 其「以 LLM 先验充当收缩目标、并以交叉验证在候选先验间做选择」的做法，可能可供核查如何把外部先验注入 latent 推理状态或监督目标，以及候选方案间选择裕度低于噪声的评估问题；与视觉/多模态推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LLM knowledge for improved Out-of-Distribution Tabular Prediction - A Cross-Validated Regularisation Approach

**abstract/A1：** Prediction models degrade when the deployment population differs from the one used to train them, and training data cannot say which feature-response relationships will transfer. Expert elicitation is slow and unscalable. We ask whether coefficient priors elicited from large language models (LLMs) improve out-of-distribution (OOD) prediction as the shrinkage target of a regularised regression instead of zero, across five tabular datasets split on real covariates, selecting providers and prior structures by source-domain cross-validation alone. The priors help on four of five datasets, by up to 7.9% OOD mean squared error, and ten of twenty-four prior-informed results resolve the mean improvement at conventional confidence; single-deployment intervals are much wider. On classification the gain is in probabilistic accuracy, not ranking, and control tests with corrupted priors attribute the benefit to feature-specific content rather than scale. Two findings temper this. Variation across candidate priors exceeds the average benefit, yet cross-validation margins fall below their own noise, so selection avoids the worst prior rather than finding the best. On three datasets the prior improves in-domain accuracy as much as out-of-domain, making it better regularisation than shift correction. Only where the training data are large enough that the in-domain gain disappears does elicited knowledge demonstrably improve transfer. Code, priors and results: https://github.com/hugohammer/llm-priors-ood.

</details>

<a id="paper-103"></a>

### 103. [SetGRPO: Calibrating Grader-Induced Policy Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36807v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36807

**作者：** Jiale Huang、Zixu Li、Zhiwei Chen、Zhiheng Fu、Jinhe Bi、Yupeng Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36807v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36807v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对 rubric-based 强化学习中评分器随机性会使 GRPO 组内训练系数整体偏移的问题，提出 SetGRPO：用联合不确定性集校准系数、按当前策略的局部更新几何确定安全恢复比例，并以独立风险校准选取阈值门控更新，在十个开放式基准上验证。

**相关性简评：** 该文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，但其「系数不确定集—局部更新几何—方向反转风险门控」的筛选思路，可供核查 latent 推理轨迹训练中噪声监督与更新取舍问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SetGRPO: Calibrating Grader-Induced Policy Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Rubric-based reinforcement learning uses fine-grained grader feedback to train large language models on open-ended tasks, but grader randomness can alter GRPO updates in ways that score discrepancies alone cannot characterize. Because GRPO jointly normalizes rewards within each response group and combines the resulting training coefficients with response-level gradients, a scoring discrepancy can shift the coefficients of the entire group and induce policy-dependent update changes. Selective execution further affects the direction-reversal risk of retained updates. To address this, we propose Set-Valued Group Relative Policy Optimization (SetGRPO). It comprises three modules: (a) Joint Reward Set Calibration constructs a joint uncertainty set for the training coefficients; (b) Geometry-Guided Safe Recovery maps candidate coefficients into the current policy's local update geometry and determines the largest safe recovery ratio for each response group; and (c) Selective Risk-Gated Optimization selects a global threshold through independent risk calibration to control the conditional direction-reversal risk of accepted updates while preserving the original GRPO objective. Under exchangeability, SetGRPO provides finite-sample marginal coverage for the joint coefficient set and simultaneous risk control across candidate thresholds. On ten open-ended benchmarks spanning three domains, SetGRPO achieves the highest macro average among all compared training methods on both Qwen3.5 and Llama3.2 model families.

</details>

<a id="paper-104"></a>

### 104. [When the Critic Falls Behind: Co-Evolving Rubrics and Harnesses for Continual Agent Self-Improvement](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36826v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36826

**作者：** Hao Chen、Yukun Yan、Ziyu Han、Ye He、Chuanzhe Guo、Shangzhan Li、Qi Shi、Qingfu Zhu、Maosong Sun、Wanxiang Che

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36826v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36826v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RuHarness，从执行轨迹中持续发现并重估行为准则（rubrics），优先针对尚未解决的弱点定向更新 agent harness，从而在多轮演化中实现持续的自我改进。

**相关性简评：** 研究对象是纯文本 agent 的交互、记忆与工具框架，并非 VLM 视觉推理，但其从轨迹自动生成并按相关性排序监督准则的做法，可能为 latent 推理轨迹中的弱点定位与监督/贡献分配提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When the Critic Falls Behind: Co-Evolving Rubrics and Harnesses for Continual Agent Self-Improvement

**abstract/A1：** Autonomous agents increasingly depend on harnesses that govern interaction, memory, tools, and execution around large language models (LLMs). While recent work has begun to evolve these harnesses from execution experience, the supervision used to guide evolution often remains relatively static, making it difficult to track changing behavioral weaknesses across rounds and potentially limiting generalization to unseen tasks. To address this issue, we propose RuHarness, a rubric--harness co-evolution framework for continual agent self-improvement. RuHarness continually discovers and reassesses behavioral criteria from execution trajectories, prioritizes the most relevant unresolved weaknesses, and uses them to guide targeted harness updates while keeping the task objective fixed. Extensive experiments across diverse agent tasks and backbone models show that RuHarness outperforms strong harness-evolution baselines, particularly on unseen held-out tasks. Further analyses show that adaptive rubric prioritization tracks shifting weaknesses and supports more sustained improvement and stable generalization across evolution rounds.

</details>

<a id="paper-105"></a>

### 105. [Drop the Details, Get the Gist: Dynamic Conceptual Representation for Video Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36832v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36832

**作者：** Ryoya Yoshiyama、Bowen Wang、Yuta Nakashima

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36832v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36832v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 将 concept bottleneck 模型从静态图像扩展到程序性视频识别：把视频表示为片段级概念得分向量的时间序列，送入 Transformer 编码器预测类别，并尝试估计各概念对预测的贡献；在烹饪与家务视频上比较三种粒度的概念集，发现粗粒度概念已足够。

**相关性简评：** 其显式概念瓶颈状态序列与概念粒度消融可供核查「内部状态需保留多少细粒度视觉细节、粗粒度概念是否已足够」这一问题，逐概念贡献估计也可能为 latent 证据筛选与贡献分配的度量提供线索；但论文做的是视频分类而非推理期状态生成，属方法借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Drop the Details, Get the Gist: Dynamic Conceptual Representation for Video Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs), which route predictions through a layer of human-interpretable concepts, have substantially improved the interpretability of visual classification by exposing an explicit, language-based rationale for each prediction. Most existing work focuses on static images, and temporal dynamics in videos have been underexplored in conceptual representation. We propose a framework that extends concept-based image recognition to procedural video understanding. It represents a multi-step video as a temporal sequence of segment-level concept score vectors and feeds this sequence into a Transformer encoder to predict the class label. We also explore a way to estimate the contribution of each concept for further interpretability. We evaluated three concept sets of different granularities on cooking and household-activity videos and found that coarser-grained concepts are sufficient for classifying these videos, which implies that fine-grained textual descriptions may be unnecessary.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 5 figures, 4 tables. Accepted to the MM4SciReal Workshop at ACCV 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-106"></a>

### 106. [MVRCap: Towards Comprehensive Video Captioning via Hierarchical Multi-Dimensional Verifiable Rewards](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36838v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36838

**作者：** Weihuang Lin、Xianing Chen、Zhibin Wang、Congzhi Zhang、Yiwei Ma、Jiayi Ji、Miao Chen、Jun Song、YuCheng、Bo Zheng、Xiaoshuai Sun、Rongrong Ji

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36838v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36838v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** MVRCap 将视频描述生成重构为强化学习任务，用由层级概念覆盖、事实性验证和简洁性约束三部分构成的细粒度可验证奖励提供密集监督，并配套构建分维度标注的 SFT 数据集与 QA 数据集。

**相关性简评：** 论文本身是视频描述任务，不研究 latent 推理；但其把视觉-语义对齐拆成可分别验证的奖励维度，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据使用与轨迹训练的密集监督设计提供借鉴线索，可供核查各维度奖励是否真正约束内部状态而非仅约束输出文本。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MVRCap: Towards Comprehensive Video Captioning via Hierarchical Multi-Dimensional Verifiable Rewards

**abstract/A1：** Despite significant progress, current models struggle to generate comprehensive video captions, constrained primarily by coarse-grained supervision, which often fails to capture spatial-temporal dynamics and the underlying logical intent. To address this issue, we introduce MVRCap, a novel framework that reformulates video captioning as a reinforcement learning task steered by structured verification. Central to our approach is a Fine-Grained Verifiable Reward mechanism, serving as dense supervision to enforce rigorous visual-semantic alignment. Specifically, we construct this reward system anchored by three complementary pillars: a Hierarchical Concept Coverage Reward utilizing our newly constructed MVRCap-QA dataset to ensure multi-dimensional completeness and semantic depth, a Factuality Verification Reward to enhance factual consistency, and a Conciseness Constraint to maximize semantic density. Furthermore, we curate the high-quality MVRCap-SFT dataset, providing structured annotations across six key dimensions, to serve as a robust foundational initialization for the RL policy in this training pipeline. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MVRCap achieves state-of-the-art performance on authoritative benchmarks including VDC, CAPability, and VC4VG.

</details>

<a id="paper-107"></a>

### 107. [When All Rollouts Fail: Policy-Compatible Joint Decoding for Hard-Problem Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36849v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36849

**作者：** Zehao Wang、Hanzhu Chen、Runquan Gui、Zhe Wang、Lin Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36849v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36849v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对 RLVR 在全部 rollout 失败的难题上优势归零的问题，提出 PICO：用 token 级联合解码让专家数据引导生成，同时以动态 top-k 和散度预算保持与当前策略的兼容性，并与 on-policy 轨迹混合优化。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本 RL 训练方法，但其「引导信号须可验证且与当前策略分布兼容、用散度预算约束引导强度」的思路，可供核查 LVR 中专家轨迹或视觉证据注入训练时的分布错配与表层模仿问题。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When All Rollouts Fail: Policy-Compatible Joint Decoding for Hard-Problem Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) derives its learning signal from binary rewards via on-policy exploration and becomes a powerful paradigm for improving the reasoning of large language models. Yet it faces a learning cliff on problems beyond model's current ability: when every rollout fails, the samples reveal nothing about a correct solution, and group-relative estimators (e.g., GRPO, RLOO) collapse the update to zero advantage. Expert trajectories offer a remedy, but they are incompatible with the current policy and often lie in the student's low-probability regions, so the surviving updates demand large parameter shifts and decay into surface-level imitation rather than transferable reasoning. We argue that a useful learning signal on hard problems must be both verifiably correct and sufficiently compatible with the current policy. We propose Policy-Compatible Reinforcement Learning (PICO), which performs distribution-constrained joint decoding: expert data guides decoding at the token level while preserving the student's relative token probabilities, and a dynamic top-k mechanism confines the sampling distribution under a finite divergence budget, yielding trajectories that are verified and policy-compatible by construction. These trajectories are optimized jointly with on-policy rollouts under a mixed-behavior objective that mitigates their distribution mismatch via importance sampling. Experiments across reasoning diverse datasets and agentic benchmarks demonstrate that PICO consistently outperforms standard RLVR and prior off-policy baselines.

</details>

<a id="paper-108"></a>

### 108. [Forget the Data, Keep the Capability: Switchable Unlearning for Retrieval](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36858v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36858

**作者：** Jingong Chen、Kyle Gao、Guangyuan Li、Chulwoo Pack、Sijia Liu、Kwanghee Won

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36858v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36858v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 MoME 可切换框架：将预训练检索骨干拆成保留检索能力的 core 参数与零初始化的 parasitic 分支承载全量知识，用梯度隔离训练，从而以开关而非重训来满足删除请求，并提出 rank-based Forgetting-Rank 统计量审计两分支的独立性。

**相关性简评：** 其「零初始化旁支 + 梯度隔离 + 审计耦合通道」的做法可供核查是否可用于分离与审计 VLM 中真正供给答案的视觉通路，即判断 latent 内容是否被实际使用，但该工作本身不涉及多模态推理或 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Forget the Data, Keep the Capability: Switchable Unlearning for Retrieval

**abstract/A1：** Privacy compliance requires re-identification models to selectively forget individuals while preserving their ability to recognize others. For this, we present MoME, a switchable framework that can adapt pre-trained backbones for privacy compliance, allowing the same model to switch between Full mode for standard inference and Core mode for unlearned inference. MoME splits one backbone into core parameters that preserve retrieval and zero-initialized parasitic parameters that carry the full-data knowledge, and training them under gradient isolation so the core never receives a forget-set gradient. Disabling the branch yields Core, which satisfies a request to delete a person's training data, and enabling it yields Full, which retains what the deleted data taught, so honoring the request is a flag flip rather than a retraining run. We prove Core independent of the forget set up to an audited set of coupling channels and introduce the rank-based Forgetting-Rank statistic to audit that independence in a trained model. On Market-1501, VeRi-776 and RegDB, Core reads at a retrained reference under three membership attacks while retrieving above it, and the retrieval advantage carries to ResNet and CLIP backbones. Code and weights are available at https://github.com/Jingong-Chen/MoME.

</details>

<a id="paper-109"></a>

### 109. [Tail Cues, Principal Corrections: Plug-and-Play Rectification for Open-Set Test-Time Adaptation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36870v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36870

**作者：** Yingkai Yang、Chaoqi Chen、Hui Huang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36870v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36870v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TALOR，一个无反向传播的轻量即插即用模块，用于开放集测试时自适应：借助源线性分类头诱导的基，把归一化特征分解为主坐标与尾部坐标，并用加权岭回归估计主空间中的软路由仿射漂移校正后再送入源头。

**相关性简评：** 其把特征分为高增益主坐标与低增益尾部坐标并显式建模尾部—主耦合的做法，可能为分析 VLM latent 状态中视觉细节是否被压入低增益方向、以及信息坍缩的检测与校正提供线索，但原文不涉及推理过程，迁移可行性尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Tail Cues, Principal Corrections: Plug-and-Play Rectification for Open-Set Test-Time Adaptation

**abstract/A1：** Open-Set Test-Time Adaptation (OSTTA) operates on non-stationary target streams where covariate and semantic shifts coexist. Existing TTA methods often obtain adaptation signals by updating model parameters or maintaining mutable memories, which can be costly and vulnerable to unknown samples. We present TALOR, a lightweight, backward-free rectification module that uses the source linear head as a geometric anchor. The head-induced basis decomposes each normalized feature into high-gain principal coordinates and low-gain tail coordinates. TALOR estimates a soft-routed affine correction in the principal subspace, combining a regime-level principal bias with a tail-conditioned slope that captures sample-level tail-to-principal coupling. Using weighted ridge regression, it subtracts this drift estimate from the principal coordinates, reconstructs the feature and feeds it into the source head. On the ImageNet-C benchmark with six csOOD datasets, TALOR outperforms the next-best UniEnt by 2.2% H-score while running 2.5x faster and using only 12% of its GPU memory; as a post-correction plug-in, it further boosts COME to 68.4% H-score with only 2% additional memory overhead.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-110"></a>

### 110. [GUI-JEPA: Candidate-Conditioned JEPA Latents as a Fast Action Policy for Mobile GUI Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36877v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36877

**作者：** Bingchao Wang、Yongcheng Jing、Shunyu Liu、Chengyu Wang、Jun Huang、Jie Yang、Wei Liu、Dacheng Tao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36877v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36877v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GUI-JEPA：用无标签 GUI 截图预训练的 JEPA 编码器构建无解码器移动 GUI 智能体，以动作和区域条件化的 latent predictor 为每个候选动作生成 latent，再由轻量头批量打分。

**相关性简评：** 其视觉 latent 候选筛选与决策监督可供 LVR 借鉴，但方法是单步动作策略而非多步推理；「随机初始化预测器表现相当」及打分依赖 latent 通路值得核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GUI-JEPA: Candidate-Conditioned JEPA Latents as a Fast Action Policy for Mobile GUI Agents

**abstract/A1：** Mobile GUI agents are typically fine-tuned 7B-scale vision-language models (VLMs) that generate each action as text. However, these VLMs decode every action token by token, and even agents that score candidate actions keep a large language model or VLM in the loop, making them slow and memory-hungry. To address these issues, we propose GUI-JEPA, to our knowledge the first decoder-free GUI agent policy built on a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), which selects actions in the latent space of a JEPA encoder pretrained on unlabelled GUI screenshots. Its action- and region-conditioned latent predictor turns the screen latent into one latent per candidate action, letting lightweight heads score all candidates against the instruction in a single batched pass, without decoding a token. We further show that the scorer relies on this latent pathway and that the predictor is trained for the decision itself: a randomly initialised predictor does just as well as one pretrained on screen dynamics. On AndroidControl, GUI-JEPA is 2.8-4.0x faster on the same GPU than a Qwen2.5-VL-7B agent we fine-tune with the same inputs, while reaching 86-92% of its in-distribution accuracy with 6% of its parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/bcwang-sjtu/GUI-JEPA

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 11 figures, 16 tables

</details>

### 111. [RubricAtlas: Mining Type-Level Rubrics from Expert Reports to Evaluate Deep Research Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36878v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36878

**作者：** Delin Wang、Sinuo Wang、Rui Sun、Beidi Luan、Qiming Shi、Jing Li、Daxin Jiang、Zuo Bai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36878v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36878v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** RubricAtlas 从 3,079 篇中文金融研报中挖掘同一研究类型下共享的「类型级」分析要求，将细粒度二元检查点与报告段落对齐，并把完整性评价与论证有效性分开，用于比较十个深度研究 agent 的分析覆盖度与排序稳定性。

**相关性简评：** 其「检查点—证据段落」可溯源对齐及完整性/有效性分离的评价设计，可能可供核查用于衡量 latent 推理轨迹对视觉证据的覆盖与贡献分配，但纯文本 Web 场景与视觉状态生成无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RubricAtlas: Mining Type-Level Rubrics from Expert Reports to Evaluate Deep Research Agents

**abstract/A1：** Deep research agents conduct multi-step Web searches and synthesise retrieved information into analytical reports. Evaluating these outputs requires checking which analyses they deliver, beyond the presence of relevant facts. Query-specific binary rubrics make individual requirements explicit, but task-level checklists may leave the overall analytical structure underexamined. We introduce RubricAtlas, which derives type-level analytical requirements from expert reports on different objects within the same research type and organises detailed checks around recurring analytical questions. The requirements give related tasks a shared standard for what to analyse, without prescribing their answers. Our framework links binary checkpoints to report passages and evaluates completeness separately from argument validity. Built from 3,079 Chinese financial research reports, RubricAtlas covers 99 research types. Reference-report selection experiments show that individual reports cover only about half of their type's analytical questions, and that selecting requirements from different reports changes evaluation scores and agent rankings. Across ten agents, two judge pipelines achieve checkpoint agreement of $κ$ = 0.71 and agent-ranking agreement of Kendall $τ$ = 0.87. Controlled deletions reduce scores on the affected analyses, while padding produces no detectable increase. These findings support type-level rubrics for comparing the analytical completeness of Web deep research outputs, with individual decisions traceable to report evidence.

</details>

<a id="paper-112"></a>

### 112. [Hyperparameter and Neural Architecture Search via Evolutionary Optimization for Reinforcement Learning: HPNAS-RL](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36882v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36882

**作者：** Nihal Acharya Adde、Hanno Gottschalk

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36882v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36882v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出双分支进化框架，在自动驾驶强化学习任务中并行搜索卷积策略网络结构与关键训练超参数，并以多目标策略、并行评估和教师—学生策略迁移提升收敛速度与泛化。

**相关性简评：** 论文本身不涉及 VLM 的 latent 视觉推理，但其联合搜索结构设计与训练动态、配合教师—学生迁移的评估流程，可能为 LVR 中推理模块结构与训练配置的自动搜索提供可核查的借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Hyperparameter and Neural Architecture Search via Evolutionary Optimization for Reinforcement Learning: HPNAS-RL

**abstract/A1：** In this study, we propose a dual-branch evolutionary framework that simultaneously performs Neural Architecture Search (NAS) and Hyperparameter Optimization (HPO) for Reinforcement Learning (RL) in Autonomous Driving (AD) tasks. While NAS has shown strong potential in automating architecture design for Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), its application to RL remains limited, especially in scenarios that require joint optimization of architectural and training dynamics. Building on our prior work on Evolutionary Multi-Objective NAS (EMNAS), the proposed framework evolves discrete network architectures and continuous hyperparameters in parallel under a unified multi-objective search strategy. In this setup, NAS targets the convolutional layers of the RL policy network - responsible for visual feature extraction, while HPO focuses on optimizing critical RL hyperparameters. To accelerate convergence and improve generalization, we incorporate parallel evaluation and a teacher-student policy transfer mechanism. Experiments in a high-fidelity driving simulator show that the joint NAS-HPO framework consistently outperforms standalone NAS, yielding policies with higher rewards, fewer parameters, and improved stability. These results highlight the benefits of jointly optimizing model structure and training dynamics for scalable and effective RL agent design.

</details>

### 113. [DCKV: Learned Transform Coding of the Prompt KV Cache in Hybrid Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36950v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36950

**作者：** Egor Maximov、Aleksei Merkelov、Vasiliy Alekseev、Ivan Krivorotov、Valentin Beliaev、Nikita Arsenin、Dmitry Bocharov、Aleksei Goncharov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36950v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36950v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 DCKV，一个针对混合语言模型中全注意力层 KV cache 的可微编解码器，将变换、量化与以提示词和已解码邻域为条件的熵模型联合训练，目标同时包括码长与冻结语言模型输出变化，从而在长上下文基准上以数十倍的压缩率把精度损失限制在数点以内。

**相关性简评：** 其「按模型输出变化而非重建误差来训练状态压缩」的思路，可能为核查 latent 推理状态的保真度、信息坍缩与预算分配提供借鉴，但原文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DCKV: Learned Transform Coding of the Prompt KV Cache in Hybrid Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Hybrid language models keep a key-value (KV) cache only in their few full-attention layers, but this cache is still the part of a prompt's state that grows with the prompt and that must be stored or moved to reuse it. We present DCKV, a differentiable codec for the KV cache and the first KV-cache codec that is trained end to end. Its transform, its quantizer and its entropy models, which are conditioned on the prompt tokens and on already decoded neighbouring positions, are optimized together against the frozen language model, both for the code length that the entropy models assign and for the change of the model's output. On Qwen3.5-2B, DCKV stores a compressed token in more than 55 times fewer bytes than bf16 at a loss of at most 1.8 points on three long-context benchmarks, and in more than 100 times fewer bytes at a loss of at most 3.6 points.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 7 figures, 14 tables

</details>

### 114. [Dense Representation Learning and Differentiable Shape Modeling for Spine Landmark Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36951v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36951

**作者：** Augustin Daridon、Sofiane Sifaoui、Ayman Assi、Mohamad Karam、Wafa Skalli、Elsa Angelini、Loic Le Folgoc

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36951v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36951v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文把脊柱 X 光解剖标志点检测重构为像素级对比表示学习问题，让稠密像素嵌入与标志点原型匹配以生成更具判别性的热图，再结合可学习的全局形状先验消解标志点歧义。

**相关性简评：** 其用原型匹配监督稠密像素嵌入替代热图回归、并用可微形状先验约束整体构型的做法，可能为 LVR 中细粒度视觉细节如何保留在内部状态、以及结构化状态约束提供可核查的监督线索，但任务本身与多模态推理无直接关联。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dense Representation Learning and Differentiable Shape Modeling for Spine Landmark Detection

**abstract/A1：** Spine landmark detection from spinal X-ray is a key step for estimating spine deformation severity, for building biomechanical models, and for pre-operative planning. We reframe anatomical landmark detection as a pixel-level contrastive representation learning problem, matching dense pixel embeddings with landmark prototypes. Surprisingly, even with a generic U-Net backbone, this formulation results in more discriminative heatmaps compared to the traditional heatmap regression paradigm, leading to improved discrimination between vertebral corners on distinct vertebra. Furthermore, we combine the resulting heatmaps with learnable shape priors over the global configuration of spinal landmarks to resolve ambiguities in landmark locations, yielding a simple and practical approach to spinal landmark detection. Our approach is competitive, in terms of landmark accuracy and clinical parameter estimation, with the state-of-the-art on the public AASCE dataset and on two private spinal X-ray datasets. Our code and pre-trained weights are available at https://github.com/BioMedTP/Pix2Point.

</details>

### 115. [Proactive Memory for Event-Driven LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36967v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36967

**作者：** Jinyang Li、Zichuan Fu、Xian Wu、Wenlin Zhang、Yimin Deng、Kaifeng Guo、Guojing Li、Junjia QI、Xiangyu Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36967v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36967v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ProactMem，将智能体历史组织为近期事件、时间轨迹与行为规律三层，先用源事件校验轨迹事实、经历史回放评估规律，再匹配当前事件以主动发现服务机会，并构建纵向流式基准 ProactiveStream。

**相关性简评：** 其「事实回溯到源事件核对」「回放验证后再用于当前决策」的监督式证据校验流程，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 内容是否真正被使用的贡献分配提供可供核查的线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Proactive Memory for Event-Driven LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Proactive LLM agents anticipate service opportunities from ongoing observations and accumulated history. Existing approaches emphasize recent context or retrieval of related memories, providing limited support for validating recurring behavioral regularities and assessing their applicability to the current event. We propose Proactive Memory (ProactMem), which organizes history into recent events, temporal trajectories, and behavioral regularities. Trajectory facts are checked against source events, and regularities are assessed through historical replay before being matched to current conditions. We also construct ProactiveStream, a longitudinal benchmark covering three application scopes, where agents process isolated event streams chronologically under episode-aware opportunity labels. On ProactiveStream, ProactMem achieves an overall effectiveness (F1) of 52.6%, a 21.4% relative improvement over the strongest of five external baselines. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProactMem-5351/.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages

</details>
