### 116. [GNN-Guided Network Reduction for Centralized Urban Traffic Routing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36981v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36981

**作者：** Nikolai Antonov、Lukáš Chrpa、Andrii Nyporko、Jan Hůla

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36981v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36981v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出用 GNN 为每个 OD 流给路段打分、据此构造约简路网，再在原 MILP 上求解的集中式交通路由加速方法，在四个城市路网上相对全图参考解的目标差距最小。

**相关性简评：** 「学习打分剪枝候选空间 + 求解器兜底校验」的分工可能与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与验证式推理相近，可供核查；但论文无多模态推理实验，迁移性不确定。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GNN-Guided Network Reduction for Centralized Urban Traffic Routing

**abstract/A1：** We consider the centralized traffic routing (CTR) problem, where a central planner assigns routes to origin-destination (OD) vehicle flows to minimize congestion-dependent segment cost. Prior work formulates CTR efficiently as a mixed-integer linear program (MILP), but scaling this formulation to the full road network is computationally prohibitive within an operational time budget. Bounded Routes and Smart Routes speed up optimization using precomputed reduced networks; however, these methods do not account for vehicle information within each OD flow. Motivated by this gap, we propose a learning-based urban network reduction approach. A dedicated graph neural network (GNN) first scores road segments for each OD flow using the road network and vehicle-flow information. Highly ranked segments are then incorporated into complete OD routes, and the original segment-flow MILP is solved on the resulting reduced road network. The GNN thus guides the reduction of the road network, while the solver enforces feasibility and determines the routing. Experiments on four real urban networks -- Prague-Dejvice, London, New York, and Sydney -- show that the proposed approach achieves the smallest relative objective difference to the $30$-second full-graph reference in every city under both traffic regimes. Averaged across cities, this difference is more than six times lower than that of the best competing method. Moreover, traffic simulation results show that the optimization gains of the proposed method lead to more efficient routing, with the highest aggregate arrival rate and a competitive trip duration per arrival percentage point.

**comment/C1：** Submitted for review to Computers &amp; Operations Research

</details>

<a id="paper-117"></a>

### 117. [Advancement or Acceleration? How Data Curation Improves LLM Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36983v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36983

**作者：** Jiaxing Wang、Zicheng Zhang、Deping Xiang、Jin Xu、Yong Li、Chao Liu、Pengzhang Liu、Qixia Jiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36983v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36983v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文将数据策展的效果区分为「提升可达性能」与「仅加速收敛」，通过解析刻画与 LLM 实验说明前者需要训练目标与目标分布之间存在可纠正差距，并提出可在策展前计算的 Δ-test 来预测收益是否持久。

**相关性简评：** 其「训练-目标差距」诊断可供核查 latent 轨迹或中间状态监督究竟是提升 VLM 推理上限还是仅加速收敛，属纯文本训练侧线索，需较多迁移判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Advancement or Acceleration? How Data Curation Improves LLM Training

**abstract/A1：** While data curation is widely credited with improving large language model training, a basic question remains unresolved: does curation expand what a model can ultimately learn, or merely reach the same solution faster? In this paper, we formalize this distinction between advancement (improving the attainable performance) and acceleration (reaching the same performance with less data). Through an analytic characterization and real-world LLM experiments, we show that advancement requires a correctable train-target gap. When the baseline training and target objectives coincide, no curation policy improves the endpoint, although curation may still accelerate training. This reframes advancement as a property of the curation task rather than the curation method. Under a common gap-centric view of data mixture and selection, we operationalize this condition as the $Δ$-test, an observable discrepancy between the uncurated baseline's training and target losses that is computable prior to expensive curation. Across extensive experiments, an appreciably nonzero $Δ$ predicts persistent gains, whereas gains near $Δ=0$ tend to diminish as the training budget grows. Beyond individual methods, our findings recenter data curation on the task itself and point to target-set construction as a promising direction for shaping what curation can achieve.

</details>

<a id="paper-118"></a>

### 118. [SpectRAG: Weighted Chunk Graphs for Token-Budgeted Context Selection in Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36991v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36991

**作者：** Zafer Sahinoglu、Abdulazeez Mohammed、Buse Yaren Kazangirler、Cahit Berkay Kazangirler

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36991v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36991v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** SpectRAG 提出图谱增强的检索框架：以混合信号构建高召回候选集，用语义相似、文档邻接、章节层级、共享实体、引用等关系建立加权 chunk 图，经图扩展与谱相关性重排后在固定 token 预算下组装多跳推理所需上下文。

**相关性简评：** 其「在图结构上打分并在固定 token 预算内去冗选证」的机制，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与计算/上下文预算分配提供可核查的线索；但该工作为纯文本 RAG，不涉及 latent 推理状态，与主线仅为间接方法借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SpectRAG: Weighted Chunk Graphs for Token-Budgeted Context Selection in Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation

**abstract/A1：** Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems ground large language models (LLMs) by retrieving external evidence and conditioning generation on the retrieved context. Although dense retrieval is effective for local semantic matching, independently ranked retrieval can return redundant passages, miss cross-chunk dependencies, and provide incomplete evidence for multi-hop reasoning. We propose SpectRAG, a spectral graph-augmented retrieval framework that combines hybrid candidate seeding, weighted chunk graph construction, graph-based expansion, spectral relevance propagation, and token-budgeted context selection. Document chunks are represented by dense semantic embeddings and graph-spectral coordinates derived from a weighted chunk graph whose edges encode semantic similarity, document adjacency, section hierarchy, shared entities, citation links, and similarity between LLM-generated utility questions. At query time, SpectRAG builds a high-recall seed set using exact inner-product search and hybrid relevance signals, expands it over the chunk graph, re-scores candidates using graph proximity and spectral information, and assembles the final context under a fixed token budget. The goal is not merely to retrieve individually similar chunks, but to construct a context that is relevant, diverse, structurally connected, and sufficient for grounded reasoning. Experiments on MultiHopRAG, UltraDomain, and long-document question-answering settings show that SpectRAG improves broad evidence recovery and achieves competitive generation quality against dense, hybrid, HyDE, LightRAG, and NaiveRAG baselines. The results further indicate that graph-spectral structure is particularly useful for inference-oriented and cross-document retrieval, while highlighting trade-offs in early precision, computational cost, and domain-dependent performance.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 5 figures, 10 tables. This work is intended for submission to an IEEE journal

</details>

<a id="paper-119"></a>

### 119. [CaLLM: Continual Adaptation of LLMs via Non-Parametric Adapter Routing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36994v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36994

**作者：** Bilge Celik、Anna Vettoruzzo、Joaquin Vanschoren

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36994v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36994v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CaLLM，用基于度量的元学习将输入批次映射到任务无关嵌入空间并匹配最近原型，再路由至对应的轻量 adapter，以模块化参数隔离实现可扩展的持续适应。

**相关性简评：** 其原型路由与模块隔离或可供核查如何按输入选择 latent 推理模块、控制任务间干扰；但路由依据是文本批次嵌入而非推理期 latent 状态，迁移性尚不明确。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CaLLM: Continual Adaptation of LLMs via Non-Parametric Adapter Routing

**abstract/A1：** Continual adaptation of large language models (LLMs) to evolving task distributions while mitigating catastrophic forgetting remains an open problem. Existing strategies often struggle to scale or lack a robust mechanism for zero-shot task-to-expert assignment. We introduce CaLLM, a framework that resolves these tensions by combining metric-based meta-learning with modular parameter isolation via lightweight adapters. First, CaLLM projects each input batch into a task-agnostic embedding space to find its nearest prototype. It then routes the batch to the specific adapter specialized for that data distribution. This formulation provides (i)~controllable cross-task interference by design, (ii)~constant computational complexity regardless of task count, (iii)~seamless integration of novel distributions through prototype expansion, and (iv)~regime agnosticism as the local routing decision enables a unified approach to both online and offline settings. Our experiments demonstrate that this routing mechanism discovers semantically coherent task groupings while facilitating constructive interference between similar tasks, and keeping overhead at a minimum. Across diverse and fine-grained continual learning benchmarks, CaLLM consistently outperforms traditional isolation and replay methods, establishing prototype-based modular routing as a principled and scalable approach to lifelong adaptation of LLMs.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at 5th Conference on Lifelong Learning Agents (CoLLAs), Main Conference Track, 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-120"></a>

### 120. [GPA-VGGT: Geometry-Preserving Acceleration of VGGT](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37000v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37000

**作者：** Muyeol Sung、Jangwon Kim、Jongwoo Lim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37000v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37000v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出免训练的 GPA-VGGT，用帧内注意力加共享跨帧 K/V 替换早期块的全局注意力，并在后续块只合并 K/V、保留全部 query 且补偿注意力质量，以加速 VGGT 的多视图几何推理。

**相关性简评：** 其「保留 query、合并 K/V 并补偿注意力质量」的视觉 token 压缩策略，可供核查 latent 状态中视觉证据压缩是否会造成几何细节坍缩，并与注意力结构对 latent 相似度的影响相关。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GPA-VGGT: Geometry-Preserving Acceleration of VGGT

**abstract/A1：** Feed-forward geometry models such as VGGT recover camera parameters, depth maps, and point maps from one to hundreds of views in a single forward pass, making them powerful backbones for multi-view 3D perception. However, VGGT's global attention is quadratic in the number of frames and dominates end-to-end runtime on long sequences, which has motivated a range of training-free efficiency methods such as token merging. These methods, however, come with a drop in accuracy that makes them hard to use in place of VGGT. We propose GPA-VGGT, a training-free acceleration method that replaces global attention in early blocks with frame attention augmented by a shared cross-frame K/V set. In the remaining blocks, we merge only keys and values while preserving all queries, restore the attention mass of the tokens each merged key represents, and randomly project the matching metric to 128 dimensions to reduce matching cost. Across diverse multi-view geometry tasks, GPA-VGGT provides training-free acceleration at speedups comparable to prior work while preserving the information required for precise geometric estimation, retaining over 99% of VGGT's pose accuracy under AUC@3.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027. Project page: https://noheat61.github.io/GPA-VGGT/

</details>

<a id="paper-121"></a>

### 121. [RePAIR: Inverting Backdoor Attacks in Semantic Segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37015v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37015

**作者：** Arnold Brosch、Kira Maag

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CR、math.PR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37015v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37015v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RePAIR，一种事后、无标签、无需知道攻击细节的语义分割后门防御：从可能被污染的图像池推断 victim-target 类别关系，构建语义参考，通过比较分类头预测与内部语义证据定位可疑像素，并在特征与输出空间做纠正，同时用保守的定位与输出约束限制对正常预测的改动。

**相关性简评：** 其「分类头预测与内部表示语义证据不一致」的定位思路与免训练特征空间校正，可能为 LVR 中判断答案是否真正使用视觉 latent 内容、以及筛选视觉证据提供可核查的方法线索，但研究对象是分割后门而非多模态推理，故仅为方法借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RePAIR: Inverting Backdoor Attacks in Semantic Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Despite significant advances in computer vision, semantic segmentation models remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks, posing a security risk. These attacks redirect pixels of a chosen victim class to an attacker-specified target class when a trigger is present, while preserving clean predictions. Existing defenses are largely derived from image classification, while dedicated segmentation defenses remain scarce and typically require retraining or model modification. We introduce RePAIR, a post-hoc, label-free, attack-unaware defense based on the observation that backdoor-induced shifts can be stronger in the classifier head than in internal representations, leaving recoverable victim-class semantics. RePAIR infers the victim-target relation from an unlabeled, potentially contaminated image pool, constructs a safety-approved semantic reference, and localizes suspicious pixels by comparing head predictions with internal semantic evidence. It then applies feature-space and output corrections toward the inferred victim semantics, while conservative localization and output constraints limit changes to benign predictions. We model the attack as adversarial probability transport and repair as its approximate reversal, deriving bounds and sufficient conditions for localization, residual attack success, clean-side changes, defense-induced target predictions, and attack-unaware pair identification. Experiments across segmentation architectures and attack settings show that RePAIR substantially reduces attack success while largely preserving clean performance.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages total, 9 pages main paper, 2 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-122"></a>

### 122. [Ground-to-Satellite Knowledge Distillation for Crop Classification under Temporal and Geographic Shift](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37021v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37021

**作者：** Miro Miranda、Rocco Sedona、Valerio Marsocci、Gabriele Cavallaro、Claudia Paris

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37021v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37021v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文考察地面照片能否作为训练期特权监督：用基于冻结 CLIP 特征的「照片教师」蒸馏监督由卫星嵌入（AlphaEarth、TESSERA）训练的分类器，在 17 类作物数据上评估时间与地理偏移下的增益，推理时不需要照片。

**相关性简评：** 训练期引入推理时不可得的辅助视觉证据来塑造学生表征，与 LVR 中「额外视觉证据是否真正被内部状态吸收并在推理时可用」的旁路/贡献归因问题可能相通，其跨分布偏移下的增益与失效模式可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Ground-to-Satellite Knowledge Distillation for Crop Classification under Temporal and Geographic Shift

**abstract/A1：** Continuous and up-to-date crop type mapping is essential for agricultural monitoring but is often hampered by limited reference data. The growing availability of ground-level field photographs offers a promising way to address this challenge. When combined with satellite time series, these images provide complementary perspectives on the same parcel, capturing crop characteristics from distinctly different perspectives. However, field photographs are not always available for each satellite pixel at inference time, limiting their potential in scalable crop type mapping. We investigate whether ground photographs can provide privileged supervision for crop classification built on pretrained satellite embeddings. A photo teacher based on frozen CLIP features supervises classifiers trained on AlphaEarth and TESSERA representations; photographs are used only during training. On a paired field photo and satellite imagery dataset with 17 crop classes, knowledge distillation consistently improves over satellite-only classification; for frozen embeddings the margin grows under temporal shift, reaching up to +5.5 macro-F1 points, and widens in geographic holdouts (+3.7 vs +3.0 in-distribution). These results show that ground-view supervision can complement satellite embeddings and improve their robustness without changing inference requirements.

</details>

<a id="paper-123"></a>

### 123. [Guided-RLVR: Guided Exploration in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37041v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37041

**作者：** Bo Yang、Zishuai Zhang、Weihua Li、Qinnan Zhang、Sijia Wen、Hainan Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37041v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37041v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对 RLVR 采样轨迹易坍缩到少数推理路径的问题，该文提出 Guided-RLVR，用一个轻量控制器向采样分布中注入有语义的引导 token，促使模型从多种推理视角解题，在数学推理与代码生成上报告了更好的性能与更高的轨迹多样性。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本 RLVR 工作，但「在关键步骤注入引导 token 以扩展探索」可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理状态的轨迹多样化与探索监督提供借鉴线索，是否可迁移到多模态需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Guided-RLVR: Guided Exploration in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown strong empirical gains in improving the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs) on tasks such as mathematics and programming. However, recent studies have shown that RLVR rollouts often collapse onto a narrow set of reasoning trajectories, limiting the explored reasoning space. Motivated by findings that thinking tokens at critical steps serve as critical structural elements in LLM reasoning pathways, we propose Guided-RLVR, a novel approach that augments RLVR with a lightweight controller. The controller strategically injects semantically meaningful steering tokens into the sampling distribution, encouraging the model to approach problems from multiple reasoning perspectives and thereby expanding the exploration space. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks show that Guided-RLVR consistently outperforms existing RLVR methods while producing more diverse reasoning trajectories.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, 7 figures, 8 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-124"></a>

### 124. [Learning to Code the World: Harness Evolution for Compositional 3D Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37046v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37046

**作者：** Haoyu Yao、Chenguo Lin、Ruize Du、Yadong Mu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37046v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37046v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SceneEvolve，把 3D 场景表示为可执行代码，结合参数化原语、层级组合与语义空间关系的 SceneAct 动作接口，以及用教师反馈迭代演化 harness（外围流程/工具编排）的 SceneHarness-RSI，使小型开源模型在参数与执行后端固定下从单图重建可编辑组合式场景。

**相关性简评：** 其固定模型参数、仅演化外围 harness 的可复用推理流程与层级语义空间关系接口，可能为 LVR 的推理期结构化状态更新、视觉证据筛选及过程监督提供可核查线索，但论文本身不研究 latent 状态或 VLM 推理，需判断迁移价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Code the World: Harness Evolution for Compositional 3D Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Reconstructing editable and compositional 3D scenes from a real image requires jointly reasoning about geometry, object structure, and spatial relationships. We introduce SceneEvolve, a framework that represents 3D scenes as executable code and improves reconstruction by evolving the harness around a small open-source agent. SceneEvolve combines SceneAct, a compositional semantic action interface built on parameterized primitives, hierarchical composition, and semantic spatial relations, with SceneHarness-RSI, which leverages a stronger teacher agent to iteratively evolve the harness using feedback from development-set reconstructions. Throughout harness evolution, the model parameters, action semantics, and execution backend remain fixed. The resulting harness provides a reusable reconstruction procedure that enables the small open-source model to reconstruct unseen scenes without parameter updates or teacher assistance. Across two in-domain datasets and one out-of-domain dataset, SceneEvolve with the comparatively compact Qwen3.8-27B model improves all four evaluated reconstruction metrics while reducing mean inference time by 47.0% relative to the base model. More notably, despite relying on a substantially smaller model, SceneEvolve achieves 32.3% higher reconstruction quality than the large proprietary GPT-5.6-Sol model, while reducing mean equivalent deployment cost by 59.8%. Furthermore, the harness evolved using Qwen3.8-27B also generalizes to Kimi-K3, achieving comparable reconstruction performance to GPT-6-Astra. The project page is https://haoyu-agent.github.io/SceneEvolve.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://haoyu-agent.github.io/SceneEvolve

</details>

<a id="paper-125"></a>

### 125. [Exploring the Limits of the Delta Learning Hypothesis: Single-Pair Generator Biases in Direct Preference Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37058v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37058

**作者：** Giuseppe Curci、Stefano Simonazzi、Andrea Molinari、Andrea Zugarini

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37058v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37058v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文检验「delta learning」假设的边界：仅用一对大小模型、以大模型回答作为偏好样本构造 DPO 数据。作者从理论与实验上说明固定模型对会引入与回答质量无关、由生成器决定的系统性偏好偏差，导致 DPO 效果不及多模型偏好数据，并归因于对这些偏差的 reward hacking；增加生成器数量可在无外部评判者时部分缓解。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理无直接关系，但其「监督信号由生成器身份而非内容质量决定、并诱发 reward hacking」的结论，可供核查以模型输出或更强模型轨迹作为正例来训练 VLM 推理轨迹时，标签优势是否实际来自内容贡献。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Exploring the Limits of the Delta Learning Hypothesis: Single-Pair Generator Biases in Direct Preference Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) typically relies on preference pairs generated from diverse model pools and ranked by an external LLM judge. The recently proposed delta-learning hypothesis suggests that comparable supervision can instead be obtained from a single pair of models by treating the larger model's response as preferred. We investigate the limits of this approach across model families and scales. We show, both theoretically and empirically, that fixed model pairs introduce systematic, generator-dependent biases that are predictive of the preference label independent of response quality. Consequently, DPO trained on single-pair data consistently underperforms multi-model preference data, despite the larger generator producing higher-quality responses, and we trace this gap to training dynamics consistent with reward hacking on these biases. We further show that simply increasing the number of generators, even without an external judge, is enough to partially mitigate this effect. Our results highlight generator diversity as a critical factor in preference-data construction.

</details>

<a id="paper-126"></a>

### 126. [Correctness Conditioning as Selection in Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness Evaluation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37070v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37070

**作者：** Yueer Pan、Yuexi Pan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37070v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37070v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用七变量结构因果模型分析「只在答对样本上评估 CoT 忠实性」这一筛选步骤，推导出观察差距 Δ=Cov(A,C)/P(C=1)，区分难度混杂与捷径导致的碰撞偏差，并在算术任务四组部署中观察到 +7.4 个百分点的正选择差距。

**相关性简评：** 其按正确性筛选样本造成的贡献估计偏差与协方差分解，可能为 LVR 中「答案是否真正使用 latent 视觉内容」的忠实性度量提供可供核查的校正线索，但结论基于纯文本算术任务，需核查假设能否移植到多模态设定。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Correctness Conditioning as Selection in Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness Evaluation

**abstract/A1：** Restricting chain-of-thought (CoT) faithfulness evaluation to correctly answered instances changes the population being measured. We study this selection step in a seven-variable structural causal model and derive the exact observed gap $Δ= \\operatorname{Cov}(A, C)/P(C=1)$. The model distinguishes difficulty confounding from shortcut-induced collider bias and states the conditional-independence assumption needed to express the gap through latent response propensities. A protocol review of 18 papers distinguishes answer-correctness restrictions from agreement-based selection, benchmark construction, and training-data filtering. We evaluate four sub-ceiling deployments on arithmetic tasks and observe positive selection gaps in all four. The primary GLM-5.3 run yields a +7.4 percentage-point gap ($n = 1000$, $p &lt; 0.0001$), and pooled Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel analysis gives OR = 1.70 ($p &lt; 10^{-6}$). An assumption-free covariance decomposition assigns 42.8% of the GLM-5.3 gap to between-stratum variation in interventionable-operation count, leaving the majority unattributed. When counterfactual compliance is observable for every instance, evaluating all traces directly estimates population faithfulness without correctness filtering.

</details>

<a id="paper-127"></a>

### 127. [On the Sparsity of Direct Preference Optimization: Weight Disentanglement in the NTK Regime](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37085v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37085

**作者：** Kensuke Sasaki、Issei Sato

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37085v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37085v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在 NTK 线性化下分析 DPO 的优化动态，提出「双重稀疏」：更新方向由少量支持对构成的数据级稀疏，与正负回答梯度抵消导致的参数级稀疏，并将其与权重解纠缠及模型合并联系起来。

**相关性简评：** 其隐式最大间隔偏置与支持对主导更新的做法，可供核查是否适用于 latent 轨迹训练中的样本贡献分配与更新结构分析；与视觉推理本身无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the Sparsity of Direct Preference Optimization: Weight Disentanglement in the NTK Regime

**abstract/A1：** Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has been widely adopted for aligning language models with human preferences. Recent empirical work has revealed that DPO induces remarkably sparse parameter updates, yet no theoretical explanation for this phenomenon has been offered. We analyze DPO's optimization dynamics under a neural tangent kernel linearization and show that the logistic structure of the DPO loss gives rise to an implicit maximum-margin bias. Building on this observation, we introduce the concept of dual sparsity: the DPO update direction is a sparse linear combination (data-level sparsity, driven by support vectors) of sparse gradient feature vectors (parameter-level sparsity, arising from gradient cancellation between preferred and dispreferred responses). We formalize this structure in our main theorem, bound the number of support pairs by a margin-dependent spectral quantity, and present a framework relating dual sparsity to weight disentanglement---a condition favorable for model merging. Experiments on GPT-2 Small and Medium and on 7--9B instruction-tuned models (Llama-3-8B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B) with the Anthropic HH-RLHF and UltraFeedback datasets confirm both sources of sparsity, and experiments on GPT-2 show that DPO reduces cross-task interference by up to $35{\\times}$ compared to supervised fine-tuning.

</details>

<a id="paper-128"></a>

### 128. [TriDifNet: A Unified Three-Task Diffusion Network for End-to-End RGB-T Perception](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37097v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37097

**作者：** Qianchao He、Jing Liu、Yunhao Li、Xiaoqing Tian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37097v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37097v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TriDifNet，用三个 diffusion 模块分别完成红外-可见光配准、融合与行人检测，并以闭环优化和渐进训练耦合三任务，在错位 RGB-T 设定下提升检测性能。

**相关性简评：** 其「检测语义反哺融合与配准」的闭环机制及 diffusion 迭代式表示更新，可供核查答案信号如何筛选视觉证据这一 LVR 问题，但属任务级系统工程，与推理期 latent 状态仍隔一层。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TriDifNet: A Unified Three-Task Diffusion Network for End-to-End RGB-T Perception

**abstract/A1：** Infrared--visible (IR--VIS) multi-modal perception supports robust scene understanding under challenging illumination. Infrared and visible images are often misaligned due to platform motion, viewpoint changes, and modality-dependent distortions, causing registration errors to propagate into subsequent fusion and detection stages. We propose TriDifNet, a unified three-task diffusion network for end-to-end RGB-T perception. To the best of our knowledge, TriDifNet is the first diffusion-based network that jointly optimizes registration, fusion, and detection for misaligned RGB-T pedestrian perception. TriDifNet consists of three diffusion-based modules, i.e., the Registration Diffusion Module (RegDM), the Fusion Diffusion Module (FusDM), and the Detection Diffusion Module (DetDM). RegDM aligns the unaligned infrared image to the optical reference by estimating a dense deformation field. FusDM performs residual conditional diffusion to generate a detection-oriented fused representation with optical structural cues and complementary infrared saliency. DetDM formulates pedestrian detection as an iterative denoising process in bounding-box space. To couple the three tasks, TriDifNet introduces a closed-loop optimization mechanism, where detection semantics guide fusion and registration, and the fusion result further regularizes geometric alignment. A stage-wise progressive training strategy and a lightweight Nash multi-task learning (Nash-MTL)-based late-stage refinement are further adopted to balance registration, fusion, and detection optimization. Extensive experiments on the KAIST multispectral pedestrian benchmark show that TriDifNet achieves superior downstream detection performance under misaligned RGB-T settings. Against strong system-level competitors, TriDifNet achieves 65.44\\% Recall, 57.55\\% mAP$\_{50}$, and 26.83\\% mAP$\_{50:95}$.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 6 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-129"></a>

### 129. [Self-Regularized Flow Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37115v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37115

**作者：** Theodoros Kouzelis、Spyros Gidaris、Nikos Komodakis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37115v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37115v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SERF 自监督框架：用 EMA 教师接收同一插值路径上更干净的输入，对学生做 noisy-to-clean 自蒸馏，含中间表示对齐（SERF-L）与输出分布对齐（SERF-P）两种变体，提升连续扩散语言模型的训练效率、生成质量与少步采样表现。

**相关性简评：** 论文沿去噪轨迹提供中间表示与输出分布的额外监督，可供核查是否能迁移为 latent 推理轨迹的分步监督与贡献分配，以及少步生成下的状态更新；其设定为纯文本，与视觉证据筛选无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Self-Regularized Flow Language Models

**abstract/A1：** We introduce SERF (Self-Regularized Flow Language Models), a simple self-supervised framework for improving continuous diffusion language models by exploiting the denoising trajectory itself. While continuous flow-based language models offer a promising alternative to autoregressive generation, their training objectives typically rely on supervision from clean target tokens, leaving the denoising trajectory underexploited as a learning signal. SERF addresses this limitation through noisy-to-clean self-distillation: an exponential moving average (EMA) teacher receives a cleaner version of the student's noisy input along the same interpolation path and provides additional supervision. We investigate two variants: SERF-L, which aligns the student's intermediate representations with deeper teacher representations, and SERF-P, which aligns their output token distributions, either at the student's output or at an early student layer. All variants augment the standard training objective without requiring external pretrained models or modifying the inference procedure. Experiments across three continuous language-model paradigms (one-hot FLM, latent ELF, and hyper-spherical S-FLM) demonstrate substantial improvements in training efficiency, generation quality, and few-step generation. In particular, SERF achieves up to 7.7x wall-clock training acceleration on OpenWebText, improves MAUVE by 24.3% and GSM8K accuracy by up to 28.8%, and matches baseline performance with up to 64x fewer sampling steps. These results establish noisy-to-clean self-distillation as an effective approach to improving continuous diffusion language models.

</details>

<a id="paper-130"></a>

### 130. [Mechanistic Failure Modes of Weight Pruning in Transformer Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37126v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37126

**作者：** Nabeelah Faumi、Deshan Sumanathilaka

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37126v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37126v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文以 GPT-2 的 IOI 电路为基准，做因子化权重剪枝实验，并用 activation/path patching 与直接 logit 归因测量电路完整性，揭示输出指标和结构检查都可能遗漏的两种机制性失效模式。

**相关性简评：** 其因果干预与贡献归因流程可供核查 LVR 中任务必要信息是否真正保留在内部状态、以及“输出正确但 latent 计算已受损”的评估盲区，但需迁移到多模态场景并重新界定视觉证据电路。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Mechanistic Failure Modes of Weight Pruning in Transformer Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Pruned language models are judged almost entirely by accuracy and perplexity, which assumes that preserved output behaviour implies preserved internal computation. We test this assumption with a factorial pruning study on GPT-2, crossing two importance criteria (magnitude, random), two component types (attention heads, MLP neurons) and three sparsity levels (3%, 50%, 70%). Using the fully characterised indirect object identification (IOI) circuit as ground truth, we measure circuit integrity with activation patching, path patching and direct logit attribution. We find two qualitatively distinct failure modes. Magnitude head pruning &gt;= zeroes the circuit: output-facing heads are removed, and the model falls to near-chance. Magnitude neuron pruning at 30% inverts it: all 26 circuit heads remain intact, but Name Mover contributions flip sign, and the model systematically prefers the wrong name (17.7% accuracy). Perplexity ranks it as less damaged than random head pruning (74.6 vs 140.1). A replication of the Greater-Than circuit shows that the critical component type depends on the circuit's architecture. These results show that output metrics and structural checks can both miss mechanistic damage, motivating circuit-level causal analysis as a complementary evaluation of compressed language models.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 10 Tables

</details>

<a id="paper-131"></a>

### 131. [Generative Replay for Equilibrium Propagation: Toward Edge-Friendly Continual Learning in Hopfield Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37139v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37139

**作者：** Victor Cook、Gilles Sassatelli、Thierry Gil、Mohamed Watfa、Marina Reyboz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37139v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37139v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 EqProp 训练 Hopfield 网络在类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘，提出 EB-Gen：让同一网络兼任分类器与样本生成器，通过弛豫冻结副本网络的内在生成动力学产生伪样本回放，无需外部缓冲区或架构开销，在 Split MNIST/CIFAR10 上报告稳定性和可塑性均有改善。

**相关性简评：** 其「靠冻结网络内部弛豫生成伪样本来替代外部数据回放」的思路，可能为 LVR 中不依赖原始视觉输入而维护、重放 latent 状态及相应训练监督提供可供核查的线索，但论文本身不涉及多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Generative Replay for Equilibrium Propagation: Toward Edge-Friendly Continual Learning in Hopfield Networks

**abstract/A1：** Equilibrium Propagation (EqProp) offers a biologically grounded and hardware-aligned alternative to backpropagation for training Hopfield Networks. However, its behavior in continual learning settings remains largely unexplored. This work confronts catastrophic forgetting in EqProp-trained Hopfield Networks from the inside out, eliminating the need for external data buffers or architectural overhead that standard Experience Replay \\textbf{(ER)} and Generative Replay \\textbf{(Gen-Replay)} inevitably require. We introduce an Energy-Based Generative Replay strategy \\textbf{(EB-Gen)} which exploits the intrinsic generative dynamics of Hopfield Networks, allowing a single model to act as both classifier and replay samples generator. Samples capturing past task knowledge are generated by relaxing a frozen copy of the network, enabling rehearsal solely through internally generated pseudo-data. We evaluate the proposed method in the Class-Incremental setting, using the split versions of the MNIST and CIFAR10 benchmarks and compare it with FineTune, Experience Replay, Generative Replay, Sleep-Like Replay Consolidation (SRC) and Joint training. Our results show that EB-Gen significantly improves stability while preserving plasticity, achieving a 75\\% and 64\\% average accuracy on Split MNIST and Split CIFAR10 respectively. These findings show that Equilibrium Propagation can be adapted to alleviate catastrophic forgetting without forgoing the traits which made it compelling in the first place.

</details>

<a id="paper-132"></a>

### 132. [Beyond Parameter Protection: Fisher-Shaped EWC for Replay-Free Continual Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37145v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37145

**作者：** Liying Wang、Simone Magistri、Joost van de Weijer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37145v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37145v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 FS-EWC，在无回放的持续学习中把 Fisher 信息矩阵的作用从「保护历史重要参数」扩展为「塑造解本身」：在任务学习时最小化给定邻域内最坏情况的 Fisher 迹，使解对有界的未来参数漂移不敏感，并在任务结束时用基于轨迹的模型合并构造保留解。

**相关性简评：** 其「在邻域上控制敏感性」和「由多个优化状态合并而非单一终态」的线索，可供核查是否能用于提升 LVR 中 latent 推理状态在多步更新下的稳定性，以及轨迹监督与贡献分配，但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Beyond Parameter Protection: Fisher-Shaped EWC for Replay-Free Continual Learning

**abstract/A1：** In replay-free continual learning, Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) uses Fisher information matrix (FIM) to protect acquired knowledge by penalizing changes to historically important parameters. However, a local importance estimate at the retained solution does not in general characterize sensitivity after finite parameter displacement. This motivates controlling Fisher sensitivity over a neighborhood of potential future updates. We propose Fisher-Shaped EWC (FS-EWC), which extends the role of FIM from parameter protection to solution formation for future retention. During task learning, FS-EWC minimizes the worst-case empirical FIM trace within a prescribed neighborhood, explicitly favoring solutions that are less sensitive to bounded future displacement. At task completion, trajectory-based model merging constructs the retained solution from multiple optimization states rather than a single terminal iterate, after which FIM is estimated for subsequent consolidation. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that FS-EWC improves performance over EWC, while controlled studies indicate a more favorable stability--plasticity balance and greater tolerance to parameter drift. These results suggest that effective knowledge retention requires both forming solutions with future retention in mind and protecting acquired knowledge.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages, 7 figures, conference

</details>

<a id="paper-133"></a>

### 133. [GCSR-OCC: Geometry Calibration and Semantic Routing for Open-Vocabulary Occupancy Prediction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37149v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37149

**作者：** Bohua Wang、Congyang Zhao、Tianxiong Lv、Chenxu Zhao、He Zheng、Hangqi Fan、Leigang Luo、Zhihao Liao、Yihong Guo、Yang Cai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37149v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37149v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** GCSR-OCC 面向单张 RGB 图像的开放词表室内 3D occupancy 预测，用几何校准算子抑制 Gaussian 尺度膨胀与局部证据过度累积，并以多模式、文本查询条件的语义路由把各 Gaussians 证据聚合到体素，在 Occ-ScanNet 上报告了最优几何与语义指标。

**相关性简评：** 其「多模式查询匹配 + 可学习路由聚合证据」的机制，可能为 latent 状态中按候选假设筛选与加权视觉证据、避免语义混合提供可供核查的方法线索；但论文是单次前馈的 occupancy 预测，不涉及推理期 latent 状态生成或迭代。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GCSR-OCC: Geometry Calibration and Semantic Routing for Open-Vocabulary Occupancy Prediction

**abstract/A1：** Open-vocabulary 3D occupancy prediction represents scene geometry and semantics in a unified voxel grid, enabling recognition beyond predefined categories. One approach reconstructs scenes with 3D Gaussians and converts them into voxel occupancy. However, Gaussian scale inflation and excessive overlap can distort voxel occupancy predictions, while semantic mixing can arise within Gaussians spanning multiple categories and during 3D--2D semantic feature alignment. We propose GCSR-OCC, which combines geometry calibration and multi-mode query-conditioned semantic routing for indoor occupancy prediction from a single RGB image. The Evidence-Conserving Gaussian-to-Occupancy operator (EC-G2O) uses kernel mass normalization, $p$-norm aggregation, and a learnable gain to suppress scale inflation and excessive local evidence accumulation while calibrating occupancy responses. Multi-mode Query-First Gaussian Semantic Routing (MM-QGSR) matches multiple semantic modes per Gaussian to text queries and aggregates query-specific evidence into voxels through a learnable text-conditioned router. Fine-grained subclass queries and smoothed semantic aggregation further refine voxel-level predictions. Without 3D semantic annotations, GCSR-OCC achieves state-of-the-art performance in open-vocabulary occupancy prediction on Occ-ScanNet, with 63.31% geometric IoU and 27.44% semantic mIoU. It also reduces geometric aggregation latency from 19.76 ms to 12.88 ms. Our code will be made publicly available.

</details>

<a id="paper-134"></a>

### 134. [LiveSearch: Questions as Executable Verifier Code for a Live Benchmark of Professional-Source Financial Search Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37166v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37166

**作者：** Sinuo Wang、Wentao Zhao、Jiacheng Lu、Rui Sun、Beidi Luan、Qiming Shi、Jing Li、Daxin Jiang、Zuo Bai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37166v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37166v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LiveSearch：把每个金融问题保存为可执行验证器代码，即对专业金融数据服务的类型化调用程序加声明式抽取器，评测时执行程序现场得出金标准，并每日自动重跑；模型只有借助工具检索与分析才能答对。

**相关性简评：** 「问题即验证程序」把金标准从固定快照改为可执行核验，可能为 LVR 中答案是否真正依赖 latent 状态或检索证据的判定与监督提供线索，其 verifier 设计与动态金标准机制可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LiveSearch: Questions as Executable Verifier Code for a Live Benchmark of Professional-Source Financial Search Agents

**abstract/A1：** In finance, the answer that matters is usually the current one: prices, rates, and disclosures hold only as of a given day, so retrieving up-to-date facts is a core capability of a financial agent. Yet most financial benchmarks overlook timeliness. They fix each answer at annotation time, so the gold goes stale as markets move and, once leaked into training data, rewards recall rather than search and analysis. We introduce LiveSearch, a live benchmark that stores each question as executable verifier code: a typed program of calls to a professional financial data service, followed by a declarative extractor. The question is frozen, while its gold answer is determined by executing the program against professional financial data at evaluation time, and a daily job re-executes it with no human or language model in the loop. A memorized answer is thus likely outdated, and a model must reach the current one through tools and analysis. Construction is equally automatic: a language model only verbalizes programs enumerated from real tool responses and never chooses an answer. Under general web search, six contemporary tool-calling models reach 70.9% to 76.7% answer accuracy. Over three consecutive days, 70% of served questions change their gold value, and even under tolerance-aware matching, a literally reused earlier snapshot fails on a majority of questions within a single day. Per-question tracking uncovers dynamic shifts in correctness masked by aggregate accuracy.

</details>

<a id="paper-135"></a>

### 135. [VGIQA: Fine-Grained Image Quality Assessment with Visual Grounding Rewards](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37186v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37186

**作者：** Weichang Wang、Liyu Chen、Shuyue Guan、Jie Hu、Fei Gao、Wei Li、Nannan Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37186v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37186v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 VGIQA，以冷启动加强化学习的两阶段范式训练 MLLM，借助局部质量评估的视觉 grounding 奖励，同时支持局部与整体图像质量评估，并构建 VG-SR-2K 数据集。

**相关性简评：** 其 grounding 奖励驱动的局部质量监督可供核查是否为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与定位式监督提供线索，但任务是 IQA，未涉及 latent 推理状态本身，联系间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** VGIQA: Fine-Grained Image Quality Assessment with Visual Grounding Rewards

**abstract/A1：** Image quality assessment (IQA) aims to evaluate the perceptual visual quality of images. Recently, the rapid development of Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has significantly advanced the IQA field. By integrating chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and reinforcement learning (RL), these models can generate more accurate and interpretable results while relying on less training data. However, existing MLLM-based methods are predominantly constrained to holistic, image-level evaluations and lack the capability to produce fine-grained, localized assessments, which substantially limits their practical applicability. To address this limitation, we propose VGIQA, a novel IQA model endowed with both local and global quality assessment capabilities. VGIQA is trained via a two-stage paradigm comprising a cold-start phase followed by reinforcement learning. Driven by carefully designed local quality assessment reward functions, our model exhibits exceptional image quality understanding on VGSR-2K, a new dataset we propose specifically for local quality evaluation, significantly outperforming existing open-source MLLMs such as Qwen2.5-VL-72B-Instruct and Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct. Furthermore, the local quality evaluation task can effectively enhance the model's performance on the degradation perception task.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 5 figures, 6 tables; main paper: 4 figures and 4 tables, appendix: 1 figure and 2 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-136"></a>

### 136. [Action-Value Calibration via Continuation Sharing for Long-Horizon Agentic Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37195v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37195

**作者：** Yingjian Chen、Lang Zhou、Zixin Zhao、Pingfan Li、Weishi Zheng、Zhilin Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37195v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37195v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 针对长程 agentic RL 中逐动作信用分配困难的问题，作者提出 ConCal：在已有 rollout batch 内按状态-动作-状态转移匹配同一任务的访问，递归合并共享的后续轨迹证据与各访问自身的估计，并将校准值反向传播以修正早期动作价值，无需额外采样。

**相关性简评：** 论文不做视觉或多模态推理，但其「用同一批轨迹中的共享后续证据校准单条采样的价值估计」的思路，可能为 LVR 中轨迹贡献分配与推理步骤监督信号降噪提供可核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Action-Value Calibration via Continuation Sharing for Long-Horizon Agentic Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Reinforcement learning for long-horizon agents relies on feedback from sequences of interactions, making credit assignment to individual actions difficult. Existing methods such as GiGPO compare actions at shared states to compute relative advantages, but estimate each action's value from a single sampled continuation. The resulting local credit remains sensitive to decisions made later in the trajectory. To address this problem, we propose ConCal (Continuation Calibration), a recursive continuation-value estimator that incorporates continuation evidence already present in a rollout batch. ConCal matches visits from the same task by their incoming state-action-state transitions, combines shared continuation evidence with each visit's own transition estimate, and propagates calibrated values backward to refine earlier action-value estimates. This reduces dependence on individual sampled continuations and mitigates local credit distortions without additional rollouts. Held-out return diagnostics show lower error in within-group value differences and higher agreement with reference action orderings than estimates based on individual continuations. With Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, ConCal improves success rates over GiGPO by 3.39 and 5.86 percentage points on ALFWorld and WebShop, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Yingjian-Chen/ConCal.

</details>

<a id="paper-137"></a>

### 137. [Does Your Generalist Skill Optimizer Really Incentivize Agentic Tasks? Rethinking Skill Optimization via Bipartite Attribution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37201v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37201

**作者：** Jiawei Zhou、Bo Peng、Yuxuan Huang、Ming Zhang、Youbang Sun、Xiang Wang、Chaochao Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37201v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37201v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 BiPA，通过重放优化器的接受规则并与同源候选对比、用「轮次—所读文本」二部图把额外输入 token 归因到具体行为，检验三种 skill optimizer 在 agentic 任务上的收益是否真实；结果显示学到的技能仅比手写种子高约 0.1 点，dev 增益在测试上消失，输入 token 增加约 16%。

**相关性简评：** 其「仅由选择产生的增益」对照和按新增轮次而非文本长度计费的成本归因，可能可供核查 latent 步骤或多轮 LOOK/THINK 的增益是否真实、以及贡献分配与实际成本评估，但对象是纯文本 agent 技能而非视觉 latent 推理，需进一步判断可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Does Your Generalist Skill Optimizer Really Incentivize Agentic Tasks? Rethinking Skill Optimization via Bipartite Attribution

**abstract/A1：** Skill optimizers adapt an agent to long-horizon tasks, such as repairing a terminal environment, by rewriting its skill, a natural-language procedure in its context, and keeping the edits that raise success on a small dev set. On such tasks success is noisy, so a gain on a small dev set does not show that an edit helps, and a success score does not show what the edit costs. We introduce BiPA, which replays an optimizer's acceptance rule on candidates identical to its seed to measure what selection alone reports, and records each episode as a bipartite graph of turns and the text they read to trace extra input tokens to the behavior behind them. Across three optimizers, three agentic benchmarks and two models, the learned skills succeed about as often as their hand-written seed, 0.1 points higher on average. Their dev gains vanish on test: keeping the best of several candidates on at most 30 tasks reports gains this large even when none changed. They read about 16% more input tokens: the optimizers almost always add rules, half to re-check work and some to act one call at a time, and the agent follows them as extra turns, each re-sending the whole conversation. Four principles follow: accept an edit only if its gain exceeds what selection alone reports; charge it for the turns it adds, not for its length; let the editor delete and merge rules; show it what earlier rules cost. Code is available at https://github.com/valleysprings/BiPA.git.

</details>

<a id="paper-138"></a>

### 138. [SimplexLoRA: Dynamic Rank Updating via Alternating Minimization with Simplex Projection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37206v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37206

**作者：** Igor Shalygin、Grigorii Davydenko、Andrey Veprikov、Aleksey Antonov、Nikolay Bushkov、Stanislav Moiseev、Aleksandr Beznosikov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37206v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37206v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SimplexLoRA：在微调初期用交替最小化在 n 维单纯形上学习各适配器的重要性权重，随后冻结，按重要性动态分配 LoRA 秩，在不增加参数预算与推理成本下替代固定秩 LoRA，并在 12 个语言理解、摘要与推理任务上验证。

**相关性简评：** 该工作属纯文本 PEFT，与 VLM 推理无直接关联；但其「按学习到的重要性权重决定各模块容量分配」的机制可供核查是否能迁移到 latent 推理中为不同层或分支分配状态容量与监督权重。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SimplexLoRA: Dynamic Rank Updating via Alternating Minimization with Simplex Projection

**abstract/A1：** The success of large language models in real-world applications relies on parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), which inserts compact, trainable adapters into selected layers. In this paper, we propose SimplexLoRA, a LoRA variant that adaptively adjusts adapter ranks during fine-tuning based on their learned importance. Unlike standard LoRA, which employs a fixed rank across all layers, SimplexLoRA optimizes per-adapter importance weights on the $n$-scaled simplex during a short warm-up and then freezes them, dynamically allocating capacity where it is most beneficial without increasing the overall parameter budget or inference cost. We evaluate SimplexLoRA across 12 language understanding, summarization, and reasoning tasks using DeBERTaV3, BART-large, Llama 3.1 8B, and Qwen3-8B. Our results show that SimplexLoRA consistently matches or outperforms standard LoRA and recent adaptive baselines, with the largest gains at low ranks. Our code is available at https://github.com/brain-lab-research/SimplexLoRA.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to Findings of EMNLP 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-139"></a>

### 139. [TopoField: Technical Report for ECCV 2026 SceneFun3D Challenge](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37218v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37218

**作者：** Zhiwei Chen、Zhiheng Fu、Zixu Li、Mingyu Zhang、Jiale Huang、Yupeng Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37218v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37218v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 技术报告提出 TopoField，在 VOLT 之上构建确定性解码器：把 13 次测试时前向累积为语义共识场，按覆盖次数归一化融合，再从高置信核心与支持区域构建图测地分区来解码实例候选并做类别感知排序，用于 SceneFun3D 室内点云功能分割，获 28.286% AP 与挑战赛第二名。

**相关性简评：** 其「多趟前向聚合成共识场」与「候选构建、排序分离」，以及 IoU-valid 候选率与 top-1 覆盖率的拆分诊断，可供核查 LVR 中多假设状态聚合与生成/验证分工的评价设计；但任务是 3D 分割，与视觉推理没有直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TopoField: Technical Report for ECCV 2026 SceneFun3D Challenge

**abstract/A1：** Functional segmentation requires locating small interactive parts, such as knobs, buttons, and sockets, in indoor point clouds and returning confidence-ranked instance masks. A point-wise semantic predictor produces a continuous probability field, whereas the benchmark evaluates a discrete set of scored instances. This creates a substantial field-to-instance gap. We present TopoField, a deterministic decoder built on top of VOLT. TopoField first converts 13 test-time forward passes into a semantic consensus field, then decodes instance candidates from the consensus geometry, and finally ranks the candidates in a class-aware manner. The augmentation stage retains the processed-point identity of every fragment prediction, accumulates probabilities in a common point array, normalizes by coverage count, and fuses the resulting caches. The decoding stage separates high-confidence cores from broader support regions, constructs a symmetric radius-pruned point graph, and uses a multi-source graph-geodesic partition to split support regions that contain multiple seeds. On 561 ground-truth instances across 30 validation scenes, an IoU-0.5-valid candidate exists for 67.2% of instances, and top-1 coverage is 20.0%. These complementary measurements motivate joint attention to candidate construction and ranking. TopoField achieves 28.286% AP, 41.383% AP50, and 47.558% AP25. TopoField achieved second place on the ECCV 2026 SceneFun3D Challenge.

**comment/C1：** Technical Report for ECCV OpenSUN3D Workshop SceneFun3D Benchmark Challenge

</details>

<a id="paper-140"></a>

### 140. [Vision-Language-Action or World-Action? Just Action Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37219v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37219

**作者：** Yonghao Yu、Lang Huang、Runyi Li、Toshihiko Yamasaki

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37219v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37219v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把 VLA 与 WAM 之争拆解为骨干初始化、未来观测上的训练目标、推理期是否保留该机制三项，提出统一框架 JAM：预训练视觉骨干加动作专家，以动作损失与 foresight loss 训练，让当前观测特征预测记录未来的冻结编码，预测头训练后丢弃，训练与推理均不做视觉生成。

**相关性简评：** 「预测未来冻结编码、随后丢弃预测头」是一种推理零额外开销的未来状态监督线索，可能可供核查 LVR 中轨迹/前瞻目标训练与推理期成本分配的关系；但该文属机器人控制，与 VLM 推理的联系尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Vision-Language-Action or World-Action? Just Action Models

**abstract/A1：** The vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action model (WAM) communities debate whether control policies should inherit pretrained knowledge from vision-language models or from visual generators. The debate, however, bundles three separable choices: backbone initialization, the objective applied to future observations, and whether the machinery for that future loss is kept at inference. A gap between a VLA and a WAM therefore cannot simply be attributed to any one of them. We show that decoupling these choices yields a common framework for both families, which we term Just Action Model (JAM): a pretrained visual backbone with an action expert, trained with an action loss and a foresight loss. The foresight loss trains current-observation features to predict frozen encodings of the recorded future through a prediction head discarded after training, so no visual generation is involved in either training or inference, and the backbone can be chosen freely. We first compare objectives within fixed policy architectures, then evaluate the foresight recipe across backbone families. The resulting JAM policies outperform their matched FastWAM and ImageWAM counterparts on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, and achieve similar or slightly higher success rates on RoboTwin. Compared with FastWAM, JAM improves the success rates from 97.0%/55.3%/91.2% to 98.2%/64.5%/91.4%, respectively. With cached foresight targets, JAM reduces estimated online training FLOPs per sample per update by 73% relative to FastWAM. Our results show that future supervision improves control without visual generation or additional inference machinery, and strong foresight performance is attainable with either generator-initialized or vision-language backbones. Project Page: https://y0uroy.github.io/JAM

</details>

<a id="paper-141"></a>

### 141. [GraphRAGProv: GraphRAG Meets How-Provenance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37237v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37237

**作者：** Wadie El Amrani、Luis Galárraga

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DB

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37237v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37237v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 GraphRAGProv，在带来源标注的 RDF 图上执行 SPARQL 查询并计算答案的 how-provenance 多项式，记录使用了哪些语句出现及其组合方式，并在两个多跳 QA 基准的 400 个案例中展示抽取与查询错误。

**相关性简评：** 其「记录哪些证据被使用、如何组合」的 how-provenance 形式化，可能为 LVR 中的证据筛选与贡献分配提供可核查的追溯线索；但该工作无视觉输入与 latent 状态，迁移需较多改造。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GraphRAGProv: GraphRAG Meets How-Provenance

**abstract/A1：** GraphRAG systems often use knowledge graphs to retrieve relevant context for an LLM that produces the final answer. That makes answers more inscrutable and therefore less appealing for domains where transparency is paramount. We present GraphRAGProv, an architecture that executes SPARQL queries over source-grounded RDF graphs to compute answers and their how-provenance polynomials. These polynomials record which statement occurrences were used and how they were combined. Because each occurrence is linked to its source text, users can follow these links to check whether the extracted statements reflect the text and whether the query combines them to answer the question. Querying thereby becomes a way to inspect and evaluate the text-to-graph transformation. Our interactive demo examines 400 cases from two multi-hop QA benchmarks, revealing extraction and query errors even when the final answer is correct.

</details>

<a id="paper-142"></a>

### 142. [Your Policy Is Secretly a Harness: The Decision Geometry of Agent Interfaces](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37255v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37255

**作者：** Jian Mu、Ruibo Shen、Yao Shu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37255v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37255v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在KL正则化控制下分析agent harness（提示、工具与控制逻辑）如何塑造策略偏好：最优策略的对数几率反映harness对各动作的偏好，部署回报可分解为harness上限减去策略错配，并在MiniWoB++与Terminal-Bench Lite上验证该分解能预测接口改动与再训练带来的收益。

**相关性简评：** 其「上限减错配」分解可能可供核查用于区分推理脚手架/接口提供的收益与模型自身决策质量，对评估THINK/LOOK/STOP等控制逻辑及固定模型比较的混淆有方法借鉴，但不涉及视觉或多模态latent状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Your Policy Is Secretly a Harness: The Decision Geometry of Agent Interfaces

**abstract/A1：** Language model agents are post-trained and deployed through a harness, the prompts, tools, and control logic that present a task to the model and execute its outputs. Harnesses are often revised and judged by how an already trained model performs under them, which assumes that what the model learned under one harness carries over to the next. We ask what a policy learns from its harness and what survives when the harness changes. Under KL-regularized control, we show that the log odds of an optimal policy recover how strongly its harness favors each action over the others, so a trained policy carries the preferences of its harness. Deployed return then equals the ceiling of the harness, the best return it offers, minus the mismatch of the policy, the return lost to its own suboptimal decisions. Training changes only the mismatch, while changing the harness moves both terms. Comparing harnesses with a fixed model therefore mixes what each harness offers with how well that model uses it. In MiniWoB++, post-training makes a model's preferences specific to its training harness. Reversing the order of the observation fields leaves every optimal decision unchanged yet lowers a trained model's success from 85.0% to 19.2%. Conversely, every new harness that a fixed-model comparison rejects but that raises the ceiling outperforms the original agent after further training under it, and ceiling and mismatch measured beforehand predict these gains. For coding-agent harnesses on Terminal-Bench Lite, ceiling minus mismatch estimated with a stronger model as a stand-in for the optimum reproduces the accuracy ranking, while either term alone does not. Code is available at https://github.com/2020-qqtcg/HRP.

</details>

<a id="paper-143"></a>

### 143. [GradCO: Behavior-Aware Gradient-Conflict Offloading for Text-Based Person Anomaly Search](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37267v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37267

**作者：** Zhiheng Fu、Zixu Li、Zhiwei Chen、Jiale Huang、Fangxu Liu、Jinhe Bi、Yupeng Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37267v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37267v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 GradCO，用于基于文本描述的行人异常检索：用行为感知 MoE 分设外观、正常与异常行为三路专家，以梯度冲突卸载锚点缓解细粒度对齐中的冲突梯度，并用查询条件加权的分层意图对齐聚合专家相似度。

**相关性简评：** 任务是检索而非视觉推理，但「强外观线索掩盖弱行为证据」的现象和查询条件加权的多流聚合、冲突卸载训练，可能可供核查如何在 VLM 推理中平衡显著视觉细节与任务关键弱证据、并在多目标监督间分配梯度。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GradCO: Behavior-Aware Gradient-Conflict Offloading for Text-Based Person Anomaly Search

**abstract/A1：** Text-based Person Anomaly Search (TPAS) retrieves a target pedestrian using natural-language descriptions of appearance and normal or anomalous behavior. Unlike conventional text-based person retrieval, TPAS must preserve identity cues while distinguishing fine-grained behaviors. It presents two challenges: same-identity, different-behavior hard negatives cause gradient conflict during granular alignment, while strong appearance cues can obscure weak behavioral evidence during aggregation. We propose GradCO (behavior-aware Gradient-Conflict Offloading), comprising three complementary components. Behavior-Aware Mixture-of-Experts (B-MoE) forms appearance, normal-behavior, and anomalous-behavior streams whose roles are learned from retrieval objectives without expert-specific semantic labels. Conflict Offload Learning (COL) uses pair-conditioned offload anchors in similarity computation to reduce the conflicting-gradient burden on the main text representations. Hierarchical Intent Alignment (HIA) aggregates expert similarities and features using query-conditioned weights. Experiments on five TPAS/TIPR benchmarks show GradCO significantly surpasses SOTA methods and validate our design.

</details>

<a id="paper-144"></a>

### 144. [Where Should the Evaluation Budget Go in LLM-Driven Agent Scaffold Evolution?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37276v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37276

**作者：** Sichun Luo、Yi Huang、Mukai Li、Haochen Luo、Guanzhi Deng、Haibo Wang、Jian Xu、Zefa Hu、Junlan Feng、Qi Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.NE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37276v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37276v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究 LLM 驱动的 agent scaffold 演化中评估预算如何在「搜索反馈」与「最终选择」两种角色间分配，发现稀疏反馈足以维持有效演化、额外定向评估能改善最终选型，据此提出结合稀疏搜索评估与定向确认的分配框架，在多数设置下以约一半评估量超过全量评估。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理本身无直接联系，但其在搜索与最终选择间分配评估预算、共享信息核心与 paired successive halving 复用观测的做法，可能为 LVR 中 latent 轨迹搜索、候选验证与评估成本分配提供可供核查的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Where Should the Evaluation Budget Go in LLM-Driven Agent Scaffold Evolution?

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM)-driven agent scaffold evolution improves agent workflows through iterative candidate generation and evaluation. Evaluation both guides subsequent search and determines the final scaffold, but evaluating every candidate on the full task set incurs substantial execution costs. We investigate how evaluation budgets can be allocated across these two roles. Our experiments show that sparse feedback can sustain productive evolution, while additional targeted evaluation improves final selection. Replay analyses further show that shared informative items and a common scoring reference support comparisons among closely matched candidates. Motivated by these findings, we propose \\method, an evaluation allocation framework that combines sparse search-time feedback with targeted final confirmation. A short fully evaluated warm-up establishes a shared informative core and calibrates a response model whose item parameters remain fixed during search. After search, \\method refits the model and applies paired successive halving, reusing accumulated observations and executing only missing evaluations. Across four benchmarks and two evolution frameworks, \\method achieves higher mean performance than full evaluation in most settings while using approximately half as many evaluation cells.

</details>

<a id="paper-145"></a>

### 145. [RPSD: Retrieval-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation with Optimal Transport](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37281v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37281

**作者：** Dongyue Li、Zebang Cheng、Zhongyi Deng、Hongboxu、Yi Bin、Fei Ma

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37281v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37281v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文针对 LLM 生成式序列推荐中 GRPO 的序列级奖励共享与单用户监督缺陷，提出 RPSD：用带特权信息的教师重评学生 on-policy 回答，以 token 级置信差把序列优势重塑为细粒度 credit，并用时序感知最优传输从相似用户轨迹中筛选互补证据。

**相关性简评：** 其「教师特权证据 + token 级 credit 重分配」可能为 LVR 的轨迹贡献分配与视觉证据筛选提供可核查的训练监督线索，但任务为纯文本推荐，未涉及视觉状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RPSD: Retrieval-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation with Optimal Transport

**abstract/A1：** Sequential recommendation predicts a user's next interaction from historical behavior. Recent LLM-based methods recast this task as generative recommendation and increasingly adopt reinforcement learning such as GRPO for post-training. However, these methods suffer from two limitations, namely credit-blind optimization, where a sequence-level reward is shared across all generated tokens, and collaborative-blind supervision, where each training signal is derived from a single user and ignores behavioral patterns shared across similar users. To address both issues, we propose Retrieval-Privileged on-policy Self-Distillation (RPSD). For credit-blind optimization, we introduce on-policy self-distillation (OPSD), in which a teacher with privileged knowledge re-evaluates the student's on-policy response, and the token-level confidence difference between the student and teacher reshapes the shared sequence-level advantage into token-specific credit. However, the privileged knowledge in vanilla OPSD is still derived from a single user, leaving collaborative-blind supervision unresolved. We therefore ground the teacher in collaborative behavioral evidence from similar users. Specifically, we use temporally aware optimal transport (OT) to compare interaction trajectories across users by jointly considering item similarity and temporal structure, and further apply diversity-aware selection to retain complementary trajectories as privileged evidence. In this way, on-policy self-distillation provides fine-grained token-level credit, while OT enriches the teacher with structured cross-user evidence. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to bring on-policy self-distillation to sequential recommendation and to use cross-user behavior as its privileged signal. Extensive experiments across multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that RPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-146"></a>

### 146. [Taming Noise Initialization for Zero-shot Video Restoration with Image Diffusion Priors](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37290v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37290

**作者：** Chong Wang、Zixuan Fu、Hao Cheng、Siyuan Yang、Alex C. Kot、Bihan Wen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37290v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37290v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究零样本视频复原中扩散噪声初始化的设计：用 DDIM inversion 从退化视频帧直接生成初始噪声以保留时序相关性，再通过自适应噪声混合抑制退化模式并逐步逼近高斯分布，并引入测量引导的轨迹注意力增强跨帧特征对齐。

**相关性简评：** 其「由观测反演得到初始状态、再逐步混合回先验」的机制，与 LVR 中如何在 latent 初态注入视觉证据并及时衰减退化信息、以及跨帧证据对齐，可能构成可核查的对应线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Taming Noise Initialization for Zero-shot Video Restoration with Image Diffusion Priors

**abstract/A1：** Recent advances in diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in restoring high-quality images in a zero-shot manner. Unfortunately, extending this success to video restoration is challenging due to the substantial computational resources required to pre-train video diffusion models. A rising trend seeks to address video restoration by utilizing a pre-trained image diffusion model, initiating restoration from either frame-wise independent or identical Gaussian noise. Yet, these approaches often fall short, as such simple noise initiations fail to capture and utilize the inherent temporal correlations within degraded video frames. In this work, we propose taming noise initialization for zero-shot video restoration, which preserves the intrinsic temporal correlations by generating initial noises directly using DDIM inversion on the corrupted video frames themselves. Building on this, we introduce an adaptive noise blending strategy that suppresses degradation patterns encoded during DDIM inversion while gradually steering the noise toward a Gaussian distribution. To further emphasize temporal consistency, we propose measurement-guided trajectory attention to better align features across frames. Extensive experiments on multiple video restoration tasks demonstrate that our method outperforms existing zero-shot approaches, consistently achieving superior results on the DAVIS, REDS4, and Set8 datasets.

**comment/C1：** IEEE Transactions on Multimedia

</details>

<a id="paper-147"></a>

### 147. [Structured Representation Probing for Risk Anticipation in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37299v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37299

**作者：** Jiawei Liang、Tianrui Lou、Le Yang、Xianghao Jiao、Wei Wang、Xiaochun Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37299v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37299v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 SRP，一个轻量探针：从冻结的端到端驾驶策略中分别读取 actor、ego、map 表示，为每个来源学习线性风险方向，并以基数归一化的 log-mean-exp 池化聚合元素分数，结合事件窗口监督来预测即将发生的硬失败，在 Bench2Drive 上取得高于运行时基线的帧级 PR-AUC/ROC-AUC。

**相关性简评：** 其在不改动策略的前提下从内部状态读出风险、并用显著性敏感且弱化 token 数的池化聚合证据，可为 LVR 中 latent 内容是否被实际使用、视觉证据筛选与停止决策的探针式诊断提供方法线索，跨模态迁移可行性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Structured Representation Probing for Risk Anticipation in End-to-End Autonomous Driving

**abstract/A1：** End-to-end driving policies form structured internal representations of traffic, ego behavior, and road constraints. These representations offer a monitoring interface for anticipating failures, complementing risk signals from trajectories, controls, and manually designed features. We introduce the Structured Representation Probe (SRP), a lightweight monitor that reads actor, ego, and map representations from a frozen policy. SRP learns a separate linear risk direction for each source and aggregates element scores through cardinality-normalized log-mean-exp pooling, retaining sensitivity to salient evidence while reducing dependence on token count. Event-window supervision links the readout to upcoming hard failures, and auxiliary event type heads jointly train the source directions across failure modes. Online alerting uses a single generic score without modifying the driving policy. Bench2Drive experiments on DriveTransformer, UniAD, and VAD show higher frame-level PR-AUC and ROC-AUC than the evaluated runtime baselines. On DriveTransformer at a horizon of 2 seconds, ROC-AUC reaches 0.833 versus 0.715 for temporal consistency, with 2.67% readout runtime overhead. The trained probes also retain the ability to distinguish impending failures on new routes.

</details>

<a id="paper-148"></a>

### 148. [ReForest: Interpretability Meets Reasoning in Isolation Forest](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37316v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37316

**作者：** Juyoung Seo、Geonwoo Bang、Dongho Kim、Moohong Min

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37316v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37316v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ReForest，把 Isolation Forest 的潜在决策路径转写为自然语言解释，再由 LLM 依据不确定性感知的动态集成细化异常检测，在四个真实数据集上提升召回与 F1。

**相关性简评：** 「潜在决策路径→语言解释」与按实例不确定性加权统计/语义信号的机制，可供核查 LVR 中 latent 内容的可解释读出与视觉—语言信号的动态门控，但该工作属表格异常检测，与视觉多模态推理关联较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReForest: Interpretability Meets Reasoning in Isolation Forest

**abstract/A1：** Statistical anomaly detection methods like Isolation Forest are efficient but suffer from a critical semantic gap. They identify numerical rarities but fail to understand the contextual meaning of data, often missing subtle, context-dependent anomalies. Conversely, Large Language Models possess strong semantic reasoning capabilities but are prohibitively expensive and slow for large-scale deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose ReForest, a novel framework that synergizes the statistical efficiency of Isolation Forest with the reasoning power of LLMs. ReForest transforms the latent decision paths of Isolation Forest into natural-language explanations, enabling an LLM to refine anomaly detection through an uncertainty-aware dynamic ensemble. This approach allows the model to adaptively weigh statistical and semantic signals based on uncertainty-driven instance-level confidence. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets (Healthcare, Finance) demonstrate that ReForest significantly outperforms baselines, achieving an average 21% improvement in Recall and a 10% gain in F1-score. Our framework not only improves detection performance but also enhances model transparency by grounding semantic narratives in statistical evidence, providing interpretable rationales essential for high-stakes decision-making.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 2 figures. Published in PAKDD 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-149"></a>

### 149. [TRIDENT-Seq: Profile-Matched Prefix-Future Contrast for Label-Efficient Payment Risk Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37365v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37365

**作者：** Weiyu Han、Haoyu Jiang、Jie Sheng、Ruobing Zuo、Huilin He、Ze Zhao、Xiao Tan、Nairong Zhang、Shuai Chen、Tianyi Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37365v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37365v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TRIDENT-Seq 交易基础模型，用「前缀—未来」对比预训练（PFC）把交易历史前缀与后续行为摘要对齐，并以活跃度画像匹配的候选、活跃度预测与离散度正则抑制过易的对比信号，在支付风险建模上验证标签效率与跨业务迁移。

**相关性简评：** 其用画像匹配候选避免粗粒度特征走捷径、并用离散度正则防止表示坍缩、以未来行为摘要监督前缀表示，可能为 LVR 中视觉旁路/信息坍缩的诊断与 latent 轨迹训练提供可核查线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TRIDENT-Seq: Profile-Matched Prefix-Future Contrast for Label-Efficient Payment Risk Modeling

**abstract/A1：** Ant International accumulates large volumes of transaction history across users and payment businesses. Confirmed fraud and chargeback outcomes, by contrast, are scarce, delayed, and specific to each business. A single encoder pretrained on unlabeled histories could serve all businesses, but only if pretraining captures the behavior that separates risk. Coarse activity differences can make contrastive pretraining on transaction logs too easy: transaction volume, amount, and timing can distinguish a history's continuation from arbitrary candidates without modeling how payment resources are used. We present TRIDENT-Seq, a transaction foundation model trained with Prefix-Future Contrast (PFC). PFC matches each prefix to a summary of its subsequent behavior among candidates with similar activity profiles; activity prediction and dispersion regularization act on the encoder output. Across three production payment businesses, the pretrained encoder improves average precision (AP) and recall at a 0.5% intervention budget over architecture-matched scratch training in all six business-budget settings. With a fixed validation set, it exceeds full-label scratch training in mean AP and recall using 10% of the training labels on Wallet and 25% on Shopping and SME. Pretraining only on another business's histories still helps. Replacing profile-matched candidates with random ones cuts recall from 24.62% to 5.64%. In Ant International's deployment evaluation, recorded amount-based chargeback and overall loss rates are 37.7% and 37.9% lower than those of the previous XGBoost-based system under the same intervention budget.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 5 figures

</details>
