### 150. [Agent-Conditioned Routing: Diverse Reasoning Paths Within One Frozen Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37375v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37375

**作者：** Ananth Kalyanasundaram、Ankush Kadu、Athira Gopal、Aswanth Krishnan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37375v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37375v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 Agent-Conditioned Routing，在冻结的稀疏 MoE 模型上为 m 条并行轨迹分配未训练的共享/私有专家可用性掩码，在保留输入相关 token 路由的同时提升同一问题下解法多样性，并在数学物理多方法基准与延迟、显存开销上做了评测。

**相关性简评：** 它并非视觉工作，但「不改参数、仅用路由掩码制造多条并行推理轨迹」可供核查 LVR 中并行 latent 轨迹易塌缩为单一模式的问题，以及轨迹多样性与正确率的权衡、掩码状态的成本。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Agent-Conditioned Routing: Diverse Reasoning Paths Within One Frozen Model

**abstract/A1：** Generating several solutions can expose alternative methods, but additional trajectories often paraphrase one dominant approach. We investigate whether the conditional capacity of a frozen sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) model can provide such diversity from one problem prompt, without user-assigned roles, prescribed methods, fine-tuning, or model copies. Agent-Conditioned Routing (ACR) assigns $m$ parallel trajectories persistent, untrained shared/private expert-eligibility masks while preserving the learned, input-dependent token router. On a screened 126-question multi-mode mathematics and physics benchmark, with model judges reading the reported derivations in full, we count the number of problems yielding at least two distinct primary methods among soft-correct solutions in four trajectories. ACR reaches approximately twice as many such problems as the strongest temperature setting under GLM 5.3 (37.3 versus 18.7), and 1.67 times as many under GPT-5.6 Sol (45.00 versus 27.00), at matched output budgets. A blinded four-expert audit on a stratified sample of 30 problems also finds more multi-method solutions with ACR on both backbones, with lower correctness. Matched-eligibility ablations show modest multi-method count advantages for ACR, while token-resampled masks attain higher correctness. An informed sequential prompt elicits more diversity still, using previous solutions and explicit requests for alternative methods. ACR adds about 160 KiB of mask state. In a three-problem microbenchmark capped at 1,024 tokens, its latency and peak VRAM usage remain within 3% of batched temperature sampling.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 4 figures, 11 tables. Extended version of a paper accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Resource-Aware Agentic AI (non-archival)

</details>

<a id="paper-151"></a>

### 151. [Clearing the FoG: Training KG Agents to Retrieve the Evidence Behind Their Answers](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37405v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37405

**作者：** Zongyuan Sun、Shuai Zhao、Yingjun Du、Ke Shu、Ziyue Zhu、Haoran Luo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37405v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37405v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FoG 框架，结合证据锚定的合成数据、KG 交互智能体与证据覆盖过程奖励（ECPR），训练 KG 智能体在给出答案的同时检索出支撑三元组，并在三个 KGQA 基准上评测。

**相关性简评：** 其「答案正确但未必有证据支撑」的诊断，以及按首次检索到某三元组的轮次分配奖励的做法，可供核查 LVR 中如何监督 latent 步骤真正使用视觉证据、避免只按答案正确给奖励；但对象是文本 KG 的显式检索，迁移到视觉 latent 状态可能需较多改造。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Clearing the FoG: Training KG Agents to Retrieve the Evidence Behind Their Answers

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) can answer many factual questions correctly, but a correct answer is not necessarily a supported one. For an LLM agent, a knowledge graph (KG) plays two roles: as a source of answers, it supplies facts that the model lacks; as a source of evidence, it supplies the supporting triples that make an answer traceable and auditable. However, existing KG agents treat the KG only as a source of answers: their training data carries answers but not evidence, and their training rewards check the answer but not the retrieved evidence, so agents often answer correctly without retrieving the supporting triples. We propose FoG, a framework that trains KG agents to retrieve the evidence behind their answers. FoG consists of evidence-grounded synthesis, a KG interaction agent, and an evidence-coverage process reward (ECPR): the synthesis recursively hides explicit entities behind verifiable sub-questions, so that every question carries its supporting triples by construction; the agent records the triples returned by the KG; and ECPR credits each supporting triple to the turn that first retrieves it. Experiments on three KGQA benchmarks demonstrate that FoG enables 3B--4B open-source agents to outperform substantially larger baselines in answer accuracy and evidence coverage, with zero-shot cross-KG transfer.

</details>

<a id="paper-152"></a>

### 152. [Displacement Moments and Path-Terminal Separation in Brownian Interpolant Sampling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37413v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37413

**作者：** Zhong Han

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.PR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37413v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37413v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 Brownian stochastic interpolant 的采样误差，在仅有二阶位移矩甚至无矩的条件下给出 Euler 采样早停的 KL 保证，并通过路径分解区分原始桥路径与 Markov 投影路径的误差阶，还构造了基于独立同分布端点对的 current-state 学习器。

**相关性简评：** 其「Markov 投影丢弃源信息」的路径 KL 分解可供核查 latent 推理状态更新中的信息坍缩，迭代采样相对廉价一步预测「无优势」的图像对照也可能类比多步 latent 推理的收益与成本评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Displacement Moments and Path-Terminal Separation in Brownian Interpolant Sampling

**abstract/A1：** Brownian stochastic interpolants connect paired endpoint samples, yet their sampling error can depend on displacement moments even when neither endpoint has a first moment. For arbitrary Borel couplings, we prove early-stopped, target-to-output KL guarantees for standard Euler sampling from the true source. A fourth displacement moment gives an explicit $O(N^{-1})$ bound; a second moment suffices for fixed-law convergence as drift error vanishes. For one fixed coupling with no displacement moment above two, original bridge-path KL stays of order one, comparison-path KL is of order $1/\\log N$, and terminal KL is at most $C/N^2$ on the same grid. The path decomposition identifies the information about the source discarded by Markov projection. When the original bridge is Markov, current-state Euler sampling instead has a second-moment $O((M\_2+d)/N)$ bound; finite-entropy Schroedinger bridges for the probability Brownian reference are included. We also construct a current-state learner with expected output KL consistency from iid endpoint pairs. Numerical diagnostics illustrate the distinct errors. Conditional diffusion identities specify a restoration interface, while image controls find no iterative advantage over cheap probabilistic predictors. Uniform terminal rates for general second-moment couplings remain open.

**comment/C1：** 56 pages, 4 figures; 8-page main text and complete appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-153"></a>

### 153. [Bayes-Adaptive MDPs for Rollout Allocation to Enhance GRPO Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37429v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37429

**作者：** Faeze Moradi Kalarde、Armin Toroghi、Nam Ho Koh、Doo Hwan Hwang、Seungjin Kim、Kee-Eung Kim、Scott Sanner

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37429v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37429v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文将 GRPO 中按题目难度分配 rollout 预算建模为序贯决策问题，用 Bayes-adaptive MDP 离线学习自适应分配策略，在固定总预算下提升 LLM 推理训练效果。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本训练侧工作，但其「按难度动态分配采样预算」的 BA-MDP 策略可供核查是否能迁移到 VLM 推理中 THINK/STOP 的计算分配与轨迹采样成本控制。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bayes-Adaptive MDPs for Rollout Allocation to Enhance GRPO Training

**abstract/A1：** Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has demonstrated strong performance in LLM post-training. However, it allocates a uniform rollout budget to every training question, ignoring variations in question difficulty. As a result, highly difficult questions may receive too few rollouts to generate informative positive reward signals, limiting their contribution to training, while moderately difficult questions often receive more rollouts than necessary. In this work, we formulate the problem of difficulty-aware rollout budget allocation to maximize training utility under the same total rollout budget as standard GRPO. We propose AdaRoll-GRPO, which reformulates rollout allocation as a sequential decision-making problem and leverages a Bayes-adaptive Markov decision process (MDP) to learn an adaptive allocation policy. The learned policy is computed offline and applied during GRPO training without introducing additional online optimization overhead. Extensive experiments on multiple reasoning datasets and LLM architectures demonstrate that AdaRoll-GRPO consistently improves GRPO training performance under a fixed rollout budget, highlighting the effectiveness of adaptive rollout allocation for GRPO in LLM post-training.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 8 Figures

</details>

<a id="paper-154"></a>

### 154. [An Input Skip Can Reparameterize a VAE Without Improving the Generator](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37456v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37456

**作者：** Kevin Lee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、physics.ao-ph、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37456v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37456v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出高斯 VAE 的常数输入 skip 可被等价重参数化：无 skip 模型可写成解码输出乘 b=1-α、噪声方差乘 b² 的 skip 形式，观测与 latent 分布及精确生成分数不变，但重构 MSE 乘 b²；并在已知密度的合成实验与七条 DART 记录上比较 energy score 与重要性采样估计的负对数密度。

**相关性简评：** 「重参数化只改重构误差而不改生成分布、评价生成不能只看重构误差」这一线索，可供核查 LVR 中用重构或探测指标推断 latent 内容是否被实际使用的做法是否可靠；但原文未涉及多模态推理与推理期状态生成，联系属方法层面。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** An Input Skip Can Reparameterize a VAE Without Improving the Generator

**abstract/A1：** An input skip can reduce reconstruction error while leaving a model's distribution of new samples unchanged. For a Gaussian variational autoencoder (VAE) with constant input skip $0\\leqα&lt;1$, a model without a skip can be written in skip form by multiplying its decoder output by $b=1-α$ and its noise variance by $b^2$. The distribution of observations and latent variables and its exact generative scores stay the same, while reconstruction mean squared error (MSE) is multiplied by $b^2$. Separate training can produce different generators because optimization depends on the parameter coordinates. In a Gaussian experiment with known density, changing skip and observation variance together reduces MSE but worsens energy score and the estimate of negative log density from importance sampling. On seven annual Deep-ocean Assessment and Reporting of Tsunamis (DART) records, energy score differences at the same observation variance have both signs on four original records and favor the skip on three records from 2018. Probabilistic principal component analysis (PPCA) has lower mean energy scores than every neural DART fit. With the encoder and decoder kept the same, estimating observation variance from training reconstruction error can also worsen energy score. Assessing generation requires more than reconstruction error.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 Principles of Generative Modeling (PriGM) Workshop. Available at: https://github.com/magnaprog/vae-skip-reparam

</details>

<a id="paper-155"></a>

### 155. [Follow the Gradient: Tracing Safety Degradation Back to Training Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37481v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37481

**作者：** Saket Reddy、Amro Abbas、Anshuman Suri、Ari Morcos、David Schwab、Pratyush Maini

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37481v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37481v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SimpleLoRIF，一种可扩展到百万级样本的梯度嵌入方法：通过简化低秩归因并将反向传播限制在最后四个 transformer 块，识别与不安全行为梯度对齐的微调样本，并据此构建去除高风险 20% 数据的 SafeTulu，以降低 GCG 攻击成功率。

**相关性简评：** 其梯度相似度与数据归因思路可能为 LVR 中的训练样本贡献分配与监督数据筛选提供线索，例如判定哪类推理轨迹真正塑造 latent 推理行为；但对象为纯文本安全微调，与视觉状态生成无直接联系，需核查其梯度嵌入相似度是否受层深与位置影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Follow the Gradient: Tracing Safety Degradation Back to Training Data

**abstract/A1：** Fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) on instruction data can quietly erode their safety, even when no training example looks harmful. Because this safety-degrading data looks benign, standard content filters struggle to catch it. We tackle this problem by developing SimpleLoRIF, a scalable gradient embedding method that identifies fine-tuning examples whose gradients align with unsafe behavior. By simplifying a low-rank attribution method and restricting backpropagation to the final four transformer blocks, SimpleLoRIF can embed datasets on the order of a million examples, boosting gradient attribution from a small-scale analysis tool into a practical filter for full post-training datasets. We first validate it on emergent misalignment, where we find that gradients can connect insecure-code training data to the broadly harmful behaviors that appear after fine-tuning, with AUC as high as 0.865 in the gradient space of misaligned models. We then use SimpleLoRIF to build SafeTulu, a version of Tulu 3 with the 20% of examples most gradient-aligned with unsafe responses removed. Training on SafeTulu reduces GCG attack success rate by up to 22% across Qwen, Llama, and Olmo, outperforming both LLM-as-a-judge and prior gradient-based filtering, while largely preserving or even improving capability. Finally, we analyzed the removed data and identify possibly under-studied sources of safety degradation, such as tool-execution failures and narrative arithmetic. We release SafeTulu along with a Hugging Face Space that lets researchers build their own customized versions of SafeTulu to fine-tune on, such as by changing the removal fraction or filtering only certain subsets.

</details>

<a id="paper-156"></a>

### 156. [From Interaction to Capability: Task Advancement as State and Supervision for GUI Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37482v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37482

**作者：** Ziyang Chen、Qian Li、Chengwei Qin

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37482v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37482v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文面向长时程 GUI 智能体提出 Task Advancement Memory：执行期用「任务推进状态」表示已验证进度如何改变剩余目标，任务结束后通过推进监督抽取可复用的局部操作，并保留成功与失败任务中的有效交互，在 MobileWorld 等基准上报告了成功率提升与 token 下降。

**相关性简评：** 其「已验证进度」状态更新与从交互中抽象可复用操作的监督方式，可能为 LVR 中的状态维护、视觉证据筛选与贡献分配提供可核查的借鉴线索，但论文本身未涉及 latent 推理或内部表示分析。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Interaction to Capability: Task Advancement as State and Supervision for GUI Agents

**abstract/A1：** GUI memory should preserve the results of past actions and learn reusable operations. This is difficult in long-horizon workflows, where agents must use information across applications and useful operations are interleaved with failed attempts. We introduce Task Advancement Memory (TAM), which defines task advancement as verified task progress, and supports ongoing task execution and learns reusable operations. During execution, Task Advancement State represents how verified task progress changes the remaining goals, allowing the agent to build on earlier achievements across applications. After each task, advancement supervision identifies and abstracts the interactions that produced a verified local result. The learned operations preserve the relation between required inputs, expected outcomes, and verification conditions. This retains locally successful operations from both successful and failed tasks. On MobileWorld GUI-only tasks, Advancement State alone raises success from 12.0% to 28.2%. Full TAM achieves a 17.6% relative gain in task success over the strongest evaluated baseline and reduces lifecycle tokens per solved task by 38.7%. On SPA-Bench, TAM achieves state-of-the-art task success in the single-app English Level 3 setting.

</details>

<a id="paper-157"></a>

### 157. [ALADDIN: Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37503v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37503

**作者：** Ivan Shchekotov、Timofei Gritsaev、Viacheslav Pirogov、Dmitry Vetrov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37503v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37503v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ALADDIN，以预训练多步 flow-matching 人脸交换模型初始化生成器，通过对抗训练与 triplet 伪标签监督蒸馏出一步生成器，实现身份迁移与目标属性保留的可控权衡；并指出标准评测偏向与打分共用同一身份编码器的方法。

**相关性简评：** 把多步生成轨迹蒸馏为一步、以对抗信号替代逐步监督的训练思路，可能为 latent 推理的步数压缩与轨迹监督提供可核查线索；其对「训练与评测共用同一编码器」的偏差观察，也可供自查 latent 相似度指标的构造。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ALADDIN: Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping

**abstract/A1：** Face swapping transfers a source identity onto a target image while preserving the target's pose, expression, hair, and lighting. The most realistic current methods are diffusion-based, but they need many network evaluations per image. GAN-based methods are fast, yet trained from scratch they must learn realism from the adversarial signal alone and produce visibly weaker results. We present ALADDIN (Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping), a one-step face swapper obtained by initialising the generator from a pre-trained multi-step flow-matching model and training it adversarially under identity and target-preservation constraints. We expose a controllable trade-off between identity transfer and target-attribute preservation by training a student model with triplet pseudo-label supervision. We additionally identify a bias in the standard evaluation protocol, which favours methods trained against the same identity encoder used to score them, and re-evaluate all baselines with an independent one. ALADDIN is state-of-the-art among one-step methods in realism and target preservation at comparable identity transfer, and matches the best multi-step model with 28 times fewer network evaluations and an order of magnitude fewer training pairs.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027. 15 pages, 5 figures, 4 tables, including references and appendices

</details>

<a id="paper-158"></a>

### 158. [Orthogonal and Parametrized Multi-Attribute Steering of LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37517v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37517

**作者：** Remy Ogasawara、Cullen Anderson、Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer、Foad Namjoo、Amir Abdullah、Jens Kristian Refsgaard Schou、Jeff M. Phillips

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37517v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37517v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究 LLM 的激活引导（activation steering）：先为每个引导向量定位最可引导区间并重标定强度范围，提出「参数化引导」直接把激活置为目标特质强度，再用正交化方法解耦多特质向量以减少相互干扰。

**相关性简评：** 其「把内部状态设定到指定强度」与多方向正交解耦，可能为 LVR 中分离视觉证据与推理状态、按深度施加可控 latent 干预提供可核查的工具线索，但原文仅面向纯文本 LLM 的属性控制，未涉及多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Orthogonal and Parametrized Multi-Attribute Steering of LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Activation steering offers an effective and efficient way to modify an LLM's behavior at inference time. However, it remains unclear how to apply steering such that the signal is consistently strong and the steering strength has a universally interpretable meaning. We address this first by identifying the most steerable interval for each steering vector and rescaling the steering range accordingly. Building on this, we introduce a more interpretable method for steering, called parameterized steering which instead of pushing activations in a direction, it sets them at the desired trait strength. Next, with multi-trait steering, we find that naively combining vectors causes them to interfere with one another. In response, we develop a method to orthogonalize the vectors; it decouples these traits, allowing a model to exhibit one behavior without inducing the others, thus enabling multiple traits to be steered simultaneously.

</details>

<a id="paper-159"></a>

### 159. [GTFormer: A Syntax-Free Graph Network Alternative to Transformers for 8192$\\times$ Length Extrapolation in Linear Time](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37524v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37524

**作者：** Elisabeth Fink

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37524v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37524v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GTFormer，用无句法的分层图网络（二叉归约树、边界桥接边、随机扩展捷径、多通道门控与自顶向下广播）替代扁平自注意力，在仅用长度 ≤512 的序列训练下零样本外推到 8192 倍长度并保持线性内存。

**相关性简评：** 其分层归约与自顶向下广播构成 log 深度的多尺度状态聚合，可能为 latent 推理工作区的长度泛化与支持组合提供结构线索；但论文面向纯序列建模、无视觉输入与推理期 latent 证据，可供核查其层次结构是否对应 LVR 的表示与计算分配。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GTFormer: A Syntax-Free Graph Network Alternative to Transformers for 8192$\\times$ Length Extrapolation in Linear Time

**abstract/A1：** Self-attention mechanisms have established the Transformer as the dominant backbone across natural language processing, source code intelligence, and automated theorem proving. However, all-to-all pairwise token comparison enforces a quadratic $\\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ compute and memory barrier alongside severe softmax attention dilution as sequence length $N$ scales beyond the training horizon. Inspired by recent advances in algebraic graph learning (\\textbf{WPNet}), this work introduces \\textbf{GTFormer}, a syntax-free hierarchical Graph Neural Network (GNN) that replaces flat self-attention for general long-context sequence modeling. GTFormer organizes raw token streams into a bounded-degree hierarchy combining a balanced binary reduction tree ($\\lceil \\log\_2 N \\rceil$ depth), horizontal boundary-bridging shift edges, random expander shortcuts, multi-channel convex highway gating, and a top-down broadcast pass. Across eight long-context code, log, and multi-hop retrieval benchmarks trained exclusively on sequences of length $N \\le 512$, GTFormer extrapolates zero-shot to $N = 4{,}194{,}304$ ($8192\\times$) on a single commodity GPU in linear $\\mathcal{O}(N)$ memory, maintaining near-perfect accuracy where standard and RoPE Transformers suffer from out-of-distribution attention collapse and out-of-memory exhaustion.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-160"></a>

### 160. [GroundControl: Satellite-Conditioned Multi-View Diffusion for Reconstruction-Free Novel View Synthesis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37545v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37545

**作者：** Akshay Krishnan、Matias Turkulainen、Jamie Wynn、Zawar Qureshi、Mohamed Sayed、Guillermo Garcia-Hernando、Sara Vicente、James Hays、Michael Firman、Daniyar Turmukhambetov

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37545v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37545v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出卫星图像条件化的多视图扩散模型，用 cross-attention 把卫星视角的场景布局与少量街景输入一起注入生成过程，在无需三维重建的情况下合成新视角，并缓解外推时的空间布局幻觉。

**相关性简评：** 其「引入额外视觉证据、经 cross-attention 约束生成布局且保留预训练先验」的做法，可供核查如何向 LVR/VLM 的内部推理状态注入视觉证据以抑制布局幻觉；但原文属图像生成任务，迁移线索仍需较多验证。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GroundControl: Satellite-Conditioned Multi-View Diffusion for Reconstruction-Free Novel View Synthesis

**abstract/A1：** Multi-view or video diffusion models have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for novel-view synthesis from sparse image collections. While they can produce high quality images, they tend to hallucinate unrealistic spatial layouts when synthesizing views outside the input views (scene extrapolation). We propose to address this challenge for outdoor scenes by using satellite images as an additional information source to condition the multi-view generative model. We introduce a new satellite-conditioned multi-view diffusion model that combines conditioning from a satellite image and street-level input images when generating novel-views. Our cross-attention based conditioning allows us to retain pretrained multi-view diffusion priors to generate seamless, realistic, novel views from a handful of source views, while also adhering to the scene layout as seen from satellite images. Our model does not use 3D scene reconstruction, and only relies on posed street and satellite views. Our experiments on outdoor datasets show that our model is better than both satellite-conditioned or street-view conditioned baselines on novel-view synthesis, especially on challenging cases where target views have very little overlap with source views. Project page: https://nianticspatial.github.io/groundcontrol

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027. Project page: https://nianticspatial.github.io/groundcontrol

</details>

<a id="paper-161"></a>

### 161. [Collective Dynamics of Self-Reinforcement: How Cross-Task Coupling Drives Policy Collapse in Unsupervised RLVR](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37553v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37553

**作者：** Peize Ding

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37553v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37553v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用可解的多问题模型解释无标签 RLVR 中自增强奖励如何因跨任务共享耦合而崩溃，给出临界问题数标度，并在小 Transformer 上验证移除共享方向可阻止崩溃。

**相关性简评：** 其「共享模式放大—逐题锐化」失稳机制与训练集规模效应，可能供核查 VLM 自训练或 LVR 监督中共享表征导致答案多样性坍缩的风险；但无视觉或 latent 状态实验，迁移需较多判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Collective Dynamics of Self-Reinforcement: How Cross-Task Coupling Drives Policy Collapse in Unsupervised RLVR

**abstract/A1：** Label-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) rewards a language model for agreeing with its own majority vote. It improves reasoning at first but eventually collapses: the self-assessed reward goes to one while accuracy falls, often until the model gives the same answer to every problem. The collapse appears only when training uses many problems. We explain this dataset-size dependence as a collective instability. In a solvable many-problem model in which problems share parameters, a population whose problems prefer different answers amplifies any shared perturbation for as long as the population-averaged slope $\\langle F'\\rangle$ of the single-problem self-reinforcement drift $F$ is positive. This holds while problems are still uncertain about their answers. Per-problem sharpening, slowed by $1/N$ for $N$ training problems, races against the shared mode, which grows at a rate $\\proptoλ^2$ independent of $N$, where $λ$ is the strength of the shared coupling. This yields a critical number of problems $N\_c\\simeq\\ln(1/δ)/(λ^2G)$, where $δ$ seeds the shared mode and $G$ decreases with the initial confidence. Simulations of the model confirm the predictions: a sharp transition, $N\_c\\proptoλ^{-2}$ up to a logarithmic correction, a single critical amplification $λ^2NG$ across couplings, confidence profiles and training variants, a logarithmic dependence on batch size, and no collapse for confident initial models. A kernel version of the theory identifies a protective coupling (generalization of sharpening) and a marginal-entropy bonus that stabilizes the uniform collective mode. In label-free RL of a small Transformer, removing the shared direction prevents collapse entirely, restoring it causes collapse, and collapse grows with training-set size and base-model entropy.

</details>

<a id="paper-162"></a>

### 162. [When uncertainty quantification fails: structural vulnerabilities and corrections for selective prediction under clinical class imbalance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37561v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37561

**作者：** Walter Endrizzi、Flavio Ragni、Raffaele Marchesi、Stefano Bovo、Monica Moroni、Giuseppe Jurman、Venet Osmani

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37561v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37561v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在 56 组合成条件与三个神经退行性疾病队列（多发性硬化、帕金森、阿尔茨海默）上检验选择性预测的不确定性量化失效：严重类别不平衡下，标准熵等指标实际度量的是类别稀疏性而非临床模糊性，转为惩罚少数类；作者提出以阈值对齐度量配合 CCR，恢复类内单调性并减轻少数类被系统性排除。

**相关性简评：** 不确定性驱动的拒答/转人工与 LVR 的 STOP 决策及其成本评价结构相似，其阈值对齐度量与分层评估可供核查是否适用于衡量停止信号与风险；但材料未涉及视觉或多模态 latent 状态，迁移方向属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When uncertainty quantification fails: structural vulnerabilities and corrections for selective prediction under clinical class imbalance

**abstract/A1：** Background. Selective prediction systems improve the safety of clinical artificial intelligence by deferring ambiguous cases to human experts while automating high-confidence decisions. However, this safety mechanism relies entirely on the premise that uncertainty scores accurately reflect clinical ambiguity. In prognostic tasks characterized by severe class imbalance, where the clinical outcome of interest is a minority, this assumption breaks down. Standard uncertainty measures cease to capture clinically aligned ambiguity; instead, they become a proxy for class membership, systematically penalizing minority patients regardless of their individual predictive clarity. Results. Across 56 synthetic conditions and three neurodegenerative cohorts spanning severe class imbalance to near-balanced conditions (multiple sclerosis, Parkinson's Disease, Alzheimer's Disease), our framework consistently maintained bounded, balanced risk across trajectories. In contrast, global metrics either collapsed minority Sensitivity or protected the minority at the expense of majority patients. Applying CCR with standard entropy fails to protect the minority because it breaks within-class monotonicity when tau=0.5. Combining CCR with threshold-aware metrics restores strict monotonicity, correctly identifies ambiguous patients, and safely mitigates minority exclusion. Conclusions. Under clinical class imbalance, standard uncertainty quantification measures dataset sparsity rather than clinical ambiguity, creating a systemic illusion of safety. This bias is a correctable structural flaw. Clinical pipelines deploying selective prediction under imbalance should adopt threshold-aligned uncertainty measures and class-stratified evaluation. This reduces class-specific risk imbalance, supporting safer selective prediction under the evaluated conditions without silently excluding the most vulnerable patients.

**comment/C1：** https://github.com/endrwalter/uncertainty\_ml

</details>

<a id="paper-163"></a>

### 163. [TeamGym: A Gym for Team Decision-Making by AI Agents with Distributed Information](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37571v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37571

**作者：** Chanwoo Park、Jaywoong Jeong、Zachary Wojtowicz、Yubin Kim、Taekyun Lee、Kaiqing Zhang、Asuman Ozdaglar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37571v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37571v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TeamGym 环境，用法律、金融、医疗、软件工程语料构造团队决策任务，通过「pivotal information」（每项必要且整体充分的极小子集）控制信息分布并追踪智能体识别与整合这些信息的情况，并在 8 个模型上测出沟通与检索缺口，再用仅基于最终决策的强化学习训练策略。

**相关性简评：** 其「pivotal information」最小充分事实集与对信息整合、检索缺口的度量，可供核查 LVR 中证据筛选、视觉细节是否被决策真正使用以及仅以最终结果作奖励时的贡献分配问题，但任务为纯文本多智能体，迁移需较多判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TeamGym: A Gym for Team Decision-Making by AI Agents with Distributed Information

**abstract/A1：** Many real-world decisions require teams to integrate information distributed across participants. Yet AI agents are still primarily trained and benchmarked in isolation. We introduce TeamGym, an environment for evaluating and training language model-based agents on team decision-making problems constructed from real-world legal, finance, medical, and software engineering corpora. TeamGym introduces a method to identify a minimal set of facts, each element of which is necessary and all of which are collectively sufficient for a correct final decision, which we call pivotal information. This enables TeamGym to both control the distribution of pivotal information and track how well agents identify and integrate pivotal information when making team decisions. Examining 8 language models on 12 source corpora, we find that current AI agents often fail to effectively share information: Distributing pivotal information across participants lowers success rate by 22.9 percentage points on average relative to giving the same information to a single model (a communication gap). We also document that participants do not effectively retrieve decision-relevant information on their own: A team's success rate is lowered by a further 19.9 points when participants must identify and extract pivotal information from source material rather than receiving it directly (a retrieval gap). To help remedy these deficits, we use TeamGym to train policies through reinforcement learning on team decision loss. Across two policy-training experiments, we find that training improves final-decision quality and induces emergent coordination in participation, speaker selection, and communication, even though the reward evaluates only the final decision.

</details>

<a id="paper-164"></a>

### 164. [Zero-Shot Multimodal Event Spotting in Soccer Broadcasts](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37583v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37583

**作者：** Sadia Kamal、Mahmudul Hasan、Tony Braskich

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG、cs.MM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37583v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37583v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出零样本足球转播事件定位框架：将比赛切分为重叠片段，用紧凑的 visual storyboard 与对齐的解说文本一起输入通用多模态模型，在无任务训练下完成事件检测、分类与时间定位，并加入带自然语言解释的兴奋度排序阶段。

**相关性简评：** 其 storyboard 压缩视觉输入（报告推理成本降 72%）与解说文本联合输入，可供核查「视觉证据筛选与保真—成本权衡」及文本旁路在 LVR 中的作用，但论文不涉及 latent 推理状态本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Zero-Shot Multimodal Event Spotting in Soccer Broadcasts

**abstract/A1：** Automatic soccer event spotting supports highlight generation, video indexing, and sports analytics, but existing methods depend on large annotated datasets and soccer-specific training. We present a zero-shot framework that detects, classifies, and temporally localizes events in full-length soccer broadcasts without task-specific parameter training. Each match is segmented into overlapping clips represented by a compact visual storyboard and the aligned commentary transcript, which a general-purpose multimodal model processes jointly. On three manually annotated matches containing 130 events, the best configuration achieves 72.0% F1@5s and outperforms SoccerNet-trained CALF and NetVLAD++ in a cross-dataset setting, while the storyboard representation reduces inference cost by 72% compared with individual frames. We further analyze the effects of model family, input representation, and prompt optimization, and introduce an evidence-grounded excitement-scoring stage that ranks detected events with natural-language explanations for highlight selection.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to the Main Conference at the IEEE International Symposium on Multimedia: ISM 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-165"></a>

### 165. [EnvSched: Environment-Aware Scheduling for Rollout-Efficient Agent Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37609v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37609

**作者：** Sihan Yang、Weichen Bai、Ruichuan An、Yiming Cheng、Zhiyuan Feng、Tianyi Jiang、Renye Yan、Ning Ding、Yifan Zhang、Renrui Zhang、Yehui Tang、Wentao Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37609v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37609v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 EnvSched：把环境—任务对组织成双关系图，沿环境内语义/工具/验证器边与环境间执行—奖励拓扑边传播历史 rollout 统计以预测任务内奖励方差，再结合覆盖度、配额与相似度惩罚做在线批次调度，在有限 rollout 预算下提升 agent RL 效率。

**相关性简评：** 用历史 rollout 统计预测奖励方差来分配采样预算的调度机制，可能可供核查用于 LVR 轨迹训练中的样本选择与 latent 计算预算分配；但论文不涉及视觉推理，联系偏间接。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EnvSched: Environment-Aware Scheduling for Rollout-Efficient Agent Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** As executable environments for language agents scale, generating and verifying their rollouts becomes increasingly expensive. Under finite rollout budgets, this raises an important question: can data management at the environment level make agentic reinforcement learning (RL) more efficient by organizing, evaluating, and scheduling task instances together with the environments that determine their tools, states, transitions, and verification? To address this question, we introduce EnvSched (Environment-aware Scheduling), an online scheduler that learns from rollout history. EnvSched assigns scheduling value through two complementary modeling choices: exploitation prioritizes tasks likely to provide informative reward differences, while coverage spreads each batch across underobserved environments and interaction patterns. EnvSched organizes environment--task pairs as nodes in a dual-relation graph, with within-environment edges capturing task semantics and shared tool, state, and verifier structure, and cross-environment edges capturing abstract execution and reward topology. By propagating historical rollout statistics along both edge types, it predicts within-task reward variance. These predictions guide a scheduler that allocates each batch to tasks with high predicted reward variance and underobserved environments and tasks. Environment quotas and a similarity penalty further limit concentration and redundancy, yielding informative and diverse batches without additional pilot rollouts. In RL on Qwen3-8B, EnvSched achieves an average score of 48.81 across BFCL-v3, tau-Bench, and ACEBench-Agent after 40 training steps, exceeding the highest score attained by random task selection after 120 steps. It also improves downstream performance across model sizes at the same training budget.

</details>

<a id="paper-166"></a>

### 166. [Repurposing Video Diffusion Transformers for Cross-View Temporal Object Correspondence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37633v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37633

**作者：** Siyoon Jin、Dahyun Chung、Honggyu An、Sangbeom Lim、Wonjin Nam、Jaewoo Jung、WonJun Moon、Seungryong Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37633v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37633v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 XTrack，将预训练视频 DiT 改造为 RGB 到 mask 的 latent 传输框架，联合建模跨视角匹配与时间跟踪，并用跨视角注意力对齐、难负例条件和局部裁剪增强，辅以伪标签稠密化流程。

**相关性简评：** 其跨视角注意力对齐与难负例条件可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选及 latent 状态跨视角身份保持提供可供核查的方法线索，但该文关注视频对应而非推理与停止决策，需较多迁移判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Repurposing Video Diffusion Transformers for Cross-View Temporal Object Correspondence

**abstract/A1：** Cross-view object correspondence identifies the same object across views. However, existing methods often rely on frame-wise matching or sequential tracking, which can propagate correspondence errors under appearance changes or occlusions. Therefore, we introduce cross-view temporal object correspondence, which requires preserving object identity across both views and time from a query mask. To address this, we propose XTrack, a unified framework which repurposes a pretrained video DiT with semantic and spatiotemporal priors for RGB-to-mask latent transport, jointly modeling cross-view matching and temporal tracking. However, naive video DiTs do not guarantee accurate cross-view grounding and often attend to semantically similar yet incorrect objects across views. Thus, we introduce focal cross-view attention alignment, hard negative conditioning, and local context crop strategies to ensure reliable grounding. Finally, beyond these architectural enhancements, we also tackle the lack of dense annotation by introducing a pseudo-label curation pipeline that converts sparse Ego-Exo4D annotations into dense supervision to enable effective video-level training. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of XTrack as a unified framework, ensuring robust temporal consistency and identity preservation.

**comment/C1：** Project Page : https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/XTrack/

</details>

<a id="paper-167"></a>

### 167. [Scaling Laws for Tool-Use Agents via Controllable Environment Synthesis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37648v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37648

**作者：** Andrew Szot、Samira Abnar、Omar Attia、Abbas Kazerouni、Michael Kirchhof、Cheng-Yu Hsieh、Raviteja Vemulapalli、Alexander Toshev

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37648v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37648v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 SynthAPI 流程，按工具、任务、数据库三个维度可控地合成多步工具调用环境，训练 849 个不同规模模型，并据此拟合数据规模与组成对下游工具使用智能体泛化性能的 scaling law。

**相关性简评：** 其可控环境合成与数据组成/复杂度的可预测缩放，可能为 LVR 智能体轨迹训练中「采什么数据、扩哪一维」提供线索，可供核查能否用于视觉证据获取类多步任务；但未涉及视觉状态或 latent 推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scaling Laws for Tool-Use Agents via Controllable Environment Synthesis

**abstract/A1：** We explore how to build performant large language model (LLM) tool-use agents that complete complex, multi-step tasks. Specifically, we study how data composition in synthetically generated tool-use environments affects the training of such agents. To do so, we propose the SynthAPI pipeline, which explicitly controls the diversity of generated environments along the axes of tools, tasks, and the databases on which the tools operate. By systematically varying SynthAPI's generation controls in 458,752 tasks across 849 trained models of four different sizes, we derive a scaling law that predicts downstream tool-use agent generalization based on the scale and composition of the synthesized environments. These predictable relationships reveal several lessons for scaling tool-use agent data. As data budgets grow, scaling tool and database diversity becomes more effective than increasing the number of tasks, and increasing data complexity yields larger gains. Scaling according to these findings yields agents that outperform models trained on data from prior work in large-scale tool-use agent and synthetic environment generation across four evaluation benchmarks.

</details>

<a id="paper-168"></a>

### 168. [Heads of Security: Learned Attention Gates Improve Prompt Injection Robustness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37657v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37657

**作者：** Igor Shilov、Xiaoxue Yang、Alexander Panfilov、Evgenii Kortukov、Matthieu Meeus、Peter Romov、Wojciech Samek、Jonas Geiping、Yves-Alexandre de Montjoye

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37657v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37657v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Heads of Security，在一小组注意力头上学习门控，使每个头在可信指令与不可信内容之间重新分配注意力，从而在推理不可信文本时不被注入指令劫持；在 Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct 和 GPT-OSS-20B 上与 SecAlign++、ASIDE 比较了自适应攻击下的鲁棒性与通用效用。

**相关性简评：** 逐头注意力门控是可直接核查的注意力结构干预手段，可能为 LVR 中视觉/证据 token 的筛选与「哪些信息进入内部状态」提供借鉴，但该工作面向安全对齐而非 latent 推理状态生成，联系需进一步判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Heads of Security: Learned Attention Gates Improve Prompt Injection Robustness

**abstract/A1：** We introduce Heads of Security (HoS), a prompt injection defense that enforces instruction-data separation via a small set of learned per-head attention gates. HoS learns how each head should redistribute attention between trusted and untrusted tokens, enabling models to reason over untrusted content without following injected instructions. We evaluate HoS on Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct and GPT-OSS-20B. We compare it against SecAlign++, a state-of-the-art finetuning-based defense, and ASIDE, a defense that, like ours, builds instruction-data separation into the model architecture. We show that HoS offers greater robustness to adaptive attacks, better performance on standard utility benchmarks, and matches top performance on standard agentic prompt injection benchmarks. More specifically, the black-box RL-Hammer attack reaches 97% ASR against GPT-OSS-20B and 85% against SecAlign, but only 10% against HoS. Claudini, a state-of-the-art white-box attack, strengthened against HoS through autoresearch, requires 3 times more FLOPs to reach the same ASR on HoS as it does on GPT-OSS-20B or SecAlign. HoS preserves base-model utility across standard multiple-choice benchmarks and prompting setups, whereas SecAlign and ASIDE degrade in few-shot settings. On agentic benchmarks with static attacks, HoS and SecAlign perform similarly and substantially improve the security-utility trade-off over the base model. ASIDE struggles with tool use, achieving only 10% utility. These results show that prompt injection robustness can be achieved and improved through targeted attention interventions.

</details>

<a id="paper-169"></a>

### 169. [When Should We Intervene at Test Time?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37658v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37658

**作者：** Jiahao Zhang、Xinling Wen、Liangliang Li、Yu Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37658v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37658v1>)

**研究分类：** METHOD · 方法迁移

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PORTAL 控制器，在测试时自适应中决定何时启用某个干预（如更新归一化统计）、优先标注哪些输入、以及何时停止查询；它把各预测规则作为共享比较的候选，并依据准确率差距与查询成本决定停止。

**相关性简评：** 其「何时干预、取多少证据、何时停止」的成本—收益控制规则，可能与 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的证据获取与停止决策形成方法线索，但原论文场景是图像损坏下的 TTA 分类而非 latent 推理，迁移性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Should We Intervene at Test Time?

**abstract/A1：** Test-time adaptation helps a deployed model respond to changing inputs, but the same intervention can rescue one batch and damage the next. Updating normalization statistics, for example, can improve corrupted images yet fail when a batch contains mostly one class. Average accuracy hides these failures and cannot tell a model when to use an intervention. Target labels can guide this decision, but they are scarce and some choices are already clear. We introduce PORTAL, a controller that chooses when to deploy an intervention, which inputs to label, and when to stop querying. Each available prediction rule is a candidate, and its predictions are fixed before current feedback arrives. Every acquired label therefore evaluates all candidates on the same input. PORTAL combines these shared comparisons and directs queries toward candidates that can still overtake the current leader. It stops when the remaining labels cannot change the choice or when the possible accuracy gain no longer justifies another query. Our analysis links the required feedback to accuracy gaps and shared mistakes, explaining why some choices need much less evidence. Experiments show that this control turns an intervention that loses accuracy overall into a useful choice on selected batches. It prevents severe failures caused by changes in arrival order and preserves gains across complete corruption streams. When a fixed rule already works well, PORTAL uses little feedback to retain its performance. Periodic label checks reveal when past evidence needs refreshing and help the controller recover as conditions change. The acquired labels also train stronger candidates for future batches, and selection brings nearly all of their accuracy gains into deployment. The same feedback therefore improves both the choice made now and the predictions available to later batches.

**comment/C1：** 79 pages; preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-170"></a>

### 170. [KORA: Deterministic-First Execution Control for AI Workloads](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35869v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35869

**作者：** Albert Heekwan Kim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35869v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35869v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** KORA 提出一个推理前控制层，将请求表示为任务路径，先尝试确定性或结构化路由并校验结果、记录遥测，仅在不足时升级为模型调用；在一个确定性任务为主的 100 任务基准上报告避免 80 次模型调用且确定性子集零不匹配。

**相关性简评：** 其「先确定性路由、验证后再升级到模型」的门控逻辑，可能为 LVR 中「是否还需继续计算」的 STOP/计算价值判断提供概念参照；但该工作处于系统编排层，不涉及 latent 状态或视觉证据，联系仅停留在问题表述层面，具体可借鉴性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** KORA: Deterministic-First Execution Control for AI Workloads

**abstract/A1：** AI applications often invoke a model before they determine whether inference is necessary. That default is useful for ambiguous generative tasks, but it is poorly matched to work that is structured, deterministic, policy-bound, lookup-oriented, or directly verifiable. KORA introduces deterministic-first execution control: a pre-inference control layer that represents requests as task paths, attempts deterministic or structured routes first, validates route outcomes, records telemetry, and escalates to a model-candidate path only when deterministic handling is insufficient. This paper presents KORA as a deterministic-first execution-control architecture and reports bounded evidence from reproducible benchmark and validation workloads. In a reproducible 100-task deterministic-heavy benchmark, KORA-controlled execution avoided 80 of 100 simulated model invocations versus a naive direct baseline, with zero mismatches in the deterministic subset. The workload is intentionally deterministic-heavy: it includes categories such as FAQ lookup, policy/rule checking, schema or format validation, deterministic transformation, and a smaller set of model-needed ambiguous or generative tasks. A separate synthetic customer-support local/no-network validation workload demonstrates route measurement and avoided model-call accounting in an application-shaped setting. These results support bounded model-invocation reduction and validation visibility in documented workloads. They do not establish production behavior, real-provider outcomes, energy outcomes, API-cost reduction, or broad superiority.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 3 figures, 3 tables. Code and benchmark available at https://github.com/Krako-Labs/KORA

</details>

<a id="paper-171"></a>

### 171. [ObjectTwin: Measuring the Asset Correspondence Gap for Capture-Anchored Digital Twins](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35904v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35904

**作者：** Zhenghe Guo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35904v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35904v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ObjectTwin 这一以扫描为锚的测量协议：冻结实例、相机、扫描投影支撑区、深度与评价分母，只替换物体表示，从而量化生成资产相对观测几何的「资产对应性缺口」。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结其余条件、仅替换内部表示、度量观测一致性损失」的干预式协议思路，可能可供核查能否用于评价 LVR 中 latent 状态是否保留视觉证据；但该文本身针对三维资产生成，与推理机制无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ObjectTwin: Measuring the Asset Correspondence Gap for Capture-Anchored Digital Twins

**abstract/A1：** Object-wise reconstruction increasingly inserts generated meshes into captured scenes as editable proxies for observed objects. This use creates a requirement absent from isolated-asset scores and pose estimates: at the captured metric state, the proxy should reproduce calibrated observations of the same instance. We present ObjectTwin, a scan-grounded measurement protocol that freezes the instance, cameras, scan-projected support and recorded depth, metric anchor, and evaluation denominator, then replaces only the object representation. The resulting asset correspondence gap measures lost observation agreement relative to scan-derived visible geometry without treating the scan as complete physical ground truth. Across two indoor RGB-D capture domains and two generation backends, visible, anchored generated assets lose 0.302-0.497 MaskIoU under this replacement. Every tested scene-level estimate is positive, while same-input generator preference reverses across capture domains. Coverage and overlap-depth analyses locate disagreement in both visible support and surface placement; the evaluated conservative pose refinement does not systematically recover it. These results establish capture correspondence as a separately measurable interface between generated-asset quality and stronger physical or task-level evaluation.

</details>

<a id="paper-172"></a>

### 172. [Counting the Bandages: On measuring the time that AI saves](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35942v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35942

**作者：** Solomon Shalom Lijo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35942v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35942v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 主张用「时间节省＋逃逸缺陷」的账本式度量取代单纯计时，提出盈亏平衡缺陷成本作为决策数，建立检查行为基线模型，并复现 METR 开发者研究、用小规模预注册实验检验其预测。

**相关性简评：** 把「是否继续核查」写成检查成本与漏检代价的权衡，可为 LVR 中 STOP 决策与视觉证据核验的收益—成本度量、过度信任分析提供概念参照，但未涉及 latent 状态或多模态推理本身，可供核查迁移可能。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Counting the Bandages: On measuring the time that AI saves

**abstract/A1：** I consider what it means for AI assistance to save time. I contrast a stopwatch view, on which time saved is whatever the clock records, with a ledger view, on which it is a counterfactual that has to be read next to the errors the work lets through, and I argue for the latter. The argument has four parts. I define a model-free pair of quantities, time saved and excess escaped defects, and one decision number built from them: the break-even defect cost. I give a baseline model of checking in which, until the model is good enough not to check, a calibrated worker's escaped-defect rate does not depend on how good the model is; checking time grows with the logarithm of the model's error rate; and over-trust buys minutes on the clock at a larger cost in total minutes. I show what randomized studies can and cannot identify, and I replicate METR's developer study from its public data: the same 246 issues support a slowdown of 19% or 24% depending on the estimand (34% without adjusting for a chance imbalance), and AI helped on somewhere between 3% and 82% of them. Finally, I restate the published record, with stated bounds in place of imported point assumptions. In GDPval's own review-every-output workflow, review time alone caps GPT-5's speedup at 3.6x; the consultants outside the frontier were faster, and more often wrong. Last, I test two of the model's predictions in a small pre-specified experiment on AI reviewers checking AI drafts of the same kind of brief, built from SEC filings made after the models' training data were collected. Told the drafter's true error rate, the reviewer checked more as it rose, but all-or-nothing, switching near where its prompt's costs break even. Told that the drafter erred ten times less often than it did, it checked 7 fewer of 9 figures and let a wrong figure through in 22.5% of briefs instead of none: faster and worse, entirely through reduced effort.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages, 2 figures. Replaces the author's earlier TSB proposal (SSRN 6682338). Code and data: https://github.com/solomonshalom/tsb-bench. Also archived on Zenodo: 10.5281/zenodo.23557773

</details>

<a id="paper-173"></a>

### 173. [Exponential Wake-Up Overshoot in Explicit Gaussian Point-Process Filters](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35958v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35958

**作者：** Hanwu Qin、Dianhai Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35958v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35958v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文研究显式高斯点过程滤波器在长时间静默后事件恢复时首次更新的指数级放大，给出该更新的精确分布与可计算的越界指数，并推导状态方差的二阶静默展开，在模拟与交易数据上验证预测。

**相关性简评：** 其「静默时长驱动状态更新过冲、方差膨胀并经方差上限抑制」的解析刻画，可能与 LVR 中长时间缺乏新视觉证据后 latent 状态首次更新过冲或失稳的问题相关，可供核查借用其度量方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Exponential Wake-Up Overshoot in Explicit Gaussian Point-Process Filters

**abstract/A1：** When events resume after a long silence, explicit Gaussian point-process filters amplify the first update exponentially in the silence duration. We give the exact distribution of this update and a computable index that predicts when it overshoots. For the explicit (single-scoring-step) filter and its implicit posterior-mode counterpart, we derive second-order silence expansions of the state variance with opposite-sign logarithmic corrections. The crossing index determines when the post-wake prediction exceeds the resumed rate, separating the bin width at which the amplification exponent reaches one from the larger width at which the true rate is overshot. At fixed bin width, the implicit filter's expected loss grows logarithmically with silence duration, whereas a continuous-time counterexample rules out a bound uniform in bin width and silence duration. Experimental evaluation confirms these predictions in all 73 crossing cells and on exchange trade data, while variance caps reduce explicit overshoot but mostly hurt the implicit filter.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 3 figures, 13 tables; main text with Appendices A-G (proofs, experimental protocol, additional results). A four-page version has been submitted to IEEE Signal Processing Letters

</details>

### 174. [A Graph-informed Kriging model for sample-efficient Bayesian optimization of wind farm layouts](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35975v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35975

**作者：** Zhen-Fan Wang、Kai Zhang、Xianping Du、Zhaolong Han、Dai Zhou、Onur Bilgen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC、physics.flu-dyn、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35975v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35975v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把风电场表示为有向气动相互作用图，将低维相对坐标下的成对叶尖相互作用聚合成农场尺度的结构化均值函数嵌入 Kriging 代理模型，使高斯过程只拟合残差与不确定性，从而在稀疏数据下提升 AEP 预测，并显著提高布局贝叶斯优化的样本效率。

**相关性简评：** 其「结构化关系先验均值＋残差与不确定性分离」的建模方式，可能为 LVR 如何在内部状态中注入实体关系先验、并用不确定性驱动证据获取与停止决策提供可核查的概念类比，但领域与多模态推理相距较远，迁移需较多工作。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Graph-informed Kriging model for sample-efficient Bayesian optimization of wind farm layouts

**abstract/A1：** Wind farm layout optimization becomes increasingly difficult as turbine count grows, especially when high-fidelity aerodynamic models are used. Conventional Kriging-based Bayesian optimization often suffers from low sample efficiency in high-dimensional layout spaces because sparse training data provide limited domain coverage. We develop a Graph-informed Kriging framework that embeds reusable aerodynamic interaction information into the surrogate mean function. The wind farm is represented as a directed aerodynamic graph, where pairwise turbine interactions defined in a low-dimensional relative-coordinate space are assembled into a farm-scale mean. This structured mean captures the dominant variation in annual energy production (AEP), allowing the Gaussian process to model only the residual structure and uncertainty. Numerical experiments show that Graph-informed Kriging consistently improves AEP prediction accuracy over ordinary and generic-trend Kriging under sparse-data conditions, with increasing benefits as layout dimension and wake-interaction strength grow. Embedded in Bayesian optimization, it substantially improves sample efficiency and achieves competitive solutions for the IEA Wind Task 37 benchmark with about 280 AEP evaluations. In a controlled 16-turbine case, it also outperforms ordinary and multi-fidelity Kriging under the same high-fidelity evaluation budget. The framework is further extended to a 48-turbine wind farm, demonstrating applicability to large-scale CFD-based layout optimization. These results show that Graph-informed surrogate modeling can enable more efficient use of high-fidelity aerodynamic models for reliable large-scale wind farm layout design.

</details>

### 175. [Exploratory creative pre-ranking: Hierarchical empirical-Bayes Thompson sampling for real-time creative optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36020v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36020

**作者：** Jiale Han、Ajith Moparthi、Dan Xie、Xiaowu Dai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.GT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36020v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36020v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出一个广告创意预排序器，在逐用户排序前按「受众段—商品—广告活动—创意」层级为召回创意打分并短名单化，用闭式估计的层级经验贝叶斯借力父级统计，并以每个创意单次 Thompson 抽样在延迟预算内平衡探索与利用，线上 A/B 后已投产。

**相关性简评：** 层级先验的部分池化与稀疏反馈下的单次抽样探索，可能为 LVR 中分步贡献分配、冷启动回退与预算受限的探索/停止决策提供可核查的抽象线索，但原文并不涉及多模态推理或 latent 状态生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Exploratory creative pre-ranking: Hierarchical empirical-Bayes Thompson sampling for real-time creative optimization

**abstract/A1：** Dynamic creative optimization decides which creative of an ad to render for each request. Recent work places that choice before ad ranking, but relies on deterministic point predictors with no calibrated uncertainty. AI-generated content has made creatives nearly free, while feedback per creative remains sparse and selection faces a tight latency budget. We introduce a creative pre-ranker that scores every recalled creative and shortlists each campaign's pool before the per-user ranker runs. Creatives sit in a hierarchy of audience segment, advertised item, campaign and creative, so a cold creative falls back on what its parent levels have earned, and how much it borrows from its parents is set in closed form from historical logs, with one parameter per level and nothing tuned. To stay within the latency budget, the pre-ranker predicts for an audience segment rather than an individual user, from per-attribute counters. It selects by a single Thompson draw per creative, balancing exploration and exploitation so that a new creative still has a chance to be learned. Replaying Roblox Sponsored-ads traffic over campaigns with competing creatives, the shortlist lifts predicted play-through rate by 8.14% against 2.80% for an equally sized random shortlist, forwards more than 40% fewer creatives than the full pool, and adds only 0.2% to serving time. After an A/B test on live traffic, the method now serves in production at Roblox.

</details>

### 176. [Minimax-Optimal Small-Loss Bounds for Online Linear Optimisation on $\\ell\_p$ Balls](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36097v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36097

**作者：** Jack J. Mayo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36097v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36097v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究单位 ℓ\_p 球上全信息在线线性优化的极小极大 small-loss 界：给出一种 FTRL 算法（有界径向正则项，至多切换一次到二次增广），其 regret 随最优固定动作的累计损失 Δ\_T 缩放，并配以匹配下界与 p→∞ 时的最优常数。

**相关性简评：** 其 regret 随 Δ\_T 与维度自适应的实例相关界，可能为推理中按难度分配计算量、设计 THINK/LOOK/STOP 的停止与预算规则提供形式化参照，但论文未涉及表示、视觉证据或 latent 状态，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Minimax-Optimal Small-Loss Bounds for Online Linear Optimisation on $\\ell\_p$ Balls

**abstract/A1：** We study full-information online linear optimisation on the unit $\\ell\_p$ ball in $\\mathbb{R}^d$, for fixed $2\\le p&lt;\\infty$ and losses of dual norm at most one. Let $Δ\_T$ denote the cumulative loss of the best fixed action above each round's minimum. A follow-the-regularised-leader (FTRL) algorithm attains, on every loss sequence and without knowing $T$ or $Δ\_T$, regret $O\_p(1+\\min\\{Δ\_T^{1-1/p},\\, d^{1/2-1/p}\\sqrt{Δ\_T}\\})$. Matching lower bounds show that this rate is minimax for each fixed $p$, and its constant $(2+o(1))p/\\log p$ is optimal as $p\\to\\infty$ among guarantees uniform in $d$ and $T$. The algorithm uses a bounded radial regulariser and switches at most once to its quadratic augmentation. On some paths where follow-the-leader (FTL) has regret one, its regret grows with $T$. A switching extension keeps the sharp constant and also guarantees $\\mathrm{Reg}\_T\\le 2\\,\\mathrm{Reg}\_T^{\\mathrm{FTL}}$. For i.i.d. losses whose mean has no zero coordinates, we compute the exact $\\log T$ coefficient of the expected regret, which separates FTL's variance term from the bias of the regulariser.

**comment/C1：** 49 pages

</details>

### 177. [Dirichlet Process Mixtures within Semiparametric Bayesian Networks: A Novel Conditional Density Estimation Approach](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36110v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36110

**作者：** Victor Alejandre、Pedro Larrañaga、Concha Bielza

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36110v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36110v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在半参数贝叶斯网络中提出用 Dirichlet 过程混合（DPM）替代核密度估计来建模非参数条件概率分布，通过数据自适应聚类获得更紧凑的混合表示，从而降低推理成本并改善密度估计与结构学习误差。

**相关性简评：** 其「自适应确定混合分量数」的紧凑离散表示思路，可能与 LVR 中离散 latent 假设集合、状态抽象与表示紧凑性的设计存在概念联系，可供核查；但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dirichlet Process Mixtures within Semiparametric Bayesian Networks: A Novel Conditional Density Estimation Approach

**abstract/A1：** Semiparametric Bayesian networks (SPBNs) are flexible probabilistic graphical models for representing joint distributions of continuous variables through lower-dimensional conditional probability distributions (CPDs). Existing SPBN models use kernel density estimators (KDEs) for non-parametric CPD modeling, but their one-component-per-observation mixture representation can lead to increased inference costs and density estimation errors, which can be limiting. In this work, we define a new CPD modeling approach for non-parametric components based on Bayesian non-parametric Dirichlet Process Mixtures (DPMs) within SPBNs. DPMs provide a data-adaptive clustering of observations, yielding a more compact representation while retaining the flexibility of non-parametric density estimation, unlike KDE. This results in lower inference costs while improving density estimation performance. We implement the proposed DPM-based SPBN (DPM-SPBN) by extending the open-source PyBNesian package and evaluate it on synthetic and real-world data. For a given network structure, DPM-based CPDs significantly outperform state-of-the-art Bayesian network models for continuous variables in density estimation, achieving lower absolute log-likelihood errors on synthetic data. We further investigate the impact of the proposed CPD modeling on structure learning methods relying on CPD-based scores. DPM-based SPBNs achieve lower structure learning and density estimation errors than KDE-based SPBNs. Experiments on real-world data corroborate these findings, identifying DPM-based CPD modeling as the most effective alternative among the evaluated non-parametric approaches for continuous variables.

</details>

<a id="paper-178"></a>

### 178. [Scaling and Renormalization in Neural Networks: An Effective Renormalization-Group Description of Grokking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36113v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36113

**作者：** Min Chen、Junqi Wang、Zeguan Wu、Wenhao Huang、Junyu Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、hep-th、quant-ph

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36113v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36113v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用标度与重整化群框架描述两层网络在模加法任务中由记忆转向泛化的 grokking 动力学：以权重范数平方为序参量，由损失二阶展开构造耦合矩阵并借 Fiedler 向量定义有效几何，再经块自旋粗粒化导出临界指数。

**相关性简评：** 其耦合矩阵谱排序与块自旋粗粒化可能为刻画 latent 推理状态的有效几何与层级粗粒度结构提供分析工具，但任务与视觉多模态推理无关，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Scaling and Renormalization in Neural Networks: An Effective Renormalization-Group Description of Grokking

**abstract/A1：** Grokking is a delayed transition from memorization to generalization in deep neural networks (NNs). We examine whether scaling and renormalization group (RG) theories can describe this dynamics, establishing a quantitative physical framework for neural generalization transitions. For modular addition on a two-layer NN, an ensemble of trained models defines an order parameter (the squared weight norm) and fluctuation proxies for the heat capacity and the susceptibility, with training epoch and weight decay serving as thermal and field control coordinates. To bridge neural architectures to real-space RG, we construct an exact coupling matrix via a second-order expansion of the loss with respect to per-neuron norms and use its Fiedler vector to define an effective geometry. This spectral ordering yields a monotonic spatial decay of coupling strength that is absent under random permutations. We then execute real-space RG by block-spin aggregating adjacent neurons and parameterizing the coupling matrix into off-diagonal band strengths. Linearizing this local RG map yields relevant scaling exponents and five derived critical exponents, with direct empirical estimates matching RG predictions within 1-10%. Overall, our framework provides a consistent effective description of grokking, suggesting the scaling and renormalization group formalism as a tool to investigate NN dynamics.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 10 figures

</details>

### 179. [Any3R: Feedforward 3D Reconstruction from Any Camera Type](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36150v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36150

**作者：** Jingong Chen、Qingwen Zhang、Youming Deng、Chulwoo Pack、Olaf Wysocki、Boris Jutzi、Kwanghee Won、Kyle Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36150v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36150v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Any3R：把位置编码与训练损失都条件化在稠密光线场（每像素的单位方向）上，用角度坐标驱动 rotary encoding、以立体角加权点损失，使前馈几何基础模型无需镜头类型或畸变参数即可处理任意中心相机，并稳健应对同场景混合相机类型。

**相关性简评：** 该文用光线方向而非镜头参数定义像素与空间方向的对应，并据此改造编码与监督，可能为视觉 token 如何在内部状态中保留几何与细粒度空间信息提供可核查线索；但它本身不做多模态推理，与 LVR 的状态生成和停止决策联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Any3R: Feedforward 3D Reconstruction from Any Camera Type

**abstract/A1：** Geometry foundation models recover camera poses and dense geometry from a set of images with a feed-forward network, but they expect pinhole images. That assumption is inside the backbone too: the positional encoding and the training loss both bind an image position to a direction in space, and both depend on the lens. We condition both on the dense ray field, the unit direction through every pixel, which describes any central camera without a lens type, distortion coefficients or a bound on field of view. Angular coordinates give the rotary encoding the measured ray angle of each patch, evaluated per view, and solid-angle weighting scales the point loss by the area each pixel covers on the sphere, so neither the encoding nor the supervision depends on the lens. Any3R is therefore a feed-forward model that takes any central camera, and is robust to mixing many camera types per scene, with no retraining per lens. On a benchmark of thirteen central cameras across three datasets, where every method reconstructs with its own predicted camera poses, it is the most consistently accurate method. Any3R enables scalable 3D reconstruction from heterogeneous crowdsourced and archival imagery collections sourced across diverse camera types. Project page: https://jingong-chen.github.io/Any3R/

</details>

<a id="paper-180"></a>

### 180. [Dynamical coarse-graining of neural-network training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36180v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36180

**作者：** Lin-Juan Wang、Da Li、Huiling Duan、Jianxiang Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cond-mat.soft、cs.NE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36180v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36180v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文为神经网络训练动力学定义学习算子并给出下降定理、精确闭合定理与动态粗粒化定理，构成构造和评估粗粒化模型的框架；并在有限宽非线性 ReLU 网络上以五个独立矩精确导出固定模式的粗粒化模型，可预测训练输出与学习算子演化。

**相关性简评：** 其用远小于参数空间的宏观状态与矩闭合精确刻画动力学的思路，可能与压缩、预测 latent 推理状态演化的工作区设想存在概念联系，可供核查；但原文不涉及视觉或多模态推理，迁移性未知。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dynamical coarse-graining of neural-network training

**abstract/A1：** Dynamical coarse-graining of neural networks gives macroscopic observables and enables training to be predicted, and controlled in a state space far smaller than the parameter space. In this paper, we first define the learning operator following the training law of neural networks and establish a descent theorem for the learning operator, which serves to determine whether the operator is sufficient to characterize the learning dynamics. Then the exact closure theorem and the dynamic coarse-graining theorem are presented in sequence. The three theorems established in this paper provide a systematic framework for constructing and assessing dynamical coarse-grained models of neural-network training. Finally, we demonstrate the applicability of the theorems with a finite-width nonlinear ReLU network. Its dynamic coarse-grained model for a fixed pattern is exactly derived and composed of five independent moments. The model exactly predict the training outputs and evolving learning operator. The framework may also guide the development of approximate coarse-grained models for more general neural networks when exact closure is unavailable.

</details>

### 181. [Learning Human Sports Motion Feasibility via Quantile Regression on Tracking Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36183v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36183

**作者：** Marc Gutiérrez-Pérez、Antonio Agudo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36183v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36183v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把体育中的到达时间（TTA）定义为球员在当前运动状态下到达场上任意位置所需的最短时间，用条件分位数回归估计这一「下包络」，在世界杯光学追踪数据上训练轻量 MLP 并与多种物理基线比较，另测试了向篮球与橄榄球数据的迁移。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 主线无直接关系，但其「给定状态预测最短可达时间」的下分位数建模范式，可能为按内部状态估计最少证据或计算开销、以及停止决策提供线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Human Sports Motion Feasibility via Quantile Regression on Tracking Data

**abstract/A1：** We study time-to-arrival (TTA) in sports: the shortest plausible time a player needs to reach an arbitrary location on the pitch given their current kinematic state, estimated as the lower envelope of observed locomotion times. Rather than modeling space ownership through position density estimation or closed-form physics-based models, we cast TTA as a conditional quantile regression problem, estimating the lower quantile of locomotion time so the model learns a player's lower-envelope arrival time. We train the model on optical tracking data from all 64 matches of the PFF FC FIFA World Cup 2022 dataset, and evaluate a lightweight multilayer perceptron against six player-motion models from the literature, re-expressed as TTA predictors, and a learned reachable- hull baseline. Across our evaluation metrics, the MLP consistently outperforms every physics-based baseline. To probe the generality of the learned locomotion model, we further evaluate it on basketball tracking data and a single-game NFL tracking dataset, analyzing the transferability of player motion dynamics across sports. Our project is available at https://github.com/mguti97/TTA-Quantile.

</details>

### 182. [Learning to Read Braille: Benchmarking and Improving Braille Recognition Capabilities of VLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36195v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36195

**作者：** Kartik Jangra、Atharva Gawane、Siddharth Pulipaka、Hemachandran Karah、Raj Dabre

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36195v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36195v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建 BrailleBench（259 张真实盲文图像基准），评测通用、开源自权重、OCR 专用及工具辅助 VLM 的盲文转写能力，并用带布局与图像扰动的合成数据微调 2B 模型，指出合成验证集高准确率与真实照片低准确率之间的巨大落差。

**相关性简评：** 盲文点阵识别依赖极细粒度空间细节，可能作为检验视觉细节能否保留在内部状态中的极端测试；其「合成集高、真实集低」的落差可供核查视觉监督与分布偏移对表征和证据使用的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning to Read Braille: Benchmarking and Improving Braille Recognition Capabilities of VLMs

**abstract/A1：** Despite rapid progress in visual understanding and document transcription, modern vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with a fundamental accessibility task: reading Braille from images. We introduce BrailleBench, a manually curated benchmark of 259 real-world Grade-1 and Grade-2 Braille images. We evaluate proprietary, open-weight, OCR-specialized, and tool-assisted multimodal systems and find that standalone general-purpose models perform poorly at directly transcribing Braille images, whereas tool-assisted systems perform much better. We design a synthetic Braille generation pipeline with diverse layouts and image perturbations. A 2B VLM model trained on our synthetic data reaches 87% exact match on Grade-1 and 61% on Grade-2 BrailleBench, outperforming standalone VLMs and achieving the highest Grade-2 exact match score among the evaluated systems. We further show that high accuracy on synthetic validation data does not guarantee high accuracy on real photographs in our experiments: models trained on prior synthetic data reach up to 96% on their synthetic validation set but at most 3.26% on Grade-1 BrailleBench. Our results identify an accessibility-relevant capability gap in multimodal systems and show that targeted synthetic supervision can improve real-world Braille transcription.

**comment/C1：** 22 pages, 8 figures, 16 tables. Code and data: https://github.com/kartik0806/BrailleBench

</details>

### 183. [UT-HMR: Uncertainty-Guided Temporal Modeling for Robust 3D Human Mesh Recovery](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36203v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36203

**作者：** Zhibo Wang、Sirisha Rambhatla、Yuhao Chen、Maria Koshkina、David Clausi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36203v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36203v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 UT-HMR 单阶段时序人体网格恢复框架：每个人查询预测 SMPL 姿态的联合高斯分布（低秩加对角协方差），转为关节级精度权重做异方差监督，并在姿态解码前早期融合邻帧证据以消解歧义，在冰球与足球数据集上取得最优结果。

**相关性简评：** 对结构化输出的相关性不确定性与解码前证据注入，可能为 LVR 中不确定性驱动的视觉证据筛选和停止决策提供概念参照，但该工作不涉及 latent 推理或 VLM，联系需进一步核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** UT-HMR: Uncertainty-Guided Temporal Modeling for Robust 3D Human Mesh Recovery

**abstract/A1：** Monocular 3D human mesh recovery (HMR) remains unreliable under weak observations caused by long camera-to-person distances, occlusion, and motion blur. In challenging scenarios such as sports, these factors make the problem highly ambiguous, as different 3D poses may yield similar 2D projections. This ambiguity is especially severe for one-stage HMR, which recovers all meshes directly from the full image without person crops or external 2D cues. To handle this ambiguity, we present UT-HMR ( Uncertainty-guided Temporal HMR), to our knowledge the first one-stage HMR framework to capture correlated pose uncertainty across joints. Each person query predicts a Gaussian distribution over the full SMPL pose with a compact low-rank-plus-diagonal covariance, which is converted into joint-wise precision weights for heteroscedastic supervision while keeping inference deterministic. Temporal early fusion further injects neighboring-frame evidence before pose decoding, supplying the visual evidence needed to resolve the ambiguity that the uncertainty captures. With a lightweight ViT-B backbone, UT-HMR achieves state-of-the-art results on VIP-HockeyPose-v2 (ice hockey) and WorldPose (soccer), reducing MPJPE, PA-MPJPE, and PVE by up to 4.7%, 3.8%, and 5.6%, and remains competitive on 3DPW against methods with larger backbones and higher input resolutions.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at WACV 2027

</details>
