### 184. [Concorde: Geometry-Aware Link Prediction via Decoupled Energy Minimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36204v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36204

**作者：** Ege Demirci、Ambuj Singh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36204v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36204v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Concorde，一个能量式链接预测框架：把链接似然拆成无参数谱锚点、混合曲率乘积流形上的残差（以特征感知 Ollivier-Ricci 曲率代理正则）和双曲切丛上的连续向量场三分支，用学习门控融合并以 N-pair 排序损失训练。

**相关性简评：** 其混合曲率乘积流形表示与能量最小化的解耦分支融合，可能与 LVR 中 latent 状态的表示几何及多路推理状态整合存在远域启发，但需核查其几何先验能否脱离图链接任务迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Concorde: Geometry-Aware Link Prediction via Decoupled Energy Minimization

**abstract/A1：** Link prediction remains difficult in sparse graphs, where diffusion heuristics lack connectivity and geometric methods suffer spectral instability when coupling learned representations with diffusion operators. We introduce Concorde, an energy-based framework that resolves this by decomposing link likelihood into three structurally decoupled branches: (i) a parameter-free spectral anchor that establishes permutation-invariant topological stability; (ii) a mixed-curvature residual on a learnable $\\mathbb{H}^{d\_h}\_{c\_h} \\times \\mathbb{S}^{d\_s}\_{c\_s}$ product manifold, regularized by a feature-aware Ollivier-Ricci curvature proxy; and (iii) a continuous vector field on the tangent bundle of hyperbolic space for non-local structural alignment across topological gaps. The branches are fused via learned gating and trained with an N-pair ranking loss aligned with retrieval-style evaluation. Across five benchmarks spanning sparse, dense, featureless, and attributed regimes, Concorde establishes state-of-the-art early retrieval precision, nearly doubling P@10% over leading baselines in sparse settings.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 185. [MARQ: A Modular, LLM-Agnostic Requirement Quality Assessor Infused with Systems Engineering Knowledge](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36234v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36234

**作者：** Jannatul Shefa、Alejandro Salado、Paul Wach、Taylan G. Topcu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36234v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36234v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向需求工程质量评估，MARQ 将 INCOSE 质量准则组织成按准则划分的知识栈并注入 LLM，在专家标注的 NASA 需求集上以三种模型、每模型 100 次执行对比现成基线，并做准则层面的得失分析。

**相关性简评：** 其「按准则模块化注入外部知识 + 多次执行的稳定性与漏检/误报权衡分析」可供核查是否能迁移到 LVR 的分项监督与推理一致性评价；该文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MARQ: A Modular, LLM-Agnostic Requirement Quality Assessor Infused with Systems Engineering Knowledge

**abstract/A1：** Requirements engineering (RE) is foundational to systems engineering (SE), as requirement defects can propagate downstream and adversely affect development outcomes. Although large language models (LLMs) are promising for requirement quality assessment, prior work shows that off-the-shelf models miss a large share of expert-identified defects, perform unevenly across quality criteria, and vary across executions. This study investigates whether infusing expert-derived SE knowledge can improve LLM-based requirement quality evaluation beyond off-the-shelf capability. We present MARQ, a modular, LLM-agnostic prototype that supplements LLMs with a criterion-specific knowledge stack operationalizing INCOSE requirement quality criteria and evaluated against off-the-shelf baselines on an expert-labeled NASA requirement set using Claude Opus 5, GPT-5.6 Sol, and the open-weight Gemma 3 12B, with 100 executions per model. MARQ achieves median F1 scores of 0.80 for Claude Opus 5, 0.62 for GPT-5.6 Sol, and 0.53 for Gemma 3 12B, compared with off-the-shelf baselines of 0.55, 0.52, and 0.18, respectively. However, the nature of these gains is model-dependent. Claude Opus 5 improves defect detection and false-alarm control; GPT-5.6 Sol gains detection and better execution-level consistency at the cost of more false alarms; and Gemma 3 12B reaches the performance of off-the-shelf proprietary models, although it remains highly variable. Criterion-level analysis shows gains concentrated in Correctness and Conformance, trade-offs between misses and false alarms within individual criteria, and model-dependent responses across several criteria. These findings position SE knowledge infusion as a promising model-agnostic pathway for strengthening trustworthy LLM-based RE support while underscoring the need for criterion-specific interventions and human-in-the-loop review.

</details>

### 186. [BehaveRec: When Does Long-Term Behavioral History Help Sequential Recommendation? A Study with Session, History, and Community Signals](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36239v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36239

**作者：** Aysenur Inan、Reza Yousefi Maragheh、Jayesh Uddhav Kudase、Selene Xu、Kaushiki Nag、Jianpeng Xu、Sushant Kumar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36239v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36239v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 BehaveRec，用会话 transformer、90 天用户历史编码器和基于行为相似度的 GraphSAGE 三路信号，并配学习门控逐用户融合，在工业与公开数据上检验长期行为历史何时真正带来收益。

**相关性简评：** 按历史丰富度分层得到的「剂量—响应」收益曲线与部署阈值，可能为「何时值得引入额外视觉证据或更长推理」这一问题提供分析范式，门控逐样本融合也可供核查是否适用于多源 latent 信号加权。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** BehaveRec: When Does Long-Term Behavioral History Help Sequential Recommendation? A Study with Session, History, and Community Signals

**abstract/A1：** Sequential recommendation models typically predict a user's next action from recent session interactions, which limits personalization when short-term signals are weak or ambiguous. Rather than assuming that incorporating longer-term history and community signals is universally beneficial, we study when it actually helps. We propose BehaveRec, a three-signal framework combining a causal session transformer, a bidirectional User History Encoder over a 90-day window, and a GraphSAGE module over a user-user graph built from behavioral embedding similarity rather than item co-occurrence; a learned gate blends the three signals per user. On an industrial dataset of 137M sequences from 12.6M users, BehaveRec improves R@5 by +3.2% over SASRec, with a larger-capacity SASRec baseline (more parameters than BehaveRec) still trailing by +2.5%, ruling out capacity as the explanation. More importantly, a user-level stratification by history length reveals a clear dose-response relationship: BehaveRec's advantage is negligible below roughly 10 interactions and grows monotonically with history richness, reaching +0.017 R@5 for users with 101+ events. This pattern is corroborated across one industrial and three public datasets spanning history-rich to history-sparse regimes, where session-only models remain preferable. These headline results, and the resulting deployment threshold, are established for category-level intent prediction; a preliminary, single-run check on the full item-level vocabulary yields a directionally mixed result and is scoped as an open question rather than a claim. We use these results to derive a practical deployment rule -- roughly 20 interactions per user -- for when long-term history and behavioral-graph signals are worth their added complexity. Despite this added complexity, BehaveRec remains lightweight: it uses 5.67M parameters and trains on a single GPU in under two days.

</details>

### 187. [Three Worlds for the Price of a Logarithm: Baseline-Protected Best-of-Both-Worlds Combinatorial Semi-Bandits](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36245v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36245

**作者：** Ting Hu、Luanda Cai、Emmanouil-Vasileios Vlatakis-Gkaragkounis

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.GT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36245v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36245v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 DUET，一种用于任意有限动作集合的组合半臂赌博机的无时域分阶段混合方案，用两个可观测检验决定何时加倍学习权重、何时加倍经验遗憾预算并重启，并以 entropy-FTRL 与 hybrid-FTRL 实例化，同时获得对数级基线遗憾、√T 对抗遗憾和 polylog 随机遗憾。

**相关性简评：** 「保护基线同时探索」与两个可观测检验触发的加倍/重启机制，可能与 LVR 中默认短推理路径的保护、以及何时增配 latent 计算或停止的决策规则有概念联系，可供核查；论文本身不涉及视觉表示或生成。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Three Worlds for the Price of a Logarithm: Baseline-Protected Best-of-Both-Worlds Combinatorial Semi-Bandits

**abstract/A1：** Improving on a baseline requires exploring away from it, precisely where its performance may be lost. We propose DUET (Doubling Under Empirical Tests), a horizon-free phased mixing scheme for combinatorial semi-bandits with arbitrary finite action sets. Two observable tests decide when to double the learning weight and when to double the empirical-regret budget and restart. We instantiate the scheme with entropy-FTRL in mixed phases and hybrid-FTRL in pure phases. Assuming the baseline selects every arm with positive probability, this instantiation keeps expected baseline regret logarithmic in $T$, while its expected regret to every fixed action is $\\widetilde{O}(\\sqrt{T})$ under non-anticipating adversarial losses and polylogarithmic, gap-dependent, under i.i.d. losses with a unique optimal action. All three guarantees hold at every deterministic horizon, and the algorithm knows neither the horizon, nor the gap, nor the environment type. Baseline protection, adversarial robustness, and stochastic adaptivity thus come from one scheme and two base learners, at the price of a logarithm.

</details>

### 188. [Freeze, Don't Clone: Fine-Tuning with Expressive Policies Without Retaining Offline Data](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36247v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36247

**作者：** Sami Nur Islam、Johan Obando-Ceron、Doina Precup、Aaron Courville、Pablo Samuel Castro

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36247v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36247v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究无离线数据保留的 offline-to-online RL：指出在线阶段继续行为克隆会抹去预训练的表达性行为先验、引发表示坍缩与动作多样性下降，并形成行为反馈回路；提出冻结先验、仅由价值学习驱动的做法，在 IFQL、FQL、QC 与七个 OGBench 域上验证其收益。

**相关性简评：** 其「冻结表达性先验以避免表示坍缩」的机制可能为 LVR 中持续微调时保护视觉 latent 信息、防止推理状态退化为捷径提供跨领域线索，但迁移可行性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Freeze, Don't Clone: Fine-Tuning with Expressive Policies Without Retaining Offline Data

**abstract/A1：** Offline-to-online reinforcement learning uses offline pretraining to accelerate subsequent online learning. While many methods assume the offline dataset remains available throughout fine-tuning, there are many settings where this is not the case. Prior work attributes failures in this setting primarily to critic recalibration under distribution shift. We identify a complementary actor-side failure: once the offline data disappears, continued behavioral cloning (BC) no longer preserves the offline behavior distribution and instead refits the behavioral model to the agent's limited, and nonstationary, online experience. We demonstrate that stopping online behavioral fitting while preserving the pretrained expressive prior substantially improves no-retention fine-tuning across implicit flow Q-learning (IFQL), flow Q-learning (FQL), and action-chunked RL (QC) on seven OGBench domains. We trace this failure to a behavioral feedback loop: continuing online behavioral cloning rapidly erases the pretrained prior, inducing representational collapse and action diversity loss that starves the critic of useful exploration. Freezing the prior breaks this cycle, preserving offline coverage while allowing value learning to drive continuous improvement.

</details>

<a id="paper-189"></a>

### 189. [AHEAD: Adaptive Neural Acceleration for Large-Scale Linear Programming](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36321v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36321

**作者：** Sihao Liu、Zhichao Hou、Huayue Gu、Raiyan Rahman、Liang Wu、Ruozhou Yu、Xiaorui Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36321v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36321v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 AHEAD：用权重共享的轻量预测器读取求解器统计量并重缩放一阶 LP 求解器的原始/对偶步长，再用学得的 handoff 策略为每个实例和目标精度决定 warm-start 迭代次数，从而在大规模线性规划上取得端到端加速。

**相关性简评：** 学得的 handoff 策略按实例与目标精度设定迭代预算，可能可供核查 LVR 中 STOP 决策与计算量—精度权衡的收益成本建模；但全文无视觉或多模态推理内容，迁移属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AHEAD: Adaptive Neural Acceleration for Large-Scale Linear Programming

**abstract/A1：** Large-scale linear programs with millions of variables and constraints strain time and memory budgets. First-order methods such as the primal-dual hybrid gradient scale to this regime because they avoid matrix factorizations, but they require many iterations. Learned accelerators can reduce these iterations, yet fewer iterations do not guarantee a shorter solve. Learning inference also takes time, and running the accelerator longer trades solver time for inference time. Existing unrolled accelerators handle this trade-off poorly: each layer processes every variable and constraint, the predicted iterates still need solver refinement, and every instance runs the same layers. We therefore ask: What neural accelerator design achieves end-to-end speedups on large-scale linear programs across sizes and target accuracies? We introduce AHEAD, an accelerator that predicts step sizes for first-order linear programming solvers while keeping their update rules. Its weight-shared predictor reads compact solver statistics and rescales the primal and dual step sizes, so each warm-start iteration stays inexpensive and a short warm-start phase hands the solver a warm start. A learned handoff policy sets the number of warm-start iterations for each instance and target accuracy, after which the solver finishes under its own termination criterion. On packing linear programs with up to $10^7$ variables, AHEAD achieves $6.5$--$11.6\\times$ end-to-end speedups over Gurobi's first-order solver from a cold start, including on instances of unseen sizes and distributions. The gains extend to PageRank linear programs and hold across difficulty levels and target accuracies.

</details>

<a id="paper-190"></a>

### 190. [Ontology-guided multi-agent reasoning for explainable differential diagnosis](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36325v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36325

**作者：** Roxana Ramona Szomiu、George Ursu、Adrian Groza

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36325v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36325v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出本体引导的多智能体鉴别诊断框架，把诊断推理约束在专家维护的医学知识库上，拆分为可审计的显式步骤，并主动挑选最具鉴别力的缺失症状生成追问以降低不确定性。

**相关性简评：** 其「按信息增益选择下一步证据并追问」的机制，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的证据筛选与停止决策提供问题表述上的启发，可供核查；但该工作限于纯文本医学诊断，未涉及视觉状态或 latent 表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Ontology-guided multi-agent reasoning for explainable differential diagnosis

**abstract/A1：** Effective differential diagnosis depends on well structured and unambiguous patient case descriptions, since incomplete or ambiguous clinical information readily leads to diagnostic errors and inconsistent reasoning. Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown promising capabilities in medical question answering and clinical reasoning; however, even when achieving competitive performance on medical benchmarks, they often rely on pattern recognition learned from training data rather than grounded clinical evidence, and can generate plausible yet unsupported diagnostic conclusions. Their reasoning remains largely opaque, making it difficult for clinicians to assess the evidence and rationale behind a given recommendation. To address this gap, we propose an ontology-guided, multiagent differential diagnosis framework that replicates the iterative reasoning process of physicians. The framework grounds diagnostic inference in a structured, expert-curated medical knowledge base, enabling interpretation of symptoms, clinical findings, or disease relationships, while decomposing diagnosis into explicit, auditable reasoning steps. This ontology-based grounding constrains inference to evidence-backed associations, reducing hallucination and improving diagnostic transparency, traceability, and consistency. The system then identifies the most discriminative missing symptoms and generates targeted follow-up questions to reduce uncertainty through a process that mirrors to clinical inquiry. Together, these components improve the reliability, explainability, and diagnostic accuracy of AI-assisted differential diagnosis. The source code is publicly available.

**comment/C1：** preprint

</details>

<a id="paper-191"></a>

### 191. [Training-Free Inference Acceleration for Video Large Language Models: Methods, Trade-offs, and Evaluation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36327v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36327

**作者：** Yuqi Li、Chuanyue Yan、Neil Rodrigues、Xianglong Wang、Xiaoqin Feng、Junming Liu、Xi Xiao、Zhongwei Cheng、Wei Li、Lin Chen、Yingli Tian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36327v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36327v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该综述梳理视频大语言模型的免训练推理加速方法，按推理流程中的干预阶段（输入、视觉编码、projector、prefill、解码）组织，并单列 PTQ 作为跨阶段扩展；结合既有研究与统一设置下的自测实验，分析精度与延迟的权衡。

**相关性简评：** 其对「干预位置如何影响任务相关时空证据保留」以及局部效率提升未必转化为端到端加速的分析，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的证据筛选与计算成本核算提供参照框架，可核查其评测口径能否迁移到 latent 推理场景。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Training-Free Inference Acceleration for Video Large Language Models: Methods, Trade-offs, and Evaluation

**abstract/A1：** Video Large Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in video understanding and reasoning, but processing long visual sequences incurs substantial computational, latency, and memory overhead. This survey examines training-free inference acceleration, focusing on reducing inference costs while preserving task-relevant spatiotemporal evidence. We organize existing methods by their intervention stages in the inference pipeline, covering input, visual encoding, projector, prefill, and decoding. Post-training quantization (PTQ) is discussed separately as a cross-stage extension with varying calibration requirements. We analyze how intervention position affects evidence preservation and computational savings, and why local efficiency improvements may not translate into end-to-end acceleration. We complement this analysis with quantitative evidence from prior studies and our own experiments under a shared evaluation setting to assess accuracy-latency trade-offs.

</details>

<a id="paper-192"></a>

### 192. [How Stable Are LLM-Generated Proofs Under Program Refactoring? A Stress Test for Verifiable Code Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36328v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36328

**作者：** Aravind Mandiga、Guoming Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LO、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36328v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36328v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 RefactorProof，从 185 个可编译的 VERINA 任务派生 458 个 Lean 认证的等价重构变体，在规格与证明逐字节不变的前提下测「不变证明存活率」，发现结构化重构会显著暴露证明脆弱性，并进一步区分存活失败与修复成本。

**相关性简评：** 其「保持语义的输入变换 + 固定输出」压力测试思路，可能可迁移为诊断 VLM 推理状态与答案是否只依赖任务必要信息、而对无关表面变化不变；但原文只测最终产物，未涉及内部 latent 状态，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Stable Are LLM-Generated Proofs Under Program Refactoring? A Stress Test for Verifiable Code Generation

**abstract/A1：** Benchmarks for verifiable code generation usually test a proof against one fixed implementation. We ask whether the same proof remains valid after a program refactoring that preserves behavior. We introduce RefactorProof, which derives 458 Lean-certified variants from the 185 VERINA tasks that compile in our pinned environment while holding the specification and evaluated proof fixed byte-for-byte. VERINA supplies reference proofs for 46 tasks, all from its basic split; the primary model-versus-reference comparisons further match on 21-25 short, non-recursive tasks. Structural refactorings expose substantial brittleness across proof sources: task-normalized structural PSR is 4.3% for the supplied references and 13.3-26.8% for the specialized provers. On the matched tasks, specialized prover proofs nevertheless have higher overall unchanged-proof survival than the supplied reference artifacts: +15.6 points for DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B, +18.8 for Goedel-Prover-V2-32B, and +20.3 for Goedel-Prover-V2-8B; all three 95% CIs exclude zero. The ordering persists after excluding universally survived T1 variants, under family-balanced aggregation, and under alternative deployable valid-proof selection rules. However, unchanged-proof failure is not the same as high repair cost: predefined one-identifier edits repair 51/54 eligible reference structural failures and 58/85 eligible prover failures. RefactorProof therefore measures unchanged-proof stability under program refactoring, not the cost of maintaining a proof after it breaks.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on AI for Verifiable Coding

</details>

<a id="paper-193"></a>

### 193. [OA-RAG: Ontology-based Agentic RAG for Faithful LLM Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36335v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36335

**作者：** Hui Yang、Jiaoyan Chen、Yongsheng Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36335v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36335v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 OA-RAG，将本体中的形式逻辑推理与 LLM 推理结合的 agentic RAG 系统，对每个答案报告所用本体证据及模型评估的支撑等级（entailed/supported/related/unrelated），并在多个医学问答基准上验证其效果及支撑等级与准确率的关联。

**相关性简评：** 该文虽为文本领域 RAG，但其对证据逐条披露并给出分级支撑强度的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与「答案是否真正使用所获证据」的核查提供可借鉴的标注思路，尚需核查其分级是否可靠。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** OA-RAG: Ontology-based Agentic RAG for Faithful LLM Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) perform well across many real-world tasks but remain prone to hallucination, limiting their reliability in domains such as biomedicine and healthcare, where answers must be grounded in established knowledge. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) mitigates this problem by incorporating external information, but often overlooks logical relationships and relies heavily on the LLM's own reasoning, which can compromise answer faithfulness. We propose OA-RAG, an agent that integrates formal reasoning with LLM reasoning, drawing on both the logical structure and informal annotations of domain ontologies. For each answer, OA-RAG reports the ontology observations used as evidence and a model-assessed grounding level (entailed, supported, related, or unrelated) that characterises the strength of that evidence. Across benchmarks derived from three large-scale ontologies and six real-world medical question-answering tasks, OA-RAG achieves the best overall performance among the evaluated ontology-fine-tuned and popular RAG methods, with particularly strong results on reasoning-intensive tasks. The reported grounding levels also offer a useful indication of answer reliability, with stronger grounding generally associated with higher accuracy. Finally, our results demonstrate the value of ontologies as knowledge sources: OA-RAG is competitive with, and sometimes outperforms, domain-specific RAG systems that draw on sources such as textbooks.

</details>

<a id="paper-194"></a>

### 194. [Executable Archaeology of Programs That Model Programming: Reanimating the Heuristic Compiler (1961) and KBEmacs (1984)](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36341v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36341

**作者：** Jeff Shrager、Kent M. Pitman、Mia Golec

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.HC、cs.PL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36341v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36341v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文以「可执行考古」方式重跑两个历史程序——Simon 1961 年的 Heuristic Compiler 与 1984 年的 KBEmacs，复现其当年的输出，并比较二者关于「人如何编程」的理论异同。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 无直接关联，但其「手段—目的分析搜索」与「复用已有 routine／cliche 组合」两种理论，可能为 latent 推理中搜索与知识先验的取舍、以及中间状态的复用抽象提供概念类比，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Executable Archaeology of Programs That Model Programming: Reanimating the Heuristic Compiler (1961) and KBEmacs (1984)

**abstract/A1：** Early AI programs were often also theories of how people think, and programs that wrote programs were, in part, theories of how people program. Here we report on the reanimation of two such programs that offer two seemingly different theories of programming and were developed for quite different purposes. The first, Herbert Simon's Heuristic Compiler (1961), was an experimental model of programming as problem-solving, applying the means-ends analysis methods of the General Problem Solver to investigate the nature of the programming task. The second, KBEmacs (1984), was a working prototype of an intelligent programming assistant developed by MIT's Programmer's Apprentice project, built on the view that expert programmers recognize and combine familiar patterns, or "cliches." It let a programmer build up programs by combining cliches through a stylized English command language. We approach both through the methodology of Executable Archaeology, the study of historical computational systems by running their original code, a process we call "reanimation." Our reanimation of the Heuristic Compiler reproduces the compiled code appearing in Simon's 1963 paper, and our reanimation of KBEmacs reproduces the SQRT sequence of its December 1984 self-test. Examined side by side, the two theories turn out to have much in common: the Heuristic Compiler also works by adapting existing routines, and Simon later argued that search alone requires a large body of programming knowledge. To highlight the reflexivity of this project, we deliberately used a modern AI-assisted programming tool, Anthropic's Claude Code (Opus 5.5), to carry out much of the detailed reanimation work, building on a significant base of existing software. The explicit, executable theories of programming that both systems embodied are largely absent from today's far more capable AI coding assistants.

</details>

<a id="paper-195"></a>

### 195. [MAREval: A Multi-Agent Framework for Evaluating Natural Language Recommendation Explanations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36389v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36389

**作者：** Reza Yousefi Maragheh、Jayesh Uddhav Kudase、Aysenur Inan、Ramin Giahi、Kai Zhao、Parth Parikh、Jianpeng Xu、Jason Cho、Evren Korpeoglu、Sushant Kumar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36389v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36389v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MAREval，一个由规划、主持、仲裁三类 LLM 智能体组成的推荐解释自动评估框架，结合 Chain of Debate 提示与蒙特卡洛采样来提升与人类判断的一致性。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉推理主线联系较弱，但其多轮采样的结果聚合与仲裁机制可能可供核查，用于多条 latent 推理轨迹的验证、汇聚与稳定性评估。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MAREval: A Multi-Agent Framework for Evaluating Natural Language Recommendation Explanations

**abstract/A1：** Evaluating natural language explanations in recommender systems is essential for fostering user trust, transparency, and engagement. However, existing evaluation approaches like human evaluations, while accurate, are resource-intensive and impractical at the scale required by modern recommendation platforms. Also, automated methods using single-agent LLMs suffer from prompt sensitivity and inconsistent outputs. To address these challenges, we propose MAREval, a structured multi-agent framework for evaluating recommendation explanations using large language models. MAREval orchestrates (i) a planner agent that uses a novel Chain of Debate (CoD) prompting strategy to coordinate agent roles and enforce logically consistent evaluation plans; (ii) a moderator agent that regulates discussions by mitigating prompt drift; and (iii) an arbitrator agent that aggregates outputs from multiple evaluation rounds and (iv) a Monte Carlo sampling method, improving robustness and alignment with human judgment. We conduct comprehensive evaluations on both public (TopicalChat) and proprietary recommendation datasets, demonstrating that MAREval outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Comprehensive experiments on a public benchmark and a proprietary e-commerce dataset show that MAREval improves alignment with human judgments over strong singleand multi-agent baselines. Stability analyses indicate substantially lower variability across repeated trials. In a large human-annotation gate, MAREval meets production quality thresholds where prior evaluators fall short, and online A/B testing demonstrates statistically significant improvements in engagement and revenue metrics. These results establish MAREval as a scalable and reliable solution for human-aligned evaluation of recommendation explanations in real-world systems

</details>

<a id="paper-196"></a>

### 196. [When Can a Learned Design Surrogate Be Trusted in a Self-Driving Microfluidics Laboratory? An Offline Closed-Loop Framework Built and Tested on Six Public Tables](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36395v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36395

**作者：** Zhiyuan Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36395v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36395v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在六个公共微流控数据表上构建离线闭环实验框架，把学习型设计代理的可信度、所需测量预算、到达目标时间与停止/升级决策表述为可测量指标，并以预注册流程和冻结协议检验。

**相关性简评：** 其关于「何时信任代理、测多少、先测什么、何时停止」的度量及无标签规则失败的负结果，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的决策形式化与证据获取评价提供参考，迁移可行性尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Can a Learned Design Surrogate Be Trusted in a Self-Driving Microfluidics Laboratory? An Offline Closed-Loop Framework Built and Tested on Six Public Tables

**abstract/A1：** Self-driving laboratories couple a learned design surrogate to an instrument, and on every new target domain it must answer four questions: when it can be trusted, how many target measurements it needs, which to make first, and when to stop adapting, switch models or escalate. We state a framework making each measurable (a competence ratio, a budget $k^\*(\\varepsilon)$, a time-to-target and a priced decision) and instantiate it without wet experiments on six public droplet-microfluidic and liposome tables as offline laboratories: 107 leave-one-group tiers whose rows are hidden labels. A sealed, pre-registered programme (two sealed negative validations and a pre-registered test on three more tables) and seven frozen closed-loop protocols, each run twice, yield 23 findings. Shift cost is stratified by shift type; no label-free statistic certifies individual candidates; random-split model-family rankings predicted neither the sealed nor the loop rankings. Warm-start residual boosting returns a surrogate to $1.25\\times$ its development reference within 20 target rows in 94% of chemistry tiers (27% under a strict noise-floor reading), 90% of laboratory and 40% of device tiers, and a budget planned from the shift type on other tables is not measurably worse on a new one. In the loop, one gain survives drift and shuffled-label controls on all tiers, carried by the sparsely sampled ones: proposing at once and warm-starting on every measurement reaches a 10% target in 2.80 measurements against 3.31 for the reference, and in 5.72 against 11.35 for random proposals on 301 hard units at a 3% tolerance. No label-free acquisition rule survives the shuffled-label control, no interval-aware proposal or other model family does better, and no threshold rule on the tested diagnostics beats always continuing once escalation is priced. No wet-lab validation is claimed.

**comment/C1：** 68 pages, 15 figures, 26 tables. Code, frozen protocols, deviation registers, number ledger and artifacts: https://github.com/LibertychaserUS/microfluidic-native-physics (tag v0.5.5-preprint)

</details>

<a id="paper-197"></a>

### 197. [kw-bench: Breaking Frontier Harnesses on Real-World Tasks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36405v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36405

**作者：** George Juraj Salapa

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36405v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36405v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 kw-bench，用真实业务（SAP 三代系统增值税五年重建）的原始系统导出数据考察 coding-agent harness 能否完成调查型知识工作，并采用确定性评分、不用 LLM 裁判，比较四种 harness 在同一模型下的表现与耗时。

**相关性简评：** 与视觉 LVR 无直接关系，但其「在长会话中维系大量弱信号跨步累积证据」的失败分析与确定性评分方式，可能为 LVR 的证据筛选、动态计算与长程状态保持评价提供参照，需核查其 harness 设计细节。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** kw-bench: Breaking Frontier Harnesses on Real-World Tasks

**abstract/A1：** Each new generation of frontier model pushes the edge of what is possible. Inside a harness -- a loop, a shell, a filesystem, the ability to write and run its own code -- a model can take on increasingly difficult work. A growing body of work treats the coding-agent harness as infrastructure for knowledge work rather than a coding tool, and a new wave of benchmarks tests it there. Most of them are generative: build a financial model, a memo, a spreadsheet from a brief, judged by a rubric or by another model. Few test the expensive part of knowledge work, which is investigative. In audit, tax, due diligence, regulatory reporting, claims or any dispute over numbers, the answer already exists -- hidden in incomplete and inconsistent evidence spread across systems -- and has to be recovered and defended to the cent. We introduce kw-bench, a benchmark built from real engagements specialists described to us. Its first task family is a five-year VAT reconstruction across three generations of SAP -- legacy ECC, a partial S/4HANA migration and a consultant's redesign -- delivered as raw system extracts: page headers, local number formats, partial link tables, no documentation of how anything connects. Grading is deterministic, with no LLM judge. We ran four harnesses -- Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode and micro-cc -- on the same model, DeepSeek v4.1 Flash, for two hours. The two independent, model-agnostic harnesses delivered (OpenCode 0.542, micro-cc 0.528); the two lab harnesses, away from their home model, delivered nothing (0.00 each). What defeated them was inertia: keeping many weak signals alive across a long session. On a frontier model the task collapses. micro-cc and Claude Code on Claude Opus 5.5 scored 0.9986 and 1.000, in 22 and 25 minutes. A weeks-long engagement now costs minutes and a few dollars; below the frontier, the harness decides how far a model gets.

**comment/C1：** 26 pages, 8 figures, 5 tables. Code and tasks: https://github.com/GSequist/kw-bench

</details>

<a id="paper-198"></a>

### 198. [Verifiable Efficiency Limits of Innate LLM Planning in Sliding Puzzles](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36415v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36415

**作者：** Yezhou Cheng、Charles Ling

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36415v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36415v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出可验证框架 SPE = R×Q×E，把 LLM 在 4×4 滑块拼图上单次无工具生成的完整规划效率分解为执行可靠性、合法方案协调性与动作经济性，并用轨迹回放和认证最优解核验。

**相关性简评：** 其「成功率×效率」分解与认证最优解的做法，可能与 LVR 中 THINK/STOP 的收益—成本评价及长 horizon 能力刻画相通，可供核查能否迁移为 latent 推理步数与计算预算的因式化评价；但任务为纯文本拼图，缺乏视觉状态证据。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Verifiable Efficiency Limits of Innate LLM Planning in Sliding Puzzles

**abstract/A1：** Tool-assisted planning success can reflect external search and verification, leaving a model's unaided planning ability unresolved. We isolate innate planning through complete plans generated in one response without tools or execution feedback. We propose a verifiable framework that decomposes success-weighted plan efficiency as $\\mathrm{SPE}=R\\times Q\\times E$: execution reliability, planning coordination among legal plans, and action economy among completed plans. Trajectory replay and certified optima verify these factors; the joint score includes unsuccessful attempts. We evaluate 8,400 full-plan outputs from seven model conditions on $4\\times4$ sliding puzzles, controlling exact horizon and obstacle count in the primary cohort. Models that complete every trial still become less economical at longer horizons, while higher efficiency among completed plans can coexist with poor goal attainment. SPE separates the overall capability hierarchy, while its factors distinguish coordination failure from inefficient completion. The framework makes long-horizon limitations in unaided planning quantitatively verifiable.

</details>

<a id="paper-199"></a>

### 199. [SO-101 Bench: Accessible Spatial Manipulation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36420v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36420

**作者：** Truman Hickok

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36420v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36420v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 构建基于真实 SO-101 数字孪生的仿真基准，用四类任务、138 个物体与 916 个评估回合考察语言条件下的空间操作，验证仿真与真机策略排序一致（ρ=1.00），并显示最佳 VLA 约 61%，而「写代码 + 学习抓取策略」的 LLM agent 达 83.2%。

**相关性简评：** 其中的遮挡记忆与 rotate-to-fit 子集需要跨时间维持空间信息，且外层 LLM harness 与学习策略分工带来大幅增益，可供核查「latent 状态 vs 外部工具/代码」的职责划分，但属具身操作设定，与 LVR 内部表示无直接检验。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** SO-101 Bench: Accessible Spatial Manipulation

**abstract/A1：** Low-cost arms such as the SO-101 have become the most common entry point to robot learning, yet no released policy performs spatially constrained, language-conditioned tasks on them out of the box, and existing SO-101 benchmarks run only on physical hardware with simple tasks and few held-out objects. We introduce SO-101 Bench, a simulated benchmark for language-conditioned spatial manipulation built on an Isaac Lab digital twin of a real SO-101 setup. It has four tasks scored as five families, 138 objects in seen, unseen-instance and unseen-class splits, 916 evaluation episodes with subsets for held-out appearances, rotate-to-fit planning and occlusion memory, and 3,629 simulated demonstrations on which every learned policy is fine-tuned under an equal compute budget. The twin ranks policies as the real robot does: five policies trained without real data, four VLAs and an LLM agent, keep their exact ranking when the same weights run on the physical arm in an unseen environment with unseen objects (Spearman rho = 1.00, MMRV = 0.000), although simulation overestimates success. Across nine VLAs and world-action models, the best learned policies score near 61%, with spatial placement, unseen object classes and rotate-to-fit planning remaining open. An LLM agent that writes code and hands grasping to a learned policy scores 83.2%, and an ablation shows that this lead comes from the choice of harness and the choice of language model.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://so101-bench.github.io/ ; code: https://github.com/5hadytru/so101\_bench

</details>

<a id="paper-200"></a>

### 200. [FORESEE-MS: Toward Prospective Spatial Forecasting of Future Multiple Sclerosis Lesions from Baseline FLAIR MRI](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36428v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36428

**作者：** Maryam Akhavan Aghdam、Sudip Mittal、Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz、Shahram Rahimi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36428v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36428v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该研究提出 FORESEE-MS 评估协议，以按折构建的人群先验图谱为基线，检验基线 FLAIR MRI 能否预测未来多发性硬化病灶出现的区域，并在 39 例 MSSEG-2 数据上比较五种 3D 模型与图谱的体素级判别和患者内 Top-K 定位表现。

**相关性简评：** 其「人群先验 vs 患者特异预测信息」的对照设计与患者内定位指标，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选和内部表示是否携带个体特异信息的评估提供借鉴线索，但属医学影像预测任务，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FORESEE-MS: Toward Prospective Spatial Forecasting of Future Multiple Sclerosis Lesions from Baseline FLAIR MRI

**abstract/A1：** Traditional multiple sclerosis (MS) image analysis has primarily focused on retrospectively detecting and segmenting lesions. This study investigates whether baseline FLAIR MRI can identify regions at risk for future lesion formation beyond population-level spatial patterns. We introduce FORESEE-MS, an evaluation protocol that uses a fold-specific population-prior atlas as a baseline and assesses predictions using pooled voxel-level discrimination (AUROC and AUPRC) and within-patient lesion localization (Recall@Top-K%), with patient-level bootstrap confidence intervals. We evaluated five deep learning models (3D U-Net, Attn+ResBlock+VAE, ViT, and two SwinUNETR variants) using 5-fold cross-validation on 39 subjects from the MSSEG-2 dataset. The atlas achieved the highest pooled voxel-level AUROC and AUPRC, with no deep learning model reliably outperforming it on either metric. However, the 3D U-Net and Attn+ResBlock+VAE achieved higher mean voxel-level within-patient Recall@Top-K% than the atlas at all evaluated thresholds, although most paired confidence intervals for the differences included zero. These findings suggest that baseline FLAIR may contain patient-specific predictive information, but in this small cohort, it remains uncertain whether this information improves lesion forecasting beyond a population-prior atlas.

</details>

### 201. [Simulated petrological labels enable probabilistic mineralogy prediction from whole-rock geochemistry](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36429v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36429

**作者：** Matthew J. Liu、Aaron D. Kaplan、Michael L. Whittaker、Kristin A. Persson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36429v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36429v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文用近百万条模拟岩石学标签监督一个三段式模型（自监督 transformer 编码器、预测矿物组合分布的头部、再校准层），从全岩化学成分预测矿物学，并检验零样本判别与少样本校准效果。

**相关性简评：** 其「不完美模拟器提供一对多标签监督、分布输出、少量实测标签再校准」的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态的训练监督、多假设表达与输出偏置修正。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Simulated petrological labels enable probabilistic mineralogy prediction from whole-rock geochemistry

**abstract/A1：** Modal mineralogy, the mineral identities and abundances in rocks, guides geological studies and industrial resource extraction. While measuring modal mineralogy is time-intensive, whole-rock chemical measurements furnish elemental concentrations at higher throughput. Predicting mineralogy from elemental data, however, poses a one-to-many inverse problem, and labeled mineralogical datasets are too sparse to train on at scale. We address both challenges by simulating petrological labels that express multiple formation conditions per composition and at a scale unattainable by measurements. Nearly one million simulated labels supervise the middle component of a three-part model: a transformer encoder self-supervised on roughly 788,000 whole-rock compositions, the simulation-trained heads that predict distributions over mineral assemblages, and a recalibration layer that aligns the predictions with measured mineralogy. Zero-shot on 640 volcanic rocks, the model discriminates the presence of most mineral categories and predicts which rocks are richer in each mineral. However, the predicted abundances are systematically offset from measured values. Recalibration fit on as few as 25 labeled rocks recovers 76 percent of the likelihood improvement from fitting on the full 368-rock fitting set, while abundance error responds more slowly (11.0% zero-shot and 9.5% with the full fitting set). This work demonstrates how a machine learning model in the geochemical sciences can both leverage an imperfect simulator and mitigate its systematic biases.

</details>

<a id="paper-202"></a>

### 202. [CoHARL: Co-Evolving Agent Policies and Executable Harnesses with Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36469v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36469

**作者：** Jianwen Chen、Peng Xia、Kaide Zeng、Siwei Han、Zhen-Yu Zhang、Charles Fleming、Xinyu Ye、Huaxiu Yao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36469v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36469v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CoHARL，用基于群体的强化学习让共享策略与一组可执行 harness 共同演化：策略在多个 harness 的 rollout 上学习，proposer 依据执行轨迹改写 harness 代码，在 SWE-rebench 等软件工程与终端任务上验证收益。

**相关性简评：** 其「产生训练经验的接口与策略共同演化」这一思路，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据获取与筛选接口、推理策略如何适配被呈现的证据这一问题相关，可供核查；但论文本身不涉及 latent 视觉状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoHARL: Co-Evolving Agent Policies and Executable Harnesses with Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents interact with their environments through executable harnesses that govern tool use and feedback. Optimizing a harness for a fixed policy overlooks how its behavior changes during learning, while training a policy under a fixed harness leaves the interface that generates its training experience unchanged. This motivates adapting the harness throughout policy training. We introduce CoHARL, a population-based reinforcement learning framework that co-evolves a shared policy and a population of executable harnesses. The policy learns from rollouts across the population, and a proposer uses its execution traces to revise harness code during training. CoHARL achieves a 27.6% mean resolved rate on SWE-rebench, improving over harness-only search by 6.4 percentage points. Experiments on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.0 show gains in software engineering and terminal-based tasks. Under matched training budgets, CoHARL also outperforms searching for a harness once before RL and alternating policy updates with revisions to a single active harness.

</details>

<a id="paper-203"></a>

### 203. [When to Intervene? An Empirical Study of Errors and Self-Recovery in LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36471v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36471

**作者：** Chiming Duan、Tong Jia、Yingxi Chen、Minghua He、Lingzhe Zhang、Ying Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36471v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36471v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文基于 1,259 条 agent 轨迹与 1,403 个错误事件构建 ReInBench，实证分析 LLM agent 出错的自我恢复频率、速度及其与任务完成的关系，并提出 ReCoF 预测某错误是否会自我恢复，以决定是否施加外部干预。

**相关性简评：** 「何时干预、何时等待自我恢复」与 LVR 中 STOP 决策、继续计算的收益判断在问题结构上相近，其可恢复性预测器或可供核查能否用来判断 latent 推理轨迹是否值得继续推进；但研究对象是 Web/computer-use 的文本动作序列，未涉及视觉证据表示。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When to Intervene? An Empirical Study of Errors and Self-Recovery in LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents interact with Web services to perform tasks on users' behalf. Errors during these interactions can disrupt execution and prevent task completion, motivating external intervention. However, agents may self-recover through continued execution, raising the question of when to intervene and when to wait for self-recovery. We present an empirical study of errors and self-recovery in LLM agents. We construct ReInBench, a dataset containing 1,259 agent traces and 1,403 error events across computer-use and Web tasks. Our study examines how often and how quickly agents self-recover, how self-recovery relates to subsequent execution and task completion, and whether self-recovery can be predicted when an error occurs. We find that 725 error events self-recover, often within a few steps, with self-recovery performance differing across Execution LLMs. Self-recovery can also help agents recover from subsequent errors of the same type and support overall task completion. Intervention should therefore prioritize errors unlikely to self-recover, while allowing agents to continue when self-recovery is likely. Building on these empirical findings, we propose ReCoF, a method that predicts whether an error will self-recover to inform whether additional intervention is needed. ReCoF achieves an average relative improvement of 9.02% in Macro-F1 over the strongest baseline on ReInBench. Our code and data can be found at https://method-notes.github.io/ReCoF/.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 8 figures, 2 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-204"></a>

### 204. [CogArena: Benchmarking Multimodal Agents on Interactive Behavioral Experiments](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36484v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36484

**作者：** Kianté Fernandez、Caitlin Chen、Jialu Zhou、Bartek Sadowski、Anthony C. Miceli、Ian Krajbich

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CY、cs.HC、q-bio.NC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36484v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36484v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 CogArena，汇集十个社会与行为科学实验范式，让多模态 agent 通过标准浏览器在与人相同的视觉刺激和响应界面上完成任务，并按「是否完成、表现多准、是否复现人类行为特征」三级评分。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 的表示与生成机制无直接联系，但其「完成任务≠复现行为特征」的评测拆分可能可供核查，用于区分多模态 agent 的界面操作、视觉证据使用与真实推理能力。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CogArena: Benchmarking Multimodal Agents on Interactive Behavioral Experiments

**abstract/A1：** Claims about the cognitive capabilities of large language models are typically tested by translating behavioral paradigms into text prompts, bypassing the visual interfaces and time pressure through which human participants actually engage with experiments. At the same time, AI agents can now complete online behavioral tasks and produce human-like data, which raises concerns that online datasets in the social and behavioral sciences are increasingly open to contamination. We introduce CogArena, a benchmark of ten experimental paradigms from the social and behavioral sciences. Agents complete the experiments through a standard web browser, working with the same visual stimuli and response interfaces that online participants use. A three-level scoring procedure evaluates whether the agent completed the task, how accurately it performed, and whether its data show the behavioral signatures each paradigm reliably produces in people. Across four multimodal models, each run ten times per task, we find that finishing an experiment and reproducing its signatures are different things. Many sessions did not finish, and those that did reproduced some signatures reliably and others not at all. CogArena provides the tasks, scoring code, and a public leaderboard for new submissions.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages, 4 figures, 11 tables. Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 (Evaluations &amp; Datasets Track). Code: https://github.com/kiante-fernandez/CogArena. Leaderboard: https://cog-arena.vercel.app

</details>

<a id="paper-205"></a>

### 205. [ATEMPO: Amortized Transfer Entropy Model Per-lag Over-Time](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36536v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36536

**作者：** Emma Andrews、Konstantinos Kontras、Keane Ong、Maarten De Vos、Gianmarco Mengaldo

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.IT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36536v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36536v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ATEMPO，用扩散模型构建按滞后与时间摊销的迁移熵估计器：训练时采样源与条件深度并显式条件于时间位置，单次拟合即可给出完整 TE 曲面，差分后检测信息到达延迟，并用于时序预测与脑机接口输入选择。

**相关性简评：** 迁移熵刻画源信息「何时」进入目标，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据的到达时机、证据价值筛选与动态计算分配提供表述；其以滞后/深度为条件、单网络覆盖整个深度网格的摊销方式，可供核查是否对应 latent 步数与推理深度的长度泛化线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ATEMPO: Amortized Transfer Entropy Model Per-lag Over-Time

**abstract/A1：** When one time series informs another, the delay at which that information arrives determines if models can utilize this relationship effectively. A source may be strongly predictive at one delay but uninformative at neighboring lags. Therefore, fixed input windows may obscure this structure by bundling informative and irrelevant history together. Transfer entropy (TE) can recover these delays by measuring how much a source's past tells us about a target beyond the target's own history. However, existing TE estimators require separate fits across source and conditioning depths, with further estimation on local windows to track delays over time. We introduce ATEMPO, a diffusion-based transfer entropy estimator amortized over lags and time. ATEMPO samples source and conditioning depths during training and conditions explicitly on temporal position, allowing a single score network to estimate the full TE surface without refitting. Differencing this surface recovers when information arrives. Across synthetic datasets with ground-truth TE, ATEMPO achieves the lowest mean relative error among baselines, and on datasets with known lags, ATEMPO achieves $0.98\\pm0.04$ lag-detection accuracy. ATEMPO requires a single fit across source depths $K=6$ and target depths $L=3$, whereas existing estimators require 18 separate fits to evaluate the same $K \\times L$ grid. On three systems with time-varying lag, ATEMPO is the only estimator to maintain perfect lag-detection accuracy across all evaluated window lengths. These recovered delays improve downstream forecasting: ATEMPO-selected inputs reduce MSE on WEATHER-5K by 7.25%, while 120 selected inputs outperform the benchmark's 240-feature default by 4.6%. Across three brain-computer interface tasks, using ATEMPO-selected lags achieves the highest or equal-best $R^2$, improving mean $R^2$ by 4.1% relative to the strongest competing baseline.

</details>

<a id="paper-206"></a>

### 206. [NextEval: An Agent-Based Solver and Benchmark for Derivative-Free Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36553v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36553

**作者：** Shuo Chen、Li Cui、Cunxin Huang、Ya-Feng Liu、Minghang Xie、Meng Xu、Zaikun Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36553v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36553v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 NextEval，一个由 LLM agent 与数值环境组成的无导数优化求解器：agent 依据历史评估记录和工具反馈选择工具、配置参数并分配评估预算，同时发布在同等预算与信息约束下比较不同 agent 配置的基准协议。

**相关性简评：** 「有限预算下选取信息量最大的评估点」及跨方法切换时保留共享历史与方法状态的设计，可能为 LVR 中 LOOK/STOP 的预算分配、证据获取与状态缓存提供类比，但需核查其非多模态设定下的可迁移性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** NextEval: An Agent-Based Solver and Benchmark for Derivative-Free Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Derivative-free optimization (DFO) addresses problems where derivatives are unavailable or costly to obtain. This setting often arises in simulation-based and experimental applications, where evaluating the black-box objective can itself be costly. Thus, it remains a central challenge in DFO to effectively exploit accumulated observations to select informative evaluation points under a limited budget. In this light, we introduce the NextEval solver, an agent-based DFO solver, which integrates the capabilities of a large language model (LLM) to guide the sequential selection of evaluation points. Specifically, we establish a system that combines an LLM agent with a numerical environment. The environment provides tools for analyzing past evaluations and executing numerical methods, while maintaining a shared evaluation history and method-specific state to support continuation across method switches. Using observations and tool feedback, the LLM agent selects tools, configures their parameters, and allocates the evaluation budget, while numerical methods compute and evaluate candidate points. Building on this system, we also introduce NextEval Bench, a benchmark protocol for comparing agent configurations on shared DFO tasks using the same numerical tools under matched evaluation budgets and information constraints. Numerical results on low-dimensional DFO problems demonstrate that the NextEval solver outperforms the state-of-the-art DFO baselines in function-evaluation efficiency.

**comment/C1：** 27 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-207"></a>

### 207. [Less is more: learning what to profile for GPU kernel optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36576v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36576

**作者：** Yihan Wang、Peiyu Liu、Runyu Chen、Wei Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36576v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36576v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文指出 GPU kernel 优化中并非 profiling 指标越多越好，且有用信息随 kernel 而异，因此提出 AutoProfiler：借助 LLM 生成候选修改、以优化反馈评测，迭代用 REWRITE/SPLIT 学出一棵确定性的、按 kernel 条件选择的 profiling 树，在预算约束下收集信息。

**相关性简评：** 其「按实例条件在预算内选择采集哪些证据」的树式选择与反馈驱动接受机制，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与 LOOK 决策提供类比，但领域为 kernel 优化、不涉及多模态推理，是否可迁移需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Less is more: learning what to profile for GPU kernel optimization

**abstract/A1：** Large language models have recently shown strong potential for automatically optimizing GPU kernels, yet their effectiveness depends critically on the hardware information available to the kernel optimization model. Existing systems typically rely on predefined profiling strategies, leaving what to profile largely outside the optimization process. We show that effective profiling is not simply about collecting more information: increasing the number of profiling metrics does not monotonically improve kernel optimization, while the most useful profiling information varies across kernels. Our theoretical analysis further shows that, under such heterogeneous profiling utility, kernel-conditioned selection can outperform any single fixed profiling configuration. Motivated by this observation, we propose AutoProfiler, a feedback-driven framework for learning a deterministic profiling tree from optimization feedback. The tree is iteratively refined through REWRITE and SPLIT operations, with an LLM generating candidate modifications and empirical evaluation determining their acceptance. Once constructed, the tree quickly gathers the required profiling information for any kernel, allowing the LLM rewriter to be invoked only once. Experiments on KernelBench show that our approach improves kernel optimization over fixed profiling configurations under constrained profiling budgets. These results suggest that hardware profiling should evolve from static metric collection toward profiling reasoning, making profiling itself a new dimension for improving kernel optimization. The codes are available at https://github.com/Satissss/AutoProfiler.

</details>

<a id="paper-208"></a>

### 208. [Follow the Herd: A Data-Driven Approach for Actionable Recourse](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36580v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36580

**作者：** Nishtha Madaan、Srikanta Bedathur

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36580v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36580v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 DDAR，用历史用户数据的轨迹训练一个动态模型，并在该模型下搜索能达到期望结果的低成本动作序列，从而无需人工设定动作代价即可给出可执行补救建议。

**相关性简评：** 以「在学得状态动态模型下按似然或代价搜索轨迹」为核心，可能为 latent 推理中的轨迹评分、候选筛选与停止决策提供概念参照，但领域相距较远，需核查其代价定义能否迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Follow the Herd: A Data-Driven Approach for Actionable Recourse

**abstract/A1：** As machine learning systems make increasingly greater decisions about people's lives, it is critical to provide users with actionable recourse about how they can achieve their desired outcomes. This problem is currently tackled by relying on expensive human supervision which involves hand-designing a cost function before searching for low-cost actions that achieve the desired outcome. In this paper, we propose \\textit{\`\`Data-Driven Action Recourse"} (DDAR), the first approach for providing action recourse without requiring costly supervision from humans about action costs. We build upon the notion that highly likely trajectories in the historical user data should be associated with lower costs. With this, DDAR first builds a dynamics model of historical user trajectories. Under this model, it then searches for trajectories that achieve the desired outcome. In experiments, we find that this simple approach recommends plausible action recourse without requiring human supervision, achieving lower action costs than the existing approaches.

</details>

<a id="paper-209"></a>

### 209. [LabWorld: Learning Interactive 4D Worlds for Scientific Experimentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36592v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36592

**作者：** Suozhi Huang、Kelsey Fu、Binxu Li、Luna Lyu、Kaize Wu、Wenbo Lu、David Smerkous、Jiahan Li、Siqi Zhu、Zhizhou Sha、Jiacheng Guo、Zhixuan Liang、Shilong Liu、Le Cong、Mengdi Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36592v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36592v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 LabWorld，一个由智能体驱动的 Real2Sim 框架，从单目 RGB 视频重建可交互的 4D 实验室世界，包括可复用 3D 资产、度量场景、时变物体状态与操作事件，并在移液与涡旋视频上以 Mask IoU、PCK-AUC 及约束满足率进行评测。

**相关性简评：** 其将时变物体状态与操作事件重建为显式可编辑状态的做法，可能为 LVR 中「保留哪些任务必要的视觉细节、如何以结构化状态替代隐式像素」提供参照，但该工作本身不做推理，联系较间接，需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LabWorld: Learning Interactive 4D Worlds for Scientific Experimentation

**abstract/A1：** Autonomous scientific agents must not only reason about experiments but also interact with the physical environments in which they are executed. Existing laboratory simulators typically rely on manually constructed assets and predefined procedures, while laboratory videos capture real equipment, layouts, and interactions but leave this information implicit in pixels. We introduce LabWorld, an agent-driven Real2Sim framework for reconstructing interactive 4D laboratory worlds from monocular RGB video. LabWorld recovers reusable 3D assets, a metric scene, time-varying object states, and procedure events. Multi-frame observations provide complementary evidence for asset and scene reconstruction, while motion reconstruction combines visual and geometric observations with event constraints encoding the spatial and temporal requirements of laboratory interactions. We evaluate LabWorld on real pipetting and vortexing videos collected in our laboratory against general-purpose agentic and dedicated reconstruction baselines. For pipetting, LabWorld achieves a mean Mask IoU of 0.501, exceeding the strongest baseline by 0.033. For vortexing, it achieves a mean Tube-Length-Normalized PCK-AUC of 0.638, exceeding the strongest baseline by 0.035. On five pipetting videos, event-conditioned reconstruction increases the fraction of modeled physical constraints satisfied from 14.3% (5/35) to 100% (35/35). The resulting worlds expose geometry, motion, articulation, and events as explicit, editable state for replay, inspection, and future embodied scientific systems.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 4 figures. Suozhi Huang and Kelsey Fu contributed equally

</details>

<a id="paper-210"></a>

### 210. [Dream-COP: Guiding Algorithm Discovery for Combinatorial Optimization Problems in an Experience World](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36618v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36618

**作者：** Kuan Xu、Jianming Wang、Ni Zhang、Yaoxin Wu、Yong Wang、Zhiguang Cao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36618v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36618v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Dream-COP：把历史搜索轨迹及其结果构成「经验世界」，通过在其中回放训练 policy agent 学得可执行的探索策略，用来指导 discovery agent 生成与改进组合优化程序，在五个 COP 上用更少搜索请求取得更优算法。

**相关性简评：** 其以回放经验学习「如何探索、如何分配搜索预算」的策略，可能为 LVR 的动态计算、停止决策与轨迹训练的信用分配提供类比线索，但能否迁移到多模态推理状态仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dream-COP: Guiding Algorithm Discovery for Combinatorial Optimization Problems in an Experience World

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) enable algorithm discovery for combinatorial optimization problems (COPs) through iterative program generation and evaluation. A central challenge is how to organize search to discover better algorithms within a limited budget. Our key insight is that past search trajectories and their recorded outcomes form an experience world, which can be used to facilitate the COP solving through replay. We introduce Dream-COP, in which a \\emph{policy agent} learns executable policies through \\emph{dreaming} in this experience world to guide a \\emph{discovery agent} in generating and refining COP programs. Specifically, policy replay provides feedback on the quality of the programs revealed and the search steps used, to guide policy selection more effectively. Across five COPs, \\textsc{Dream} discovers better algorithms than fixed exploration in both heuristic component design and complete solver generation. Particularly, it matches the optimal values on KP and reduces the gap on OBPP from 2.112\\% to 1.199\\% while using 42.5\\% fewer search requests. Moreover, the learned exploration policies transfer effectively across different COPs with the assitance of the experience world.

</details>

<a id="paper-211"></a>

### 211. [Polarity--Time Interaction and Multi-Level Reliability for Event-Based Spacecraft Pose Estimation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36625v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36625

**作者：** Yonglong Zhang、Zongwu Xie、Yang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36625v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36625v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出面向事件相机航天器位姿估计的可靠性感知框架：用正事件、负事件与时间近因三分支表示定位关键点并估计对应精度，结合事件边缘与 CAD 投影一致性做局部位姿精化，并在序列层面用十维描述子调节各观测的权重。

**相关性简评：** 其按对应关系可靠性加权几何求解、并以描述子为连续多次观测定权的做法，可能为 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选、多步观测贡献分配与置信度门控提供可供核查的线索，但领域与推理状态生成相距较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Polarity--Time Interaction and Multi-Level Reliability for Event-Based Spacecraft Pose Estimation

**abstract/A1：** Sparse event responses produce uneven keypoint errors whose effects on spacecraft pose depend on correspondence geometry and the reliability of successive observations. We present a reliability-aware framework connecting polarity--time localization, geometric recovery, event--CAD refinement, and trajectory optimization. A three-branch representation combines positive events, negative events, and recent-event timing through cross-branch attention and gating, followed by spatiotemporal aggregation. An ordered semantic-point head estimates relative correspondence precision for geometrically constrained pose recovery. Event-edge and CAD-projection agreement then guide local pose refinement under keypoint-geometry safeguards. At the sequence level, a ten-dimensional descriptor combines heatmap shape, reprojection consistency, and geometric conditioning to set time-aligned observation scales. On the official SPADES challenge test set, the complete system achieves a combined pose score of 0.0474, approximately 5.4\\% below PASAN+IRIS. Validation ablations reduce the score from 0.0772 for base localization with uniform PnP to 0.0514 for the complete cascade; on identical single-frame observations, quality weighting reduces the score from 0.0549 with constant-weight optimization to 0.0514.

</details>

<a id="paper-212"></a>

### 212. [TACO-Graph: Automated Construction of Text-Atributed Graphs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36650v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36650

**作者：** Amiri Hayes、Kristina Wicke

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36650v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36650v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 TACO-Graph：将多属性表格数据转为 text-attributed graph 时，枚举候选构图方案（节点实体、边关系、节点文本），用小规模代表性子集做快速代理评测打分，再训练可解释决策树预测构图质量，用于节点分类与 GraphRAG 检索。

**相关性简评：** 其「哪些实体成为节点、哪些关系成为边、节点承载何种文本」的选择问题与用廉价代理评测筛选候选表示，可能为 LVR 中视觉实体/关系如何写入内部状态、以及候选潜在表示的低成本比较提供评价思路，可供核查迁移价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TACO-Graph: Automated Construction of Text-Atributed Graphs

**abstract/A1：** Graph-structured representations of text data have become foundational across machine learning, yet it remains unclear how to systematically construct a graph from a given dataset for a target task. Choices about which entities become nodes, which relationships become edges, and what text each node contains can substantially affect downstream performance. In practice, finding optimal constructions often requires expert knowledge or manual trial and error. We introduce TACO-Graph, a decision tree framework for learning which graph construction choices are most effective for a given dataset and task. Specifically, we focus on text-attributed graphs (TAGs) constructed from multi-attribute tabular data. TACO-Graph enumerates valid TAG representations, scores each using fast proxy evaluation on small representative subsets, and trains an interpretable decision tree on those scores. Across five datasets spanning citation networks, product reviews, and bibliographic relationships, our decision trees predict construction quality on separately drawn evaluation samples with mean Spearman $ρ$ above $0.9$ for node classification and above $0.8$ for GraphRAG retrieval. Our trees systematically identify which constructions perform better across both GNN and GraphRAG tasks, and the full pipeline is released as an open-source library to support evaluation on new tasks and datasets at https://github.com/AmiriHayes/text\_attributed\_graphs. TACO-Graph provides a systematic and interpretable guide for determining how to convert tabular data to text-attributed graphs for downstream tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-213"></a>

### 213. [The View from Nowhere: Population-Centered Agent Evolution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36660v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36660

**作者：** Shuo Yan、Ningning Hou、Haoyu He、Haochen Pei、Shang Gao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36660v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36660v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 Population-Centered Evolution，把群体中的任务记录与自我修改历史蒸馏成 population block，用于指导单个父代 agent 的 harness 改进，并在 SWE-bench Verified 与 Polyglot 上检验其相对群体演化基线的提升。

**相关性简评：** 论文强调「群体覆盖」与「最佳个体」之间的差距，并把跨个体成功/失败模式当作诊断证据；这可能为 LVR 中 latent 轨迹的贡献分配与证据筛选提供评价视角，但论文本身不涉及视觉或多模态推理，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The View from Nowhere: Population-Centered Agent Evolution

**abstract/A1：** Self-improving AI systems can revise the designs that govern their own behavior. Yet existing approaches center improvement on selected parent agents and their evolutionary experience, leaving the broader population largely underused. We identify a substantial gap between population coverage (i.e., the tasks solved by at least one evolved variant) and the performance of the best individual agent. This gap shows that useful capabilities and diagnostic evidence emerge across the population without being consolidated into its leading agents. These observations motivate the view from nowhere, a principle for agent evolution: an agent should be improved by interpreting its behavior through population-wide patterns of success, failure, and prior self-modification. We instantiate this principle as Population-Centered Evolution (PCE). PCE distils task records and modification histories from the population into a population block, which captures recurring failures, complementary successes, and the effects of previously attempted modifications to guide improvements to a selected parent's harness. Under matched models, initial harnesses, and evolution schedules, PCE achieves 72.1% success on SWE-bench Verified and 96.7% on Polyglot, improving over a strong group-evolution baseline by 3.2 and 5.9 percentage points. In our evaluation, PCE reduces the gap between population coverage and best-agent performance by one-third on SWE-bench and closes it on Polyglot. These results suggest that treating the population as evidence--not merely as candidates for selection--provides a complementary principle for agent evolution.

</details>

<a id="paper-214"></a>

### 214. [Learning Where to Trust: Reliability-Guided Expert Routing and Velocity Gating for 3D Deformable Registration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36672v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36672

**作者：** Chenyu Guo、Wenjie Dai、Yiqian Chang、Zhiyuan Zhang、Yunfan Wei、Shuhan Qi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36672v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36672v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ReliMorph 三维形变配准框架：用局部相关分布刻画对应关系的模糊性，据此对空间专家进行路由，并对残差速度做门控，在由粗到细的金字塔迭代中用逆一致性反馈更新可靠性信号。

**相关性简评：** 其「按可靠性筛选空间上下文并调节更新强度」的思路，可能与 LVR 中视觉证据可信度筛选及 latent 状态增量控制相关，但属医学配准领域，迁移性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning Where to Trust: Reliability-Guided Expert Routing and Velocity Gating for 3D Deformable Registration

**abstract/A1：** Deformable image registration relies on accurate anatomical correspondences. However, explicitly coupling local matching reliability with spatial context selection and deformation update strength remains insufficiently explored in learning-based registration. This challenge is particularly pronounced in homogeneous, weakly textured, and severely deformed regions, where ambiguous matches may induce erroneous deformation updates. We propose ReliMorph, a reliability-routed symmetric framework for 3D deformable registration that couples correspondence assessment with spatial context selection and update strength.Local correlation distributions characterize correspondence ambiguity using shared anatomical features with modality-specific adaptation. These reliability cues guide spatial expert fusion and gate residual velocities during coarse-to-fine refinement. Updated inverse-inconsistency feedback guides routing and gating at finer pyramid levels. To our knowledge, ReliMorph is the first 3D deformable registration framework to jointly condition spatial expert routing and residual velocity gating on correspondence reliability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReliMorph consistently outperforms competing methods in anatomical overlap, highlighting its effectiveness across unimodal and multimodal registration tasks.

</details>

<a id="paper-215"></a>

### 215. [PsyJev: Psychological Analysis via System-1 Judgment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36689v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36689

**作者：** Fangyuan Liu、Sirui Zhao、Xiaohao Wang、Guo Cheng、Peng Zhang、Enhong Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36689v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36689v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文整理涵盖 13 个数据集、82 个问题的心理学内容分析基准 PsyJudgeBench，并提出单步判定的 System-1 模型 PsyJev，直接输出请求指定的答案分布，通过联合监督训练与 JA-RLCD（logit 空间奖励优化）适配异构心理判断任务。

**相关性简评：** 其「单步直接给出答案分布」并以推理成本与概率质量作为分析维度，可能为 LVR 中「是否值得多步/latent 推理」以及答案置信度如何支撑停止决策提供廉价对照线索，但全文不涉及视觉证据或内部 latent 状态，联系较弱，需核查其概率质量分析的具体设定。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PsyJev: Psychological Analysis via System-1 Judgment

**abstract/A1：** Self-disclosures on the Web support diverse psychological analyses, from recognizing expressed experiences to identifying symptom-relevant information. We study a shared, question-conditioned decision interface for this heterogeneous problem in content analysis. We organize PsyJudgeBench, comprising 13 annotated datasets and 82 questions across six psychological areas, while preserving source-specific criteria and masks for unobserved labels. We introduce PsyJev, a System-1 model that directly produces distributions over answers specified in each request. Joint supervised training establishes the shared judgment function, followed by Judgment-Anchored RLCD (JA-RLCD), which adapts logit-space reward optimization to heterogeneous psychological judgments through balanced supervision, selective retention of correct SFT predictions, and question-group reward optimization. The Qwen3.5-based 2B, 4B, and 9B models achieve 64.33, 66.21, and 67.56 average test F1, respectively. The 9B model exceeds the strongest external comparison by 2.54 points. Controlled 2B comparisons examine the training stages and anchored refinement. Further analyses examine question-conditioned judgment, inference cost, and probability quality, complemented by qualitative comparisons across models.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 5 figures, 14 tables

</details>

<a id="paper-216"></a>

### 216. [HeteGCD: Statistics-Aware Dual-Prototype Learning for Generalized Category Discovery on Heterophilous Graphs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36696v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36696

**作者：** Xuanzhi Xi、Tongze Zhang、Zhili Qin、Yulin Tao、Hongliang Wang、Zhong Zhang、Qinli Yang、Junming Shao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36696v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36696v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 HeteGCD，面向异配图上的广义类别发现（GCD），用门控双流编码器融合节点属性与局部属性差异统计，并以双原型分类器结合特征相似度与分布一致性区分已知与新型类别。

**相关性简评：** 图 GCD 与视觉多模态主线相距较远，但其在无新类监督下融合邻居统计信号与分布一致性的做法，可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选与 latent 状态的原型/一致性使用提供概念线索，需进一步核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HeteGCD: Statistics-Aware Dual-Prototype Learning for Generalized Category Discovery on Heterophilous Graphs

**abstract/A1：** Heterophilous graphs are prevalent in real-world applications, where connected nodes often exhibit dissimilar attributes or belong to different classes, posing significant challenges to conventional graph learning methods based on homophily. This limitation becomes particularly pronounced in generalized category discovery (GCD), which requires models to recognize known classes and discover novel ones using labels from known classes only. However, existing graph GCD methods typically rely on aforementioned homophily-based inductive biases, which are ill-suited to heterophilous graphs. Meanwhile, although current heterophilous graph learning methods exploit diverse neighborhood signals, their selection and fusion are often guided by supervision across all classes, including novel classes whose labels are unavailable in GCD. These limitations raise a key question: how can heterogeneous neighborhood information be effectively leveraged for category discovery without any novel-class supervision? To answer this question, in this study we propose HeteGCD, a new statistics-aware dual-prototype learning perspective for generalized category discovery on heterophilous graphs. The key idea is to examine both statistics of local attribute differences and neighborhood class distribution to extract discriminative information in feature and probability spaces simultaneously. Specifically, a gated dual-stream encoder integrates both node attributes and local attribute-difference statistics to learn informative node representations. Afterwards, a dual-prototype classifier combines feature-based similarity with distribution-based consistency to support category discovery. Experiments on five heterophilous graphs show that HeteGCD achieves competitive performance against 24 baseline combinations.

</details>

<a id="paper-217"></a>

### 217. [Conservative Dueling Bandits: Preference Learning under Baseline Constraints](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36708v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36708

**作者：** Rohan Deb、Mohammad Ghavamzadeh、Arindam Banerjee

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36708v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36708v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究保守的上下文对偶赌博机：每轮展示的两个候选中较优者的累积得分须不低于可信基线的（1-α）比例，并指出直接照搬 CLUCB 的安全检验会导致线性 regret。作者提出 MaxInP-BC 与 BTIGW-BC，借助效用差和在线逻辑回归 oracle 实现随时安全、有限回退与次线性 regret。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 无直接关联，但「可信基线 + 有限回退且回退仍带来信息」的保守探索框架，可能为 latent 推理中的停止决策与安全回退策略提供概念参照，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Conservative Dueling Bandits: Preference Learning under Baseline Constraints

**abstract/A1：** Contextual dueling bandits learn from pairwise comparisons, the same feedback used to fine-tune language models from human preferences. Standard algorithms explore aggressively and can show users two poor options. We study conservative contextual dueling bandits, which require that, at every round, the cumulative score of the better displayed item stay above a $(1-α)$ fraction of a trusted baseline's. An item's score is its probability of beating the best available item, so the constraint depends only on utility differences and needs no absolute rewards. This also changes how safety can be certified: we show that transferring the safety test of the conservative bandit algorithm CLUCB unchanged can incur linear regret, even when the baseline's utilities are known exactly.
