For linear utilities, we develop MaxInP-BC, which certifies scores through utility gaps and, when it must fall back, pairs the baseline with an informative partner so that fallback rounds still drive learning. It is safe with high probability at all times, with regret $\\tilde{O}(d\\sqrt{T}/κ)$ plus an additive cost of conservatism that does not grow with the horizon. For general utility classes, we develop BTIGW-BC, which turns the guarantee of an online logistic-regression oracle into cumulative safety certificates. It is safe at all times, makes finitely many fallbacks, and has sublinear regret whenever the oracle's logistic regret is sublinear. In experiments, both satisfy the constraint, which their unconstrained counterparts often violate, at a moderate cost in regret.

</details>

<a id="paper-218"></a>

### 218. [Readout and Operating-Point Sensitivity in Event-Stream Candidate Selection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36743v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36743

**作者：** Yifan Shuai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36743v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36743v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文在 DailyDVS-200 事件流片段上比较冻结编码器（DINO、MobileNet、LeJEPA 等）用于“是否触发录制”的候选选择，系统考察读出方式（CLS 与 patch 均值）、累积时长、视角数、阈值校准及重复划分对捕获率与低误报部分 AUC 的影响，并报告未发现 JEPA 特有优势。

**相关性简评：** 其“读出方式与工作点会改变表示比较结论”的负面结果及多重比较校正做法，可能启发 LVR 中比较 latent 内容（池化选择、阈值设定、低误报区间指标）与触发/停止决策评价时的可核查方法学风险，但不涉及语言模型推理本身。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Readout and Operating-Point Sensitivity in Event-Stream Candidate Selection

**abstract/A1：** Event-triggered recording needs a useful combination of target capture and false triggers, yet representation comparisons can change with readout and threshold calibration. We evaluate real event accumulations from 520 DailyDVS-200 clips and 47 participants using participant-disjoint training, validation, and testing. The original study varies frozen encoder scale, accumulation duration, and temporal view count; a post-review extension adds two supervised-pretrained convolutional encoders, a 61,586-parameter trained CNN with three seeds, ten repeated participant partitions, and calibration sensitivity. At 50 ms/eight views under the primary handshake-or-fall policy, DINO-S CLS captures 101/132 targets with 40/388 false triggers, versus 78/132 and 35/388 for patch mean. DINO-B CLS has lower selected-threshold capture but higher fold-wise low-FPR partial AUC (0.746 versus 0.732). MobileNetV3-Small captures 97/132 with 53/388 false triggers, demonstrating why lightweight baselines and actual operating points matter. Across ten dependent repartitions, mean normalized partial AUC is 0.738 for DINO-B CLS and 0.674 for MobileNet. The original LeJEPA S-to-B capture difference is 8.3 points, but its multiplicity-adjusted interval includes zero. Duration and view-count effects remain policy dependent. We find no established JEPA-specific advantage. The evidence concerns offline action-defined candidate selection on previously used development data; it does not establish personal recording value, continuous false alarms per hour, or eyewear energy.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 4 figures, including supplementary material

</details>

<a id="paper-219"></a>

### 219. [Dependency Graphs of Mathematical Arguments: Representation, Structural Measurement, and Proof Review](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36776v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36776

**作者：** Eunji Park

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36776v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36776v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出一个借助 LLM 的、可溯源数学论证表示与度量框架：将陈述、等式、假设与构造组织成带证据和审阅状态的依赖 DAG，定义深度、bulk 等结构度量并给出 union 递推与保深度的可达性条件，配套版本化的提示协议与评估设计，但未报告实证收益。

**相关性简评：** 论证依赖图、深度/bulk 度量与「精化是否保结构」的分析，可能为 latent 推理状态的结构表示、步骤贡献分配与推理轨迹审查提供概念参照；但该文不涉及视觉证据或 latent 生成，可供核查的程度有限。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Dependency Graphs of Mathematical Arguments: Representation, Structural Measurement, and Proof Review

**abstract/A1：** We propose a source-grounded framework for representing and measuring mathematical arguments with assistance from large language models. It includes numbered and unnumbered statements, equations, assumptions, and constructions, distinguishing source occurrences from contextual states and inference records. Proposed dependencies retain source evidence and review status. A measurement contract fixes manuscript versions, boundaries, granularity, proof choices, and macro anchors. On a selected finite directed acyclic graph, depth is the longest prerequisite-path length, and bulk counts distinct ancestor edges. We give a union recurrence that accounts for shared subarguments and identify reachability conditions preserving depth and the macro skeleton; unrestricted refinement need not preserve depth or bulk. A standardized, versioned prompt protocol separates annotation discovery, deterministic recomputation, and review assistance. Cross-paper links support a research agenda on theorem co-use, rare combinations, and connections between dependency communities. These patterns motivate probabilistic premise suggestions and review of possible omissions or unchecked application conditions in human-written and AI-generated proofs. Further applications include source-linked reading support and comparison of individual and collaborative argument shapes. We provide an evaluation design rather than empirical evidence of these benefits. Suggested premises require mathematical validation, and the measures describe a specified representation rather than intrinsic mathematical difficulty, originality, or importance.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages

</details>

<a id="paper-220"></a>

### 220. [API-TAIL: Detecting Model Substitution in LLM APIs through Temperature-Aligned Likelihood Tails](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36801v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36801

**作者：** Haocheng Mei、Yuchuan Tian、Ye Yuan、Chao Xu、Allen Chen、Kai Han、Yunhe Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36801v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36801v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 API-TAIL，一种面向商业 LLM API 的审计方法：先用单一有效温度对齐整批标准化 surprisal，吸收合法解码与服务配置带来的整体分布偏移，再依据剩余尾部响应数量判断是否存在部分请求被替换为廉价模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「整批典型性对齐 + 尾部响应异常计数」的复合零假设检验思路，可能可供迁移到 LVR 中核查回答是否真的由所声称的视觉条件支持（如证据被忽略或旁路时响应呈现分布异常），但与原研究主线无直接交集，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** API-TAIL: Detecting Model Substitution in LLM APIs through Temperature-Aligned Likelihood Tails

**abstract/A1：** Commercial language model APIs return only text, allowing a provider to cut costs by quietly routing a fraction of user requests to a cheaper substitute model while billing for the advertised one. Detecting such partial substitution is challenging because honest providers also alter the output distribution through undocumented decoding and serving choices, such as temperature scaling, sampling truncation, repetition and presence penalties, and default system prompts, forcing an audit to test against a composite null of legitimate configurations rather than a single reference. Our key insight is that these legitimate variations primarily shift response typicality across the entire batch, whereas partial substitution injects individual responses that remain anomalous under the claimed model. Building on this, we introduce API-TAIL, a typicality audit that first absorbs batch-level shifts by aligning a single effective temperature to center standardized surprisal scores across the audited batch, and then flags the endpoint if the number of remaining tail responses exceeds a threshold calibrated across legitimate configurations. Evaluated on real-world commercial APIs, API-TAIL reliably controls false alarms across diverse honest providers while detecting even low-rate partial substitution with a small query budget, substantially outperforming existing rank- and MMD-based audits that lose detection power under the same composite calibration.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://github.com/TokenRhythm/API-TAIL

</details>

<a id="paper-221"></a>

### 221. [An Incremental Knowledge-Graph Learner Digital Twin for Evidence-Grounded Early Warning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36820v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36820

**作者：** Ngoc Luyen Le、Marie-Hélène Abel、Bertrand Laforge

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36820v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36820v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出用轻量知识图谱实现的增量「学习者数字孪生」：在时间检查点重放学习轨迹与测评记录，把特征、归因、告警、证据条目和原始记录都落成可查询图实体，并以图查询抽取预测特征，在学习分析基准上按时间协议评估。

**相关性简评：** 其把风险决策与特征归因、证据条目、原始记录串联成可查证据链、并在检查点增量更新状态的做法，可能为 LVR 中「证据筛选—归因—答案」的可核查评价提供线索；但任务与模态都与视觉推理相距较远，借鉴性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** An Incremental Knowledge-Graph Learner Digital Twin for Evidence-Grounded Early Warning

**abstract/A1：** Early-warning models in learning analytics can identify learners at risk of failure or withdrawal, but the resulting alerts are often only weakly connected to inspectable learning evidence. A risk score may indicate that intervention is needed without showing which traces, assessment events, or temporal patterns justify the alert. This paper introduces an incremental Learner Digital Twin for evidence-grounded early warning, implemented as a lightweight knowledge graph. The twin is updated at temporal checkpoints by replaying learning traces and assessment records, and it serves both as a feature-extraction substrate and as an explanation-grounding substrate. Predictive features are derived through graph-mediated queries, while features, attributions, alerts, evidence items, and raw records are represented as queryable graph entities. The framework is instantiated and evaluated on a public learning-analytics benchmark under a temporal protocol. The results show that graph-mediated feature extraction preserves the predictive behavior of equivalent raw-table features across model families and checkpoints. Generated alerts are materialized as evidence chains linking risk decisions to feature attributions, evidence items, and raw platform records. The study shows that early-warning prediction can be routed through a semantic and provenance-aware representation while preserving predictive performance and adding traceability, verifiability, and evidence-grounded explanation.

</details>

<a id="paper-222"></a>

### 222. [Conservative Policy Iteration For Zero-Sum POSGs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36847v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36847

**作者：** Erwan C. Escudie、Matthia Sabatelli、Jilles Steeve Dibangoye

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.GT

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36847v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36847v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出面向有限时程零和部分可观测随机博弈的近似策略迭代算法：为每方维护一个策略，沿当前联合策略诱导的占用轨迹联合更新，用单个支撑超平面替代中心价值函数近似，并交替进行局部策略改进、保守更新与持续探索。

**相关性简评：** 以占用状态（本质是历史/信念上的分布）而非单一价值函数承载不确定性，并用保守更新约束状态漂移，可能为 LVR 中 latent 推理状态的表示、逐步更新以及探索与停止的取舍提供概念线索，可迁移性尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Conservative Policy Iteration For Zero-Sum POSGs

**abstract/A1：** Occupancy-state reductions make dynamic programming possible for zero-sum partially observable stochastic games (zs-POSGs). Existing point-based solvers usually optimise a central value function. This restores Bellman recursion, but it requires point-based updates over high-dimensional occupancy spaces. We introduce an approximate policy-iteration algorithm for finite-horizon zs-POSGs. The method maintains one policy per player and updates both jointly along occupancy trajectories induced by conservative policy updates. Instead of approximating a full central value function, each update uses a single supporting hyperplane induced by the current joint policy. The algorithm alternates local policy improvements, conservative updates, and persistent exploration. Computation is therefore concentrated on occupancy regions where the current joint policy is exploitable. On finite-horizon zero-sum POSG benchmarks, the method is competitive at small horizons and often improves the runtime-exploitability trade-off at larger horizons compared with PBVI, HSVI, and CFR+.

</details>

<a id="paper-223"></a>

### 223. [Evolving Agent Teams](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36848v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36848

**作者：** Shiyi Cao、Ziming Mao、Dacheng Li、Joseph E. Gonzalez、Ion Stoica

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36848v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36848v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出「Evolving Agent Teams」多智能体框架：由中央监督者诊断局部最优并重组智能体团队，并以持久文件系统沉淀学习文档、演化历史与团队产物，用于从零优化 GPU kernel。

**相关性简评：** 「诊断停滞→重组计算资源＋持久经验状态」的控制结构可能为 LVR 中动态计算分配、停止与策略切换的决策提供类比线索，但其与多模态推理状态的实际关联仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Evolving Agent Teams

**abstract/A1：** The rapid progress of software engineering agents drives interest in applying them to open-ended problems such as GPU kernel optimization. However, we find that existing coding agents converge to local optima on real-world, expert-written kernels (e.g., FlashInfer). We introduce Evolving Agent Teams, a multi-agent framework that escapes such local optima. It features (1) a central supervisor that diagnoses each local optimum and restructures a team of agents; and (2) a persistent file system that comprises a learning document, an evolution history, and per-team artifacts, grounding restructuring with accumulated experience. Under a matched token budget, Evolving Agent Teams consistently outperforms strong coding-agent baselines. With an extended token budget, Evolving Agent Teams generates a CUDA kernel that reaches 1.2x mean speedup over the production FlashInfer MLA paged decode kernel from scratch and wins on 36 of 47 production workloads from FlashInfer-Bench (0.86-0.99x on the remaining 11). It also demonstrates superior performance on tasks such as KDA prefill on AMD MI350X (3.01x over vLLM) and NVIDIA B200 (3.44x over FlashKDA), MoE expert-offload serving on Apple silicon (30.3 tokens/s decode for Qwen3.8-Flash-Next in MLX 4-bit on a 48 GB M4 Pro), and RoboDojo robot manipulation (62.1 on held-out seeds vs. 55.3 for the best leaderboard entry). Code is available at https://github.com/caoshiyi/EAT.

</details>

<a id="paper-224"></a>

### 224. [Evaluating AlphaEarth Embeddings for Crop Classification: Separability, Temporal Dynamics, and Cross-Regional Transferability](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36857v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36857

**作者：** Priyanka Goyal、Maitreyee Sar、Adway Mitra、Manjira Sinha

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36857v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36857v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一个与表示无关的评估框架，从几何可分性、时间动态、空间稳健性与跨区域泛化四方面比较 AlphaEarth 嵌入与 Sentinel-2 反射率、NDVI 物候及谐波特征在美印作物分类中的表现，发现嵌入分布可分性强但跨区域迁移较弱。

**相关性简评：** 其 Fisher 比、Bhattacharyya/Mahalanobis 距离与残差空间自相关等指标，可能可供核查 latent 视觉状态是否保留任务必要信息，以及 latent 相似度度量与分布对齐对跨域泛化的影响。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Evaluating AlphaEarth Embeddings for Crop Classification: Separability, Temporal Dynamics, and Cross-Regional Transferability

**abstract/A1：** Earth Observation (EO)-based agricultural monitoring is shifting from pixel-level spectral analysis toward representation-driven modeling, yet systematic evaluation of learned representations across spatial, temporal, and domain-generalization dimensions remains limited. Geospatial foundation models generate high-dimensional embeddings encoding spatio-temporal land-surface properties, but their utility for agricultural applications remains insufficiently understood. This study presents a representation-agnostic evaluation framework integrating geometric separability, temporal dynamics, spatial robustness, and cross-regional generalisation. A 64-dimensional AlphaEarth embedding space is benchmarked against Sentinel-2 reflectance, NDVI-derived phenology, and harmonic features using field-scale data from the Conterminous United States (CONUS; 2017-2023) and Northern India (2021). Traditional features achieve strong linear separability (Fisher ratio), while embeddings provide superior distribution-aware separation -- Bhattacharyya and Mahalanobis distances up to an order of magnitude higher for spectrally similar pairs (e.g. Corn/Soybeans: 3.9 vs. 0.10). Embeddings uniquely capture multi-year crop rotation dynamics, reaching a separability ratio of 1.784 versus a maximum of 1.003 across all engineered features, and exhibit lower residual spatial autocorrelation of errors (mean Moran's $I \\approx 0.17$). However, harmonic and NDVI features show stronger cross-continental transferability (US $\\to$ India Macro F1: 0.493 vs. 0.326 for embeddings), reflecting better distributional alignment. The combined feature space yields balanced performance, supporting hybrid modeling. These findings reveal a fundamental trade-off between representational richness and cross-region transferability, providing a structured framework for feature selection in large-scale crop classification.

</details>

<a id="paper-225"></a>

### 225. [HoloRecon: Incremental Reconstruction of Long-Range Panoramas with Global Consistency](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36876v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36876

**作者：** Zhuoxiao Li、Xinyi Liu、Hongye Hou、Jing OU、Kang Du、Shuai Zhang、Dongli Wu、Taoyu Wu、Siyuan Feng、Xiaohui Zhu、Wufan Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36876v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36876v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出全景重建框架，将基于 ORB 的稀疏全景建图前端与微调后的前馈模型耦合，用预测的局部几何对远距离重访的特征匹配做 3D 验证以形成长程相机约束，并优化全局位姿图，同时发布七场景基准 PanoRecon。

**相关性简评：** 其「用预测局部几何反向验证跨重访匹配、再据此维护全局一致的稠密状态」可供核查是否能启发 LVR 中的视觉证据筛选与长程状态一致性；但论文属几何重建/SLAM 领域，与多模态推理的联系需较多推断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HoloRecon: Incremental Reconstruction of Long-Range Panoramas with Global Consistency

**abstract/A1：** Long-sequence panoramic reconstruction requires detailed scene geometry and globally consistent camera poses across distant revisits. Panoramic SLAM and incremental SfM rely on feature correspondences, but repeated, similar structures and small partial overlap make reliable long-range connections difficult to establish, leaving accumulated drift unresolved. Feed-forward models offer detailed local geometry, but their high computational costs limit inference window size, necessitating additional constraints for global consistency. This motivates combining continuous feature-based tracking and sparse mapping with rapid, high-quality local reconstruction from feed-forward models to achieve accurate localization and globally consistent dense mapping over long sequences. We therefore propose a panoramic reconstruction framework that couples an ORB-based sparse panoramic mapping frontend with a fine-tuned feed-forward model. Our key insight is that predicted local geometry enables 3D verification of feature matches across distant revisits, yielding long-range camera constraints even between regions. We calibrate predicted depths with sparse landmarks and use verified connections to optimize the global pose graph, updating dense geometry with the corrected camera poses. We also introduce PanoRecon, a seven-scene benchmark with repetitive architecture, large and small loops, and reference camera poses and LiDAR geometry for evaluating global consistency. Our method achieves the lowest mean absolute trajectory error and high F-1 Score among the evaluated methods. Project Page:https://zhuoxiaoli.github.io/HoloRecon\_page/

**comment/C1：** Project Page:https://zhuoxiaoli.github.io/HoloRecon\_page/

</details>

<a id="paper-226"></a>

### 226. [PREEN: Exemplar-Based Color Style Transfer with Smartphone Editor Presets](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36896v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36896

**作者：** Beomjin Ahn

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36896v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36896v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** PREEN 将颜色风格迁移重构为回归问题：用风格迁移教师生成伪 preset 监督，先预训练 CLIP 条件回归器再用 LoRA 精调，从内容图与参考图直接预测 29 维可编辑的编辑器参数向量，而非输出图像，并可在设备端亚秒级推理。

**相关性简评：** 把输出压缩到低维可解释参数子空间、并用单一强度旋钮在一次前向中给出连续轨迹，可能为 latent 推理状态的紧凑表示与连续强度/深度控制提供类比线索，可供核查；但论文本身属颜色编辑应用，与视觉推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PREEN: Exemplar-Based Color Style Transfer with Smartphone Editor Presets

**abstract/A1：** We present PREEN (Preset Regression via an Exemplar-based Editor Network), which casts color style transfer as regression onto the 29 parameters of a deterministic image editor. Given a content image and a style reference, PREEN predicts a preset, a vector of editor slider values that is interpretable and editor-consumable, not a raster image. The key obstacle is supervision, since an arbitrary real reference lacks a preset ground truth. We therefore supervise with a style-transfer teacher, whose target looks we invert into the editor's parameter space as pseudo-presets, then pretrain a CLIP-conditioned regressor and refine it with LoRA under an image-level CIELAB loss. On the human-curated PST50 benchmark, PREEN applies the reference's color while preserving structural fidelity, leading the baselines on pixel- and perceptual-fidelity metrics. The preset is compact and predicted at sub-second on-device latency. The low-dimensional subspace also exposes a single inference-time strength knob that traces a continuous default-to-vivid trajectory in one forward pass, with every intermediate state a valid editor preset. A two-axis human study shows that raters prefer the vivid end for matching the reference but the default as a standalone photo, a trade-off the knob spans without retraining.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to WACV 2027

</details>

<a id="paper-227"></a>

### 227. [Provenance Signatures for Low Rank Adaptations of Image Diffusion Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36933v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36933

**作者：** Tobias Cheung、Mohan Dantam、Marc Juarez、Rik Sarkar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36933v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36933v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究如何判断某个在线图像服务是否使用了标准扩散模型的 LoRA 适配版本，在开放世界（适配与替代模型家族均未知）设定下定义两类溯源问题，并提出仅依赖生成图像集合内在维度（intrinsic dimension）的黑盒签名与假设检验协议。

**相关性简评：** 其「用内在维度刻画一组生成样本的有效自由度」的黑盒签名思路，可能与 LVR 中 latent 推理状态的表示几何、信息坍缩程度及轨迹多样性度量相通，可供核查能否迁移为刻画推理状态集合的诊断量。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Provenance Signatures for Low Rank Adaptations of Image Diffusion Models

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion models have revolutionized the generation of realistic artificial images. Efficient techniques such as Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA) can modify these models for particular use cases, making them more diverse and widely usable. At the same time, open base models may be adapted to deliver biased, harmful or illegal images in an online service. In this paper, we develop a protocol to detect when a service may be using an adaptation of an otherwise standard model. We describe the precise setup and threat model for this provenance question and define two different problems arising due the presence of multiple model families and multiple adaptations. In contrast to previous works that have considered model identification within lists of known candidate models, we consider the realistic open world scenario where the set of possible adaptations and alternative models are unknown. Using a hypothesis testing approach, our protocols can distinguish between the base model, a LoRA adaptation and other completely different families of models. For this test, we develop a black box signature of the models based only on intrinsic dimension of sets of images generated by the models.

**comment/C1：** Under review

</details>

<a id="paper-228"></a>

### 228. [Gaussian Process Bandits for Constraint Black Box Lever Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36935v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36935

**作者：** Bram van den Akker、Hidde Fokkema、Anastasiia Kornilova

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36935v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36935v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究共享预算下跨细分群体的离散权重分配问题，提出用 GP-UCB 在组合动作空间中借助高斯过程先验共享邻近配置信息以减少探索，并通过高保真合成模拟器与大规模线上 RCT 检验不同核配置与最终效果。

**相关性简评：** 其「在稀疏、延迟反馈和调整频率约束下，用 GP 平滑邻近配置以分配探索预算」的思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中 look/compute 预算的序列分配与停止决策；但与视觉或多模态推理无直接联系，迁移性需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Gaussian Process Bandits for Constraint Black Box Lever Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Optimizing discrete allocation weights across competing segments of a shared budget is a fundamental challenge in resource-constrained, sequential decision-making environments. In practice, this optimization is often hindered by delayed, noisy, and aggregate feedback, alongside strict operational constraints on how frequently weights can be adjusted. Because of the resulting combinatorial action space and extreme data sparsity, classical multi-armed bandit algorithms that evaluate arms independently are rendered highly inefficient. To overcome these limitations, we propose a segmentation-agnostic approach utilizing Gaussian Process Upper Confidence Bound (GP-UCB) algorithms. By placing a Gaussian Process prior on the reward function, our method effectively shares information across neighboring configurations, drastically reducing the necessary exploration horizon. In this paper, we bridge the gap between theoretical bandit research and production-scale deployment. We first introduce a high-fidelity synthetic simulator to evaluate various kernel configurations under stationary, drifting, and reactive environments, identifying a composite Matérn + Linear kernel as the most robust choice for overcoming local noise and global shifts. Finally, we validate our findings through a large-scale Randomized Controlled Trial (RCT) in a live production environment. Our empirical results demonstrate that the GP-UCB approach delivers a statistically significant efficiency lift compared to a standard $\\varepsilon$-greedy baseline, offering a highly practical solution for real-world black-box lever optimization.

**comment/C1：** 7 pages, 3 figures, 1 table. Includes results from a large-scale randomized controlled trial in a production environment

</details>

<a id="paper-229"></a>

### 229. [Algorithmic Monoculture in Machine Learning-based Site-Specific Fertilizer Recommendation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36941v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36941

**作者：** Abdelghani Belgaid

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CY、econ.TH

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36941v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36941v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究机器学习推荐系统在多方决策者间部署时的「算法单一化」问题：将推荐误差分解为共享成分与农场地特异成分，用 HHI 与协方差广义形式刻画误差集中度，提出组合化分配规则与 regret 上限，并在五个摩洛哥作物系统、九类模型族上做基准标定。

**相关性简评：** 该文关于共享误差与个体误差分解、误差相关性对组合收益的削弱以及带 regret 约束的组合选择，可能可供核查：用于聚合多个 latent 假设或推理轨迹时，如何区分共有偏差与个体偏差并设定选择准则。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Algorithmic Monoculture in Machine Learning-based Site-Specific Fertilizer Recommendation

**abstract/A1：** Machine learning recommendations are often evaluated one model at a time, despite deployment across interconnected decision makers. For continuous NPK fertilizer prescriptions, correlated errors can aggregate across farms and generate systemic costs. A model of shared and farm-specific recommendation errors links expected social loss to model adoption shares; the Herfindahl-Hirschman Index characterizes concentration effects when common model errors are independent and predictive risks are equal. A covariance-generalized formulation captures the dependence between model families. A two-model threshold characterizes when privately optimal monoculture is socially dominated, and a convex portfolio rule limits average private predictive regret. Nine model families across five Moroccan cereal systems provide a benchmark-based calibration. Under illustrative parameters and a 10% regret cap, accuracy-constrained diversification lowers modeled social loss in all five systems by 10.89-41.53% (mean 19.41%). Uniform allocation reduces modeled loss in this setting, but breaches the regret cap in every crop. Correlation sensitivity shows that highly dependent model errors attenuate the benefits of diversification. These counterfactual outcomes are not estimates of agronomic or monetary welfare; the framework identifies adoption architecture and error dependence as ecosystem-level evaluation variables.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Economics for Machine Learning (EconML)

</details>

<a id="paper-230"></a>

### 230. [Do bots feel bad? Assessing empirical evidence for functional valence in LLMs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36962v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36962

**作者：** Iwan Williams

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36962v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36962v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 一篇评估性论文，审视近期「LLM 具有功能性效价（functional pleasure/pain）」的实证证据，论证效价的关键方面尚未被检验，且现有范式无法把功能性效价与偏好、价值判断、效价归因和角色扮演区分开，最后提出新的实验思路。

**相关性简评：** 其对内部状态「因果角色」证据与混淆控制的批判，可能可供核查 LVR 中 latent 状态是否真被答案使用：如何区分真实状态使用与角色扮演、事后归因，以及如何设计对照范式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Do bots feel bad? Assessing empirical evidence for functional valence in LLMs

**abstract/A1：** Do LLMs have valenced states, i.e. states that feel pleasant or unpleasant? Recent empirical work has claimed to show that LLMs possess functional valenced states (e.g. \`\`functional pleasure and pain'' \[Ren et al. 2026\], \`\`functional emotions'' \[Sofroniew et al. 2026\], and \`\`functional welfare'' \[Han et al. 2026\]) that play the same causal role as valenced states in cognition and action, without necessarily being consciously experienced. But it remains unexplored how well the identified states fit the functional role of valence as understood in the philosophical and cognitive science literature. Here, I assess the current body of evidence, and argue (i) that key aspects of valence remain untested in LLMs, and (ii) that extant empirical paradigms inadequately distinguish (functional) valence from neighbouring constructs like preferences, value-judgments, valence attributions and role-play. I conclude by sketching some novel experimental approaches to better target (functional) valence itself.

</details>

<a id="paper-231"></a>

### 231. [On the VC Dimension of Autoregressive Linear Threshold Functions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36970v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36970

**作者：** Ilan Doron-Arad、Idan Mehalel、Elchanan Mossel

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36970v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36970v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究自回归线性阈值函数（LTF）的 VC 维度：证明当 T≥d² 时，确定性自回归 LTF 的 VC 维度为 Θ(d²)，并证明把 sign 换成 logistic 采样后最终多数预测类的 VC 维度可由二次升至指数级。

**相关性简评：** 其刻画「自回归迭代步数 T 如何放大容量」以及随机采样与确定性更新的表达力差异，可能为 AR 式 latent 状态迭代推理的计算能力、长度泛化与样本复杂度提供理论参照，但是否可迁移到 VLM latent 推理仍需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** On the VC Dimension of Autoregressive Linear Threshold Functions

**abstract/A1：** Linear threshold functions (LTFs) are a canonical class of Boolean predictors. In the autoregressive setting, the same LTF reads the most recent $d$ bits, generates the next bit, and returns the bit produced after $T$ steps. While the one-step class has VC dimension $d$, for general $T$ the counting upper bound is $O(d^2)$. We prove that the VC dimension is $Θ(d^2)$ for every $T\\ge d^2$, resolving the quadratic gap for deterministic autoregressive LTFs. We then adapt the same technique to show an exponential separation between deterministic and stochastic autoregressive models: replacing the sign of LTFs by logistic sampling can increase the VC dimension of the final majority prediction class from quadratic to exponential in $d$.

</details>

<a id="paper-232"></a>

### 232. [Explicit Regional Representations For Video Quality Assessment](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36978v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36978

**作者：** Fuyi Yang、Lei Yang、Andrew Liu、Chenyu Wei、Xiaoyi Guo、Ming Kai Hsu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36978v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36978v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在视频质量评估（VQA，非视觉问答）中引入显式像素级区域表示——Quality-Relevance Heatmap，在 VLM 引导下结合局部退化证据与感知先验且无需像素级标注，并以分布保持采样与质量感知采样两种确定性片段采样策略接入现有 VQA 模型（如 DOVER）。

**相关性简评：** 该工作属视频质量评估这一较远领域，但其免标注的区域相关性热图与按分数优先选择区域的机制，可能为 latent 推理中「哪些视觉证据被保留/进入内部状态」的筛选与评价提供线索，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Explicit Regional Representations For Video Quality Assessment

**abstract/A1：** End-to-end video quality assessment (VQA) methods leave the regional evidence underlying their predictions largely implicit. We introduce an explicit pixel-level regional representation for VQA, instantiated as a Quality-Relevance Heatmap (QRH) that combines localized degradation evidence with perceptual priors under vision-language model guidance, without requiring pixel-level annotations. We apply QRH to existing VQA models through two deterministic fragment-sampling strategies: distribution-preserving sampling approximates the original sampler's QRH-score distribution without retraining, while quality-aware sampling prioritizes regions with high QRH scores and is paired with fine-tuning. Experiments with DOVER on multiple benchmarks show that the former closely approximates predictions averaged over random samples, while the latter improves most evaluated metrics across popular VQA benchmarks. These results support explicit regional representations as a useful interface for downstream VQA.

**comment/C1：** Preprint. This work is an initial version and may be updated in future revisions

</details>

<a id="paper-233"></a>

### 233. [AutoVend: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Autonomous Vending Operations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36980v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36980

**作者：** Qiming Shi、Yikun Li、Beidi Luan、Rui Sun、Yan Ma、Jing Li、Daxin Jiang、Zuo Bai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36980v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36980v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 AutoVend 基准：单个 LLM agent 通过 Web 操作界面在 180 个模拟日内运营 20 台自动售货机，模拟器包含共享仓库、采购提前期、付费补货、库存过期与经常性成本，并将五种 LLM agent 与规则库存控制基线对比，分析其长期执行与轨迹失败模式。

**相关性简评：** 其长时程轨迹诊断与「每美元推理成本收益」的评测思路，可能可供核查 LVR 中动态计算、停止决策与实际成本的评价设计；但任务不含视觉或 latent 状态生成，联系较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AutoVend: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Autonomous Vending Operations

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents are extending agent-mediated commerce on the Web from shopping for consumers to operating businesses for sellers. In online-to-offline (O2O) commerce, decisions are issued through Web-based platforms but realized through costly physical execution. Evaluating such agents requires going beyond individual transactions to ask whether decisions made online turn into sustained economic returns. We introduce AutoVend, a benchmark in which one LLM agent manages 20 vending machines across five site types through a Web-based operating interface for 180 simulated days. Its data-grounded simulator combines a shared warehouse, procurement lead times, paid replenishment, inventory expiration, and recurring operating costs. We evaluate five LLM agents against a rule-based inventory-control baseline and diagnose their site coordination, execution, and performance over time. Four of five evaluated LLM agents fall short of this baseline, and returns per dollar of inference cost rank models differently from returns alone. Operational analyses show that gains in individual metrics do not consistently yield stronger economic growth, as inventory and execution costs erode these gains. Trajectory analysis further reveals difficulties in grounding feasible actions, managing inventory commitments, and converting feedback into effective policy revisions.

</details>

<a id="paper-234"></a>

### 234. [Meta Models: Models that Model Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37001v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37001

**作者：** Peihang Li、Ziming Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37001v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37001v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「元模型」概念，用真实语言模型训练记录作为数值数据，由 TabPFN 等表格型 in-context 学习器预测其他模型的最终性能与学习曲线，并尝试加大型训练运行的预测检验。

**相关性简评：** 这与视觉推理主线无直接关联，但其「以廉价预测器刻画训练/计算响应曲线」的框架可能可供核查，例如用于预判增加 latent 思考步数或训练轨迹监督的收益与停止时机。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Meta Models: Models that Model Learning

**abstract/A1：** Every neural-network training run produces not only a model, but also evidence about how models learn. Can these accumulated experiments enable us to predict and compare new training runs before executing them? We study meta-models: models that predict the learning behavior of other models. From this perspective, final-performance prediction, learning-curve prediction, and hyperparameter selection are different queries to the same training response function. We take a first, humble empirical step toward this goal: treating real language-model training records as native numeric data, we find that an off-the-shelf tabular in-context learner, TabPFN, can match or outperform a fine-tuned LLM meta-model in multiple evaluated settings. Whereas recent work fine-tunes LLMs on tokenized configurations at a cost of tens to hundreds of GPU-hours, TabPFN requires seconds of fit-and-predict time without task-specific weight updates. We argue that meta-modeling is better treated as its own numeric modality rather than as a text task for LLMs. Going one step further, augmenting TabPFN with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) further improves accuracy on selected prediction tasks. Finally, we pre-registered a forecast of the Marin Hero Run, a 535B-parameter public training run, whose ground truth is revealed point by point as training progresses, putting meta-model predictions to the test against training outcomes not yet observed at the time of forecasting.

</details>

<a id="paper-235"></a>

### 235. [A First-Step Bias Restores Lost Diversity in Diffusion Model Distillation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37110v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37110

**作者：** Wenwu Tang、Dong Wang、Yun Cheng、Olga Saukh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37110v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37110v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文定位扩散模型蒸馏中学生模型对初始噪声响应多样性丢失的位置，发现损失集中在首个高噪声采样步，并提出 ReSeed：仅在第一步加入 seed-response bias adapter 对齐教师首段割线速度，冻结其余学生参数与后续步，在多个文生图与文生视频模型上提升多样性并保持画质。

**相关性简评：** 其沿采样轨迹逐步比较师生响应、定位信息坍缩发生的位置并只修补单步的框架，可能可供核查 LVR 中视觉信息在推理轨迹哪一步被压缩，以及单步偏置修正能否恢复 latent 状态多样性；但对象为图像生成，与 VLM 推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A First-Step Bias Restores Lost Diversity in Diffusion Model Distillation

**abstract/A1：** Distribution-matching distillation enables high-quality image generation in only a few denoising steps. However, distilled student models often map different initial noise samples to similar outputs under the same prompt. To locate where diversity is lost, we compare the teacher and student along the sampling trajectory. The largest measured reduction in the response to different initial noises occurs during the first, high-noise step. When we replace the first step with the teacher's corresponding trajectory and leave the final three student steps unchanged, most of the output diversity returns. This suggests that the first step is a key point for restoring diversity. Motivated by this finding, we introduce \\emph{ReSeed}, a post-distillation method that adds a seed-response bias adapter only to the first student step and aligns its velocity with the teacher's first-interval secant velocity. All student weights and later denoising steps remain unchanged. We evaluate ReSeed on three text-to-image models (SD3-Medium, SD3.5-Medium, and FLUX.1-dev) and one text-to-video model (Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B). For a distilled 4-step FLUX.1-dev model, \\emph{ReSeed} requires only $0.00196\\%$ additional trainable parameters relative to the base model and is trained on 8,000 prompt--noise pairs, while keeping the distilled student fully frozen and requiring no target images, perceptual losses, or adversarial losses. It improves DINO diversity by $48.3\\%$ while maintaining competitive visual quality. These results show that substantial diversity can be recovered through a lightweight, data-efficient tuning of only the first denoising step, without finetuning the full student model.

</details>

<a id="paper-236"></a>

### 236. [Variational Optimal Control for Diffusion Guidance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37146v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37146

**作者：** Ruoyu Wang、Xinyi Ye、Yunshuo Shi、Liangyu Yuan、Beier Zhu、Chi Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37146v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37146v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出一个变分最优控制框架，用共享的 Jacobian pullback 把采样阶段的扩散引导与基于奖励的模型适配联系起来，由此得到状态空间的 VOG 及通过端点蒸馏摊销到参数的 DVOC，并在 ImageNet 与 Z-Image-Turbo 上报告 FID 和评分改进。

**相关性简评：** 该文面向图像生成的奖励对齐，不直接涉及多模态推理，但其「推理期引导与蒸馏入参数共享同一变分规则」的结构，可能为 LVR 中推理期 latent 调节与训练期轨迹监督之间的对应关系提供形式化线索，值得核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Variational Optimal Control for Diffusion Guidance

**abstract/A1：** We present a variational control framework that connects sampling-time guidance and reward-based model adaptation through a shared Jacobian pullback. Starting from terminal reward maximization with a path-space KL penalty, the Gibbs variational principle and Doob transform yield a correction to the generative dynamics. A local denoising approximation exposes the pullback that transports output preferences to the controlled variable. This construction gives Variational Optimal Guidance (VOG) in state space and extends to Distilled Variational Optimal Control (DVOC) by amortizing locally improved endpoints into model parameters. The resulting controls share a local variational rule: VOG acts through the denoising Jacobian, while DVOC realizes the parameter pullback by endpoint distillation. Under a linear MMSE model, VOG's exact correction lies in the data subspace. On ImageNet, VOG reduces DiT-XL/2 FID from 2.27 to 2.04 at 250 sampling steps and from 7.58 to 4.44 at 25 steps. After 20 updates on Z-Image-Turbo, DVOC improves HPSv3++ and four held-out evaluator scores over the untrained model, with the highest corresponding means among the trained methods compared. These results connect inference-time control and reward adaptation through a common geometric principle.

</details>

<a id="paper-237"></a>

### 237. [Physics-Guided Flow Matching: A Generative AI Framework for Global Total Electron Content Forecasting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37159v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37159

**作者：** Si-Tong Yang、Feng Ye、Xian Lu、Dong Lin、Benjamin C. Martinez

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、physics.space-ph、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37159v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37159v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出物理引导的 flow matching 生成框架，用 PDE 描述的电离层动力学引导从初始分布到目标 TEC 场的变换，并按位置自适应调节物理项与数据统计项的贡献权重，用于全球 TEC 提前一天预报。

**相关性简评：** 空间天气预报本身与 VLM 推理无关，但其「结构化先验引导生成路径 + 按位置自适应分配物理与数据贡献」的机制，可能为 latent 状态更新中先验与学习信号的贡献分配提供可核查的类比线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Physics-Guided Flow Matching: A Generative AI Framework for Global Total Electron Content Forecasting

**abstract/A1：** Accurate forecasting of global total electron content (TEC) is important for space weather monitoring and the reliable operation of satellite communication and navigation systems. We propose a physics-guided generative artificial intelligence (AI) framework for day-ahead global TEC forecasting. The framework builds on flow matching, which learns to gradually transform samples from an initial distribution into new samples that share the statistical characteristics of the target data. The proposed model incorporates ionospheric dynamics described by a partial differential equation (PDE) into this transformation process, guiding the changes between initial and target samples using physical knowledge. The model further combines the physical dynamics with statistical patterns captured from data to learn this transformation, adaptively adjusting their relative contributions at each location. The model is trained and evaluated using one year of global TEC data generated by the Thermosphere Ionosphere Electrodynamics General Circulation Model (TIEGCM). The results demonstrate that our model improves both spatial and temporal forecasting accuracy over representative benchmarks, including statistical, deep learning, and generative AI methods at all lead times and across latitudes. The average root mean square error (RMSE) is 39.2% lower than that of ConvLSTM and 23.8% lower than that of the same framework without physical guidance. The learned contribution weights vary systematically with latitude and across geographic locations, showing how the physical contribution changes across the forecast domain. These findings demonstrate that explicit physical guidance improves generative forecasts of global TEC and highlight the value of incorporating physical dynamics into generative AI modeling.

</details>

<a id="paper-238"></a>

### 238. [WorkFactory: Turning Professional Intents into Executable Work for Scalable Agent Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37161v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37161

**作者：** Ziyang Zeng、Tingyang Chen、Jia Deng、Qiyao Wang、Shuo Tang、Yike Yang、Tao Ren、Wayne Xin Zhao、Feng Chang、Jujie He、Chuan Hao、Ran Tao、Bryan Dai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37161v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37161v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 WorkFactory：从职业意图出发构建信息支持图以实例化任务，再用「探测执行」定位信息支撑失败并修复，产出 11 万余条任务并据此微调 Qwen 得到 WorkPilot 系列模型。

**相关性简评：** 其「信息支持图 + 探测执行诊断缺失依赖」的做法，可能可供核查用于判断推理任务所需证据是否齐备，启发 LVR 数据与基准的构造与缺陷检验，但该文不涉及视觉或 latent 推理状态。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WorkFactory: Turning Professional Intents into Executable Work for Scalable Agent Training

**abstract/A1：** Scaling supervised training for working agents requires diverse professional tasks that yield reliable expert demonstrations. However, missing or inconsistent task information can obstruct teacher execution and weaken the resulting supervision. We propose WorkFactory, a scalable framework for constructing coherent and executable professional tasks. Starting from professional intents, information-level planning builds an information support graph to guide task instantiation, explicitly representing the dependencies and locations of information required for the intended outputs. Probe executions then identify support failures and guide repairs while preserving the intended work. We construct 112,686 tasks spanning 612 occupations and use the collected teacher trajectories to fine-tune Qwen models at 4B, 27B, and 35B-A3B scales, yielding the WorkPilot family. All three models improve over their respective base models across four professional-work benchmarks, with WorkPilot-35B-A3B improving from 879 to 1565 Elo on GDPval-AA v2.1. Scaling analyses reveal model-dependent diminishing returns, with execution length first increasing and then decreasing. Controlled component analyses show that planning and repair reduce probe-diagnosed task environment defects and improve downstream performance. The data and models are available at https://github.com/IQuestLab/WorkFactory.

</details>

<a id="paper-239"></a>

### 239. [Rènyi-Regularized Flow-Based Variational Inference for Bayesian Calibration](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37169v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37169

**作者：** Exaucè Luweh Adjim Ngarti、Élise Arnaud、Léo Nicoletti、Victor Trappler、Arthur Vidard

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37169v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37169v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文在双参数孪生实验中考察基于 normalizing flow 的变分推断在双峰后验下的模式坍塌问题，并提出基于 Rényi 散度的正则项促使近似后验覆盖先验支撑，使近坍塌比例由 50% 降至 25%，同时承认引入精度权衡且未根本解决坍塌。

**相关性简评：** 其多峰覆盖、坍塌比例度量与散度正则化思路，可能为 LVR 中多 latent 假设是否被保留、多样性坍塌如何评估与缓解提供概念类比，可供核查迁移可行性。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Rènyi-Regularized Flow-Based Variational Inference for Bayesian Calibration

**abstract/A1：** Variational inference with normalizing flows provides a flexible posterior approximation for Bayesian model calibration, but struggles when the posterior has several modes separated by regions of low probability mass. We study this behavior in a two-parameter twin experiment and regularize the variational objective. Under Gaussian observation errors and a Gaussian prior, the corresponding minimization objective is the expected Tikhonov calibration objective minus the differential entropy of the approximating distribution. However, the expected Tikhonov term favors concentration near one of the modes, while entropy maximization does not guarantee multimodality. We introduce a regularization term based on Rényi divergence that encourages the approximate posterior to cover the prior support. In the connected bimodal case, a flow approximation represents both modes; in the case with well-separated modes, the flow and Gaussian mixture frequently miss one mode. The introduced regularization reduces the near-collapse fraction from 50% to 25%. While the regularization improves mode coverage in this experiment, it changes the Bayesian variational objective, introduces a trade-off in approximation accuracy, and does not provide a general solution to mode collapse.

</details>

<a id="paper-240"></a>

### 240. [MLE-World: Predicting Machine Learning Experiment Outcomes with a Learned World Model](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37175v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37175

**作者：** Brian Ko、Han Wang、Hejia Geng、Li Erran Li、Sherry Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37175v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37175v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文微调 Qwen3.5-4B 构建世界模型 MLE-World，从代码与数据描述直接预测 ML 程序的最终输出（报错、训练日志、验证分数）并给出置信度，再据此设计多种 test-time scaling 策略来选择候选程序。

**相关性简评：** 其「预测执行结果＋置信度决定是否真实执行」可类比 LVR 中廉价预测状态与按不确定性触发的 LOOK/STOP 决策，可能为视觉证据获取与停止准则提供借鉴。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MLE-World: Predicting Machine Learning Experiment Outcomes with a Learned World Model

**abstract/A1：** AI research agents can propose, execute, and improve programs that solve machine learning tasks. However, executing each candidate program requires training and evaluating ML models, limiting how many candidates an agent can explore at each step. We introduce MLE-World, a world model finetuned from Qwen3.5-4B that predicts a program's terminal output (error traces, training logs, and validation scores) from its code and data description without executing it, together with a confidence score estimating the prediction's correctness. Because a prediction costs one inference call rather than a training run, MLE-World makes test time scaling (TTS) affordable for these agents. We build five TTS policies, guided by MLE-World, that compare several candidates per step by their predicted outputs: Sequential revision, Best-of-N, Pairwise judging, Fusion, and a UCB style rule that also adds an exploration bonus from this confidence score. Since predictions can be wrong, we run a candidate for real only when its confidence score is low. On 17 MLE-bench Lite tasks, MLE-World matches the real terminal output on 77.9% of held out programs, versus 41.1% for much larger prompted models. MLE-World guided TTS achieves up to 22.1% higher observed mean performance than the agent without a world model, with less real execution. Project webpage: https://mle-world.github.io/.

</details>

<a id="paper-241"></a>

### 241. [INTR++: INterpretable TRansformer with better Accuracy, Interpretability, and Adaptability](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37178v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37178

**作者：** Dipanjyoti Paul、Arpita Chowdhury、Maneet Chatterjee、Daichi Kitaguchi、Tanya Berger-Wolf、Tetsuya Sakurai、Wei-Lun Chao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37178v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37178v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** INTR++ 在 Prompt-CAM 基础上引入轻量 Prompt Adapter Module，用域特定知识丰富可学习 prompt，使可解释 Transformer 在动物、植物、医学、显微与遥感等多域同时保持精度与解释一致性。

**相关性简评：** 其基于注意力的可解释定位与自适应 prompt 可能为 LVR 中视觉证据筛选和内部状态的域条件化提供线索；但论文面向图像分类解释，未涉及推理轨迹，可供核查注意力解释对推理期状态是否有参考价值。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** INTR++: INterpretable TRansformer with better Accuracy, Interpretability, and Adaptability

**abstract/A1：** Interpretable Transformers often perform well in domain specific settings but lose effectiveness beyond that. Achieving ViT-like broad applicability requires models with consistent performance across domains. Recent works on Interpretable Transformers, INTR and Prompt-CAM, were developed keeping domain adaptability in mind. Prompt-CAM leverages learnable prompts to promote adaptability. However, these prompts are fixed in nature and therefore struggle to adapt to domains with substantial variation in input image characteristics, resulting in reduced interpretability. To address this limitation, we propose INTR++, which extends Prompt-CAM by incorporating a lightweight Prompt Adapter Module (PAM). PAM enables prompts to be adaptive by enriching them with domain-specific knowledge, thereby ensuring consistent interpretability across diverse domains including those with varying image characteristics such as medical imaging, micro-scopic imaging, and remote sensing imagery. INTR++ retains the standard architecture and end-to-end learning of Interpretable Transformer based models, while adding only a small number of additional parameters compared to Prompt-CAM. We demonstrate that INTR++ delivers consistent improvements in all aspects across domains like animals, plants, medical imaging, microscopic imaging, remote sensing imagery and object-centric data.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the IEEE/CVF Winter Conference on Applications of Computer Vision (WACV 2027)

</details>

<a id="paper-242"></a>

### 242. [HDVS: semi-supervised semantic segmentation via heterogeneous dual-branch voting supervision](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37236v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37236

**作者：** Yongqi Shan、Yunzhi Zhuge、Huchuan Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37236v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37236v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出半监督语义分割框架 HDVS：在同质交叉监督基础上引入异构双分支的伪标签投票机制，并配合特征通信模块在特征层制造扰动，以提高伪标签可靠性、缓解协同训练中的误差累积，并在 PASCAL VOC 2012 与 Cityscapes 上取得较好结果。

**相关性简评：** 其「异构分支投票生成高可靠伪标签」与特征级扰动保多样性的做法，可能可供核查多 latent 假设的候选筛选与监督信号去噪，但与视觉推理状态本身无直接关联，需较多迁移核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** HDVS: semi-supervised semantic segmentation via heterogeneous dual-branch voting supervision

**abstract/A1：** Semi-supervised semantic segmentation, which aims to utilize large volumes of unlabeled images to achieve accurate segmentation results with fewer human annotations, has attracted increasing attention. Prior methods were primarily based on the assumption that the pseudo-labels generated by each branch were sufficiently reliable to supervise the remaining branches. However, there is a concern that the errors in pseudo-labels could accumulate during co-training, potentially resulting in suboptimal performance. To this end, we present a novel framework called heterogeneous dual-branch voting supervision (HDVS), which is designed to enhance the reliability of pseudo-labels and mitigate the issues arising from pseudo-labeling. Specifically, based on the cross-supervision framework, we introduce a voting mechanism that correlates pseudo-labels from heterogeneous branches to produce pseudo-labels with enhanced reliability. Concurrently, we employ a feature communication module to introduce perturbations at the feature level in each branch to maximize prediction diversity across the dual branches while maintaining convergence. Comprehensive evaluations of the proposed HDVS on two benchmark datasets (PASCAL VOC 2012 and Cityscapes) demonstrate its superiority to the state-of-the-art approaches.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 9 figures, 7 tables. Published in Visual Intelligence

</details>

<a id="paper-243"></a>

### 243. [When Is a Relation Worth Trusting? Manipulation Costs, Exposure, and the Trust Trap in Multi-Relational Graph Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37239v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37239

**作者：** Vincess Dongmo、Astride Melvin Fokam Ninyim

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37239v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37239v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究多关系图学习中「何时信任某个关系」，证明在部分节点被隔离且攻击者负担不起门控成本时度门控严格优于关系选择或重加权，并提出按每节点曝光界挑选应加固的关系，附带 R-GCN 等模型的认证区间与对抗验证的有限样本保证。

**相关性简评：** 其「用某条证据是否不如弃用」的成本门槛、门控与弃权判据，可能与 VLM 中视觉证据筛选、视觉旁路及 STOP 决策的问题表述形成类比，但属图学习远领域，可迁移性需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Is a Relation Worth Trusting? Manipulation Costs, Exposure, and the Trust Trap in Multi-Relational Graph Learning

**abstract/A1：** Multi-relational graph detectors learn from clean data how much to trust each relation, but an adversary pays a different price to manipulate each relation and each action; taking such costs as given, we ask when using a relation is worse than ignoring it. Our first main result proves that degree gating strictly beats every selection or reweighting of a relation when part of the population is isolated on it and the attacker cannot afford to open the gate, and that this window is sharp; on real graphs, validated gating gains exactly on the relations that meet these conditions and nowhere else. Our second shows that a per-node exposure bound, which extends to message passing, tells a platform which relation to harden: exposure per unit cost, computed without any attack, picks the relation whose hardening raises the exact trap threshold most in 15 of 16 cases, against at most 7 for informativeness, cheapness, or an analysis with equal edit costs. Supporting tools give exact worst cases for linear relational models, certified brackets for R-GCN and tree ensembles, and a finite-sample guarantee for adversarial validation, under which clean-trained graph detectors fall below their graph-free counterparts in 58 of 90 settings on six graphs. A selection protocol built on these results is never significantly worse than ignoring the graph; with at most four relations it mostly abstains, as validation alone would, and its cost-aware proposals help when relations are many, so far shown on synthetic graphs. Without cost information, the safe choice is to abstain.

**comment/C1：** Preprint. October 27, 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-244"></a>

### 244. [GameVerse: A Minute-Scale Gameplay Dataset for Long-Horizon Interactive World Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37257v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37257

**作者：** Kang He、Wenshuo Peng、Chuanhao Li、Zihui Gao、Xiaojie Xu、Zhengyuan Lin、Yuzhe Ding、Donghong Ji、Kaipeng Zhang、Yongtao Ge

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37257v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37257v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GameVerse，一个面向长时程交互式世界建模的分钟级 gameplay 视频数据集（69.7K 片段、1,655 小时），经场景一致分割、质量过滤、相机位姿估计与事件级字幕标注构建，并显示在其上训练可延长世界模型的生成时程。

**相关性简评：** 其长时程连贯性数据构造与「静态场景上下文／动态前景事件」分离、按事件边界对齐的标注方式，可能为 LVR 中长程 latent 状态的维持与事件级监督、贡献分配提供线索，需核查是否可迁移；但论文本身聚焦视频生成数据集，与视觉推理关联较远。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GameVerse: A Minute-Scale Gameplay Dataset for Long-Horizon Interactive World Modeling

**abstract/A1：** Recent advances in interactive world models have made it possible to generate controllable, dynamic environments from action inputs. Yet current models struggle to maintain coherence beyond roughly one minute of rollout, a limitation rooted not in model capacity but in the scarcity of long-horizon training data. Existing gameplay video datasets typically consist of clips under ten seconds, with captions that describe whole clips in coarse and undifferentiated text, which is insufficient for controllable long-horizon generation. We address this with GameVerse, a minute-scale gameplay video dataset purpose-built for long-horizon interactive world modeling. GameVerse contains 69.7K clips spanning 1,655 hours and 349.6 million frames across diverse first- and third-person gameplay, including 1,358 hours of continuous two-minute sequences, an order of magnitude longer than prior datasets. Clips pass through a unified pipeline of scene-consistent segmentation, quality filtering, camera pose estimation, and event-based caption annotation. Each clip is paired with a temporally stable camera trajectory that remains coherent across minutes, together with captions that separate static scene context from dynamic foreground events and align to event boundaries rather than fixed time windows. Comprehensive analysis shows that GameVerse surpasses existing public datasets in scale, diversity, and annotation quality. Experiments further demonstrate that training on GameVerse substantially extends the temporal horizon of interactive world models.

**comment/C1：** Accepted by NeurIPS 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-245"></a>

### 245. [Bayesian Inference of Expensive Simulators: Survey and Comparison of Design Choices](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37284v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37284

**作者：** Šimon Soldát、Václav Šmídl

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37284v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37284v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文综述并实证比较昂贵模拟器的贝叶斯推断方法，将 Bayesian optimization 类方法统一为一般框架，系统比较各设计选择对仿真预算效率的影响，并提出保留更多信息的自定义 proxy variables。

**相关性简评：** 其核心发现「响应变量的选择决定被建模函数的复杂度，标量似然会丢失关键信息」可能与 LVR 中视觉证据筛选、latent 状态信息坍缩与表示保真度的权衡形成概念类比，可供核查其信息保留思路是否可迁移。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bayesian Inference of Expensive Simulators: Survey and Comparison of Design Choices

**abstract/A1：** We present a survey of approaches to Bayesian inference with simulator forward models, focusing on settings with prohibitively expensive simulators. In this domain, Bayesian optimization-based methods have emerged as the most promising approach. We unify existing methods into a general framework, enabling consistent reasoning about shared algorithmic structure across the fragmented literature. Within this framework, we identify the primary design choices and perform a systematic empirical comparison of their effects on inference efficiency in terms of required simulation budget. A key result of our study is that inference efficiency is often dominated by the choice of surrogate-model response variable, as it directly governs the complexity of the modeled function. Specifically, we demonstrate that the prevalent scalar log-likelihood is a poor response variable, as it discards crucial information. Based on this insight, we introduce the concept of custom \\emph{proxy variables}: transformations of simulator outputs engineered to retain more information and provide smoother response surfaces, substantially improving inference efficiency over standard response variables. We provide actionable guidance and a numerically robust Julia package to facilitate practical adoption of Bayesian optimization for simulator inverse problems.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages, 8 figures. Accepted for publication in Statistics and Computing. This is the submitted manuscript; the revised version, including changes based on peer review, is available from the authors upon request

</details>

<a id="paper-246"></a>

### 246. [ScIntBench: Physical Interaction Plausibility in 3D Scene Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37326v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37326

**作者：** Wendi Ying、Daochang Liu、Leonid Sigal、Ajmal Saeed Mian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37326v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37326v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ScIntBench，一个评测文本条件 3D 室内场景「交互可用性」的基准：在物理仿真中执行推动、开门、表面探测等指定操作，按任务成功、交互后完整性、动态合理性等指标系统评测 12 个场景生成系统在 10 类房间上的表现。

**相关性简评：** 「静态物理合理不等于可交互使用」的评测思路，可能为考察 latent 视觉状态是否真正支撑后续推理与操作提供类比指标，但研究对象是 3D 场景生成，与 VLM 推理距离较远，可供核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ScIntBench: Physical Interaction Plausibility in 3D Scene Generation

**abstract/A1：** Existing evaluations of text-conditioned 3D indoor scene generation largely emphasize semantic fidelity and physical validity at rest, leaving interaction-time physical reliability insufficiently assessed. To address this gap, we introduce ScIntBench, a unified benchmark centered on interaction readiness: it executes prescribed manipulation actions (e.g., pushing, opening, and surface probing) in physics simulation and measures task success, capability and progress, post-interaction integrity, and dynamic plausibility. Multi-view semantic and static physical evaluations complement these metrics and help explain interaction outcomes. We systematically evaluate 12 state-of-the-art scene generation systems across 10 room types. Our results reveal that strong semantic and static physical performance does not necessarily translate into interaction readiness: a system with near-perfect static plausibility completes only 11.54% of interactions, 7 of the 12 systems produce scenes that cannot support any interaction, and even the best system succeeds in only 36.92% of prescribed tasks. These findings highlight two fundamental challenges in generating interaction-ready scenes: providing the necessary physical interaction capabilities and ensuring reliable physical behavior under active manipulation.

</details>

<a id="paper-247"></a>

### 247. [WCC: A Learning-Based Framework for Deciding When to Apply Cooperative Coevolution in Large-Scale Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37329v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37329

**作者：** Wenjie Qiu、Hepei Li、Jianfeng Wang、Jun Zhang、Yue-Jiao Gong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37329v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37329v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** WCC 将「何时使用协同进化（CC）而非非分解算法」建模为马尔可夫决策过程，用学习到的自回归策略在两类算法间细粒度切换并按需分配计算预算，强调低成本训练一次即可跨问题、跨优化长度和基线复用。

**相关性简评：** 其「学习何时切换两种求解模式并条件分配预算」的元决策视角可能为 LVR 中的 THINK/LOOK/STOP 切换与动态计算分配提供概念参考，但领域相距较远，迁移价值需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WCC: A Learning-Based Framework for Deciding When to Apply Cooperative Coevolution in Large-Scale Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Cooperative Coevolution (CC) and Non-Decomposition Algorithms (NDAs) are two primary approaches for large-scale global optimization (LSGO). Although both have achieved success, they exhibit markedly different performance across problem types and each faces approach-specific limitations, leaving the question of "when to use CC" unresolved. We address this gap with WCC, a novel learning-based framework that dynamically decides when to deploy CC in LSGO. WCC employs a streamlined design for efficient adaptive learning. We formulate the decision as a Markov decision process, designing a targeted feature set, a robust reward, and a tailored network architecture to provide the agent with rich information about the problem state and optimization progress. Our auto-regressive policy enables WCC to perform fine-grained, dynamic switching between CC and NDAs while conditionally allocating the computational budget without expert specifications. WCC shows strong competitiveness and superior generalization across problems, optimization horizons, and baseline optimizers. It realizes a "train-once and reuse" paradigm under a low budget, significantly curbing LSGO cost. Additionally, in-depth analyses yield insights not captured by existing theory, offering practical guidance on when and how to effectively deploy CC.

</details>

<a id="paper-248"></a>

### 248. [Normative Deviation Modeling for EEG-based Depression Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37338v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37338

**作者：** Wan Jiang、Jingqi Cheng、Hui Zhang、Xu Zhao、Xiaojing Chen、Shengeng Tang、Jingjing Wu、Richang Hong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37338v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37338v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用健康对照 EEG 分别训练结构性与分布性参考模型，把偏离健康范式的标量分数或逐维残差与深度 EEG 表示结合，用于跨被试、跨数据源的抑郁症检测，并辅以目标域校准。

**相关性简评：** 其「相对参考分布的偏离度量」及标量级/特征级残差加注意力聚合，可能为刻画 latent 状态相对基线表示的偏移与异常提供探测思路，但是否能对接 VLM 推理状态评价尚需核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Normative Deviation Modeling for EEG-based Depression Detection

**abstract/A1：** Web-enabled digital health services are expanding access to physiological monitoring and mental health screening. However, electroencephalography (EEG)-based major depressive disorder (MDD) detection remains challenging across heterogeneous data sources, where individual variability and acquisition differences may affect disease-related representations. In this work, we investigate the use of healthy normative references for EEG-based MDD detection. Specifically, structural and distributional reference models are independently trained using healthy control (HC) recordings to characterize departures from healthy EEG patterns. We develop two diagnostic approaches that incorporate normative deviations at different representation levels. Scalar-level Normative Deviation-guided Diagnosis (S-NDD) summarizes structural and distributional deviations into scalar scores and integrates them with deep EEG representations. Feature-level Normative Deviation-guided Diagnosis (F-NDD) retains dimension-wise residuals and combines them with deep EEG representations through attention-based aggregation and auxiliary supervision. We also employ target HC-assisted score calibration for cross-source evaluation without updating the trained models. Experiments on two public EEG datasets examine both approaches under subject-independent within-source and bidirectional cross-source settings. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of normative deviation-guided diagnosis across different evaluation settings.

</details>

<a id="paper-249"></a>

### 249. [TimeLineXR: An Interactive Exploration of Time in Vision-Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37349v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37349

**作者：** Nidham Tekaya、Markus Passecker、Matthias Zeppelzauer

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37349v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37349v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建 TimeLineXR 交互演示：用 CLIP 编码图像，经 cosine-kernel KPCA 投影并贴合到 Bézier 时间轴，再以网页和 Three.js WebXR 三维场景把 VLM 嵌入空间中估计的图像「首现年份」呈现为可浏览、可查询的时序空间。

**相关性简评：** 该工作本身不涉及 latent 推理状态的生成或答案使用，属表示几何可视化演示；但其「核 PCA 降维＋曲线拟合时间轴」的探针流程可能可供核查 latent 相似度中内容、深度、位置等因素在内部表示空间的组织方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TimeLineXR: An Interactive Exploration of Time in Vision-Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Recently, it has been shown that vision-language models (VLMs) have a certain intrinsic understanding of time. They can estimate the temporal origin of objects and encode time along a low-dimensional, non-linear manifold inside their embedding space~\\cite{tekaya2025matter}. That result is quantitative and reproducible, but the structure itself stays out of reach for end users: they cannot see it, query it with their own images, or inspect where a query lands relative to other years on the time axis. We present TimeLineXR, an interactive demonstration that turns this time axis into an explorable 3D scene. A web demo lets users upload or select images and see each placed on a color-coded 3D time axis; a Three.js WebXR scene renders the same time axis as a room-scale representation that can be grabbed, scaled, inspected, and queried by throwing image cards into it. Both clients run the same prediction pipeline against the same cached temporal index: encode the query with CLIP, project it through cosine-kernel KPCA, and snap it to the Bézier time axis. The system turns each image into a point on the time axis, so users can compare where different objects land and read off their estimated first year of appearance.Code source and reproduction steps can be found in: https://github.com/sterngefeuert/timeline-xr.

</details>

<a id="paper-250"></a>

### 250. [Euclidean Crowding-Driven Adaptive Euclidean-Hyperbolic Graph Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37373v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37373

**作者：** Hao Chen、Jian Sun、Chuan Shi、Hongying Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37373v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37373v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文从几何角度量化欧氏空间中的层间节点表示拥挤，给出双曲空间在层次数据上泛化优势的理论解释，并提出自适应融合两种几何的 EH-Fusion 框架。

**相关性简评：** 其层间最小间隔、表示拥挤与泛化界的分析可能为 LVR 中 latent 状态的层级分离、容量限制及相似度结构提供核查视角，但图表示学习与视觉推理的迁移路径尚不明确。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Euclidean Crowding-Driven Adaptive Euclidean-Hyperbolic Graph Learning

**abstract/A1：** Hyperbolic graph representation learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for modeling hierarchical data. Unlike Euclidean space, which exhibits polynomial volume growth, hyperbolic space grows exponentially with radius, naturally accommodating tree-like structures without the geometric distortion known as Euclidean crowding. Despite strong empirical performance, the theoretical mechanisms underlying hyperbolic embeddings remain poorly understood. In this work, we provide a geometric quantifcation research for Euclidean crowding, a generalization-theoretic explanation for the superiority of hyperbolic space on hierarchical data, and further develop an adaptive Euclidean-hyperbolic fusion learning framework toward bridging this gap. Specifically, we \\textbf{first} establish a geometric quantification of Euclidean crowding by analyzing layer-wise minimum separation in node embeddings, proving that fixed-dimensional Euclidean embeddings of tree-like graphs inevitably exhibit layer-wise crowding that leads to weaker margin-based generalization bounds. \\textbf{Second}, we provide a generalization-bound for the superiority of hyperbolic geometry on hierarchical data. \\textbf{Third}, we propose EH-Fusion, a geometry-adaptive framework that lifts Euclidean GNN representations into hyperbolic space via exponential mapping and adaptively fuses both geometries based on node-level neighborhood expansion patterns. Extensive experiments validate our theoretical insights and demonstrate the effectiveness of EH-Fusion on benchmark datasets.

</details>

<a id="paper-251"></a>

### 251. [LinMix: Data Mixing as Linear Domain Attribution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37390v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37390

**作者：** Hubert Baniecki、Przemyslaw Biecek、Martin Pawelczyk

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CL、stat.ML

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37390v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37390v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把预训练数据域混合视作域归因问题，提出 LinMix：在 capped simplex 顶点处采样混合配比（每个域取上限或不出现），从而去相关各域效应、拟合系数即归因值的线性模型，并论证顶点采样最优、Dirichlet 采样会放大归因误差；在 OLMo2-1B 与 Qwen3.5-4B 上验证收益。

**相关性简评：** 其「用去相关的实验设计把各因素贡献分离为可解释线性系数」的归因思路，可能可供核查于 LVR 中 THINK/LOOK/STOP 或视觉旁路贡献的分配实验；但论文面向纯文本预训练数据配比，与 latent 视觉推理无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LinMix: Data Mixing as Linear Domain Attribution

**abstract/A1：** Language model performance depends strongly on the mixture of domains in the pretraining data corpus. Popular data mixing methods select these domain weights by training many small proxy models on random mixes and then fitting a regression model to predict performance from the mix. We recognize this practice as a domain attribution problem, where the regression measures each domain's contribution to the target metric. Through this lens, the standard Dirichlet sampling of proxy data mixes is a poor regression design that requires tuning and cannot support a linear fit for hundreds of domains. Building on classical optimal experimental design, we introduce LinMix, which samples mixes at the vertices of the capped simplex so that each domain is at its cap or absent. This hyperparameter-free vertex sampling decorrelates domain effects, enabling an interpretable linear model whose coefficients are the domain attributions. The optimal mix given by LinMix provably keeps only domains with positive attribution values and requires no search over candidate mixes. We further establish the optimality of vertex sampling, and prove that Dirichlet sampling inflates the attribution error when its mixes concentrate around the prior data mix. Pretraining experiments with 1B-parameter OLMo2 and 4B Qwen3.5 language models show that LinMix outperforms Dirichlet-based data mixing across reasoning, code, and math benchmarks, with relative gains of 46% on MBPP and 14% on GSM8K. At 288 fine-grained domains, only vertex sampling supports a linear fit, and LinMix improves benchmark accuracy by 11% on average over the best nonlinear baseline.

**comment/C1：** NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Attributing Model Behavior at Scale

</details>

<a id="paper-252"></a>

### 252. [Constraining the Commit Boundary: A DIO/ODI-Informed Authorization Guardrail for Agentic Entity Resolution](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37407v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37407

**作者：** Daniel Beránek

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CR、cs.DB

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37407v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37407v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向 agentic entity resolution，提出一种基于 DIO/ODI 的授权护栏概念架构：区分「可供推理使用的证据」与「有权授权持久身份变更的证据」，并用抽象两阶段 supervisor 规定仅在命题覆盖、冲突消解、范围绑定和状态/版本有效性均满足时才允许提交。

**相关性简评：** 其「证据准入不等于提交授权」的分层与 commit 门控思路，可能为 LVR 中区分推理期证据使用与最终答案提交（STOP/commit 决策）提供概念参照；但论文为纯概念设计、无实现与实证，迁移可行性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Constraining the Commit Boundary: A DIO/ODI-Informed Authorization Guardrail for Agentic Entity Resolution

**abstract/A1：** Agentic Entity Resolution (ER) systems iteratively plan actions, retrieve evidence, revise hypotheses, and propose graph mutations. An erroneous identity hypothesis may condition later retrieval and produce apparent confirmation. This paper proposes a Digital Identity Optimization (DIO) and Ontology of Digital Identity (ODI)-informed authorization boundary between evidence available for agent reasoning and evidence eligible to authorize persistent identity-changing mutations. A domain-governed constraint-binding function maps selected reconstruction-failure distinctions to explicit identity-preserving constraints. An abstract two-phase supervisor permits a commit only when claim-specific authorization-eligible evidence supports every required proposition, relevant conflicts are resolved, the operation remains within its authorized scope, and relevant state and policy versions remain valid.
