Admission as UNVERIFIED does not confer authorization eligibility. Separate claim-specific promotion governs evidentiary use; insufficient support or unresolved material conflict leads to deferral. The contribution is a specification connecting diagnostic-to-constraint mapping, evidence promotion, proposition coverage, conflict handling, scope binding, and commit-time version integrity. A proposed evaluation protocol is designed to assess the mapping's incremental value beyond generic authorization and conventional identifier rules. This is a conceptual architecture without implementation or empirical validation, not a theorem of semantic correctness. Its property depends on domain-policy adequacy, evidence promotion, and enforcement. It does not prevent erroneous hypotheses or hypothesis-conditioned retrieval; dependency-aware evidence flow remains future research.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 1 figure

</details>

<a id="paper-253"></a>

### 253. [Segmentation Confidence for Arbitrary CNNs](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37431v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37431

**作者：** Baris Oguz、Xing Yao、Claudia Tawil、Alison Pouch、Brett Byram、Gabriel Arenas、Nadav Schwartz、Ipek Oguz

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37431v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37431v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究如何在不重新训练的前提下为现有 CNN 医学分割管线加入体素级不确定性估计，并提出把不确定性图汇总为单一置信度分数的方法，在多 ROI 与模态上验证该分数可预测未见数据的分割表现。

**相关性简评：** 其把逐体素不确定性聚合成单一置信分数的思路，可能与 LVR 中由内部状态导出停止或验证信号的评价方式形成类比，可供核查；但任务与模态差异大，未涉及多模态推理。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Segmentation Confidence for Arbitrary CNNs

**abstract/A1：** Convolutional neural networks (CNN) are widely used for medical image segmentation. However, they lack an inherent measure of confidence. Existing methods for estimating prediction uncertainty often require a custom network architecture and/or dedicated training setups and need to be trained from scratch. In addition, the deduction of a single confidence score per input from the uncertainty maps is not always straightforward. In this study, we explore voxel-based uncertainty estimation methods that can be added to existing segmentation pipelines without training from scratch. We also propose a novel method to estimate a single confidence score from the uncertainty maps. Our evaluations on different ROIs and modalities suggest that our confidence score can predict segmentation performance in unseen data.

**comment/C1：** 5 pages, 6 figures, published in ISBI 2026

</details>

<a id="paper-254"></a>

### 254. [Interpretable Meta-Policies for Neural Decision Making](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37451v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37451

**作者：** Raban Emunds、Jannis Blüml、Quentin Delfosse、Kristian Kersting

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37451v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37451v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 NEXUS 分层强化学习框架，将神经技能与符号元策略结合，把透明推理放在高层技能而非原子动作上，借助对象中心观测与 LLM 设计奖励和元策略，并比较神经、符号、神经符号三类元策略架构。

**相关性简评：** 其「高层符号选择 + 低层神经技能」的状态抽象与决策分层，可能为 LVR 中在哪里施加可解释的高层选择与停止决策提供参照；但该文不涉及视觉语言推理与 latent 证据使用，需较多核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interpretable Meta-Policies for Neural Decision Making

**abstract/A1：** Deep Reinforcement Learning remains difficult to interpret and align due to the black-box nature of neural networks. While (neuro-)symbolic approaches improve transparency by incorporating symbolic reasoning, applying them directly to low-level actions results in overly complex, unreadable policies. We introduce NEXUS, a hierarchical RL framework that resolves this tension by combining neural skills with symbolic meta-policies. By shifting transparent reasoning to high-level skills rather than atomic actions, NEXUS drastically reduces symbolic complexity without sacrificing environment performance. Object-centric observations enable high-level reasoning and the design of reward functions and meta-policies by interaction with LLMs. We demonstrate that NEXUS agents learn disentangled skills, are interpretable regarding high-level goals, are better aligned to goals described in the game manual, less prone to reward hacking and more robust to game simplifications than deep RL. Finally, we isolate and evaluate the impact of different meta-policy architectures (neural, symbolic, neuro-symbolic) on environmental return and aligned task performance.

</details>

<a id="paper-255"></a>

### 255. [Concordat: Typed Sessions for LLM Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37461v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37461

**作者：** Milad Sikaroudi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.MA

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37461v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37461v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** Concordat 是一个 Python 库，把 agent 的接口定义为有限状态协议（借鉴 typestate 与 session types），在运行时只提供当前状态合法的工具、拦截越权调用、把需要人类决定的动作降为请求，并在 handoff 间线性分摊 token 与工具调用预算；作者在 tau-bench 的 50 个航空任务中加入施压客户，标准 agent 多次未经确认就改动，Concordat 为零且任务成功率无显著差异。

**相关性简评：** 其显式的协议状态机与预算记账或许可供核查，用于形式化约束 LVR 中「何时取证据、何时停止、计算预算如何分配」，但与视觉表示和 latent 推理生成无直接联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Concordat: Typed Sessions for LLM Agents

**abstract/A1：** Agents built on large language models are increasingly allowed to take consequential actions: cancelling bookings, issuing refunds, changing accounts. The rules that govern those actions, such as "obtain the customer's explicit confirmation before changing anything", are usually stated in the prompt and followed only as reliably as the model chooses to follow them. We present Concordat, a Python library that treats an agent as an object whose interface is a finite-state protocol, in the spirit of typestate and session types, and enforces that protocol on the model at run time. On every turn the model is offered only the tools that are legal in the current protocol state, calls outside that set are rejected before they have side effects, decisions reserved for humans can only be requested, and token and tool-call budgets are charged and split linearly across handoffs. Protocols are checked when they are defined, and a subclass of an agent is rejected if it could bypass its parent's protocol, a behavioural form of Liskov substitution. We evaluate a Concordat agent against the standard agent of tau-bench on its 50 airline tasks, adding simulated customers who pressure the agent to skip confirmation. The standard agent made changes without the required "yes" in 13 of 50 tasks with gpt-4.1 and in 29 of 128 task-runs with Claude Haiku 5.5; the Concordat agent made none, with no significant difference in task success. tau-bench's own reward did not detect most of these violations. Concordat is open source.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 1 figure, 1 table. Code, benchmark harness and results: https://github.com/miladsikaroudi/concordat

</details>

<a id="paper-256"></a>

### 256. [How Reliable Is Individualized Functional-Network Attribution in Resting-State fMRI Cognitive Prediction in ADNI?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37472v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37472

**作者：** Sheethal Bhat、Mahfuzur Rahman Chowdhury、Imam Ahmed Shaik、Rose Dawn Bharath、Siming Bayer、Andreas Maier

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37472v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37472v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在 ADNI 上评估冻结的静息态功能连接预测器（预测 MMSE/ADAS-Cog13）的逐被试网络消融归因的测试—重测可靠性，比较分半相关与跨扫描重测相关，并引入被试错配的置换零假设；结果显示跨扫描可靠性明显下降，个体特异性成分存在但较小。

**相关性简评：** 其「同会话分半相关高估、跨扫描重测+被试错配置换零假设」的设计可能为 LVR 中区分个体特异 latent 内容与人群共享结构的贡献分配与因果作用评价提供可核查的检验范式，但任务领域相距较远，需较多迁移判断。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Reliable Is Individualized Functional-Network Attribution in Resting-State fMRI Cognitive Prediction in ADNI?

**abstract/A1：** Individualized attribution estimates which functional networks drive a model prediction for a given subject and is often proposed as an interpretable resting-state fMRI (rs-fMRI) biomarker, but its test-retest reliability is rarely quantified. We evaluate per-subject network-ablation attribution for a frozen resting-state functional connectivity (FC) predictor (Schaefer-200 FC predicting MMSE/ADAS-Cog13) trained on N=875 subjects from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI), using, and (iii) a subject-level permutation null. Split-half reliability is near-ceiling (Spearman p=0.948/0.950) but drops on scan-rescan (p=0.609/0.616). Under the mismatched-subject null, correlations remain substantial (p~0.46-0.47), indicating strong population-shared structure, yet matched pairs exceed this null (z~6.6-6.9, p&lt;=10-4). Thus, in ADNI, individualized network attribution contains a modest but non-zero person-specific component atop a strong population-shared pattern. We recommend reporting scan-rescan reliability together with a permutation-null control, rather than within-session proxies alone, to avoid overstating individual specificity.

</details>

<a id="paper-257"></a>

### 257. [The Structure Gap: When More Data Cannot Replace Structured Reasoning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37523v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37523

**作者：** Kheer Sagar Patel、Mojtaba Soltanalian

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37523v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37523v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「结构鸿沟」概念，界定输入输出映射由已知结构（求解器、对称群、图、物理定律、语法等）决定的「结构编码问题」，在高斯逆问题上证明：已知算子 Wiener 解可达 Bayes 风险，而仅见样本的稠密学习器存在持续超额风险，并给出覆盖下界与未观测轨道不可辨识性结果，辅以逆问题、稀疏恢复与图可达性实验。

**相关性简评：** 其关于有限数据下可辨识性、覆盖下界与尺寸外推的论证，可能为 LVR 中「latent 状态是否保留任务必要结构、长度泛化与何时需外部结构约束」的评价提供概念线索，但形式化设定能否迁移到多模态推理尚待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Structure Gap: When More Data Cannot Replace Structured Reasoning

**abstract/A1：** A persistent belief in modern AI is that sufficiently large learners trained on sufficiently much data can make explicit domain knowledge optional. This paper examines a limitation of that belief. We define structure-encoded problems: tasks whose input-output map is determined, or sharply constrained, by known structure such as a solver, symmetry group, graph, physical law, measurement operator, grammar, combinatorial search space, or optimization objective. For such tasks, the central question is not expressivity--a dense neural network may contain the desired map--but finite-data identification, extrapolation, and certification. We introduce the structure gap: the finite-resource gap between a method that receives the governing structure and a learner that receives only examples. The strongest useful form of the claim is asymptotic rather than absolute: structure-agnostic learning may approach a structured solver as coverage grows, but every finite dataset can leave residual uncertainty that the structure resolves by construction. We prove this exactly for a Gaussian inverse problem: the known-operator Wiener solver attains Bayes risk, while the dense empirical learner in the correct linear class has persistent expected excess risk. We also prove a cell-coverage lower bound and an unobserved-orbit non-identifiability result. Controlled experiments in known-operator inversion, sparse recovery, and graph reachability with size extrapolation instantiate the mechanisms. When validated structure is available, it can constrain the hypothesis space before learning begins, leaving learning to address components the structure does not specify.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages, 6 figures, 2 tables. Includes supplementary material and reproducibility code/data

</details>

<a id="paper-258"></a>

### 258. [The Noise Plane: A Diffusion Bridge Across Exponent and Dispersion](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37576v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37576

**作者：** Xuran Hu、Yuru Jia、Tengxi Wang、Miloš Daković、Ljubiša Stanković、Wufan Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37576v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37576v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 p-Bridge，以噪声指数 p（方差—强度关系）与累积测量 ν（噪声水平）构成「噪声平面」统一 Gaussian、Poisson、Gamma 等 Tweedie 噪声的去噪，用测量索引的随机桥连接分布并支持指定起止索引，配合基于 deviance 的目标与单一指数条件化网络，并在合成图像、荧光显微与 SAR 图像上验证跨噪声类型鲁棒性。

**相关性简评：** 其「测量索引扩散桥 + 可指定起止索引 + latent 复合泊松—Gamma 采样」可能为 diffusion 式 latent 推理状态的噪声/证据水平控制与停止决策提供可核查的形式化线索，但论文本身是纯图像噪声建模，与 VLM 推理状态的生成和使用尚无关，迁移性需阅读全文确认。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The Noise Plane: A Diffusion Bridge Across Exponent and Dispersion

**abstract/A1：** Diffusion-based denoising commonly uses Gaussian perturbations, whereas sensor noise follows diverse signal-dependent statistical laws. In this paper, we propose $p$-Bridge, a unified measurement-indexed diffusion framework for Tweedie noise. The noise exponent $p$ describes the variance--intensity relation, while accumulated measurement $ν$ controls the noise level. Together, they define a noise plane that includes Gaussian, Poisson, and Gamma models. The stochastic bridge connects measurement distributions through independent increments, giving denoising a physical interpretation as progressive measurement accumulation. Both the starting and stopping indices can be specified, allowing restoration from an observed measurement level to a prescribed higher level. Measurement superposition yields a shared reverse-mean update, paired with a deviance-based objective for learning the required conditional mean. A latent compound Poisson--Gamma construction enables sampling at intermediate exponents. A single exponent-conditioned network adapts to different Tweedie noise models without separate training for each exponent. Experiments show that exponent conditioning improves cross-exponent robustness over fixed-exponent models. Using estimated exponents, the shared network achieves robustness comparable to an oracle-selected set of five specialist models. Experiments on synthetic images, FMD fluorescence microscopy, and multi-sensor SAR images further demonstrate its effectiveness across different signal-dependent noise regimes.

**comment/C1：** 23 pages, 15 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-259"></a>

### 259. [As the Story Unfolds: Watching a Film and Identifying Characters as a Human Does](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37579v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37579

**作者：** Zhongrui Gui、Junyu Xie、Tengda Han、Weidi Xie、Andrew Zisserman

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37579v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37579v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文研究仅凭电影视频与对白识别主要角色并为其命名，构建了角色命名与首次出场的数据集，提出视频-对白模型，并设计随剧情推进逐步建立角色库的框架。

**相关性简评：** 其流式累积的角色库可作为外部状态更新与证据筛选的类比，可能启发 LVR 中跨片段维护视觉实体状态；但任务本身并非 latent reasoning，需核查其状态表示与证据使用方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** As the Story Unfolds: Watching a Film and Identifying Characters as a Human Does

**abstract/A1：** Films introduce characters incrementally as they progress. We study the problem of identifying principal characters and assigning their names directly from the movie itself, without using external cast lists, actor photographs, or pre-built character banks. We introduce the character naming task: given a name mentioned in dialogue and the associated video context, the model must determine which visible character bears that name. This task is challenging because name resolution often depends on cinematic cues such as shot-reverse-shot structure, self-introduction, direct address, third-party reference, gaze, and temporal continuity. We provide a dataset and annotations for evaluating character naming and first character appearances, develop a video-and-dialogue model for the task, and compare it with proprietary multimodal large language models. We further introduce a progressive framework for building a character bank as the film unfolds. Together, these contributions support online, human-like movie understanding in which character identities are inferred from the film itself.

**comment/C1：** Project Page: https://www.robots.ox.ac.uk/~vgg/research/StreamCast/

</details>

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37585v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37585v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Timestral 系列四个时间序列基础模型（5M–209M），用于概率预测，采用解耦视界预测、原生支持已知未来协变量的因果框架和先合成后混合数据的两阶段训练，并在 GIFT-Eval、TIME、fev-bench 上报告领先成绩。

**相关性简评：** 其「预测 token 与因果编码上下文分离、长视界无需掩码上下文、观测值兼作目标与后续上下文」的设计，或可供核查 LVR 长轨迹训练与长度泛化中的目标与状态分离问题，但跨模态迁移尚属推测。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Timestral

**abstract/A1：** We introduce Timestral, a family of four time series foundation models for probabilistic forecasting, with 5M, 28M, 123M, and 209M parameters. Timestral sets a new state of the art on GIFT-Eval, TIME, and fev-bench, where Timestral-209M and Timestral-123M take the top two places on every metric. We achieve this with three ingredients: Decoupled Horizon Forecasting (DHF), which separates prediction tokens from the causally encoded context, so that longer horizons are trained without masking context and each observed value is both a forecast target and context for later forecasts; a covariate framework that natively supports known-future covariates within a causal architecture; and a two-stage pipeline that pretrains exclusively on a diverse, large-scale synthetic corpus before post-training on a mix of real and synthetic data with extended context. This report describes the model design and its benchmark results. Weights for all four models are available on Hugging Face and inference code on GitHub.

**comment/C1：** Webpage: https://mistral.ai/news/timestral ; Weights: https://huggingface.co/collections/mistralai/timestral ; Code: https://github.com/mistralai/timestral

</details>

<a id="paper-261"></a>

### 261. [Know, then Draw: A New Learning Paradigm for Visual Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37626v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37626

**作者：** Yucheng Mao、Chongjian Ge、Zhuowen Tu、Hao Tan、Hanwen Jiang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37626v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37626v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出「先知识、后渲染」的两阶段范式：用自回归预训练获取高层语义知识，再用扩散后训练完成高保真渲染，并比较目标、表示与规模，提出仅用语言监督、无图像解码器与重建损失的纯语义 tokenizer，给出算力最优 scaling law，训练出 3B 文生图模型。

**相关性简评：** 其把「语义知识获取」与「像素细节渲染」显式分离、并指出语义质量比 AR 单独生成质量更关键，可能为 LVR 中任务必要语义保留与细节保真的权衡提供概念参照；但论文任务是图像生成而非多模态推理，迁移可行性有待核查。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Know, then Draw: A New Learning Paradigm for Visual Generation

**abstract/A1：** Visual generation requires both high-level semantic knowledge and fine-grained pixel details. We propose a learning paradigm that assigns these roles to autoregressive pretraining for knowledge acquisition and diffusion post-training for high-fidelity rendering. Within this framework, we study the objective, representation, and scaling of knowledge acquisition and rendering. Controlled comparisons identify autoregression as the stronger pretraining objective for downstream rendering, improving both learning efficiency and final generation quality. We further find that AR-only generation quality does not necessarily correlate with final performance; semantic quality matters more. This motivates a purely semantic tokenizer trained with language supervision alone, without an image decoder or reconstruction loss. We establish compute-optimal scaling laws for knowledge acquisition and rendering, and find that autoregressive pretraining lowers the fitted asymptotic rendering loss compared with pixel diffusion models trained from scratch. Guided by the laws, we train a 3B-parameter text-to-image model with only 6,600 H100 GPU-hours, achieving 86 on GenEval and 87 on DPG-Bench and validating the proposed paradigm at scale.

</details>

<a id="paper-262"></a>

### 262. [What Improves During Pretraining? Tracking Knowledge Acquisition with Point-in-Time Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37631v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37631

**作者：** Chang Su、Bin Wu、Emine Yilmaz、Yao Lu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37631v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37631v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 temporal variability score（TVS），用不同年份网页快照训练的多时点模型计算条目负对数似然的变异系数，并在六个基准上分析预训练过程中高、低 TVS 条目的学习速度与最终表现差异。

**相关性简评：** 与 LVR 无直接关联，但以条目级 NLL 跨时点变异刻画知识获取动态的诊断思路，可能可供核查用于衡量视觉证据或 latent 状态在训练过程中的一致性与习得时机。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** What Improves During Pretraining? Tracking Knowledge Acquisition with Point-in-Time Models

**abstract/A1：** Language models pretrained on web corpora collected over multiple years learn both general and domain-specific knowledge. How these different kinds of knowledge are learned as pretraining progresses remains unclear. Recent point-in-time models trained on web snapshots from different years provide a way to probe how knowledge prevalence varies over time. Using these models, we propose a temporal variability score (TVS), defined as the coefficient of variation of an item's negative log-likelihood across models. A higher score suggests that the knowledge may be less consistently represented across years of web text. We evaluate six benchmarks covering diverse topics using six models from multiple open-source model families. We find that low- and high-TVS items are learned similarly early in training, but high-TVS items improve faster later on. At the end of the pretraining run, high-TVS items outperform low-TVS items in 34 of 36 model-benchmark settings, with an average advantage of 7.3 points of chance-normalized accuracy. This pattern is most robust on knowledge-intensive tasks, even when comparing items of similar difficulty. Our findings reveal differences in how knowledge is learned over the course of pretraining, with temporal variability providing a diagnostic of these learning dynamics beyond aggregate benchmark scores.

**comment/C1：** Accepted to AACL 2026. This version corrects minor errors in the appendix

</details>

<a id="paper-263"></a>

### 263. [MuonGS: Pretraining LLMs with Relaxed Weight-Norm Control](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37647v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37647

**作者：** Athanasios Glentis、Dawei Li、Mingyi Hong

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、math.OC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37647v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37647v1>)

**研究分类：** EXPLORATORY · 探索性关联

**优先级：** P2

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文分析 Muon 优化器中权重范数控制的作用，以累积有效学习率刻画训练全程的权重位移，并提出用 Gram–Schmidt 投影去除更新径向分量的 MuonGS，给出范数按 √t 增长及收敛性证明，在 160M–1B 规模预训练上验证困惑度收益。

**相关性简评：** 纯文本预训练优化器工作，与 LVR 无直接关联；但其对权重范数增长与累积有效学习率的定量刻画，可能可供核查多步/循环 latent 推理训练中深度迭代稳定性与长度泛化的描述方式。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MuonGS: Pretraining LLMs with Relaxed Weight-Norm Control

**abstract/A1：** Weight decay and explicit weight-norm constraints improve optimizer performance in language model pretraining, yet a unified understanding of why such control helps and how much is needed remains incomplete. We analyze norm control in Muon over the entire training trajectory using the \\emph{accumulated effective learning rate}, $C\_T=\\sum\_{t&lt;T}η\_t/\\\|W\_t\\\|\_F$, where $η\_t$ is the nominal learning rate and $η\_t/\\\|W\_t\\\|\_F$ is the effective learning rate relative to the weight norm. For bounded update directions, accumulation over any training interval bounds relative weight displacement. We show that vanilla Muon can accumulate only logarithmically, potentially limiting exploration and helping explain its slower empirical progress, whereas MuonW and MuonH guarantee at least linear accumulation under the stated conditions. Going beyond bounded-norm control, we show that sufficiently slow norm growth guarantees faster accumulation than the logarithmic worst case, opening up less restrictive optimizer designs. Based on this insight, we propose MuonGS, which uses Gram--Schmidt (GS) projection to remove the radial component of each Muon update without fixing the weight norm. With bounded update directions and a constant nominal learning rate, we prove that the weight norm grows at most proportionally to $\\sqrt{t}$ after $t$ steps. Consequently, MuonGS guarantees $C\_T=Ω(\\sqrt{T})$, with accumulation sustained even in later training. Further, for smooth scale-invariant objectives we establish its $O(T^{-1/2})$ stationarity guarantee. In pretraining experiments with 160M- and 400M-parameter dense models as well as a 135M active- and 1B total- parameter MoE model, MuonGS achieves lower validation perplexity than MuonH at long training horizons. In particular, for the dense model it achieves an estimated token-equivalent speedup up to about $15\\%$ at matched validation loss to MuonH.

**comment/C1：** 21 pages, 9 figures

</details>

### 264. [Physiognomy Revisited: How Vision-Language Models Encode Sensitive Attributes Through Image Transformation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35910v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35910

**作者：** Soumyajit Datta、Arka Dutta、Ashiqur R. KhudaBukhsh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.CL、cs.CY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35910v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35910v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 研究两个 VLM 在按种族、性取向等敏感身份转换人脸图像时如何编码这些属性，发现遵从与拒绝不确定且群体敏感，生成图像在统计上可区分并放大刻板印象，且相关偏差会延续到文本推理与人物刻画任务。

**排除原因：** 该工作是对图像编辑与人物刻画中的敏感属性偏差审计，未涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据筛选、计算分配或停止决策，也无可直接核查的状态表示或训练线索，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Physiognomy Revisited: How Vision-Language Models Encode Sensitive Attributes Through Image Transformation

**abstract/A1：** Emergent generative AI technologies, while remarkably beneficial across several tasks and domains, can suffer from unforeseen vulnerabilities and difficult-to-anticipate failure modes. Such failure modes can disparately affect vulnerable communities. This paper investigates two well-known vision language models (VLMs) and inspects how these models encode protected identities while generating images. We show that when VLMs are requested to transform human face images to reflect race identities (e.g., Hispanic, Black, White, or Asian) and sexual orientation (e.g., gay or straight), VLMs often comply with such requests. We demonstrate that compliance (and refusal) is non-deterministic, group-sensitive, and generated images are statistically distinguishable, often accentuating deep-seated stereotypes. We further show that representation biases introduced during image generation can re-emerge in textual reasoning and characterization tasks. Finally, we show that this worrisome finding is not limited to racial identity or sexual orientation, image generation can perpetuate bias around criminality, raising concerning possibilities of AI-assisted profiling.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at EMNLP 2026 (Main Conference Track)

</details>

<a id="paper-265"></a>

### 265. [ResCom: Activation Residual Compensation for Large Language Model Quantization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35955v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35955

**作者：** Ning Yang、Bohan Cui、Zhuowen Liu、Yuting Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35955v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35955v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ResCom，用激活量化舍入残差的低秩校正来补偿低比特量化造成的层输出误差，并以输出感知的闭式标定选取校正方向，在 Llama-3 等模型上报告困惑度与下游任务提升。

**排除原因：** 该工作是纯部署阶段的量化压缩技术，关注数值精度损失的补偿与显存/延迟开销，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取、停止决策等主线问题，且残差补偿线索与 latent 推理状态的内容保真缺乏具体可迁移联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ResCom: Activation Residual Compensation for Large Language Model Quantization

**abstract/A1：** Low-bit weight--activation quantization reduces the memory and computation needed to deploy large language models, but activation rounding can still degrade accuracy after calibration. Reconstruction from rounded inputs cannot distinguish activations within the same quantization bin. We propose ResCom, a low-rank correction that uses the activation rounding residual to compensate for the resulting layer-output error. An output-aware calibration procedure selects residual directions by their contribution to output reconstruction and computes the correction in closed form, without gradient training or updates to the calibrated weights. On Llama-3-8B with 4-bit weights and activations, rank-32 attention corrections reduce WikiText-2 perplexity from 7.708 to 7.147, improve MMLU by 2.89 percentage points, and raise five-shot GSM8K accuracy from 23.96 to 36.16 percent. Evaluations on models from 1B to 70B parameters and on held-out C4 test the method beyond this setting. The evaluated FP32 implementation adds 2.25 percent to 4K prefill time; a separate compact-storage implementation retains a 27.7 percent peak-memory reduction relative to FP16.

**comment/C1：** 25 pages (10 pages main text + appendix), 9 figures, 20 tables

</details>

### 266. [From Slices to Volumes: Comparing 2D, 2.5D, and 3D Residual Networks for Pulmonary Nodule Malignancy-Risk Classification](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35960v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35960

**作者：** Bahodir Nazarov、Zineb Gharib、Fatima Zazoul

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35960v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35960v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是在 LIDC-IDRI 上比较 2D/2.5D/3D ResNet 的肺结节恶性风险分类工程对照，属常规医学影像任务应用，未涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部状态、证据筛选或停止决策，缺少可迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** From Slices to Volumes: Comparing 2D, 2.5D, and 3D Residual Networks for Pulmonary Nodule Malignancy-Risk Classification

**abstract/A1：** Purpose: To compare 2D, 2.5D, and 3D residual networks for pulmonary nodule malignancy-risk classification and evaluate momentum-contrast pretraining, mild augmentation, and exclusion of indeterminate ratings. Methods: A retrospectively prepared LIDC-IDRI cohort contained 2,654 nodules labeled benign, indeterminate, or malignant using median radiologist ratings. Patient-disjoint splits comprised 1,876 training, 382 validation, and 396 test nodules. Representations included one axial slice, five neighboring slices, and a local CT volume. Each track evaluated a randomly initialized baseline, momentum-contrast pretraining followed by fine-tuning, and supervised training with mild augmentation. Checkpoints were selected by validation Macro-F1. The 2D experiments used three seeds; 2.5D and 3D results were single-run estimates. A separate binary experiment excluded indeterminate nodules. Results: Augmented models achieved test Macro-F1 values of $0.6134\\pm0.0075$ for 2D, $0.6537$ for 2.5D, and $0.5403$ for 3D; the 2D uncertainty denotes population standard deviation across seeds. Corresponding accuracies were $0.6128$, $0.6869$, and $0.5455$. Augmentation improved Macro-F1 within every track, whereas the tested pretraining recipes reduced it. Binary Macro-F1 values were $0.8227$, $0.8664$, and $0.7554$, respectively. Conclusion: Augmented 2.5D achieved the highest observed performance. Differences in preprocessing, optimization, capacity, and replication limit attribution to dimensionality. Labels reflect radiologist-assessed malignancy risk rather than pathology-confirmed cancer. Code and experimental results are available at https://github.com/CT-DeepLearning-Team/CT-2D-2.5D-3D-DeepLearning.

</details>

### 267. [Knowledge distillation across teacher architectures for compact diabetic retinopathy grading](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35961v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35961

**作者：** Mossa Manisa、Habiba Jahan Priya、A. K. M. Fakhrul Hossain

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35961v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35961v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 在 APTOS 2019 视网膜眼底图像五级分级任务上，比较三种教师架构的离线 response-based 知识蒸馏，蒸馏得到 1.52 M 参数的 MobileNetV3-Small 学生模型并做多种子评估。

**排除原因：** 属于医学图像分类的模型压缩与部署研究，仅输出单一类别预测，不涉及推理期 latent 状态、视觉证据筛选或动态计算，蒸馏线索也无具体的 LVR 借鉴点。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Knowledge distillation across teacher architectures for compact diabetic retinopathy grading

**abstract/A1：** Diabetic retinopathy grading from retinal fundus photographs is important but difficult to deploy on resource-constrained hardware. We conducted a controlled study of offline response-based knowledge distillation for five-class diabetic retinopathy grading on APTOS 2019, comparing ResNet-50, MobileNetV3-Large, and Swin Transformer-Tiny (Swin-T) teachers distilled into a MobileNetV3-Small student. A 3 x 4 x 2 grid of teacher, temperature, and hard-label weight was screened at one seed, while six configurations and the no-distillation baseline were evaluated across three seeds. The highest observed single-seed quadratic weighted kappa was 0.9093 (macro-F1 0.6851) for Swin-T at temperature 4 and hard-label weight 0.7; the corresponding three-seed mean was 0.9024, compared with 0.8804 for the baseline. The student used 1.52 M parameters and 5.94 MB, representing an 18.07x parameter reduction relative to Swin-T. Temperature and hard-label weight effects were teacher- and configuration-dependent, while small performance differences were sensitive to random seed. Knowledge distillation produced a compact student with competitive performance under the evaluated protocol, but independent external validation remains necessary.

**comment/C1：** 17 pages, 3 figures, 18 tables. Submitted to PLOS ONE

</details>

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35965v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35965v1>)

**排除原因：** 研究对象是纯理论的训练动力学与泛化现象，未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，也未提供可核查的状态表示或计算线索，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

**title/T1：** Generalization in Learning: Eyring Formula and Predator-prey Model

**abstract/A1：** In this work, generalization in machine learning is investigated using a predator-prey model. We overview biological and chemical interpretation of the stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics and generative adversarial network. For a particular example of recovering an unknown function by a polynomial of fixed degree from a set of noisy data, the predator-prey model provides better approximation compared to the gradient descent method. Further, thermodynamical arguments, in particular Eyring formula, are used to explain grokking (delayed generalization) phenomenon in machine learning.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 4 figures

</details>

### 269. [Multiple Leak Localization in Water Distribution Networks through Graph Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35972v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35972

**作者：** Arvin Ajoodani、Sara Nazif、Pouria Ramazi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35972v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35972v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 GNN 模型，在供水管网中无需预设漏点数或枚举组合即可同时定位多个漏点。

**排除原因：** 该文属管网监测的传感器定位分类任务，与 LVR 的视觉证据筛选、推理状态更新及计算决策无具体联系，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multiple Leak Localization in Water Distribution Networks through Graph Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** Leak localization in water distribution networks is challenging when multiple leaks occur simultaneously, particularly under sparse monitoring. Existing data-driven methods often assume a single or predefined number of leaks or require extensive enumeration of leak combinations. This study proposes a graph neural network (GNN) model that localizes a variable number of simultaneous leaks without specifying the leak count in advance or exhaustively generating every possible combination of leak locations for training. The model is evaluated on two benchmark networks of different sizes, the 31-node Hanoi network and the larger 92-node NET3 network, under variable demand, sparse pressure-sensor coverage, variable leak magnitudes, and varying numbers of simultaneous leaks. The GNN outperforms multilayer perceptron, support vector machine, K-nearest neighbors, and random forest classifiers by up to 12% and 20% in accuracy respectively in Hanoi and NET3 networks. Consistent performance across both networks supports the scalability of the proposed approach.

</details>

### 270. [Layerwise Repacking: Variable-Length Hybrid-Attention Training without an Extra Redistribution Pass](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35989v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.35989

**作者：** Jiahao Zhang、Junwei Feng、Dongliang Huang、Xiaoling Zhou、Yiqiao Liao、Chengru Song

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35989v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.35989v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出「分层重打包」的分布式训练方案，在混合注意力模型的可变长训练中，把样本在全注意力父组与节点内稀疏子组之间重排，以避免额外的数据再分配开销。

**排除原因：** 属分布式训练的通信与并行布局优化，未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，也未见可供借鉴的 latent 表示线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Layerwise Repacking: Variable-Length Hybrid-Attention Training without an Extra Redistribution Pass

**abstract/A1：** Variable-length training creates conflicting parallelism requirements across the layers of a hybrid-attention model. The longest samples require wide sequence-parallel (SP) groups of 16 or 32 ranks for full attention, whose cost grows quadratically with sequence length. Sparse-attention layers, dominated by near-linear MoE work, can instead balance most evaluated workloads within node-local SP8 groups. Reusing the wide groups for these layers pushes their all-to-all communication across nodes, where the observed effective rate is 7.0x lower. Yet switching to smaller groups changes which ranks own each sample; a separate redistribution at every full-sparse boundary would introduce another data transfer. Layerwise Repacking keeps each SP16/32 parent for full attention and repacks its samples into node-local sparse children at SP{1, 2, 4, 8}, where hidden states persist between full layers. The layout change rides on the two existing full-layer Ulysses sites: at each, one variable-size all-to-all performs both the head exchange and the sample repacking, so no redistribution pass is added. Because the balance the children can reach depends on which samples share a parent, an auxiliary parent solver rebalances parent membership before the children are formed. On all 22 steps with SP16/32 parents among the first 200 training steps of a 1F3S hybrid-attention model (one full-attention layer per three sparse-attention layers), repacking alone reduces the mean end-to-end time by 5.95% and raises model FLOPs utilization (MFU) by 3.75 percentage points over the baseline with repacking disabled; with the improved solver, end-to-end time falls by 7.61% and MFU rises by 4.83 percentage points.

</details>

<a id="paper-271"></a>

### 271. [DuplexTune: Coupled-State Replay for Memory-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Adaptation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36009v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36009

**作者：** Paul Raj、Simon Caldwell、Daniel Whitmore、Mutlu Mete

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36009v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36009v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是 MoE 语言模型参数高效微调的显存与吞吐优化，靠重放隐状态减少存储并分析状态恢复条件，属训练工程层面；与视觉/多模态推理中的 latent 状态生成、答案对 latent 内容的使用、证据获取与停止决策无具体联系，「coupled-state」等术语仅字面相近，未见可供核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** DuplexTune: Coupled-State Replay for Memory-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Adaptation

**abstract/A1：** Sparse expert execution does not remove the stored states needed to adapt a mixture-of-experts language model. We present DuplexTune, a framework for replaying coupled hidden states through pretrained attention and expert modules. Trainable width bridges connect two residual streams to the backbone. Bridge warm-up precedes backbone adaptation, with router weights kept frozen. We analyze the implicit state-recovery equation and identify the convergence and replay conditions needed for reconstruction. On Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B, the single-H800 evaluation reports 38.8 GB peak memory, versus 64.8 GB for supervised fine-tuning with checkpointing, and throughput of 25.1 versus 19.9 samples/s. DuplexTune achieves 67.2% MMLU, 75.6% GSM8K, and 7.69 MT-Bench, with a weaker multilingual score than the checkpointed baseline. The results characterize a configuration-dependent tradeoff among state storage, throughput, and adaptation quality.

</details>

### 272. [Jaxnasium: Embracing PyTrees For Any-Agent, Cross-Suite Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36075v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36075

**作者：** Koen Ponse、Aske Plaat、Thomas M. Moerland

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.DL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36075v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36075v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Jaxnasium：一个基于 JAX 的强化学习库，用 PyTree 统一表示观测与动作空间，使同一份算法实现可跨离散、连续、图像、复合及多智能体环境运行，并提供环境套件封装与评估工具。

**排除原因：** 内容为 RL 训练基础设施与接口统一工程，未涉及内部表示、推理期状态生成、视觉证据使用或停止决策等机制，缺乏可供 LVR 借鉴的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Jaxnasium: Embracing PyTrees For Any-Agent, Cross-Suite Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Hardware-accelerated reinforcement learning in JAX has gained rapid popularity in recent years and has proven to be a successful method of scaling online reinforcement learning. The resulting ecosystem, however, is fragmented: environment suites define their own interfaces, and algorithm implementations are typically written for a single suite, a single type of space, and either JAX-native or host-based environments. Here we present Jaxnasium, a library for JAX-based reinforcement learning, aiming to make the field more accessible. Jaxnasium represents observation and action spaces as PyTrees, and uses this structure to build networks and support multi-agent processing. Through this, its near-single-file implementations run on discrete, continuous, image, and composite spaces, as well as in multi-agent environments, without any changes to the algorithm code. The same files serve as readable, research-friendly algorithms, as well as importable algorithms for more applied workloads. Through a minimal Gymnasium-style interface and wrappers for 11 suites, over 300 environments are available on which a small PPO implementation with default hyperparameters is able to run. Additionally, through a simple wrapper, we can run Gymnasium and EnvPool environments with the same algorithm and training loop, with no performance loss compared to architectures specifically built for training JAX algorithms on host-based environments. Jaxnasium further provides tools for multi-seed evaluation, hyperparameter sweeps, and project scaffolding. Jaxnasium is open-sourced at https://github.com/ponseko/jaxnasium

**comment/C1：** 10 pages

</details>

### 273. [Why and How to Do Science When AI Is Better at It: A Framework Grounded in Human Understanding and Direction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36116v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36116

**作者：** Hoda Heidari、Aydin Mohseni

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CY

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36116v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36116v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 讨论科研任务能否委托给 AI 的哲学与政策框架，聚焦人类理解与科研方向，未涉及视觉/latent 推理的状态、证据使用、训练或评价，也无具体可借鉴的方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Why and How to Do Science When AI Is Better at It: A Framework Grounded in Human Understanding and Direction

**abstract/A1：** As AI begins to match or exceed human researchers at core scientific tasks, two questions become pressing: \\emph{why} should humans keep doing science, and \\emph{how} should they use AI when they do? This paper argues that the second can be answered only by answering the first. It holds that one indispensable aim of science is \\emph{durable, collective human understanding of the world, pursued in service of human ends and values}. From this principle it derives a two-part test for when a research task can be delegated to AI. The first part, \\emph{human understanding}, requires that the scientific community remain able to explain, defend and contest the resulting claims, and retain the skills to do so. The second, \\emph{human direction}, requires that judgments about which questions matter and what findings mean stay with accountable humans. Applying the test to each phase of research in the Contributor Roles Taxonomy (CRediT) yields recommendations that fields can tailor to their questions and methods. The framework differs from existing guidance in three ways. It rests on an account of what science is for rather than on integrity norms. It holds even if AI outperforms human researchers, rather than depending on AI's current limitations. And it judges each use of AI by its effects on human understanding and direction rather than by the type of tool, across every phase of research, including peer review and the training of new researchers. It closes with the reforms needed to keep science aimed at human understanding and direction.

</details>

### 274. [PrinGS: Privacy Preservation in Gaussian Splatting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36168v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36168

**作者：** Gaurav Behera、Abhinav Raundhal、Archisha Panda、Saumya Balina、Gargi Shroff、Ponnurangam Kumaraguru

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36168v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36168v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究 3D Gaussian Splatting 表示中的隐私保护，提出在训练后的 splat 中定位并匿名化敏感内容，以及在训练期阻止敏感内容写入表示。

**排除原因：** 论文关注 3D 重建表示的隐私擦除与匿名化，不涉及 VLM 推理状态生成、证据筛选或 latent 推理评价，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系与可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PrinGS: Privacy Preservation in Gaussian Splatting

**abstract/A1：** 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has rapidly become the preferred representation for high-fidelity novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction. However, the learned representation encodes sensitive visual information including human faces, license plates, documents, and computer screens, raising significant privacy concerns when trained splats are shared or deployed. In this work, we address the underexplored problem of privacy preservation in Gaussian Splatting by proposing a representation-level anonymization framework that directly anonymizes a trained splat. Given a trained splat and a user-provided reference view identifying sensitive content, our method localizes the corresponding regions in the underlying 3D representation and directly anonymizes them, ensuring that the sensitive information is absent from all subsequently rendered novel views while preserving the overall scene geometry and visual fidelity. We achieve this post-training anonymization in less than 1 second. Furthermore, we extend our framework with a privacy-by-design training strategy that prevents sensitive content from being embedded into the representation during optimization, enabling the generation of privacy-preserving splats directly from training images. Our method achieves a remarkably low Face Similarity Measure (FSM) of 9.79% at a retained Face Detection Rate (FDR) of 39.47% and makes sensitive text completely unreadable with an OCR Character Error Rate of 1.00. Our work aims to serve as the primary stepping stone towards privacy-aware 3D representations and motivates further research along these directions.

**comment/C1：** https://privacypreserving3dgs.github.io/

</details>

### 275. [Skill Dreaming: Evolving Repository Skills through Pull Request Streams](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36188v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36188

**作者：** Xinyuan Song、Hasibul Haque、Jay Somasundaram、Ryan Turner、Bowen Zhu、Liang Zhao

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36188v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36188v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文面向软件仓库的 PR 流构建并演化可执行技能库，属于代码智能体的工程流程，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据筛选与停止决策，也未提供可核查的 latent 表示线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Skill Dreaming: Evolving Repository Skills through Pull Request Streams

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on reusable skills for complex software engineering tasks, making skill libraries essential for repository development and maintenance. However, repository-specific skills must evolve with changing code structures, implementation conventions, and maintenance requirements. Pull requests (PRs) provide a natural source of such knowledge by recording how repositories evolve through implementation and review. Meanwhile, new PRs must follow repository-specific implementation patterns, modify related components consistently, and ensure that existing tests continue to pass while adding coverage for new functionality. This creates a mutually reinforcing cycle: PRs provide knowledge for updating skills, while these skills improve future PRs, maintain repositories, and support further skill evolution. To enable this co-evolution of PRs, repositories, and skills, we propose Skill Dreaming, a framework that learns from PR streams to guide continuous repository development and maintenance. Given a repository and its PR history, Skill Dreaming builds a repository-specific skill library by extracting executable knowledge from validated PRs and continuously refines the skills as new PR information becomes available. These skills guide coding agents not only in implementing requested features but also in maintaining associated tests, documentation, compatibility, and repository conventions. By integrating PR analysis, skill management, code execution, and continuous integration, Skill Dreaming provides an end-to-end workflow for applying learned skills to repository changes and incorporating subsequent PR outcomes into future skill updates.

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36280

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 EVA-Flow，一个按环境条件生成分子三维构象的统一框架，通过在真空、蛋白—配体对接、溶剂化和晶体堆积之间预训练与跨环境微调，共享化学结构知识并适应不同环境，重点改善数据稀缺场景。

**排除原因：** 研究属于分子构象生成与跨环境迁移学习，虽涉及条件生成与多环境采样，但与 LVR 关注的内部推理状态、视觉证据筛选、动态计算与停止决策无实质联系，也无可核查的方法借鉴线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EVA-Flow: Learning Shared Chemistry for Unified Environment-Aware Molecular Conformation Generation

**abstract/A1：** Molecular 3D structures underpin simulations of thermodynamics, kinetics, crystallization, and nucleation, making their accurate prediction central to drug discovery and materials design. However, molecular geometry can change dramatically across environments. Existing models either ignore environmental effects or train separate models for each environment. We hypothesize that transfer learning across contexts is possible for conformer generation and that a general model will perform better when training data are limited, as in ligand binding and crystallization. We introduce EVA-Flow, a unified framework for environment-aware molecular conformation generation that learns shared chemical structure and adapts it through environment-conditioned generative modeling. Across vacuum, protein-ligand docking, solvation, and crystal packing, pretraining and cross-environment finetuning provide their largest gains in the three data-scarce settings. Evaluation against multiple reference conformations shows that EVA-Flow covers a broad range of solvation conformations. Analyses of molecules shared across environments support its ability to generate different conformations for the same molecule in different environments.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at Transactions on Machine Learning Research (TMLR), 2026

</details>

### 277. [A Smart Instant Book Filter Model based on Dual-Objective Optimization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36287v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36287

**作者：** Yi Hou、Li Fan、Huizhong Duan

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36287v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36287v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一个「智能即时预订筛选」系统，在搜索排序中筛选即时预订房源，以「提升即时预订率且不降低整体预订率」为双目标优化，并用线上 A/B 测试验证效果。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是双边市场房源搜索与预订转化优化，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据筛选与停止决策，也无迁移线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Smart Instant Book Filter Model based on Dual-Objective Optimization

**abstract/A1：** Hospitality service in online marketplaces has become increasingly popular over the past few years, not only because it helps people easily rent short-term lodging but also because it empowers travelers to connect with locals and feel belonging. A typical pattern of these industries is a central platform mediating the economic interactions between guests and hosts, where a successful booking experience is the key to user satisfaction. However, one of the biggest challenges is the possible delay of response and chance of rejection when guests try to book a request-to-book listing. Previously, we enabled the instant book feature to allow hosts to instantly accept qualified guests and guests to confirm bookings immediately. Yet the symptom has not been alleviated given mixed request-to-book and instant book listing supply in search results. One possible solution is to promote all instant book listings in search results; however, booking rate will be damaged due to some improperly promoted low quality listings. In this paper, we present a novel smart instant book filter system that applies the instant book filter intelligently and shifts request-to-book to instant book without hurting booking rate. Compared with traditional two-sided markets research that targets finding strategies to maximize a single overall social welfare goal, our proposed system aims to maximize the instant book rate with the constraint that booking rate should not decrease, forming a dual-objective optimization problem. We show A/B testing results for our system exposed to global users, demonstrating that our model performs better than previous human-expert-tuned models in terms of both instant book rate and overall booking rate. We also achieved improved metrics such as the host instant book adoption rate. The proposed system has been deployed to production and is serving millions of users every day.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 5 figures. Presented at TSMO'18: WSDM 2018 Workshop on Two-sided Marketplace Optimization: Search, Pricing, Matching &amp; Growth, Marina Del Rey, CA, USA

</details>

### 278. [Spiking Neural Networks Are Harder to Steal: A Benchmark Study of Model Stealing Attacks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36298v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36298

**作者：** Ayana Moshruba、Maryam Parsa

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36298v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36298v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该工作在 MNIST、Fashion-MNIST 和 CIFAR-10 上基准评测四种模型窃取攻击对 SNN 与 ANN 的复制效果，发现 SNN 替代模型与目标的一致性、对抗迁移性均更低。

**排除原因：** 主题为图像分类模型的安全性与功能复制难度，未涉及视觉证据使用、推理状态生成或监督分配，膜电位时序状态仅用于解释抗窃取性，缺乏可供核查的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Spiking Neural Networks Are Harder to Steal: A Benchmark Study of Model Stealing Attacks

**abstract/A1：** Machine learning models deployed behind query interfaces expose more than their predictions, because an adversary can use those predictions to rebuild the model itself. Model stealing attacks (MSAs) exploit this exposure by training a surrogate model that replicates the decision behavior of a target through black-box queries alone, without access to its parameters or training data. These attacks are well characterized for conventional artificial neural networks (ANNs), yet spiking neural networks (SNNs) process information in a fundamentally different way. SNNs communicate through sparse, discrete spikes, and their outputs depend on membrane potentials that evolve over time rather than on a static mapping from input to output. This temporal mode of computation suggests that the decision behavior of an SNN may be harder to reconstruct from its responses. This work tests that possibility by benchmarking four representative MSAs, namely the Papernot, Tramèr, T-RND, and color perturbation attacks, against SNNs and their ANN counterparts on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10. Across every attack and dataset, SNNs prove consistently more difficult to steal. Surrogates of SNNs achieve 10% to 20% lower agreement with the target model and 15% to 25% lower adversarial transferability than surrogates of ANNs. We attribute this resistance to the way SNNs compute, as their spike-based and time-dependent dynamics yield decision boundaries that ANN-based surrogates struggle to approximate, even under large query budgets. These findings extend the known resilience of SNNs beyond attacks on training data to the replication of model functionality, strengthening the case for neuromorphic architectures in security-sensitive applications.

</details>

<a id="paper-279"></a>

### 279. [Bridging Exploration and Loss of Plasticity in On-Policy Reinforcement Learning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36309v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36309

**作者：** Andrea Vasco Grieco、Evelyn D'Elia、Edoardo Caldarelli、Raffaello Camoriano

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36309v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36309v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文分析 PPO 各向同性高斯策略中的可塑性丧失（LoP），提出以策略标准差 σ 刻画其影响并解释常见缓解手段。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是连续动作控制 RL 的训练可塑性，未涉及 latent 视觉推理状态、视觉证据使用或 VLM 推理的获取与停止决策，也无可具体核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Bridging Exploration and Loss of Plasticity in On-Policy Reinforcement Learning

**abstract/A1：** Deep reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms have been observed to lose the ability to adaptively learn from new data, a phenomenon termed Loss of Plasticity (LoP). While many metrics have been shown to correlate with it, the causal mechanisms and the geometric conditions from which it can arise have not been clearly identified. We analyze LoP in Proximal Policy Optimization with isotropic Gaussian policies, the state-of-the-art formulation for many continuous action spaces in RL. We report multiple metrics which characterize the effect that the policy standard deviation $σ$ has on LoP, and we also suggest related explanations for the effectiveness of widely adopted LoP mitigation techniques. We observe a significant effect of $σ$ on the extent of LoP, and accompany this with both theoretical and experimental supporting arguments. Our experimental validation consists of LoP benchmark evaluations against standard baselines as well as real-world policy deployment on a 7-DoF robotic arm.

</details>

### 280. [Plenty of Room in the Middle: Revealing Opportunities on a GPU Cluster via Fine-Grained Monitoring](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36329v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36329

**作者：** Andrew Sheinberg、Samyak Gupta、Kai Li

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36329v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36329v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文对一个高校 GPU 集群做 100 毫秒粒度的使用情况监测研究，基于 DCGM 构建轻量工具并在 319 块 GPU 上采集数月数据，分析作业的资源利用、申请时长与实际时长偏差以及多 GPU 通信模式。

**排除原因：** 内容属于集群调度与资源利用率测量，未涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据筛选与停止决策，也无可迁移的状态表示或监督方法线索，与 LVR 研究无实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Plenty of Room in the Middle: Revealing Opportunities on a GPU Cluster via Fine-Grained Monitoring

**abstract/A1：** GPU clusters have become essential computing resources for AI and scientific research in academia and industry. However, their high cost and growing demand often result in long queueing delays. While previous monitoring efforts have operated at second- or minute-level frequencies, they primarily informed job-level scheduling for clusters and lacked the detail needed to optimize GPU-level utilization. This paper presents an in-depth study of a university research GPU cluster, analyzing resource usage at 100 ms intervals. We have developed a lightweight monitoring tool that leverages Nvidia's DCGM library to capture fine-grained "compute utilization" metrics within GPUs. Deployed on a cluster with 89 nodes and 319 GPUs, our tool collected data over several months. This paper focuses on a 10-day period with 7,399 jobs submitted by 150 users from 12 departments. Our study reveals three key findings: (1) the GPU cluster exhibits significant underutilization, with over 70% of jobs using less than 20% of GPU memory and 50% averaging below 5% Streaming Multiprocessor (SM) utilization; (2) 80% of jobs have a drastic mismatch between their requested and actual durations, significantly limiting the scheduler's effectiveness; and (3) the multi-GPU jobs we examined show highly periodic communication patterns. These findings suggest there is plenty of room in the middle: substantial utilization gains are available through better job scheduling and GPU multitasking, independent of hardware or application improvements. Our monitoring solution is available as open-source software at https://github.com/als244/gpu\_server\_monitoring.

**comment/C1：** 19 pages, 12 figures, 4 tables. Originally written in fall 2024

</details>

### 281. [CrowdCC: Jointly Benchmarking Corruption Robustness and Deployment Quantization in Crowd Counting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36340v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36340

**作者：** S Mahmudul Hasan、Lu Peng

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36340v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36340v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CrowdCC 基准，在 23 种部署相关腐蚀、3 个严重度、4 个计数数据集与 26 个模型上，联合评估人群计数的腐蚀鲁棒性与 FP32/FP16/INT8 量化效果及吞吐、能耗。

**排除原因：** 该工作聚焦人群计数的部署量化与输入腐蚀鲁棒性评测，属一般视觉任务的应用与工程基准，未涉及推理期 latent 状态、视觉证据筛选或计算/停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CrowdCC: Jointly Benchmarking Corruption Robustness and Deployment Quantization in Crowd Counting

**abstract/A1：** Crowd-counting models deployed on edge devices face two shifts: inputs are degraded along the capture and transmission pipeline, while models are quantized to satisfy resource constraints. Yet corruption robustness and deployment quantization are typically evaluated separately. We introduce CrowdCC, a controlled corruption benchmark that jointly evaluates these effects using 23 deployment-motivated corruptions at three severity levels, four crowd-counting datasets, and 26 models using A100 TensorRT engines at FP32, FP16, and post-training INT8 precision, paired with throughput and energy measurements from corresponding Jetson Orin NX configurations. The corruptions span six families, with head annotations adjusted when geometry or visibility changes. Our evaluation shows that capture-chain corruptions consistently produce the largest degradation across the evaluated models, while crowd density affects absolute and relative error differently. Clean accuracy does not fully predict corrupted performance. INT8 alters corruption robustness substantially more than FP16, with no systematic direction, and does not consistently improve latency or energy. INT8 inference efficiency is strongly associated with reformat overhead in the compiled engine, while slowdowns are also observed under limited INT8 coverage. Precision changes corrupted-error rankings and robustness-cost frontiers, showing why model selection must consider corruption robustness and measured deployment cost together. The code is available at https://github.com/Tulane-Computer-Architecture-Systems/CrowdCC-Release.

**comment/C1：** Accepted in WACV 2027

</details>

### 282. [WAKMan: Workload Aware KVCache Manager](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36355v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36355

**作者：** Suhas Kumar Morisetty、Vaibhavi Lokegaonkar、Sachin Mathew Varghese、Wesley Harris

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36355v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36355v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 WAKMan，一个根据在线流量特征自动选择 KV cache 淘汰策略的前缀缓存管理器，在混合负载下相对 LRU 提升约 5 个百分点的命中率。

**排除原因：** 工作聚焦 LLM 服务端的 KV 前缀缓存淘汰策略与命中率，未涉及视觉信息表示、推理状态生成、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线及 latent 相似度中的缓存作用无实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** WAKMan: Workload Aware KVCache Manager

**abstract/A1：** Prefix caching enables a Large Language Model (LLM) servers to reuse the Key-Value (KV) states and accelerate prefill computation stage for requests that share a prefix with earlier prompts, thus reducing latency and serving cost. With limited memory resource, the KV cache eviction policy becomes a decisive factor in determining the overall benefits of prefix caching. Production traffic may have single-turn, multi-turn, agentic, or a mixture of any combination of these request types. Our findings indicate that agentic steps return within seconds and favor recency, while popular prompts and long conversations return after many other requests and favor frequency. Across 20 mixes, no single policy is always best: vLLM's default strategy LRU provides 12% higher cache hits as compared to the frequency-oriented S3FIFO on agentic-heavy mixes but trails it by up to 22% elsewhere, and even a single workload parameter can flip the winner even for any particular mix. We present WAKMan, a workload-aware KV cache manager that picks its eviction policy from live traffic without knowing request types or reading prompts. It improves hit rate over LRU by 5 percentage points on average, with the lowest mean and worst-case optimality gap of all policies.

**comment/C1：** Work done during internship at Capital One

</details>

### 283. [ORBITAL: Optimization of Robust Brain--Computer Inference Through Architecture-Aware Low-Precision Compression](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36411v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36411

**作者：** Saim Rehman、Muhammad Shafique

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.CR、cs.LG、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36411v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36411v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 ORBITAL 框架，在 EEG 脑机接口解码器上联合结构化低精度压缩与对抗鲁棒性恢复，并在 Jetson 边缘设备上验证能耗与时延。

**排除原因：** 研究重点是 EEG 解码器的压缩、对抗鲁棒性与硬件部署成本，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的 latent 状态、证据使用或停止决策，也未提供可迁移的内部表示线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ORBITAL: Optimization of Robust Brain--Computer Inference Through Architecture-Aware Low-Precision Compression

**abstract/A1：** Compact electroencephalography (EEG) decoders enable embedded brain--computer interfaces (BCIs), but compression can alter adversarial robustness without proportional hardware savings. We present ORBITAL, a robustness-constrained framework combining hardware-realizable structured compression, recovery-aware operating-point selection, multi-attack evaluation, and native edge validation. Across nine subjects and three seeds on BCI Competition IV-2a, S25-TRADES achieves the highest observed mean true-union robustness among compressed EEGNet models (23.06% vs 21.27% for standard Full), with 26.27% fewer parameters and 25.10% fewer MACs. Matched controls identify TRADES as the main source of robustness recovery, improving S25 union accuracy by 6.74 percentage points over clean recovery (p=0.0039), while per-example analysis reveals boundary redistribution rather than uniform margin expansion. On Jetson Orin Nano, FP16 compression reduces board energy by up to 16.20% and latency by 8.00%. Despite 99.3% prediction agreement, native INT8 Q/DQ incurs about twice the FP16 latency and energy, demonstrating the need for joint robustness and hardware-aware optimization.

**comment/C1：** Submitted to IEEE ISCAS 2027 for Review

</details>

**排除原因：** 论文属于数值实现与库级测试，虽提及注意力掩码，但不涉及 latent 状态表示、推理生成或证据使用，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Property-Based Testing for Robust Softmax and Cross-Entropy

**abstract/A1：** A recent paper of Barron et. al. (2026) identified two numerical issues with the softmax and cross-entropy functions in virtually all standard machine learning libraries (e.g., PyTorch and JAX). We show that these issues can be recovered through simple property-based tests in Hypothesis. However, our tests identify an additional edge case where their implementation fails. We then propose an even more robust softmax implementation which passes these tests and also supports use in causal attention masking. We compare our implementation with theirs and discuss some of the differences. Overall, this work shows that property-based testing can be a powerful tool for discovering and fixing numerical issues in machine learning libraries. Our implementations and property-based tests can be found at https://github.com/mmaaz-git/softmax-fix.

### 285. [LM-DSB: A Lightweight Multi-agent System for Automated Daily Safety Bulletin Generation in Construction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36433v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36433

**作者：** Xianxiang Zhao、Advik Mehta、Falak Sethi、Brian Gue、Qipei Mei、Lingzi Wu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36433v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36433v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 LM-DSB，一个由 planner、researcher、writer 智能体组成的轻量多智能体系统，结合 RAG 与本地可部署 LLM，自动生成建筑工地每日安全简报，并用自动指标和专家评分验证其可行性与可接受度。

**排除原因：** 这是面向建筑施工安全文档生成的领域应用，虽涉及 RAG 证据检索与多智能体分工，但既不研究视觉或多模态内部表示，也未涉及 latent 推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，缺少与 LVR 主线的实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LM-DSB: A Lightweight Multi-agent System for Automated Daily Safety Bulletin Generation in Construction

**abstract/A1：** Construction safety management still struggles to produce daily safety bulletins (DSBs), structured daily communications that identify site-specific hazards, convey safety protocols, and set preventive measures for workers. Manual DSB creation takes 2-3 hours daily and yields inconsistent quality, with 25% of crews never conducting pretask planning (Olson et al., 2016), diverting safety personnel from field supervision. This study proposes the Lightweight Multi-Agent DSB System (LM-DSB), which integrates retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with a locally deployable Large Language Model (LLM) to automate DSB generation on consumer-grade personal computers. LM-DSB coordinates specialized planner, researcher, and writer agents in a hierarchical architecture, enhanced by domain-specific fine-tuning on construction safety documentation and RAG grounding in 37 health, safety, and environment (HSE) documents. A two-phase validation assessed feasibility. Phase 1 comparative evaluation showed the multi-agent RAG system (Llama 3-8B) outperformed all alternative configurations, with strong lexical alignment and coherence (TF-IDF: 0.70, BLEU: 0.39, ROUGE-1: 0.66) and near-complete document generation (length ratio: 1.01 vs. 0.39 for single-agent RAG). Phase 2 expert evaluation (N=40) confirmed practical acceptability (mean scores 3.50-4.20 on a 5-point scale), particularly in regulatory compliance (4.20) and factual accuracy (4.10). The findings show that architectural design enables lightweight models to handle safety-critical documentation on consumer-grade hardware, advancing technology democratization in construction safety management. Limitations include knowledge base scope, language constraints, and lack of field deployment validation; future work should address multimodal integration and workflow adoption.

</details>

### 286. [Your Agent Acts Differently When You're Around: How User Presence Changes Agent Behavior in Negotiation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36435v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36435

**作者：** Jane Pan、You Lyu、Abe Bohan Hou、Valerie Chen、He He

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36435v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36435v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 PRESENT 控制实验框架，在固定博弈与用户偏好的条件下比较有无交互用户参与时 LLM 谈判智能体的行为与收益差异，并报告用户在场带来的更激进谈判、更高收益以及向对手和用户两侧出现的错位行为。

**排除原因：** 该工作聚焦多智能体谈判中的用户在场效应与对齐评测，不涉及视觉信息保留、latent 推理状态生成或证据筛选，也无可核查的状态更新、监督或停止决策线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Your Agent Acts Differently When You're Around: How User Presence Changes Agent Behavior in Negotiation

**abstract/A1：** LLM agents increasingly interact with other agents on behalf of users, yet how users in the loop affect their agents' behavior--for better or worse--remains poorly understood. We study this question through the lens of negotiation, where agents must represent users while strategically bargaining with other agents. To isolate the effect of user presence, we develop PRESENT, a controlled evaluation framework that compares negotiation settings with and without an interactive user, while holding the game, objectives, and user preferences fixed across conditions. Across 11.2k runs spanning 7 games and 8 models, we find that user presence significantly changes both outcomes and behavior. Specifically, negotiation parties with a user obtain higher payouts (+13.7% on average) and their agents negotiate more aggressively. Surprisingly, user presence also induces misaligned behavior, both toward counterparties (where its rate roughly doubles) and toward users (where it emerges in 14.3% of games). These misaligned behaviors are uncorrelated with outcomes and persist even when the user sends only templated, neutral messages. These findings expose two blind spots in current evaluation practice: static instructions miss behaviors that emerge only when users are present, and outcome-level metrics cannot detect them.

</details>

### 287. [Learning atomic interactions for the thermodynamics of liquid mixtures](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36440v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36440

**作者：** Marco Hoffmann、Sebastian Schmitt、Hans Hasse、Fabian Jirasek

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36440v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36440v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 COGNAC，一个学习原子级几何与邻域相关相互作用参数的图神经网络，并嵌入 UNIFAC 框架以预测液体混合物的活度系数，用约 82 万个实验数据点训练。

**排除原因：** 属于化工热力学与过程模拟的分子性质预测工作，虽使用图神经网络，但不涉及多模态推理、内部状态生成或证据使用，缺少可供 LVR 借鉴的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Learning atomic interactions for the thermodynamics of liquid mixtures

**abstract/A1：** Predictive models for activity coefficients, which describe the non-ideality of liquid mixtures, are foundational for process design and optimization. Group-contribution methods, such as UNIFAC, are computationally cheap and well established in process simulators. Modern machine learning models, such as HANNA, offer higher accuracy and broader applicability, but their integration into process simulation is expensive or even infeasible. In this work, we introduce COGNAC, a graph neural network that learns atom-level geometry and interaction parameters that depend on the molecular neighborhood and evaluates them in the UNIFAC framework. Trained on about 820,000 experimental data points, COGNAC achieves significantly higher accuracy than UNIFAC and HANNA for binary and multi-component mixtures, reduces error relative to HANNA by 13-63%, reliably predicts multi-phase equilibria and identifies azeotropes, yields interpretable parameters, and is strictly thermodynamically consistent. COGNAC evaluations run at a cost comparable to UNIFAC, thereby unlocking the potential of machine-learning models for process simulations.

**comment/C1：** 30 pages, 13 figures, 1 table

</details>

### 288. [High-Fidelity Aerostructural Dataset of Transonic Wings in Cruise Conditions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36442v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36442

**作者：** Pavankumar Koratikere、Abhijnan Dikshit、Leifur Leifsson

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CE、physics.flu-dyn

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36442v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36442v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文构建了跨声速机翼巡航条件下高保真气动-结构耦合数据集（2471 个样本及 3 个分布外样本），并用机器学习模型预测气动表面量、整体性能与翼盒失效分布，形成多物理场基准。

**排除原因：** 属于工程多物理场代理建模数据集，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部表示、证据获取或停止决策，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的状态更新或监督线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** High-Fidelity Aerostructural Dataset of Transonic Wings in Cruise Conditions

**abstract/A1：** Recent advances in machine learning (ML) have the potential to substantially accelerate aircraft wing design, a process that has traditionally required extensive high-fidelity simulation and design iteration. Existing public datasets for ML-based wing design have focused primarily on aerodynamic performance, with limited consideration of the multidisciplinary interactions that govern practical aircraft design. In particular, efficient wing design requires simultaneous consideration of aerodynamic performance and structural integrity, yet no publicly available datasets capture this coupled aerostructural behavior at high-fidelity. To address this need, a high-fidelity multidisciplinary dataset for static aerostructural analysis of transonic wings is introduced. The dataset includes 2471 samples, along with three out-of-distribution samples. The wing planform geometry is parameterized by span, sweep, dihedral, tip chord, and tip twist, while the airfoil cross-sections are parameterized using the class-shape transformation method. The analysis is further parameterized by Mach number, angle of attack, altitude, and structural load factor. The utility of the dataset is demonstrated by training state-of-the-art ML models to predict aerodynamic surface quantities, integrated aerodynamic performance quantities, and the failure distribution of the wingbox. ML models achieve at most 0.0309 normalized root mean squared error (NRMSE) in the prediction of aerodynamic quantities and at most 0.1189 NRMSE in the prediction of failure distribution of the wingbox. By providing coupled aerodynamic and structural responses for a diverse set of transonic wings, this dataset establishes a benchmark for developing, evaluating, and comparing multi-physics ML models for wing design. Broadly, it is intended to encourage creation of similar high-fidelity multi-physics benchmark datasets across engineering domains.

**comment/C1：** The dataset is available at: \\url{https://huggingface.co/datasets/computationaldesignlab/transonic-wing-aerostruct-dataset}

</details>
