### 289. [LociArt: A Unified Paradigm for Free-Style Local Video Stylization](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36456v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36456

**作者：** Yuxuan Chen、Feng Xie、Jiagao Hu、Fuhao Li、Zepeng Wang、Fei Wang、Daiguo Zhou

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.MM

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36456v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36456v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究局部视频风格化的区域—风格绑定与配对监督，属图像/视频生成编辑；其等范数风格方向与掩码几何条件仅是风格表示，未涉及视觉推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LociArt: A Unified Paradigm for Free-Style Local Video Stylization

**abstract/A1：** Video stylization typically transforms entire scenes, while local artistic editing requires precise region--style association, spatial control, and temporal consistency. We introduce LociArt, a unified paradigm for free-style local video stylization that explicitly specifies where to stylize and what style to apply while preserving non-target content. At its core, GRNStyle builds on Generative Refinement Networks (GRN) and introduces a polar style-aware mask that embeds style identities as equal-norm directions within selected regions. Complementary representation--geometry conditioning combines encoded mask features with raw-mask spatial support, integrating spatiotemporal context with geometric control. To provide paired supervision, we develop an automated pipeline combining global stylization, video object removal, matting, and compositing, and construct the LociArt-100K Dataset with 100K local stylization pairs. We further establish the LociArt Benchmark, comprising 80 source videos and 84 target masks covering 168 foreground/background stylization cases, with a Style Prototype Similarity (SPS) evaluation scheme for regional style quality and complementary measures of non-target preservation and temporal consistency. Experiments show that GRNStyle outperforms existing methods in regional style quality and spatial control while maintaining strong temporal consistency. Qualitative examples further illustrate joint stylization of multiple regions with the same or different learned styles, despite training only on single-region, single-style pairs. Together, these components provide a unified foundation for learning and evaluating controllable local video stylization. Project page: https://higherhu.github.io/lociart/.

**comment/C1：** Project page: https://higherhu.github.io/lociart/

</details>

### 290. [Split Federated Unlearning](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36465v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36465

**作者：** Jia Chen、Yecheng Lv、Xinrong Hu、Mingdi Huang、Jun Bai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36465v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36465v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文首次系统形式化「拆分联邦遗忘」（SFU），刻画跨分区耦合与历史影响传播，并提出 SURE 方法（双重重要性估计、选择性参数扰动、重要性引导恢复）以实现客户端级与类别级贡献移除。

**排除原因：** 该工作关注分布式拆分训练中的贡献移除与隐私合规，不涉及视觉/多模态推理状态、证据获取或停止决策；其重要性估计仅用于参数级遗忘，与 LVR 的表示保留或贡献分配问题缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Split Federated Unlearning

**abstract/A1：** Split federated learning (SFL) combines partitioned model execution with federated aggregation to support collaborative learning across distributed Web and edge environments. However, removing learned contributions in SFL is challenging due to cross-partition coupling and historical influence propagation through dual-side aggregation. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first systematic formulation of Split Federated Unlearning (SFU), encompassing client-wise and class-wise removal, counterfactual retraining, and partition-specific update constraints. We theoretically characterize historical influence propagation and mode-dependent unlearning feasibility, revealing structural limitations of restricted model updates. As an initial solution, we propose SURE, integrating dual-importance estimation, selective parameter perturbation, and importance-guided recovery. Extensive experiments with existing unlearning methods adapted to SFL reveal substantial forgetting-utility trade-offs across model architectures, data distributions, and update modes. SURE achieves competitive agreement with mode-matched retraining at modest additional cost, while further analyses highlight the impact of split position and update constraints on unlearning behavior. These findings establish SFU as a distinct research problem and provide a foundation for unlearning in partitioned federated systems.

**comment/C1：** 13 pages

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36513

**作者：** Yibo Yang、Zerui Huang、Haomiao Yang、Zixia Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36513v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36513v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 MedConstBench，将模型部署时的伦理「constitution」作为可控变量，围绕 Beauchamp 四原则构建医疗伦理困境数据集，并用人工校准的多模型互评打分，比较十种开源 LLM 在四种 constitution 下的表现。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MedConstBench: Benchmarking How Constitutions Steer LLMs in Medical Ethics

**abstract/A1：** As large language models advance rapidly, they are increasingly used to answer medical questions, and many such models are now deployed locally. Once a model is deployed, its behavior is governed mainly by the ethical policy written into its constitution. However, most existing work focuses on factual correctness rather than the trade-off between competing ethical values. Even benchmarks that do evaluate ethics treat it as a fixed property of the model rather than as a variable, so how a constitution reshapes medical-ethics behavior remains largely unexplored. We introduce MedConstBench, a medical-ethics benchmark that treats the deployment constitution as a controlled variable, is organized around Beauchamp's four principles, and is scored by human-calibrated multi-model peer review. It includes MedDilemma, our own dataset of clinical dilemmas, and supports a systematic analysis of how constitutions steer LLMs. Evaluating ten open-source LLMs under four constitutions, we find that the effect of a constitution is dimension-selective and model-dependent, and that no constitution is universally optimal. Motivated by these findings, we propose a do-no-harm adaptive selector that chooses a constitution per model, matching the per-model oracle while guaranteeing a worst-case floor and generalizing to unseen models at low cost.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 3 figures, 8 tables

</details>

### 292. [PRISM: Pathology Reliability In Scarce-label Medicine. A Tile-level Benchmark of Pathology Foundation Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36517v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36517

**作者：** Alicem Koyun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36517v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36517v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 PRISM，在切片级数据上从模型选择、标签比例、校准与跨机构迁移四方面评测八个病理基础模型的可靠性。

**排除原因：** 属于医学影像基础模型的分类与校准基准，不涉及 latent 推理状态、视觉证据筛选或推理期计算决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** PRISM: Pathology Reliability In Scarce-label Medicine. A Tile-level Benchmark of Pathology Foundation Models

**abstract/A1：** Pathology foundation models are typically benchmarked on classification performance at full label availability and on in-distribution test sets, while calibration, behavior at low label budgets, and robustness under cross-institutional shift are seldom evaluated jointly. We introduce PRISM, a tile-level benchmark that measures the reliability of pathology foundation models along four axes: model choice, label fraction, calibration, and out-of-distribution transfer. PRISM evaluates eight foundation models (CLIP, PLIP, CONCH, UNI, Virchow2, GigaPath, H-Optimus-0, Midnight) on six tile-level datasets at six label fractions (1% to 100%) with three seeds (864 linear probe runs), extended with a five-hospital Camelyon17 study of 20 directed transfer pairs. Four phenomena emerge. (i) Under full supervision, AUROC rank predicts post-temperature calibration rank consistently across datasets (Spearman rho = 0.52, I^2 = 0%), whereas at 1% labels the relationship becomes dataset-specific (rho = -0.24, I^2 = 67%). (ii) On harder datasets the linear probe collapses to a single predicted class below a dataset-specific label fraction. (iii) Whether additional source-domain labels help or harm target calibration tracks how well the probe transfers: below a transfer AUROC of roughly 0.85, more labels worsen target ECE in 86-88% of combinations. (iv) Temperature scaling fails to reduce ECE in 49 of 288 cells, miscalibration is predominantly under-confidence, and a source-fitted temperature is harmful under failing transfer. PRISM is released as a pip-installable package with reference results for all 288 cells. Code and data: https://github.com/alicemkyn/prism-benchmark

**comment/C1：** 30 pages (9 main), 21 figures. Accepted at the Third Workshop on GenAI for Health (GenAI4Health) at NeurIPS 2026

</details>

### 293. [Frugal Handwritten Text Recognition for Sub-Saharan Civil-Status Registers: A Smartphone-to-Records Pipeline with CPU-Only Inference](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36540v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36540

**作者：** Ousmane Hamadou、Jean Louis Kedieng Ebongue Fendji

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文构建乍得民事登记手写记录的手机拍摄语料，并搭建一套纯 CPU 推理的廉价流水线（经典预处理加微调的 Kraken 识别器），评估识别错误率、资源开销及其对下游信息抽取的影响。

**排除原因：** 属于低资源场景的手写 OCR 应用与可行性评估，既无内部视觉推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策问题，也未提供可核查的表示或训练线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Frugal Handwritten Text Recognition for Sub-Saharan Civil-Status Registers: A Smartphone-to-Records Pipeline with CPU-Only Inference

**abstract/A1：** Handwritten civil-status registers across Sub-Saharan Africa are being photographed, but rarely transcribed, which leaves their content unsearchable. Local administrations that hold these records typically lack GPUs, reliable connectivity, and the budget for external digitization services. We study whether a frugal pipeline, with consumer-grade capture and CPU-only inference, can make such registers machine-readable. We introduce a corpus of 1,568 smartphone images of handwritten birth, marriage, and death records from a municipal registry in Chad, with line-level transcriptions for 185 records. Our pipeline combines a fully classical preprocessing stage, running at about 2.6 images per second on a laptop CPU, with a Kraken recognizer fine-tuned from a pretrained model in about four hours on a free-tier GPU. On manually corrected line segmentation and a single-registry test set whose writers also appear in training, fine-tuning reduces the character error rate from 14.53% to 0.50% and the word error rate from 56.93% to 3.37%. On a six-core laptop CPU, the recognizer weighs 61 MiB, peaks below 2.6 GiB of RAM, and processes an image in under 6 s on average, excluding line segmentation. Finally, a downstream information-extraction task shows that residual recognition errors cost about 9 F1 points on role resolution, compared with a 36.7-point gap between the fine-tuned and pretrained recognizers. These results are evidence of feasibility within one registry, not of performance on unseen writers or sites; we discuss these limitations in detail.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at the GlobalSouthCV Workshop, BMVC 2026. 17 pages, 3 figures

</details>

### 294. [Holomorphic Cauchy Networks for Efficient Stationary Tail Computation in High-Dimensional Reflected Brownian Motions](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36558v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36558

**作者：** Kehan Wang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.NA、math.PR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36558v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36558v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 Holomorphic Cauchy Networks，用复值有理 Cauchy 子网络强制全纯性，以加速高维反射布朗运动平稳尾分布的计算，并给出导数逼近与显存/耗时优势。

**排除原因：** 属数值概率与科学计算代理模型工作，虽涉及复数网络与结构约束，但既无视觉/多模态推理状态，也不涉及证据获取、停止决策或 latent 内容使用，缺少可核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Holomorphic Cauchy Networks for Efficient Stationary Tail Computation in High-Dimensional Reflected Brownian Motions

**abstract/A1：** Stationary tail computation for high-dimensional reflected Brownian motions (RBMs) remains computationally challenging. Building on NN4MGF's shared-coordinate basic-adjoint-relationship (BAR) framework, we develop Holomorphic Cauchy Networks (HCNs). Native complex-valued rational Cauchy subnetworks enforce holomorphicity on pole-free domains, removing sampled Cauchy-Riemann penalties and their input-derivative graphs. Exact origin anchoring and boundary-coordinate exclusion enforce structural identities, while anchored interactions represent dependent transforms. Under holomorphic-log assumptions, we establish simultaneous value and first-derivative approximation for the constrained architectures and a mixed-derivative lower bound for coordinate-only representations under nonzero cross-covariance. Closed-form real-axis derivatives and sequential backpropagation further control memory. On the same NVIDIA H100 NVL, the tested 30D HCN configuration uses approximately tenfold less peak allocated memory and has 9.7-fold lower mean update time than NN4MGF; extrapolation to the respective scheduled update counts gives a projected full-schedule update-time ratio of 58.2. With 64 million sampled base inputs per high-dimensional run, HCN reaches 50 dimensions on a laptop GPU with 6.367% mean relative error over thresholds 1 through 8. Degree-matched comparisons yield lower reported mean relative errors over the primary evaluation ranges, including 1.422% versus 6.684% on the 2D Harrison non-product benchmark. An exploratory 10D correlated example illustrates the interaction extension.

**comment/C1：** 29 pages, 3 figures, 14 tables. Code available at https://github.com/KehanWang126/holomorphic-cauchy-networks-rbm

</details>

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出多模态多任务网络 MM-Net，融合 EEG-EOG 表征与心肺特征，同时进行卒中后睡眠分期与呼吸事件检测，并用交叉拟合与岭回归对齐预训练编码器的嵌入。

**排除原因：** 属于临床多导睡眠图的特定任务建模，其嵌入对齐仅用于缓解患者间表示不一致，未涉及 latent 视觉推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可迁移的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MM-Net: A Multimodal Multi-Task Network for Sleep Staging and Sleep Apnea Assessment After Ischemic Stroke

**abstract/A1：** Automated polysomnography after ischemic stroke is challenging because of altered sleep-related neural activity and concurrent respiratory disturbances. Existing stroke-specific approaches mainly address sleep-stage classification, leaving joint analysis of sleep architecture and respiratory events underexplored. Moreover, adapting pretrained EEG encoders introduces a representation inconsistency when downstream models are trained on embeddings from previously seen patients but evaluated on unseen individuals. We propose MM-Net, a multimodal multi-task framework that resolves this inconsistency through cross-fitting and ridge-based embedding alignment. Sleep-adapted EEG-EOG representations and engineered neural descriptors are fused with learned cardiorespiratory features through cross-attention and shared temporal modeling for simultaneous sleep staging and respiratory-event detection. On iSLEEPS (99 patients with subacute ischemic stroke), ten-fold patient-independent evaluation achieved 76.3% staging accuracy (Cohen's $κ$ = 0.656) and 0.825 respiratory-event AUC, outperforming the evaluated baselines under matched protocols. The sleep-adapted encoder improved the downstream model when its embeddings were aligned, raising staging accuracy by 1.4 percentage points over the frozen encoder ($p$ = 0.020) versus 0.2 points without alignment. MM-Net also achieved 86.4% staging accuracy on Sleep-EDF, above every single-EEG architecture reimplemented on the same recordings, and 80.3% staging accuracy with 0.889 respiratory AUC on ISRUC-I, exceeding its retrained staging baselines. Without retraining, the iSLEEPS-trained model retained respiratory AUCs of 0.833 on ISRUC-I and 0.841 on UCD. These findings support representation-consistent neural adaptation and joint physiological modeling across stroke, healthy-sleep, and sleep-disorder cohorts.

</details>

### 296. [Efficient Quantization Training with Stochastic Rounding via Structured Subspace Randomness](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36587v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36587

**作者：** Zeyu Wang、Yiheng Duan、Tao Chen、Donghyun Koo、Sihan Liu、Wenbo Zhang、Chuan He、Liang Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36587v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36587v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究低精度训练中的随机舍入效率问题，提出按 token 共享阈值的结构化子空间随机舍入 S3R 及其误差修正 S3R-EC，以降低随机数生成开销并保持收敛。

**排除原因：** 内容属于量化训练与硬件吞吐优化，虽涉及舍入误差的跨层传播与修正，但未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，缺乏可供 LVR 核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Efficient Quantization Training with Stochastic Rounding via Structured Subspace Randomness

**abstract/A1：** Low-precision training often relies on stochastic rounding (SR) to keep quantized gradients unbiased and prevent small updates from rounding to zero. Standard SR draws an independent random threshold for every gradient element. However, accelerators generate random numbers on vector units whose throughput lags far behind their matrix units. Drawing one threshold per element saturates the vector unit and can make INT8 training with SR slower than BF16 training. Sharing random thresholds across tensor elements removes this cost but forces those elements to round in the same direction and shifts their rounding errors by a common offset. We find that this offset degrades training primarily through the activation gradient, which propagates the error backward into earlier layers. Because earlier layers cannot cancel an incoming offset using their own local rounding errors, the offset must be removed at the layer where it originates. We propose Structured Subspace Stochastic Rounding (S3R), which quantizes each upstream gradient once for both the activation and weight gradient matrix multiplications and shares a single threshold per token across the hidden dimension. Its error correction, S3R-EC, subtracts the structured mean rounding error from the activation and weight gradients while leaving the optimizer unchanged. In LLM pre-training experiments, uncorrected threshold sharing degrades convergence, whereas S3R-EC eliminates this degradation and matches the validation loss of independent SR. Furthermore, S3R-EC accelerates full INT8 training steps of dense LLMs on TPU v5p by 1.24x to 1.32x over BF16 and by 1.11x over independent SR.

</details>

<a id="paper-297"></a>

### 297. [InfraDev: Can Coding Agents Develop AI Infrastructure, and How Can We Tell?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36589v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36589

**作者：** Yangshen Deng、Jiaqi Ruan、Yeqi Huang、Nick Lau、Guangming Sheng、Luo Mai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.DC

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36589v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36589v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** InfraDev 是一个面向 AI 基础设施开发的 coding agent 基准，基于 Megatron-LM、vLLM、verl 的真实 PR 构建 36 个多 GPU 多文件改写任务，并用隐藏探针与接口说明进行联合评分，另含共享执行环境与失败分析。

**排除原因：** 该工作是代码智能体的软件工程评测，既不涉及视觉或多模态推理，也未涉及 latent 推理状态的表示、生成或训练监督，缺乏可供核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** InfraDev: Can Coding Agents Develop AI Infrastructure, and How Can We Tell?

**abstract/A1：** Coding agents are beginning to develop the production-grade AI infrastructure that trains and serves large models, yet existing benchmarks cover only single kernels or single-GPU tasks, and grade with tests and prompts that misjudge both correct and incorrect patches. We introduce InfraDev, a benchmark of 36 tasks built from real-world pull requests to Megatron-LM, vLLM, and verl, covering infrastructure development for training, inference, and reinforcement learning. Each task asks an agent to reimplement a real change that spans many files and must run correctly across many GPUs. To grade such changes, we jointly synthesize hidden probes, which enforce the requirements the original tests leave unchecked, and a prompt that states every interface the probes call without revealing the solution. We also design a shared execution environment that reuses dependencies and artifacts across tasks, cutting storage by 52-57% and build time by 13-14% on Megatron-LM and vLLM. Across six frontier models, the best resolves only 38.9% of the tasks; the probes reject 32% of the submissions that pass every official test, and with the original pull request descriptions as prompts, no model resolves a single task. Failure analysis shows that structural and state errors dominate and that agents' own tests rarely check memory, time, or traffic. Turning these findings into an agent skill raises the resolved rate of a frontier model on the Megatron-LM tasks by 10.5%.

</details>

### 298. [4D Reconstruction with Events &amp; RGB](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36610v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36610

**作者：** Dulanga Weerakoon、Darshana Rathnayake、Van Minh Do、Hemanth Sabbella、Kaichen Zhou、Fangneng Zhan、Alok Prakash、Sanjay E. Sarma、Archan Misra、Paul Pu Liang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36610v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36610v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究单目动态场景的 4D 几何重建，仅通过增加事件相机输入分支增强 Monst3R 的鲁棒性，既不涉及推理期 latent 状态生成、证据筛选，也不涉及答案使用、停止决策或贡献分配，与 LVR/VLM 推理主线缺少具体可借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** 4D Reconstruction with Events &amp; RGB

**abstract/A1：** Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes over time (4D reconstruction) from a single moving camera is central to robotics and AR/VR applications, yet state-of the-art feed-forward methods rely solely on RGB frames and therefore inherit the failure modes of conventional cameras, including motion blur and temporal aliasing under fast or non-uniform camera and object motion. Event cameras report per-pixel brightness changes asynchronously at microsecond resolution, offering a complementary signal that is largely immune to these artifacts, but whether this modality can be incorporated into existing RGB based 4D reconstruction pipelines without a full architectural redesign remains an open question. In this preliminary study, we investigate a minimal extension: we augment Monst3R, an existing RGB-only 4D reconstruction model, with an additional event-camera input branch, leaving the rest of the architecture unchanged. Across 3 benchmark datasets, this simple RGB-event fusion reduces Abs Rel by 3.4% on average relative to the RGB-only Monst3R baseline, and a sensitivity analysis shows that the improvement grows with the magnitude of camera and object motion, with the largest gains observed under high object motion conditions where RGB-only reconstruction degrades most. These early results suggest that even a lightweight, non-invasive fusion of event data can meaningfully improve 4D reconstruction robustness, motivating a more thorough architectural investigation in future work.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeVi 2026 workshop at the 19th European Conference on Computer Vision (ECCV 2026)

</details>

### 299. [Normalized Cuts and Data Clustering via Matrix Fractional Programming](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36616v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36616

**作者：** Yannan Chen、Beichen Huang、Licheng Zhao、Chris Ding、Kaiming Shen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、eess.SP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36616v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36616v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文提出多维二次变换以处理多比值型 NCut 优化并用于聚类与图像分割，属经典分割/聚类优化问题，未涉及视觉推理中的 latent 状态、证据获取或停止决策，也无可直接核查的状态或训练线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Normalized Cuts and Data Clustering via Matrix Fractional Programming

**abstract/A1：** The normalized cut (NCut) problem is a fundamental one in the area of data clustering. Despite its fractional structure, the NCut problem is seldom considered from a fractional programming (FP) perspective in the existing literature. The main reason is that the conventional FP method is inadequate for this problem case: the classical Dinkelbach's transform can only handle a single ratio and hence is limited to the two-class clustering, whereas the multi-class clustering involves multiple ratio terms. This work proposes a novel so-called multidimensional quadratic transform that is capable of dealing with the multi-ratio case; it recasts the NCut problem into a bipartite matching problem---which can be immediately solved by linear search. Furthermore, we explore the connection between the proposed FP method and the minorization-maximization (MM) theory, and in turn characterize the convergence behavior of our algorithm. Finally, we conduct experiments to numerically demonstrate the advantages of the proposed FP-based algorithm in a variety of data clustering and image segmentation tasks.

**comment/C1：** 15 pages, 15 figures

</details>

### 300. [God Does Not Play Dice: Enforcing Batch Invariance in Agentic Kernel Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36623v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36623

**作者：** Youwei Bao、Chengpeng Wang、Simin Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.LG、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36623v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36623v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文关注 LLM 服务中 GPU kernel 的批不变性（batch invariance）：统计了 83 个已发布产物中 19 例批不变性违反，并提出一个可插入 kernel 生成 agent 优化循环的组件，用静态依赖分析加跨 batch 上下文的位级一致性动态验证来作为接受门。

**排除原因：** 内容属 GPU kernel 生成的数值可复现性与性能优化，不涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的状态或监督线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** God Does Not Play Dice: Enforcing Batch Invariance in Agentic Kernel Generation

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) serving systems dynamically batch user requests for GPU execution, so the same request may be processed alongside different requests across runs. If the GPU kernels lack batch invariance (BI), this changing batch context can alter the numerical output for an unchanged input, undermining reproducibility. Existing kernel generation agents focus on correctness and efficiency but \\emph{overlook BI}. We find 19 confirmed BI violations among 83 batch-applicable released artifacts. To address this limitation, we propose \\tool, which combines contract-guided static dependency analysis with dynamic validation of bitwise consistency across batch contexts. \\tool is designed as a plug-in component for general kernel agents, allowing their original optimization capabilities to be reused while adding BI enforcement. We integrate it as successive acceptance gates in the optimization loop, rejecting structurally risky or dynamically non-invariant candidates before performance benchmarking and returning feedback to the agent. Experiments across three agent frameworks under baseline and BI-aware generation on real world LLM serving tasks show that \\tool improves the BI pass rate from 67.6\\% to 91.9\\% while retaining 91.8\\% of the baseline geometric mean speedup. These results demonstrate that \\tool can be incorporated into kernel optimization by agents as an additional reliability objective alongside correctness and speed.

</details>

<a id="paper-301"></a>

### 301. [The uneven rise of AI-generated web content](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36631v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36631

**作者：** Adam Visokay、Soumyakanti Pan、Molly Griston、Polina Iasakova、Mark Nepf、Denis Peskoff、Riya Sinha、Jikai Sun、Isabelle Thiara、Letao Wang、Yiwei Wu、Lauren Yan、Tyler H. McCormick

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.CY、cs.NI、stat.AP

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36631v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36631v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文用 500 多万网页样本估计 AI 生成文本在公共网络中的占比，并按语言、域名与网络中心性分析其分布差异。

**排除原因：** 属于网络内容生态的计量研究，虽使用 AI 检测器与贝叶斯层次模型，但对象是网页文本占比估计，与 VLM 内部表示、latent 推理状态及证据筛选决策无实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** The uneven rise of AI-generated web content

**abstract/A1：** In less than five years, generative artificial intelligence (AI) has dramatically reshaped the public-facing web. Using a sample of more than 5 million crawlable webpages, we demonstrate that the share containing AI-generated text has increased precipitously, but unevenly. Our analyses reveal substantial differences in AI composition across language and domain categories (e.g., .edu, .com, or .gov). A higher fraction of English-language webpages contain AI-generated text compared to pages in other languages, and prevalence is substantially lower on .gov and .edu domains compared to .com. These distinctions persist even after accounting for compositional effects. We also find heterogeneity based on a page's position in the connectivity structure of the web. AI-generated text is most common and has increased most rapidly in pages that are the least central in the graph, which drives much of the overall growth in AI-generated text. A slower increase has taken place for more central pages, with the most central tier emerging as the least likely to contain AI-generated text. Methodologically, we use multiple AI detection tools, and generate estimates using a Bayesian hierarchical model that incorporates corrections for the detectors' statistical properties (both false positives and false negatives) and allows for different detector performance across groups, while propagating uncertainty in detector error rates into prevalence estimates. Our work suggests that AI is a prominent, but varied, contributor to public information and internet-based communication.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 4 figures

</details>

### 302. [When Is a Cheap Fairness Edit Safe? Screening Post-Hoc Edits in Two-Sided Matching Markets](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36649v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36649

**作者：** Moirangthem Tiken Singha、Rabinder Kumar Prasada

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36649v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36649v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 论文研究双向匹配市场中推理期公平性编辑（线性投影去除受保护属性影响）的安全性筛查与福利界，涉及排序稳定性和公平约束，与视觉或多模态推理中的 latent 状态、证据使用和停止决策无实质联系，也无可核查的方法借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** When Is a Cheap Fairness Edit Safe? Screening Post-Hoc Edits in Two-Sided Matching Markets

**abstract/A1：** Two-sided matching systems, such as admissions pipelines, allocate opportunities jointly. Reducing a protected at tribute's influence usually requires encoder retraining. We study a closed-form linear projector that does this at inference time. For markets that stay vertically ordered before and after the edit, we bound the mean welfare change by the edit-induced appeal shift and an explicit remainder. A probabilistic refinement replaces worst-case constants with a data-dependent deviation term. A pre-deployment screen combines a verticality test with a welfare sweep and returns PASS, KILL, or OUT-OF-SCOPE. An exact tier requires every opportunity to rank applicants by appeal, and a graded tier admits near-vertical markets. On 30 held-out synthetic markets, the screen agrees with every pre-specified regime verdict, with no observed false PASS. On 60 exactly vertical markets, no bound is violated, and the probabilistic bound is informative in 69% to 87% of configurations. On 2700 configurations from three realistic markets, which do not reach the exact tier, no plug-in bound expression is violated. A closed-form factorization stays within graded scope in 90% to 100% of instances, whereas two-tower and graph neural network encoders fall outside it in every instance. Architecture choice thus appears to affect whether a post-hoc edit is admissible.

</details>

**title/T1：** Progressively Refining SAM for Accurate Phytoplankton Segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Accurate phytoplankton segmentation is essential for ecological monitoring, yet it remains largely underexplored due to the subtle visual characteristics of phytoplankton and the scarcity of pixel-level annotations. To address these challenges, we propose PhySeg, a SAM-based segmentation framework equipped with a lightweight refinement module that progressively restores fine-grained details through multi-scale feature fusion. In addition, we construct a phytoplankton segmentation dataset by manually annotating instance-level masks for an existing dataset, providing a valuable resource for future research. Experimental results demonstrate that PhySeg improves segmentation accuracy, validating the effectiveness of the proposed module. Code is available at https://github.com/lijialin404/PhySeg.

### 304. [Multi-task Disentangling Fusion Mamba-CNN for MODIS Spectral-Temporal Unmixing with Endmember Variability Modeling](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36750v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36750

**作者：** Motasem Alkayid、Yimin Zhu、Naser El-Sheimy、Lincoln Linlin Xu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36750v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36750v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文研究 MODIS 时序光谱解混，核心是端元变异性建模与专家表示融合，不涉及 VLM 推理或 latent 推理状态；其中的 Mamba、多专家融合线索过于通用，未构成可供核查的具体借鉴点。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multi-task Disentangling Fusion Mamba-CNN for MODIS Spectral-Temporal Unmixing with Endmember Variability Modeling

**abstract/A1：** Spectral-temporal unmixing of MODIS time series is essential for applications such as abundance mapping and spectral library construction, yet it remains challenging due to (i) spectral-temporal information coupling in MODIS data, (ii) intra-class endmember spectral variability and inter-class signature ambiguity, and (iii) the instability of naively fusing multiple expert representations. This paper presents the Multi- task Disentangling Fusion Mamba (MDF-Mamba) to address these limitations. To resolve spectral-temporal coupling, we design a disentangling architecture that pairs per-channel spectral experts with a dedicated bidirectional state-space (Mamba) temporal branch. Convolutional modules each process a single spectral channel, while the Mamba branch encodes the cross-channel seasonal trajectory, forming a multi-branch expert ensemble with soft inter-expert reweighting based on squeeze-and-excitation. For the integration of these spectral and temporal expert outputs, we introduce mHCFusion, adapting the manifold-constrained hyper-connection framework to merge the outputs in a stable and interpretable manner. Endmember variability is addressed by extending the linear mixing model with pixel-wise learnable spectral variability weights that capture intra-class endmember variation. Finally, we design a multi-task training objective that combines supervised abundance estimation with self-supervised spectral reconstruction and endmember regularization terms on the same training data, yielding a highly efficient framework for spectral-temporal unmixing. Experiments on MODIS time series covering the Alberta and Saskatchewan provinces show that MDF-Mamba achieves the best performance on 12 of 14 evaluation metrics across both datasets (7 of 7 on Alberta; 5 of 7 on Saskatchewan), ranking second on the remaining two.

</details>

### 305. [Efficient Remote Sensing Image-Text Retrieval via Interaction-Free Recall and Text-Guided Reranking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36756v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36756

**作者：** Xi Chen、Xu Chen、Xiamgyang Jia、Wei Wang、Xu Zhang、Zhenyuan Shun

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36756v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36756v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 fast-then-fine 的遥感图像-文本检索框架：先用无需跨模态交互的多粒度召回预计算图库嵌入，再对 top-K 候选做文本引导的局部语义重排，并联合优化跨模态对齐。

**排除原因：** 研究目标是遥感检索的精度—效率权衡，不涉及内部推理状态、latent 表示或答案对视觉内容的依赖；其两阶段召回-重排属检索流水线设计，与 LVR 的推理期证据获取与状态更新无实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Efficient Remote Sensing Image-Text Retrieval via Interaction-Free Recall and Text-Guided Reranking

**abstract/A1：** Remote sensing (RS) image-text retrieval plays a critical role in understanding massive RS imagery. However, the dense multi-object distribution and complex backgrounds in RS imagery make it difficult to simultaneously achieve fine-grained cross-modal alignment and efficient retrieval. Existing methods either rely on complex cross-modal interactions that lead to low retrieval efficiency, or depend on large-scale vision-language adaptation, which requires massive data and computational resources. To address these issues, we develop a fast-then-fine (FTF) retrieval framework centered on interaction-free multi-granular recall and parameter-free text-guided cross-modal reranking with the Balanced Text-guided Interaction Block (BTIB). Specifically, interaction-free multi-granular recall pre-computes gallery embeddings and matches them with the corresponding query embedding without full-gallery token-level text-image interaction, while text-guided cross-modal reranking with BTIB refines local semantic correspondence within the top-K candidate set. Furthermore, an inter- and intra-modal loss is designed to jointly optimize cross-modal alignment across multi-granular representations. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate that the FTF achieves competitive retrieval accuracy while significantly improving retrieval efficiency compared with existing methods.

</details>

### 306. [GeoGSplat: Geometry-Grounded Non-Pixel-Aligned Gaussian Splatting](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36760v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36760

**作者：** Jiale Wang、Chen Zhao、Haoyang Zhang、Yanhao Wu、Fei He、Yanhu Shan、Rui Wu、Wei Ke、Tong Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36760v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36760v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文是前馈 Gaussian Splatting 的 3D 重建与新视角合成工作，以双分支编码器和分层解码器输出显式几何图元用于渲染，不涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、视觉证据筛选或停止决策；其「固定数量、非像素对齐」表示与 LVR 中细节保留和状态坍缩问题的联系过于间接，缺少可供核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GeoGSplat: Geometry-Grounded Non-Pixel-Aligned Gaussian Splatting

**abstract/A1：** Feed-forward Gaussian Splatting enables efficient novel view synthesis, but pixel-aligned prediction makes the primitive count grow with image resolution and view count, leading to substantial redundancy and increased computational cost. Non-pixel-aligned methods address this limitation by predicting a fixed number of Gaussians. Without explicit ray and depth constraints, these methods yield misplaced Gaussian centers, causing rendering artifacts under large viewpoint changes. To address this issue, we introduce GeoGSplat, a geometry-grounded feed-forward model that explicitly incorporates geometric priors into the non-pixel-aligned framework. A dual-branch encoder constructs Anchors and Triplane to capture coarse 3D structure and fine-grained scene features, respectively. Subsequently, a hierarchical decoder progressively decodes dense 3D points and Gaussian primitives, enabling 3D reconstruction and novel view synthesis. GeoGSplat surpasses both state-of-the-art pixel-aligned and non-pixel-aligned Gaussian Splatting methods on DTU and NRGBD in terms of novel view synthesis and reconstruction. The advantage of our geometry-grounded paradigm further increases as target viewpoints move farther from the input cameras, demonstrating stronger generalization to large viewpoint changes. Project Page: https://comedy0913.github.io/projects/geogsplat

</details>

### 307. [Does LLM Pre-Training Typically Occur at the Edge of Stability?](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36778v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36778

**作者：** Yuhang Cai、Haodong Wen、Haofeng Huang、Deyi Liu、Yiyuan Ma、Kaifeng Lyu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36778v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36778v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文检验二次近似作为神经网络训练动力学模型的失效模式，提出持续负曲率导致的「Edge of Convexity」现象，并给出自适应优化器随机训练下 Edge of Stability 的广义判据，在最大 787M 参数的 LLM 预训练中观察其出现条件。

**排除原因：** 研究内容是 LLM 预训练的学习率、Hessian 曲率与优化稳定性，未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据获取或答案决策，也未见可供迁移到 LVR 训练的具体机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Does LLM Pre-Training Typically Occur at the Edge of Stability?

**abstract/A1：** Quadratic approximations provide a common lens for studying neural network optimization, but recent evidence challenges their predictive validity. In full-batch gradient descent with learning rate (LR) $η$, Cohen et al. (2021) observed the Edge of Stability (EoS), where the largest Hessian eigenvalue concentrates near $2/η$, seemingly at odds with classical stability conditions predicted by quadratic approximations. In this work, we revisit the fidelity of quadratic approximations as a model of neural network training dynamics, with a particular focus on their failure modes in LLM training. We first highlight a distinct failure mode of the quadratic approximation, where persistent negative curvature causes the quadratic dynamics to diverge regardless of the LR, even though the real dynamics remain stable. We term this phenomenon the Edge of Convexity (EoC). After removing the effect of this LR-independent instability, we then propose a generalized definition of Edge of Stability (EoS) for stochastic training with adaptive optimizers, based on whether increasing the LR induces significant divergence in the quadratic dynamics while leaving the real dynamics largely unchanged. Across different LLM pretraining runs with various model sizes up to 787M, we find that (1) EoC is always observed across LLM pretraining, and (2) EoS is also prevalent but not universal: it disappears when the LR becomes sufficiently small (e.g., after decay) or when the batch size falls below a critical threshold that has a positive correlation with the critical batch size. Together, these findings characterize when and how quadratic approximations fail and provide a foundation for future work on understanding training dynamics.

**comment/C1：** 90 pages, 17 figures

</details>

### 308. [CVBL-CCTV-Face Dataset for Real-World Face Enhancement and Recognition in Surveillance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36781v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36781

**作者：** Manish Das、Ajay Kumar Yadav、Shiv Ram Dubey、Satish Kumar Singh

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36781v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36781v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文构建监控场景下的真实人脸超分与身份识别框架，并发布由低质量监控抓拍与高质量参考图组成的非配对 CVBL-CCTV-Face 数据集。

**排除原因：** 属于图像复原与识别任务的工程流程和数据集工作，未涉及多模态推理中的内部状态、证据获取、停止决策或训练监督，也没有可供迁移的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CVBL-CCTV-Face Dataset for Real-World Face Enhancement and Recognition in Surveillance

**abstract/A1：** In real-world surveillance settings, facial captures from closed-circuit television systems suffer from degradations such as low resolution, motion blur, noise, and occlusions, which undermine dependable face recognition. In this study, we propose a framework for super-resolution and identity recognition, aimed at surveillance applications. For this purpose, we present the CVBL-CCTV-Face Dataset which is a dataset of real-world images taken in an unpaired manner where each pair consists of a low-quality (LQ) image of a face captured by a 8MP IP Dome CCTV camera together with a high-quality (HQ) reference image that is taken in controlled settings. In order to cope with the various problems posed by such datasets, we construct a modular system that involves face detection, deep learning-based super-resolution of low-quality face images, and identity recognition through L2-normalized embeddings extracted by an embedding model. Enhancement models are trained and tested according to three different setups, and the system is deployed for identity verification in a real-time unconstrained environment.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, conference submission

</details>

### 309. [AxisQ: Depth-Adaptive Channel--Frequency Quantization for Camouflaged Object Detection](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36844v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36844

**作者：** Chi Ruan、Tianqi Li、Xin He、Xue Geng、Ming-Ming Cheng、Yun Liu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36844v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36844v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出 AxisQ，一种面向伪装目标检测的后训练量化框架，按特征深度在通道域与局部频率域间自适应分配比特精度。

**排除原因：** 该工作只关注 COD 模型的量化精度分配与重建误差，未涉及 latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策；深度自适应比特分配与 LVR 的计算分配仅有字面相似，缺少可核查的方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** AxisQ: Depth-Adaptive Channel--Frequency Quantization for Camouflaged Object Detection

**abstract/A1：** Camouflaged object detection (COD) depends on weak texture and boundary cues that can be destroyed by aggressive post-training quantization (PTQ). Existing PTQ methods mainly optimize quantizers or reconstruct network blocks, while using a single precision-allocation domain throughout hierarchical backbones. Instead, we introduce AxisQ, a depth-adaptive bit-allocation framework motivated by the observation that the useful allocation axis changes with feature depth. Under a fixed per-layer average activation-bit budget, AxisQ allocates precision in the channel domain for shallow features and in the local-frequency domain for deep features, using COD-aware sensitivity to better preserve subtle foreground regions and ambiguous boundaries. We also formulate a boundary-aware Hessian approximation for reconstruction by incorporating boundary-weighted gradient and error statistics. Experiments across multiple COD models and diverse benchmarks demonstrate improved accuracy over the strongest baselines at low bit widths, with relative weighted F-measure gains of 12.7% for CFRN and 33.3% for CamoFormer-P on CAMO under W2A2 quantization. These results support adapting the allocation domain to feature depth as a transferable quantization strategy for hierarchical COD models.

</details>

### 310. [uTHCD-C: A Corruption-Robustness Benchmark for Tamil Handwritten Character Recognition](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36850v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36850

**作者：** Preethi S、Meeradevi T、Arun Prakash A

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36850v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36850v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该工作仅针对孤立泰米尔手写字符分类构建十类腐蚀鲁棒性基准并比较九种卷积/Transformer 架构的效率，不涉及多模态推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，也没有可供迁移的 latent 表示或训练线索，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** uTHCD-C: A Corruption-Robustness Benchmark for Tamil Handwritten Character Recognition

**abstract/A1：** Evaluation of Tamil handwritten character recognition (HCR) has largely focused on clean test images, and a standardized multi-corruption robustness benchmark for isolated Tamil HCR has not been established. We introduce uTHCD-C, a corruption-robustness benchmark based on the unconstrained Tamil Handwritten Character Database (uTHCD). The benchmark defines ten corruption types, including a handwriting-specific stroke-thinning corruption, at five severity levels. We evaluate nine convolutional and transformer-based architectures using five-fold stratified cross-validation and writer-independent testing. Robustness is quantified using mean Corruption Error (mCE), normalized against a VGG16-BN reference. We additionally compare parameter count, computational complexity, and inference throughput to characterize the computational requirements of the evaluated architectures. The results establish comparative robustness baselines for Tamil HCR, revealing substantial differences across architectures: ConvNeXt and Swin achieve the lowest mCE values (0.75 and 0.83, respectively), whereas ShuffleNet and MnasNet exhibit higher values (1.55 and 1.85, respectively). The accompanying efficiency measurements characterize the trade-offs between corruption robustness and computational requirements. By providing standardized corruption configurations, evaluation protocols, and comparative results, uTHCD-C establishes a reproducible basis for future research on robust and computationally efficient Tamil HCR.

**comment/C1：** 14 pages, pre-print

</details>

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** LingGS: Decoupling Feed-Forward Geometry Prediction from Camera Tracking for High-Fidelity Gaussian SLAM

**abstract/A1：** Achieving high-quality reconstruction and responsive camera tracking remains challenging for monocular RGB Gaussian splatting SLAM under a limited optimization budget. We present an online system that addresses this challenge by combining learned geometric priors with explicit geometric camera tracking, which can substantially outperform learned camera prediction in terms of tracking accuracy. A frozen streaming 3D foundation model serves as a virtual RGB-D sensor, providing depth estimation from monocular video. An explicit geometric solver then estimates camera motion through feature reprojection, and a streaming Gaussian mapper integrates the resulting observations into a dense scene representation. We further show that geometric tracking improves camera pose accuracy across representative streaming geometry backbones, demonstrating the broader applicability of our approach. Extensive experiments on indoor and outdoor datasets show that our method significantly improves reconstruction quality over existing approaches, achieving about 7 FPS on a single RTX 4090 and delivering 2-3 times the throughput of the evaluated prior SLAM systems.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 4 figures

</details>

<a id="paper-312"></a>

### 312. [RangeRectQuant: Compressing Scientific Fields for Every Future Rectangular Sum](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36873v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36873

**作者：** Jiahao Zhang、Xinling Wen、Yu Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36873v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36873v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 RangeRectQuant，通过前后向两遍扫描在整数区间内选取量化像素值，使保留像素范围与精确采样点的同时，任意轴对齐矩形求和都满足给定误差预算，实现科学栅格数据的线性时间压缩。

**排除原因：** 该工作属科学栅格数据的存储压缩与量化误差控制，围绕矩形求和预算与内存占用，未涉及视觉表征、latent 状态生成或推理决策，也未提供可直接借鉴的状态更新或监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** RangeRectQuant: Compressing Scientific Fields for Every Future Rectangular Sum

**abstract/A1：** Scientific rasters are often stored before the regions to be measured are known. Independent pixel-error bounds can force exact integer reconstruction when one absolute error budget covers every future rectangle. Coordinated changes allow every pixel to change while every rectangular sum remains accurate. A prefix sum accumulates values from the top-left corner. Four such sums determine any rectangular total. RangeRectQuant preserves the pixel range and designated exact samples while controlling errors in prefix sums. At each row position, we characterize exactly which errors let the remaining pixels meet these constraints. These errors form a single integer interval, even when many completions exist. A backward pass finds its endpoints; a forward pass uses them to choose quantized pixels. The resulting field meets the prescribed error budget for every axis-aligned rectangle, with linear work and workspace proportional to one row. Experiments on geographically held-out built-surface and water-occurrence maps show substantial storage savings over alternatives meeting the same requirements. Data producers encode once; later users measure arbitrary rectangles without the source, re-encoding, or query-specific correction.

**comment/C1：** 10 pages, 6 figures. Code, data, core experiments and extended figures: https://github.com/Marchematics/RangeRectQuant

</details>

### 313. [Natural Hard-Negative Mining from Positives for Instruction-Following Retrieval](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36900v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.36900

**作者：** Chaolang Li、Jingyu Li、Zhaocheng Du、Xuyang Xie、Gang Wang、Pengwen Dai

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36900v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.36900v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文针对指令遵循式检索（IFIR）的对比监督稀缺问题，提出自然难负例挖掘方法，把原始正例改造为指令条件下的难负例，并构建 NHNM-MARCO 训练集。

**排除原因：** 研究对象是纯文本检索的指令条件对比监督与训练数据构造，不涉及多模态内部状态、推理轨迹生成或证据获取与停止决策；难负例挖掘属于通用表示学习手段，与 LVR 的视觉证据筛选或 latent 状态监督之间缺乏可供核查的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Natural Hard-Negative Mining from Positives for Instruction-Following Retrieval

**abstract/A1：** Instruction-following information retrieval (IFIR) requires retrievers to rank documents by both a query and its instance-specific instructions, yet instruction-conditioned contrastive supervision remains scarce. Existing synthetic methods typically fix a positive passage and generate additional negatives, overlooking the hard-negative potential of the original positive. We propose Natural Hard-Negative Mining (NHNM), which promotes convertible negatives under query-compatible instructions and repurposes original positives as natural instruction hard negatives. Its automated Candidate Cleaning, Reversal Construction, and Geometry Filtering pipeline produces compact, high-contrast supervision aligned with the pretrained retrieval space. Applied to MSMARCO, NHNM yields NHNM-MARCO, an IFIR training set of nearly 7K instances. On InstructIR and FollowIR, models with 568M to 4B parameters fine-tuned on NHNM-MARCO achieve strong instruction-following performance and match or surpass substantially larger retrievers in several settings. Controlled analyses and ablations attribute these gains to natural hard-negative supervision rather than model scale or training-data volume.

</details>

### 314. [Crop Yield Prediction Using Regression Models: A Comparative Evaluation of Model Performance](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37022v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37022

**作者：** Saptak Das、Arijit Sarkar、Soumik Neogi、Suborno Naskar

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37022v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37022v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文比较线性回归、随机森林、决策树、岭回归与 lasso 在农作物产量预测数据上的表现，以 R²、MSE、RMSE 在不同训练/测试划分下评测。

**排除原因：** 属于农业产量预测的表格回归模型对比，不涉及视觉或多模态推理的内部状态、证据获取与停止决策，也没有可供借鉴的状态更新或贡献分配线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Crop Yield Prediction Using Regression Models: A Comparative Evaluation of Model Performance

**abstract/A1：** Reliable crop-yield prediction can support agricultural planning and resource allocation. This study evaluates five regression models for predicting crop yield from environmental, crop-related, and agricultural management variables. The dataset initially contained 1,000,000 observations and ten variables, with 231 observations having negative target values removed during preprocessing. Categorical variables were represented using one-hot encoding, and Boolean variables were converted into binary values. The models considered were multiple linear regression (LR), random-forest regression (RF), decision-tree regression (DT), ridge regression, and lasso regression. Their performance was evaluated using 80:20 and 70:30 training-testing splits based on the coefficient of determination (R-squared), mean squared error (MSE), and root mean squared error (RMSE). Applying an 80:20 split, LR achieved a test R-squared of approximately 0.9130 and a test RMSE of 0.5008. Applying a 70:30 split, lasso regression yielded the highest test R-squared (approximately 0.9131) and the lowest test RMSE (approximately 0.5008), with performance almost identical to LR and ridge regression. DT also showed improved test performance under the 70:30 split, whereas RF exhibited a substantial gap between training and testing performance. These results indicate that the evaluated linear regression model provides competitive baselines for this dataset. Further experimentation, including validation testing, will help determine the models' performance and predictability.

**comment/C1：** Experimental evaluation of crop-yield prediction using regression models

</details>

**内容简述：** 该论文在 BreakHis 组织病理数据集上以患者级划分基准测试十种 ImageNet 预训练模型做良恶性分类，并用 Grad-CAM、LIME、Integrated Gradients 和 LRP 生成事后解释，DenseNet121 取得最高准确率。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Interpretable Deep Learning Models for Breast Cancer Diagnosis Using Medical Imaging

**abstract/A1：** Deep learning shows promise for breast cancer diagnosis, but limited interpretability hinders clinical adoption. We benchmark ten ImageNet-pretrained models (ResNet50, VGG19, InceptionV3, DenseNet121, Xception, MobileNetV2, EfficientNet, ConvNeXt, ViT, and Swin Transformer) for benign-versus-malignant classification on the BreakHis histopathology dataset using a patient-level split, and explain their predictions with Grad-CAM, LIME, Integrated Gradients, and LRP. DenseNet121 performed best, with 99.4% test accuracy and an AUC-ROC of 0.999, and significantly outperformed all other models (p &lt; 0.001). Transformer-based models trailed the CNNs. The explanations consistently highlighted nuclei-rich and glandular regions of diagnostic relevance. Combining transfer learning with explainable AI can thus yield accurate, interpretable tools for breast cancer diagnosis.

### 316. [GraftRay: Physically Based Mesh Insertion into Gaussian Splatting Scenes via Path Tracing](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37036v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37036

**作者：** Jinxin Ai、Matthias Niessner

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37036v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37036v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出 GraftRay，在用 2DGS/3DGS 重建的室内场景中物理一致地插入网格物体：借助扩散先验恢复材质与法线、显式建模空间扩展光源，并用混合渲染器对高斯场景与插入网格联合路径追踪，以获得多视角一致的着色与软阴影。

**排除原因：** 工作聚焦三维高斯场景的材质恢复与路径追踪渲染保真度，不涉及 VLM 推理、latent 状态生成或证据与停止决策，也未见可供迁移的推理期表示或训练监督线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** GraftRay: Physically Based Mesh Insertion into Gaussian Splatting Scenes via Path Tracing

**abstract/A1：** Inserting virtual objects into scenes reconstructed via 2D or 3D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS/3DGS) is a key capability for content creation, AR, and synthetic data generation. However, realistic insertion requires the object to be seamlessly lit by the scene and, conversely, to cast physically plausible shadows onto it. Existing approaches either harmonize appearance in image space, sacrificing multi-view consistency, or approximate scene illumination with an environment map at the insertion point, which cannot represent near-field area lights such as windows and lamps that dominate indoor lighting. To address this, we present GraftRay, a unified method for physically consistent insertion of mesh objects into indoor scenes represented by either 2D or 3D Gaussians. We recover scene materials and surface normals with diffusion-based priors, and explicitly model spatially-extended emitters, solving their radiance in closed form. A hybrid renderer then jointly path-traces the scene Gaussians and inserted meshes, producing view-consistent shading, interreflections, and soft shadows. On FIPT, GraftRay sets a new state of the art, outperforming recent baselines such as ComGS and EAG-PT in rendering fidelity and shadow accuracy, while maintaining highly competitive rendering efficiency.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages + references, 6 figures, 2 tables

</details>

### 317. [EnergyCal: Taming Loss Spikes in Muon-Family Optimizers for Sparse MoE Training](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37062v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37062

**作者：** Masato Fujitake

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI、cs.CL

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37062v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37062v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文针对稀疏 MoE 语言模型训练中 Muon 系列优化器出现的 loss spike，提出 EnergyCal：按路由专家有限步 Newton–Schulz 方向的归一化平方 Frobenius 范数缩放其更新，并在 4B MoE 模型上验证其抑制 spike 与降低困惑度的效果。

**排除原因：** 内容属于优化器稳定性与 MoE 训练工程，未涉及视觉或多模态推理状态、证据使用或停止决策，也没有可供迁移的状态更新或表示线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EnergyCal: Taming Loss Spikes in Muon-Family Optimizers for Sparse MoE Training

**abstract/A1：** Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) language-model training can exhibit large loss spikes with Muon-family optimizers. We introduce EnergyCal, a lightweight rule that attenuates routed-expert updates according to the normalized squared Frobenius norm of their finite-step Newton-Schulz directions. EnergyCal leaves the spectral transform unchanged and adds no persistent optimizer state. We evaluate it on a 4B-parameter MoE model. Against a development-selected, lower-learning-rate MuonH recipe, EnergyCal-MuonH reduces mean thresholded loss-spike burden by 99.51% and final geometric-mean perplexity by 4.06%, with a modest throughput cost. With plain Muon at matched nominal learning rate and weight decay, the reduction in mean spike burden averages 95.98% across the two tested exponents, accompanied by small endpoint improvements. These findings support stabilization both with and without Hyperball in the evaluated setting.

**comment/C1：** 9 pages with appendices

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37118v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37118v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文面向黄瓜物候阶段分类这一农业应用，提出一种多分支集成头：训练时搭配低/高容量分支并随机采样单分支，推理时在概率空间平均两分支，以在数据稀缺下改善 DINOv2 等视觉基础模型的精度—效率权衡。

**排除原因：** 属于一般下游分类刷分与部署效率工作，未涉及视觉推理状态、证据筛选、动态计算或停止决策，多分支集成仅作正则化，难以提供 LVR 相关的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Multibranch Ensemble Head in Vision Foundation Models for Efficient Cucumber Phenological Stage Classification

**abstract/A1：** Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) pretrained with self-supervised learning, such as DINOv2, provide highly transferable features, yet adapting them to small, domain-shifted datasets is still challenging. We propose a regularizing multibranch ensemble head: a training and inference scheme that pairs low- and high-capacity branches, samples a single branch stochastically at each training step, and averages both branches in probability space at inference. The design adds negligible parameters and essentially no inference overhead over a single heavy head, while improving the accuracy-efficiency trade-off under data-scarce conditions. Accurate classification of plant growth stages is critical for precision agriculture, enabling timely decisions on fertilization, irrigation, and harvesting. Therefore, as a challenging practical case we use cucumber phenological stage classification, in which flowers and fruits are frequently occluded by foliage, and stage transitions manifest primarily as subtle changes in leaf morphology. The NALEF Food Safety dataset contains images of five cucumber growth stages captured under diverse real-world conditions. On it we evaluate a broad range of backbones, including DINOv2 and DINOv3 ViT and ConvNeXt variants, against strongly supervised counterparts (ResNet, EfficientNet, ViT). The proposed DINOv2 ViT-S with multibranch head achieves a Macro F1 of 0.978 and Cohen's Kappa of 0.971, matching the performance of models more than ten times larger in parameters and GFlops, while requiring only 12.2 GFlops and 22.3M parameters, making it deployable on smartphones and edge devices for phenological stage classification.

</details>

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37127v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37127v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向经皮冠脉介入的机器人导丝导航，基于仿真X光图像训练多任务网络联合分割血管、定位导丝尖端与狭窄，并构建血管拓扑图以生成路径图。

**排除原因：** 属于医学影像多任务感知与导航应用，未涉及 VLM 推理、latent 状态生成或证据使用与停止决策，其中的结构化血管图仍属常规几何后处理，缺乏可供借鉴的推理状态线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MCP-Net: Real-Time Coronary Perception Network and Topological Analysis for Robotic Guidewire Navigation

**abstract/A1：** Coronary guidewire navigation during percutaneous coronary intervention requires continuous interpretation of vessel anatomy, guidewire position, and target stenosis under two-dimensional X-ray fluoroscopy. While existing approaches commonly address vessel segmentation or device localization as separate tasks, a robotic system requires a more structured perception of the coronary scene to support downstream navigation. In this work, we present a simulation-driven framework centered on a Multi-Task Coronary Perception Network (MCP-Net) for robotic guidewire navigation, integrating coronary vessel segmentation, vascular topology extraction, and roadmap generation. Starting from three-dimensional coronary CT angiography, we generate simulated fluoroscopy images containing vascular structures, simulated guidewires, stenotic lesions with varying severity, and diverse vascular geometries, together with simulation-derived ground truth. MCP-Net jointly performs coronary vessel segmentation, guidewire-tip localization, and stenosis localization from a single fluoroscopic frame. These predictions are subsequently converted into a structured vascular graph containing centerlines, bifurcations, endpoints, branch directions, and branch angles. By associating the predicted stenosis with this graph, the framework generates a roadmap from the automatically selected graph root toward the target lesion. The results demonstrate that the proposed framework can jointly recover coronary perception targets and transform them into an interpretable vascular representation suitable for downstream robotic navigation.

</details>

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37134v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37134v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出三阶段脊柱 MRI-to-CT 合成框架：先用解剖引导的椎体匹配与刚性配准从未配对扫描构造伪配对数据，再训练对抗扩散模型做跨模态翻译，最后将切片堆叠成 3D 体并做层间连续性精修。

**排除原因：** 属医学影像跨模态生成与术前可视化任务，虽涉及扩散模型和结构一致性，但既无 VLM 推理状态、证据筛选或停止决策，也未讨论 latent 表示的使用与监督，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MRI-to-CT Translation of Spinal Images via Anatomy-Guided Pseudo-Pairing and Adversarial Diffusion

**abstract/A1：** Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is routinely used to diagnose spinal disorders, but surgical planning often requires Computed Tomography (CT) for better visualization of vertebral bone anatomy. Synthesizing CT from MRI could avoid additional radiation exposure and streamline preoperative assessment. We propose a three-stage framework for spine MRI-to-CT synthesis. First, an anatomy-guided vertebral matching system identifies similar sagittal MRI and CT slices using spine segmentations and aligns them through rigid registration, creating pseudo-paired data from unpaired scans. Second, these pairs train an adversarial diffusion model for MRI-to-CT translation. Third, synthesized slices are assembled into 3D volumes and refined by a network that improves inter-slice continuity and structural coherence. The framework was trained on over 10,000 unpaired sagittal MRI and CT slices and evaluated on 1,911 slices from 83 MRI volumes spanning all spinal regions. Assessment combined distribution-based measures with vertebral segmentation metrics. Compared with diffusion alone, the proposed approach increased binary 3D vertebral Dice from 0.666 to 0.739. Volumetric post-processing reduced visible discontinuities between stacked slices and improved vertebral coherence in axial and coronal views. Combining anatomy-guided pseudo-pairing, adversarial diffusion and 3D refinement improves spine MRI-to-CT synthesis. Vertebral matching primarily enhanced anatomical alignment and fidelity, while volumetric refinement improved 3D consistency. These findings support further development of MRI-derived CT-like visualization for preoperative planning, particularly to preserve fine intensity details while maintaining volumetric coherence.

</details>

<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Physics Guided Diffusion Model Based Exposure Interpolation for High Dynamic Range Imaging

**abstract/A1：** Ghosting artifacts are an open problem for popular multi-shot based high dynamic range (HDR) imaging. An effective solution is to capture two large-exposure-ratio (LER) standard dynamic range (SDR) images simultaneously via a single-shot. Brightness order reversal (BOR) artifacts are generated when the two LER images are directly fused together. It is crucial to interpolate an image with an exposure time in between the exposure times of two LER images. In this paper, a novel physics-guided diffusion model based exposure interpolation algorithm is proposed to address the BOR artifacts. An initial image is interpolated by a physics-driven component, and the residual high-frequency details are generated by a new diffusion model based component. The new diffusion model is based on the correlation analysis of generative models and includes a new predicted target to reduce the variation of the Pearson correlation between the noisy data and the target, as well as the amplification factor of the fitting error. Compared with existing neural augmented exposure interpolation algorithms on top of convolution neural networks, the proposed exposure interpolation algorithm has an inherent advantage to generate more high frequency information. Experiment results validate the proposed exposure interpolation algorithm.

**comment/C1：** 11 pages, 6 figures

</details>

### 322. [MixSIS: Mixed-supervised instance segmentation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37138v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37138

**作者：** Christian Wilms、Emre Gülsoylu、Sven Schreiber、Simone Frintrop

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37138v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37138v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文提出混合监督实例分割任务 MixSIS，允许数据集中混合使用图像级、点、框和掩码标注，并给出基线 AnnsMixer，从各标注层级生成伪掩码。

**排除原因：** 该工作是实例分割的标注成本与伪掩码监督问题，不涉及 VLM 内部表示、视觉证据筛选或推理期状态生成；虽涉及异构监督，但未提供可直接核查的 latent 推理线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MixSIS: Mixed-supervised instance segmentation

**abstract/A1：** Weakly-supervised instance segmentation (WSIS) based on either box-, point-, or image-level annotations has been proposed to overcome the labor-intensive annotation process in fully-supervised instance segmentation. Despite decent results, most WSIS methods only focus on a single annotation level, ignoring that some objects are easy to spot from point annotations, while others require box or even mask annotations. Therefore, we propose the Mixed-Supervised Instance Segmentation (MixSIS) task, which allows a flexible mix of annotation levels (image, point, box, and mask) within a dataset. MixSIS has a high practical relevance, as it allows optimizing the annotation process by annotating challenging objects in more detail, while saving time on simpler instances with less detailed annotations. To address MixSIS, we propose AnnotationsMixer (AnnsMixer) as a baseline. AnnsMixer is a truly mixed-supervised instance segmentation system, which generates pseudo mask annotations from all annotation levels, even mixed within a single image. We evaluate AnnsMixer on both WSIS and MixSIS tasks, largely outperforming state-of-the-art methods. In three case studies, we also demonstrate how the unique features of MixSIS and AnnsMixer can substantially reduce the annotation time (-90%) or improve the results (+34%) in instance segmentation with a simple annotation rule or leveraging heterogeneous data. Our code is available at https://github.com/chwilms/MixSIS

**comment/C1：** Accepted at BMVC 2026. Code available at https://github.com/chwilms/MixSIS

</details>

### 323. [FastBEVFusion: Simple Multi-Modal Fusion for 3D Object Detection at 50 FPS](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37153v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37153

**作者：** Tom Sanitz、Emre Gülsoylu、Simone Frintrop、Christian Wilms

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.RO

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37153v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37153v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 FastBEVFusion：将 LiDAR 与相机特征投影到半对齐的模态特定 BEV 空间，用轻量 cross-attention 动态融合，实现 50 FPS 以上的实时 3D 目标检测。

**排除原因：** 属于自动驾驶 3D 检测的实时工程与融合架构优化，不涉及推理状态生成、视觉证据筛选、latent 使用或停止决策，缺少可供 LVR 借鉴的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** FastBEVFusion: Simple Multi-Modal Fusion for 3D Object Detection at 50 FPS

**abstract/A1：** Reliable real-time 3D object detection based on LiDAR and camera data is critical for safety in autonomous driving, where systems must accurately perceive their surroundings with minimal latency. Many state-of-the-art systems achieve high-quality detection results but fail to meet real-time constraints, limiting their practical use. To address this challenge, we propose FastBEVFusion, a simple, fast, and robust fusion system for 3D object detection. Based on modality-specific features, FastBEVFusion projects these features into semi-aligned modality-specific BEV spaces, without relying on a computationally intensive and potentially error-prone depth estimation. Given these BEV spaces, we propose a light-weight, soft, and dynamic fusion module based on cross-attention, which adapts dynamically to the scene context and enables feature fusion across the entire BEV spaces. Experiments on the complex nuScenes dataset show that FastBEVFusion outperforms state-of-the-art real-time systems, and achieves over 50 FPS without deployment libraries. Additionally, our system shows strong robustness to calibration errors, making FastBEVFusion a powerful 3D object detection system for real-world applications.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at Iberian Robotics Conference (ROBOT2026)

</details>

### 324. [Zhengrong-Spatial-Aware 1.0: Advancing 3D Multimodal Intelligence](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37216v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37216

**作者：** Yu Zhang、Xiaogang Wang、Yuhang Cheng、Jingrui Li、Wenbo Chen

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37216v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37216v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该技术报告实现了两条端到端 3D 空间 VQA 微调流水线：一是在 RGB 外加入米制 3D point map 的 HiSpatial-3B 微调，二是基于 QLoRA 的 Qwen3-VL（8B/32B）监督微调，并报告了自建空间基准与风格化切分上的准确率提升。

**排除原因：** 主要是数据转换、几何预处理、指令构造与 QLoRA 训练的工程实现与刷分结果，未涉及 latent 推理状态生成、视觉证据是否被答案使用或停止决策，缺少可供核查的机制线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Zhengrong-Spatial-Aware 1.0: Advancing 3D Multimodal Intelligence

**abstract/A1：** This report presents the implementation and validation of two end-to-end multimodal adaptation pipelines for 3D-aware visual question answering on a custom spatial dataset. The first pipeline fine-tunes HiSpatial-3B, which augments RGB observations with metric-scale 3D point maps; the second adapts Qwen3-VL through memory-efficient QLoRA-based supervised fine-tuning. The completed engineering stack covers dataset conversion, image-annotation alignment, monocular geometry preprocessing, instruction construction, mixed-domain training, checkpoint management, batch inference, and automated evaluation. On the target spatial benchmark, HiSpatial adaptation increases overall accuracy from 1.73% before fine-tuning to 79.89% at 3,600 steps. On a separately collected styled split, accuracy improves from 14.9% to 55.1%, indicating transfer beyond the original training set. The Qwen3-VL pipeline is likewise implemented end-to-end for both 8B- and 32B-scale models using mixed-domain QLoRA adaptation. The resulting accuracy improves from 20.74% to 45.27% for Qwen3-VL-8B and from 30.79% to 47.60% for Qwen3-VL-32B. Together, these results establish two complementary and reproducible adaptation pipelines for geometry-aware and general-purpose multimodal spatial reasoning. These results establish a reproducible engineering foundation for subsequent work on metric 3D reasoning, spatial grounding, and domain-adapted multimodal systems, while also identifying training-data composition as a central factor in preserving general multimodal capability during specialization.

**comment/C1：** 6 pages, 3 figures, 4 tables. Technical report

</details>
