### 325. [Sharpness-Aware Muon: Matrix-Aware Optimization with Momentum-Guided Perturbations](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37225v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37225

**作者：** Zhiwei Zhai、Wenjing Yan、Ying-Jun Angela Zhang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37225v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37225v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文为 Muon 优化器引入 sharpness-aware 形式，提出 MuSAM 与 MoMuSAM，用归一化动量构造扰动以省去额外梯度计算，并分析其收敛性与稳定性。

**排除原因：** 内容属通用矩阵优化器的平坦性与泛化改进，未涉及多模态推理状态、视觉证据使用、动态计算或停止决策，也未见可供核查的 LVR 方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Sharpness-Aware Muon: Matrix-Aware Optimization with Momentum-Guided Perturbations

**abstract/A1：** Muon is a matrix-aware optimizer that combines momentum with orthogonalization to produce structured update directions with strong empirical behavior. However, it only shapes the geometry of the update itself and does not explicitly control neighborhood worst-case loss or bias training toward flat regions, which are often linked to stronger generalization. To address this limitation, we introduce the sharpness-aware formulation of Muon. We first develop \\textsc{MuSAM}, which incorporates Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM)-style perturbation into Muon's orthogonalized update and establishes a principled bridge between sharpness-aware training and matrix-aware optimization. We then show that this formulation faces an efficiency--stability tension: constructing the perturbation requires an extra gradient evaluation, while the perturbation itself is generated from a single minibatch gradient and is therefore noisy. To resolve this issue, we propose \\textsc{MoMuSAM}, which turns Muon's momentum from an update variable into a sharpness-probing signal. By using normalized momentum as the perturbation direction, \\textsc{MoMuSAM} removes the extra clean-gradient computation and replaces the single-batch perturbation proxy with a temporally aggregated one. We prove that both \\textsc{MuSAM} and \\textsc{MoMuSAM} attain the same asymptotic first-order stationarity order, and further show that \\textsc{MoMuSAM} yields a more stable stochastic linearization than \\textsc{MuSAM}. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that sharpness awareness consistently improves Muon.

</details>

<a id="paper-326"></a>

### 326. [Subspace Clifford Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37226v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37226

**作者：** Zhongping Ji

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37226v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37226v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 Subspace Clifford Networks：用分组低维子空间内的楔积与坐标乘积替代全局循环移位，配合 1×1 投影实现跨组通道混合，并用融合 CUDA kernel 与重计算反向传播降低开销；在 CIFAR-100 上验证参数与训练时间下降。

**排除原因：** 主要面向视觉骨干的效率与参数压缩，楔积仅作为廉价特征交互算子，未涉及推理状态、视觉证据获取、停止决策或监督分配，与 LVR 主线缺少实质性联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Subspace Clifford Networks

**abstract/A1：** In this work, we propose Subspace Clifford Networks, a reformulation that replaces global cyclic rolling with grouped subspace interactions. Instead of sampling sparse cyclic diagonals across the entire $D$-dimensional channel space, we project the features into an aligned space $\\R^{\\tilde{D}} = \\bigoplus\_{g=1}^{G} V\_g$ partitioned into $G$ low-dimensional groups $V\_g \\cong \\R^k$ with $k \\in \\{2, 3, 4\\}$. Within each $k$-dimensional group, we compute all $\\binom{k}{2}$ wedge coordinates and concatenate them with $\\operatorname{SiLU}$-activated coordinate-wise products, while sandwiching the grouped interaction between learnable $1\\times 1$ linear projections to enable cross-group channel mixing. This design reduces intermediate feature expansion from $2\|\\mathcal{S}\|D$ (typically $10D$ for 5 shifts) down to $1.5\\tilde{D}$ ($k=2$), $2.0\\tilde{D}$ ($k=3$), or $2.5\\tilde{D}$ ($k=4$). Furthermore, exploiting the small, fixed group size $k \\in \\{2, 3, 4\\}$, we implement a fused CUDA kernel that evaluates the forward interaction and a recomputation-based analytical backward pass using thread-local variables without allocating global-memory buffers for elementwise intermediates. Finally, we extend the backbone from an isotropic columnar layout to a multi-resolution Hierarchical Clifford Network with configurable per-stage subspace dimensions. Experiments on CIFAR-100 show that the proposed models substantially reduce parameter count and training time across both regimes, while the hierarchical variants also improve classification accuracy over the evaluated shift-based hierarchical baselines.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages

</details>

### 327. [Grounding LLM Agents in Business Processes: Process-Aware Knowledge Graphs for Reliable Multi-Step Service Invocation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37242v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37242

**作者：** Saiful Khan、Afrah Farea

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI、cs.SE

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37242v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37242v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 论文把 OData 元数据与 BPMN 参考流程构建成类型化属性图，用稠密嵌入加流程边检索为 LLM agent 的多步企业 API 调用提供端点、顺序与 schema 约束，并在 42 个服务与 81 条多步查询上评测。

**排除原因：** 属于企业 API 流程检索与 schema 约束的工程工作，未涉及视觉或多模态内部状态、latent 推理状态生成与使用、动态计算或停止决策，检索流程边也未给出可迁移到 LVR 的具体机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Grounding LLM Agents in Business Processes: Process-Aware Knowledge Graphs for Reliable Multi-Step Service Invocation

**abstract/A1：** Large language model (LLM) agents that call enterprise APIs must select the correct endpoints, follow process order, use valid payload keys, and choose coded values from predefined lists. However, current retrieval-augmented service discovery treats endpoints independently and therefore misses process and schema constraints. We address this limitation by integrating Open Data Protocol (OData) metadata and Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) reference processes in a typed property graph. A hybrid retriever identifies candidate endpoints using dense embeddings and follows process edges to retrieve subsequent endpoints. We evaluate the method on 42 enterprise services with process-chain ground truth. For queries with low lexical overlap between the query and downstream entities, it improves Recall@3 by 5.7 points (n=74), with consistent gains across embedding models and cut-offs. It matches dense retrieval on other queries and provides no improvement on OpenAPI services without a process layer, indicating that the gains arise from process structure. We also evaluate an LLM agent on 81 multi-step queries. It completes 38.7% of tasks, compared with 10.3% for a chunk-based baseline. In an elicitation probe, providing valid code lists eliminates invented codes but leaves invalid keys in 46% of write runs. Full typed context reduces invalid calls to 3% (3 of 101 runs), with no invented codes. These results show that exposing process and schema constraints during retrieval improves the reliability of agent-driven API calls.

</details>

### 328. [TRUCO: A Scalable Lock-Free Algorithm for Parallel Contour Extraction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37297v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37297

**作者：** Rafael Munoz-Salinas、Francisco J. Romero-Ramirez、Manuel J. Marin-Jimenez

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37297v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37297v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 TRUCO，一种锁自由的并行轮廓提取算法，通过起点归属规则与推测式追踪，在多核 CPU 上从二值分割掩码高效提取几何边界。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于分割后处理的几何提取工程优化，只关注并行加速与可扩展性，不涉及视觉细节的内部表示、推理状态生成或证据筛选，缺乏可迁移的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** TRUCO: A Scalable Lock-Free Algorithm for Parallel Contour Extraction

**abstract/A1：** Transforming binary masks into geometric contours remains a critical step in many computer vision pipelines, particularly for post-processing high-resolution segmentation outputs. Yet, standard algorithms such as the Suzuki-Abe method are limited by their sequential nature and the overhead of building topological hierarchies. As modern applications transition to high-resolution imaging, there is a critical need for contour extraction that leverages multi-core CPU architectures without the complexity of tile stitching or heavy synchronization. This paper presents TRUCO (Threaded Raster Unrestricted Contour Ownership), a lock-free parallel algorithm designed for high-performance geometric boundary extraction. TRUCO introduces a start-point ownership rule, combined with a speculative tracing model, in which threads trace contours downward into adjacent regions and use an upward-termination logic to ensure each contour is processed exactly once. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets, conducted on both consumer and server-grade CPUs, demonstrate that TRUCO significantly outperforms existing baselines. The method achieves speedups of over an order of magnitude compared to the industry standard while maintaining robust scalability on high-resolution scenes across diverse hardware architectures.

</details>

### 329. [MvT: Event-based Multi-view Projection for Multiple Object Tracking](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37309v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37309

**作者：** Muxi Zha、Banglei Guan、Minzu Liang、Zibin Liu、Yang Shang、Qifeng Yu、Laurent Kneip

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37309v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37309v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 面向事件相机的多目标跟踪，提出由多视角投影与融合组成、无需训练的多视角流水线，以缓解伪帧上轨迹重叠导致的关联歧义。

**排除原因：** 属于事件相机 MOT 的工程流程与几何优化关联，不涉及 VLM 内部推理状态、视觉证据筛选或 latent 计算，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** MvT: Event-based Multi-view Projection for Multiple Object Tracking

**abstract/A1：** Event cameras are increasingly used for Multiple Object Tracking (MOT), but their asynchronous event output often requires specialized methods. Existing processing methods primarily follow two paradigms, pseudo-frames and event-by-event. The former is the prevailing approach since its data format aligns with images, making image-based techniques applicable. However, it suffers from tracking failures when trajectories overlap or are spatially close on pseudo-frames. Facing this challenge, we propose a multi-view pipeline, Multi-view Tracking (MvT), which preserves the 2D data format to leverage image-based techniques directly while introducing additional spatio-temporal views to resolve tracking ambiguities in a single view. MvT comprises a Multi-view Projection (MvP) module and a Multi-view Fusion (MvF) stage. MvP encodes events into three complementary spatio-temporal views while mitigating the pattern discretization. Within MvF, multi-view results are unified into a 3D coordinate system, and tracklets are associated through an optimization model subject to specific criteria combination. Evaluations on four datasets, including our self-collected Small Objects Dataset (SOD), show that MvT seamlessly integrates image-based methods and outperforms existing non-learning and learning trackers in generalized scenarios, and effectively resolves the single-view tracking ambiguities. Being training-free, MvT is applicable when ground-truth annotation is infeasible, thereby highlighting its practical, data-efficient potential. Code is available at https://github.com/zhazhabiu/MvTracking.

**comment/C1：** 16 pages, 20 figures, published to IEEE T-IP

</details>

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37314v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37314v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文提出一个带通道注意力的轻量级 CNN，用于皮肤镜图像的良恶性二分类，并用显著图可视化决策依据，在参数量和存储减半的情况下取得与 MobileNet 相当的准确率与 AUC。

**排除原因：** 属于标准医学图像分类与常规可解释性可视化，注意力仅用于分类特征加权，未涉及推理期状态生成、视觉证据在内部表示中的保留或答案是否使用 latent 内容，缺乏可供核查的 LVR 借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A Lightweight Attention-Based CNN with Saliency-Map Interpretability for Skin Cancer Detection

**abstract/A1：** Skin cancer is among the most prevalent and life threatening forms of cancer worldwide. Early and accurate diagnosis significantly improves patient survival rates. However, manual dermoscopic assessment remains challenging due to visual similarity. This study proposes an Explainable AI-driven framework for automated binary classification of skin lesions using a lightweight attention-based convolutional neural network (CNN-Attention). The model integrates channel attention to focus on informative regions of dermoscopic images while maintaining low computational complexity. A publicly available dataset comprising 2,637 training and 660 test images was used, with balanced class weights. The proposed model achieved 87 percent accuracy with 1.95 million parameters and 7.45 MB of storage, matching the best baseline (MobileNet) while using approximately half as many parameters and half as much storage. ROC AUC and PR AUC scores reached 0.9475 and 0.9430, respectively. Additionally, saliency maps were incorporated as an explainability technique to visualize regions that influence model decisions. Comparative analysis with VGG16,VGG19,ResNet101,and MobileNet demonstrates the proposed models effectiveness in balancing classification results, computational efficiency, and interpretability.

</details>

### 331. [CoS-VQ: Coordinated Shared-Scale Vector Quantization for Large Language Models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37336v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37336

**作者：** Zukang Xu、Zhixiong Zhao、Xing Hu、Zhe Jiang、Xin Si、Dawei Yang

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37336v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37336v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CoS-VQ，一种面向 LLM 权重的低比特向量量化方法，在保持低维码字的同时联合优化硬索引与跨位置共享尺度。

**排除原因：** 该工作是纯权重量化压缩，服务于部署显存效率，不涉及视觉细节保留、latent 推理状态生成、证据获取或停止决策，其「索引-尺度耦合」优化也缺少可直接迁移到 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CoS-VQ: Coordinated Shared-Scale Vector Quantization for Large Language Models

**abstract/A1：** Large language models (LLMs) require substantial memory, motivating low-bit weight quantization for efficient deployment. Existing vector quantization (VQ) methods often improve accuracy by increasing the effective coding dimension, which can complicate independent lookup and local codeword updates. We propose CoS-VQ (Coordinated Shared-Scale Vector Quantization), which preserves low-dimensional codewords while jointly optimizing hard indices and their shared scale across positions. The key challenge is scale-induced coupling: the optimal scale depends on the entire codeword combination and, in turn, changes candidate rankings across positions, so pointwise updates can stall even after scale re-optimization. CoS-VQ collects candidates at multiple probe scales and constructs a tangent separable upper bound for the residual-aware quadratic objective. By reducing candidate comparisons to lower envelopes of lines over positive scales, it identifies finitely many representative intervals for joint index--scale updates without exhaustively enumerating codeword combinations. On Qwen3.8-27B, CoS-VQ outperforms QTIP and LLVQ on the evaluated language modeling, coding, and reasoning benchmarks. Ablations from the same initialization further show larger gains from joint updates than from scale-only refinement, highlighting joint selection as an alternative to increasing codeword dimensionality. The code is available at https://github.com/xzktx003/Cos\_VQ.

**comment/C1：** 20 pages, 3 figures. Code: https://github.com/xzktx003/Cos\_VQ

</details>

**排除原因：** 属规范性设计与制度分析，未涉及视觉或多模态推理、latent 状态生成或训练监督，其「制度状态」与操作过程的对应也难形成具体可核查的 LVR 方法线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Aligning Institutional Power and Operational Ability

**abstract/A1：** To be socially sustainable, Agentic AI systems require normative designs that align responsibilities with the ability to fulfil them. Identifying incongruences such as unsatisfiable expectations or privilege escalation is crucial in avoiding normative pitfalls. In this study, we discuss design dimensions suitable for describing multi-agent systems in light of this norm-ability alignment requirement. We propose a formal framework centred on process-oriented modelling and institutional power for the analysis of a given normatively relevant process, discuss strategies for repairing it, and elaborate on how the symmetrical relation between operational process and institutional state affects the lifecycle of normative positions.

</details>

**内容简述：** 提出 EmbeddingGemma 2，一个统一表示文本、图像、音频、视频及其组合的开源多模态嵌入模型，通过逐层嵌入简化与多阶段课程训练提升跨模态检索质量，并支持可选编码器加载、可调视觉 token 预算与可截断维度。

**排除原因：** 该工作聚焦多模态检索嵌入空间与部署效率，未涉及推理状态的生成、更新或答案对视觉证据的使用分析；可调视觉 token 预算等属工程选项，未提供与视觉细节保留或证据筛选相关的机制线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** EmbeddingGemma 2: Unified and Efficient Multimodal Representations

**abstract/A1：** We present EmbeddingGemma 2, an open multimodal embedding model which natively represents text, images, audio, video, and combinations thereof in a unified space. Our approach introduces improvements such as simplified per-layer embeddings and a multi-stage curriculum with modality and objective progression which ensure strong quality across unimodal, cross-modal, and mixed-modal evaluations. Notably, in comprehensive embedding benchmarks across all modalities, EmbeddingGemma 2 establishes a new Pareto frontier, outperforming both specialist models and models two to twelve times larger. Furthermore, our design offers flexibility for resource-constrained environments, supporting selective modality encoder loading, adjustable vision token budgets, truncatable embedding dimensions, and quantized weights. We present ablations analyzing key decisions and release EmbeddingGemma 2 to promote further research.

**comment/C1：** 28 pages. Models are available on HuggingFace (at https://huggingface.co/google/embeddinggemma-2) and LiteRT (at https://huggingface.co/litert-community/embeddinggemma-2-740m-litert-lm)

### 334. [Para-Gaussian Approximations of Random Deep Neural Networks](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37388v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37388

**作者：** Mihai Nica、Janosch Ortmann

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG、math.PR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37388v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37388v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文研究随机初始化深层网络的分布近似：用 Hermite 多项式构造「para-Gaussian」分布，对有限宽度、深层网络给出比高斯近似更准确的递归近似，并在线性网络与 ReLU 网络上验证。

**排除原因：** 内容属于随机网络分布理论与逐层统计近似，未涉及视觉或多模态推理、latent 状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，缺乏可迁移到 LVR 的具体线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Para-Gaussian Approximations of Random Deep Neural Networks

**abstract/A1：** It is known that randomly initialized neural networks have Gaussian behaviour in the $n\\to \\infty$ limit. Unfortunately, this approximation can be poor for finite $n$ and gets worse as one goes deeper into the network. In this work, we develop a more accurate approximation that is \\emph{slightly} non-Gaussian, which we call the para-Gaussian distribution and is constructed from the Hermite polynomials. We show how this para-Gaussian approximation can lead to a practical recursive method to approximate the deeper layers of the network. Finally, we apply this theory to linear neural networks (where things can be solved exactly) and to ReLU neural networks (where things can be approximated).

**comment/C1：** 34 oages, 9 figures

</details>

### 335. [How Many and How Much? Contrastive Learning with Negative Samples for Knowledge Graph Embedding](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37389v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37389

**作者：** Sooho Moon、Yunyong Ko

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37389v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37389v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该论文针对知识图谱嵌入的对比学习提出 RADAR 框架，用风险感知的负样本预算分配和基于负样本分数分布校准的加权策略，回答「每个正样本用多少负样本」和「每个负样本贡献多少」两个问题。

**排除原因：** 内容为知识图谱嵌入的负采样与损失加权，属于通用对比训练技巧，既不涉及视觉或多模态内部表示，也没有 latent 推理状态、证据获取或动态计算等具体线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** How Many and How Much? Contrastive Learning with Negative Samples for Knowledge Graph Embedding

**abstract/A1：** Knowledge graph embedding (KGE), a fundamental technique for knowledge-centric Web and KG-augmented AI applications, is commonly trained through contrastive learning with negative samples to learn discriminative representations. Despite its empirical success, two crucial questions regarding negative samples remain underexplored: (Q1) how many negative samples should be used for each positive sample and (Q2) how much should each negative sample contribute to learning? To answer these questions, we propose RADAR, a novel contrastive learning framework for KGE models with two effective strategies, respectively: (1) Risk-aware negative budget allocation quantifies false-negative sampling risk by modeling an open-world KG as a stochastic partial observation of its closed-world counterpart and adaptively assigns a negative budget to each positive sample based on its risk; (2) Distribution-calibrated negative weighting assigns negative weights by calibrating the weighting strength for each positive based on two complementary characteristics of the negative-score distribution: dispersion and concentration. Extensive experiments on 3 real-world KGs with 4 KGE models reveal that RADAR achieves the best KGE performance on average across all model--KG combinations and reduces sampled false negatives by over 90% compared with conventional fixed-budget methods.

**comment/C1：** 12 pages, 12 figures, 5 tables

</details>

### 336. [CTBakeGS: training-free baking of photorealistic CT volume renderings into 3D Gaussian splats](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37406v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37406

**作者：** Haruki Fukuda、Constantin Lux、Wolf Schweitzer、Tadashi Hosoya、Lars Ebert

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.GR

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37406v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37406v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 该文提出 CTBakeGS，无需训练地把 CT 体渲染结果烘焙为 3D Gaussian splats：在自适应八叉树单元上放置 splat，用渲染器光照在 GPU 上计算颜色与不透明度并解析转换为 splat 不透明度，在六例公开 CT 上与收敛参考比较保真度与渲染速度。

**排除原因：** 属于医学影像的可视化保真与实时渲染工程，不涉及视觉推理中的内部表示、latent 状态生成、证据筛选或停止决策，也无可迁移的推理监督与计算分配线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CTBakeGS: training-free baking of photorealistic CT volume renderings into 3D Gaussian splats

**abstract/A1：** Photorealistic volume rendering of computed tomography (CT) may convey anatomy intuitively, but it needs the CT data, dedicated software and a powerful graphics processor. Three-dimensional Gaussian splatting (3DGS) displays photorealistic scenes in real time on less powerful devices, but existing medical approaches train each scene for minutes to hours. We aimed to convert a CT volume rendering directly into a 3DGS model without training. Splats were placed on an adaptive octree of millimetre-sized cells. Their colour and opacity were computed on the graphics processor with the renderer's lighting, cell opacity was converted analytically into splat opacity, and faint splats were retained stochastically above the 1/255 opacity floor of standard 3DGS rasterisers. Six public CT scans (1.5-mm voxels) were converted with three transfer functions and compared with converged references by peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity and a learned perceptual metric. Two-million-splat models reached a PSNR of 32.6 +/- 0.9 dB for bone, 30.1 +/- 1.0 dB for contrast-enhanced vessels and 32.3 +/- 1.5 dB for skin and bone. A conversion pass (usually two or three per model) took 4-30 s on a current laptop and 9-60 s on a desktop with a 4-GB graphics card. On the laptop, the models rendered at 68 frames per second at 800 x 800 pixels. Training-free conversion produced real-time 3DGS models of photorealistic CT renderings in about a minute or less per conversion pass on ordinary computers, with fidelity that we consider sufficient for interactive viewing.

**comment/C1：** Code and project page: https://github.com/BocchiSlice/CTBakeGS

</details>

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37442

**作者：** Congwei Zhang、Youming Tao、Yifei Zou、Yu Yang、Ruirui Zhang、Xiaoyu Zhang、Falko Dressler、Xiuzhen Cheng、Dongxiao Yu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37442v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37442v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 CRISP，针对显存受限边缘平台上的 MoE 推理，将神经元重要性重排、按专家组合频率与角色差异化执行宽度、以及专家加载与计算重叠联合设计，以提升吞吐并控制精度损失。

**排除原因：** 该工作属 MoE 服务系统的剪枝与调度优化，不涉及视觉或多模态推理中的内部表示、证据筛选或停止决策，也无可迁移到 LVR 的具体状态或监督线索，故排除。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** CRISP: Co-designing Pruning and Scheduling for Efficient MoE Inference on Edge Servers

**abstract/A1：** Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference on memory-constrained edge platforms is often dominated by expert-weight movement rather than computation. Although each token activates only a small subset of experts, routing is input-dependent, so all experts must remain accessible and memory movement dominates latency. Existing approaches such as uniform pruning and expert dropping overlook two properties of MoE execution: expert combinations are highly skewed in frequency, and experts contribute asymmetrically within each combination. We present CRISP, a combination-aware framework that co-designs pruning and runtime scheduling for efficient MoE serving on memory-constrained edge platforms. CRISP reorders neurons by importance so one stored expert can support multiple execution widths, assigns differentiated execution to hot, warm, and cold combinations based on frequency and expert role, and exploits heterogeneous widths to overlap expert loading with computation. Across Mixtral-8x7B and DeepSeek-MoE-16B, CRISP improves throughput by up to $2.1\\times$ at the same memory budget while limiting accuracy loss to 3.6\\% relative to full-width inference. Our results highlight that efficient edge MoE serving requires jointly optimizing which expert capacity is preserved and how expert execution is scheduled.

</details>

### 338. [M4Bench: Evaluating Procedural Specification for Clinical EHR Derivation Agents](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37459v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37459

**作者：** Hannes Ill、Rafi Al Attrach、Rajna Fani、Ahram Han、Ryohei Yamamoto、Marlene Louisa Moerig、Dukyong Yoon、Leo Anthony Celi

**arXiv 学科：** cs.AI

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37459v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37459v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** M4Bench 构建 28 个临床 EHR 派生变量任务，考察编码 agent 能否复现带时间窗与缺失规则的派生定义，并分解「临床医生审阅过的技能说明」带来的增益来源。

**排除原因：** 该工作评估的是文本规范完整性与 schema 熟悉度对代码 agent 的影响，不涉及视觉或多模态内部表示、latent 推理状态、证据获取或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少可核查的具体联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** M4Bench: Evaluating Procedural Specification for Clinical EHR Derivation Agents

**abstract/A1：** Clinical research over electronic health records depends on derived variables whose definitions sit outside the schema, in item codes, time windows, missingness rules, and output-grain choices that change cohorts and downstream conclusions. We introduce M4Bench, 28 clinical derivation tasks across 15 task families on MIMIC-IV and eICU, to test whether AI coding agents can reproduce such derivations and how clinician-reviewed agent skills change their results. Its controls decompose the value of skills into specification completeness, delivery format, and schema familiarity. In a Docker-isolated Codex campaign (GPT 5.4 Mini and GPT 5.5), skills raise reward by 0.136 over the no-skill baseline, with 26 of 28 tasks improving. Specification completeness accounts for most of this gain: on an eight-task control subset, an operational spec alone raises reward by 0.162, and skills add only 0.024 on top, indistinguishable from zero. On a ten-task subset, decoy skills score like no skill and raw reference SQL nearly matches targeted skills, arguing against generic context or the skill format as the explanation. Schema obfuscation and restructuring preserve 86% and 70% of the skill gain (GPT 5.4 Mini, 26 MIMIC-IV tasks), a descriptive robustness check rather than a bound on memorization. A descriptive Claude Code replication on all 28 tasks (Opus 4.7 and Sonnet 4.6, 560 runs) is directionally consistent (+0.120, 21 of 28 tasks improving). These gains are partial agreement with the reference, not completed tasks: only 25 of 280 with-skill and 13 of 280 no-skill Codex runs pass every strict check, and skills barely change which rows agents return (key F1 +0.003). Skills are released as a versioned, clinician-reviewable markdown delivery format, with a family-level reference-validity audit. M4Bench releases tasks, skills, evaluation code, and sanitized per-run evidence as an open-source benchmark.

**comment/C1：** Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 (Evaluations and Datasets Track). 10 pages main text, 33 pages total. Code: https://github.com/hannesill/m4/tree/m4bench-neurips2026-camera-ready/benchmark . Evidence: https://huggingface.co/datasets/hannesill/m4bench-evidence

</details>

### 339. [A note on data-efficient finetuning of vision foundation models](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37510v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37510

**作者：** Niclas Hergenröther、Antonio Orvieto

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37510v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37510v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**排除原因：** 该文系统比较 visual prompting、linear probing、LoRA 与全量微调在 Attention/卷积/Mamba 视觉基础模型上的数据效率，属通用迁移与刷分评测，不涉及推理期 latent 状态、视觉证据使用或停止决策，缺乏可供核查的具体借鉴线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** A note on data-efficient finetuning of vision foundation models

**abstract/A1：** Adapting foundation models to a new task within or beyond the training domain is the de facto standard for building specialized machine learning models. Recently, Visual Prompting (VP) has emerged as a promising technique for efficient knowledge transfer. As new foundation model families~(like Mamba) get introduced and VP pipelines such as AutoVP reach greater maturity, we find a growing need for a systematic evaluation of current approaches. In this work, we assess the performance of the latest models, comparing them to earlier architectures and alternative fine-tuning methods, to better understand the progress, challenges and opportunities in the field of efficient fine-tuning under resource limitations. Towards this goal, this paper provides a concise empirical overview of the interactions among foundation model families (Attention-, Convolution-, and Mamba-based) and transfer paradigms: VP, Linear Probing (LP), Full Fine-tuning (FFT) and Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA). Our work builds up on previous findings by broadening the selection of evaluated models and techniques while tuning hyperparameters. In the interest of delivering practical guidelines for the user, we also explore application of prevalent regularization techniques to boost performance in the context of VP.

**comment/C1：** 8 pages, 3 figures, Accepted at ES-FoMo III: 3rd Workshop on Efficient Systems for Foundation Models at ICML 2025

</details>

**title/T1：** Pneuma: A Large-Scale Real-World Time Series Benchmark for Commercial Aircraft Predictive Maintenance

**abstract/A1：** Predictive maintenance is vital for commercial aviation, yet progress remains hindered by the lack of public, large-scale real-world datasets. As a result, most methods are developed and evaluated on proprietary data, limiting reproducibility. We introduce Pneuma, the first publicly available large-scale benchmark for predictive maintenance using onboard time series from in-service commercial aircraft. Built around bleed air systems, Pneuma contains over 920 million multivariate time steps across four aircraft types and 9 verified fault scenarios, directly aligned with real maintenance logs. The dataset exhibits key real-world challenges, including substantial class imbalance, subtle fault evolution patterns, and significant variance across aircraft configurations. We structure the benchmark around three tasks: multivariate forecasting, supervised fault classification, and unsupervised anomaly detection, with labels derived from actual maintenance records. Under unified settings, we evaluate over 25 models from four methodological families on 9 carefully curated subsets. The results reveal that even strong contemporary models struggle with subtle fault signatures. Further analyses show consistent gains from additional in-context examples and multivariate inference, while forecasting quality is strongly associated with anomaly detection performance. Together, these findings provide practical insights into effectively modeling complex real-world aviation time series. We hope Pneuma will serve as a rigorous and reproducible foundation for advancing data-driven predictive maintenance toward real-world deployment in commercial aviation.

</details>

<a id="paper-341"></a>

### 341. [ReferStream: Interactive Subject Injection for Streaming Video Generation](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37566v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37566

**作者：** Shuolin Xu、Guanyuan Li、Boyu Li、Kelu Yao、Xiaogang Xu、Hongchuan Yu、Shuicheng Yan、Xiaobin Hu

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37566v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37566v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出 ReferStream，一种免训练框架：把参考主体的 latent 放进当前场景 latent 的目标区域以在原位提取特征，并用「瞬态缓存注入」在条件生成后恢复被覆盖的全局缓存，从而在流式视频生成中按计划注入新主体；同时构建带定时参考到达与主体存在标注的 ReferStream Bench 基准。

**排除原因：** 属于流式视频生成的应用与基准工作，其「Joint Latent」「缓存恢复」服务于视觉保真与上下文不干扰，而非推理状态生成、证据使用或停止决策，与 LVR 主线缺少实质联系。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** ReferStream: Interactive Subject Injection for Streaming Video Generation

**abstract/A1：** Injecting new subjects naturally during streaming video generation requires integrating newly supplied visual references into the existing video while preserving established subjects and the scene. Encoding references independently of the current scene provides little guidance for this integration, while keeping their features in persistent memory can interfere with established context. We propose ReferStream, a framework that requires no additional training and combines reference construction using the current scene with transient cache injection. Joint Latent places reference latents in target regions of the latest scene latent, allowing reference features to be extracted in context. Cache Restoration uses these features to condition the injection chunk, then restores the overwritten global cache entries while retaining the newly generated history. This procedure incorporates new visual conditions without retraining the generator or revising emitted frames. We further introduce ReferStream Bench, a new benchmark with scheduled reference arrivals, evolving instructions, and explicit annotations of subject presence for InShot and MultiShot generation. Evaluation follows the event schedule and measures both the fidelity of new subjects and the preservation of established subjects, alongside video quality, text adherence, and count accuracy. Experiments on ReferStream Bench show higher scores for text adherence, subject similarity, and count accuracy than the compared baselines, including offline methods given all references in advance. Project page: https://xushuolin.github.io/ReferStream/.

**comment/C1：** 18 pages. Project page: https://xushuolin.github.io/ReferStream/

</details>

### 342. [Honeycomb-Surfels: Compact Hexagonal Primitives for Efficient Scene Rendering and Reconstruction](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37668v1>)

**arXiv ID：** 2610.37668

**作者：** Prakhar Kaushik、Soumava Paul、Alan Yuille

**arXiv 学科：** cs.CV、cs.AI、cs.GR、cs.LG

[论文页面](<http://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37668v1>) · [PDF](<https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.37668v1>)

**研究分类：** EXCLUDE · 排除

**优先级：** NONE

摘要级简评；深入判断请结合原文人工阅读。

**内容简述：** 提出六边形平面基元「Hexels」，用一阶局部颜色场表示曲面外观，以更少更大的基元在低显存下提升新视角合成与三维表面重建质量。

**排除原因：** 该工作属于三维场景表示与渲染的基元设计与效率优化，不涉及多模态推理状态生成、视觉证据使用或停止决策，也未提供可迁移到 LVR 的具体方法线索。

<details>
<summary>来源材料：标题、摘要与备注</summary>

**title/T1：** Honeycomb-Surfels: Compact Hexagonal Primitives for Efficient Scene Rendering and Reconstruction

**abstract/A1：** Novel view synthesis and 3D reconstruction have advanced rapidly with radiance fields and splatting, yet their memory and computational demands limit their use in resource-constrained settings. We introduce Hexels: novel planar hexagonal surface elements with a first-order spatial color field for efficient 3D scene and 2D image representation. Hexels exploit locally planar regions with smoothly varying appearance using fewer, larger primitives. Their hexagonal support provides an area- and boundary-efficient domain, while the local color field captures appearance variation within each primitive. Across 34 scenes, Hexels improve PSNR over memory-matched 2DGS by 4.53-7.38 dB across datasets while requiring lower training VRAM. We further ablate Hexels by showing that per primitive, Hexels outperform 2DGS by upto 4.74 dB while using lower training VRAM and having 2.31 times faster FPS. Even in unconstrained settings, Hexels offers complementary quality-memory trade-offs. For efficient 3D surface reconstruction, Hexels reduce error by 28.9% compared with memory-matched 2DGS across 15 DTU scans. On 6 Tanks and Temples surface scenes, Hexels achieves the highest mean F1 among compared methods (34.38%, versus 31.72% for 2DGS). We further also show that Hexels, as a general primitive, improves 2D image representation over Image-GS. Hexels push the Pareto frontier on the quality-efficiency trade-off of surface-aligned primitives.

**comment/C1：** Project page at https://honeycomb-surfels.github.io/

</details>
